#include <windows.h>
#include <d3d12.h>
#include <dxgi1_6.h>
#include <wrl/client.h>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include "core/frame_contract.hpp"
using Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr;
int run_window(bool smoke);
int main(int argc,char** argv) {
    if(argc==1 || (argc==2 && std::strcmp(argv[1],"--ui")==0)) return run_window(false);
    if(argc==2 && std::strcmp(argv[1],"--ui-smoke")==0) return run_window(true);
    if(argc>2 || (argc==2 && std::strcmp(argv[1],"--status"))) {
        std::puts("Usage: NeuralAliasAbundance [--ui | --status | --ui-smoke]");return 2;
    }
    std::puts("NeuralAliasAbundance 0.1 - standalone DX12 foundation\nNo ReShade or Alien dependency.");
    ComPtr<IDXGIFactory6> factory;
    if(FAILED(CreateDXGIFactory2(0,IID_PPV_ARGS(&factory)))) { std::puts("DXGI initialization failed");return 1; }
    bool found=false;
    for(UINT i=0;;++i) {
        ComPtr<IDXGIAdapter1> adapter;
        const auto result=factory->EnumAdapterByGpuPreference(i,DXGI_GPU_PREFERENCE_HIGH_PERFORMANCE,IID_PPV_ARGS(&adapter));
        if(result==DXGI_ERROR_NOT_FOUND) break;
        if(FAILED(result)) return 1;
        DXGI_ADAPTER_DESC1 desc{};adapter->GetDesc1(&desc);
        if(desc.Flags & DXGI_ADAPTER_FLAG_SOFTWARE) continue;
        ComPtr<ID3D12Device> device;
        if(SUCCEEDED(D3D12CreateDevice(adapter.Get(),D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL_11_0,IID_PPV_ARGS(&device)))) {
            std::printf("DX12 GPU: %ls (%llu MiB dedicated memory)\n",desc.Description,
                static_cast<unsigned long long>(desc.DedicatedVideoMemory/(1024*1024)));found=true;
        }
    }
    std::puts("Input route: waiting for an observed upscaler call or a validated adapter.");
    std::puts("DLAA / DLSS: backend not connected to standalone host.");
    std::puts("Neural Rendering: experimental backend integration pending.");
    std::puts("Frame Generation: Not implemented yet [disabled].");
    std::puts("This diagnostic does not scan or modify games. Run abundance_dx12_fixture for GPU validation.");
    return found?0:1;
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\app\window.cpp ---

#include <windows.h>
#include <string>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cstdio>
namespace {
HWND fg=nullptr, route=nullptr, description=nullptr;
HFONT font=nullptr;
HWND control(HWND parent,const wchar_t* kind,const wchar_t* text,DWORD style,int x,int y,int w,int h,int id) {
    const auto result=CreateWindowExW(0,kind,text,WS_CHILD|WS_VISIBLE|style,x,y,w,h,parent,
        reinterpret_cast<HMENU>(static_cast<INT_PTR>(id)),GetModuleHandleW(nullptr),nullptr);
    if(result && font) SendMessageW(result,WM_SETFONT,reinterpret_cast<WPARAM>(font),TRUE);
    return result;
}
LRESULT CALLBACK procedure(HWND window,UINT message,WPARAM w,LPARAM l) {
    if(message==WM_CREATE) {
        font=static_cast<HFONT>(GetStockObject(DEFAULT_GUI_FONT));
        control(window,L"STATIC",L"NeuralAliasAbundance - DX12 foundation",0,24,20,580,24,0);
        control(window,L"STATIC",L"Standalone project. No ReShade or Alien: Isolation dependency.",0,24,52,580,24,0);
        control(window,L"STATIC",L"Input route preference (preview only):",0,24,96,560,24,0);
        route=control(window,L"COMBOBOX",L"",CBS_DROPDOWNLIST|WS_TABSTOP,24,124,560,160,100);
        for(const auto* label:{L"Automatic: validated upscaler, otherwise adapter",L"Existing upscaler integration",L"Engine/game adapter (games without an upscaler)"})
            SendMessageW(route,CB_ADDSTRING,0,reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(label));
        SendMessageW(route,CB_SETCURSEL,0,0);
        description=control(window,L"STATIC",L"No game attached. DLL presence alone does not establish usable inputs.",0,24,166,560,50,101);
        const auto sr=control(window,L"BUTTON",L"DLAA / DLSS - backend connection pending",BS_AUTOCHECKBOX,24,230,560,28,102);
        const auto nr=control(window,L"BUTTON",L"Neural Rendering - experimental integration pending",BS_AUTOCHECKBOX,24,268,560,28,103);
        fg=control(window,L"BUTTON",L"Frame Generation - Not implemented yet",BS_AUTOCHECKBOX,24,306,560,28,104);
        EnableWindow(sr,FALSE);EnableWindow(nr,FALSE);EnableWindow(fg,FALSE);
        control(window,L"STATIC",L"This first milestone tests inputs and DX12 independently.\r\nIt does not install anything into a game or enable enhancement effects.",0,24,356,560,56,0);
        control(window,L"BUTTON",L"Close",BS_DEFPUSHBUTTON|WS_TABSTOP,464,422,120,30,IDCANCEL);
        return 0;
    }
    if(message==WM_COMMAND) {
        if(LOWORD(w)==IDCANCEL) { DestroyWindow(window);return 0; }
        if(LOWORD(w)==100 && HIWORD(w)==CBN_SELCHANGE) {
            const auto selection=SendMessageW(route,CB_GETCURSEL,0,0);
            const wchar_t* text=selection==1?L"Requires observed compatible upscaler evaluations and validated resources.":
                selection==2?L"Requires a validated engine/game adapter. Optical flow alone does not supply depth or camera data.":
                L"Prefer validated upscaler inputs; fall back to a validated adapter. Otherwise remain inactive.";
            SetWindowTextW(description,text);return 0;
        }
    }
    if(message==WM_DESTROY) { PostQuitMessage(0);return 0; }
    return DefWindowProcW(window,message,w,l);
}
}
int run_window(bool smoke) {
    WNDCLASSW wc{};wc.lpfnWndProc=procedure;wc.hInstance=GetModuleHandleW(nullptr);
    wc.lpszClassName=L"NeuralAliasAbundanceSettings";wc.hCursor=LoadCursorW(nullptr,IDC_ARROW);
    wc.hbrBackground=reinterpret_cast<HBRUSH>(COLOR_WINDOW+1);
    if(!RegisterClassW(&wc)) return 1;
    const auto window=CreateWindowExW(0,wc.lpszClassName,L"NeuralAliasAbundance",WS_OVERLAPPED|WS_CAPTION|WS_SYSMENU|WS_MINIMIZEBOX,
        CW_USEDEFAULT,CW_USEDEFAULT,640,510,nullptr,nullptr,wc.hInstance,nullptr);
    if(!window) return 1;
    if(smoke) {
        bool ok=fg && !IsWindowEnabled(fg) && SendMessageW(fg,BM_GETCHECK,0,0)==BST_UNCHECKED;
        ok=ok && SendMessageW(route,CB_GETCOUNT,0,0)==3;
        for(int selection=0;selection<3;++selection) {
            SendMessageW(route,CB_SETCURSEL,static_cast<WPARAM>(selection),0);
            SendMessageW(window,WM_COMMAND,MAKEWPARAM(100,CBN_SELCHANGE),reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(route));
            ok=ok && GetWindowTextLengthW(description)>20;
        }
        DestroyWindow(window);std::puts(ok?"PASS: standalone settings, both routes, FG disabled":"FAIL: settings smoke");return ok?0:1;
    }
    ShowWindow(window,SW_SHOW);UpdateWindow(window);MSG message{};
    BOOL result;
    while((result=GetMessageW(&message,nullptr,0,0))>0) {
        if(!IsDialogMessageW(window,&message)) { TranslateMessage(&message);DispatchMessageW(&message); }
    }
    return result<0?1:0;
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\frame_contract.cpp ---

#include "frame_contract.hpp"
#include <cmath>
namespace abundance {
const char* route_name(Route route) {
    switch(route) {
    case Route::native_upscaler:return "Existing upscaler";
    case Route::adapter:return "Engine/game adapter";
    case Route::optical_flow_experimental:return "Experimental optical flow";
    default:return "No validated input provider";
    }
}
Route choose_route(ProviderEvidence native, ProviderEvidence adapter) {
    if(native.route==Route::native_upscaler && native.evaluation_observed && native.inputs_validated)
        return Route::native_upscaler;
    if(adapter.adapter_matched && adapter.inputs_validated &&
        (adapter.route==Route::adapter || adapter.route==Route::optical_flow_experimental))
        return adapter.route;
    return Route::none;
}
Decision validate_inputs(const Frame& f, Feature feature) {
    const auto reject=[](const char* text) { return Decision{false,false,text}; };
    if(feature==Feature::frame_generation) return reject("Frame Generation: not implemented yet");
    if(f.route==Route::none) return reject("No validated input provider");
    if(!f.id || !f.generation || !f.output.valid()) return reject("Invalid frame identity or output extent");
    if(!f.color.valid() || !f.motion.valid()) return reject("Missing color or motion input");
    const auto current=[&](const Image& image) { return image.frame==f.id && image.generation==f.generation; };
    if(!current(f.color) || !current(f.motion)) return reject("Stale color/motion resource");
    if(f.color_space==ColorSpace::unknown || f.motion_space==MotionSpace::unknown)
        return reject("Unknown color or motion convention");
    if(!f.clean_scene) return reject("HUD-free scene has not been validated");
    if(feature==Feature::reconstruction) {
        if(!f.depth.valid() || !current(f.depth)) return reject("Missing or stale depth");
        if(!(f.depth.extent==f.color.extent)) return reject("Depth/color extent mismatch");
        if(!(f.motion.extent==f.color.extent) && !(f.motion.extent==f.output))
            return reject("Unsupported motion extent");
        if(!f.jitter_known || !std::isfinite(f.jitter_x) || !std::isfinite(f.jitter_y))
            return reject("Unknown or invalid camera jitter");
        if(!f.exposure_policy_known) return reject("Exposure policy not validated");
        if(!f.motion_excludes_jitter) return reject("Motion jitter conversion required");
        if(f.output.width<f.color.extent.width || f.output.height<f.color.extent.height)
            return reject("Output smaller than reconstruction input");
        const double aspect=double(f.color.extent.width)*f.output.height /
            (double(f.color.extent.height)*f.output.width);
        if(aspect<.995 || aspect>1.005) return reject("Input/output aspect mismatch");
    }
    return {true,f.reset || f.camera_cut,"Input metadata accepted; resource contents require provider validation"};
}
Decision evaluate(const Frame& f, Feature feature, const BackendSupport& backends) {
    auto result=validate_inputs(f,feature);
    if(!result.ready) return result;
    if(feature==Feature::reconstruction && !backends.reconstruction)
        return {false,false,"Reconstruction backend not connected"};
    if(feature==Feature::neural_rendering && (!backends.neural_rendering || !backends.neural_contract_qualified))
        return {false,false,"Neural Rendering backend/contract not qualified"};
    return result;
}
Decision Continuity::observe(const Frame& f, Feature feature, const BackendSupport& backends) {
    auto result=evaluate(f,feature,backends);
    if(!result.ready) { valid_=false; return result; }
    result.reset=result.reset || !valid_ || f.id<=previous_.id || f.id-previous_.id!=1 ||
        f.generation!=previous_.generation || f.route!=previous_.route ||
        !(f.color.extent==previous_.color.extent) || !(f.output==previous_.output) ||
        f.color_space!=previous_.color_space || f.motion_space!=previous_.motion_space;
    previous_=f;valid_=true;
    return result;
}
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\frame_contract.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>

namespace abundance {
enum class Route { none, native_upscaler, adapter, optical_flow_experimental };
enum class Feature { reconstruction, neural_rendering, frame_generation };
enum class ColorSpace { unknown, linear_hdr, linear_sdr, srgb_sdr };
enum class MotionSpace { unknown, pixels_current_to_previous, normalized_current_to_previous };
struct Extent {
    uint32_t width=0, height=0;
    bool valid() const { return width>0 && height>0; }
    bool operator==(Extent b) const { return width==b.width && height==b.height; }
};
// Non-owning identity only. Providers must keep actual GPU resources alive until
// their submission fence completes; this metadata is not permission to release them.
struct Image {
    uint64_t identity=0, frame=0, generation=0;
    Extent extent{};
    bool valid() const { return identity!=0 && extent.valid(); }
};
struct Frame {
    uint64_t id=0, generation=0;
    Route route=Route::none;
    Image color{}, depth{}, motion{};
    Extent output{};
    ColorSpace color_space=ColorSpace::unknown;
    MotionSpace motion_space=MotionSpace::unknown;
    float jitter_x=0, jitter_y=0;
    bool jitter_known=false, exposure_policy_known=false;
    bool clean_scene=false, motion_excludes_jitter=false;
    bool reset=false, camera_cut=false;
};
struct ProviderEvidence {
    Route route=Route::none;
    // Merely finding a DLL is deliberately insufficient.
    bool evaluation_observed=false, inputs_validated=false, adapter_matched=false;
};
Route choose_route(ProviderEvidence native, ProviderEvidence adapter);
struct BackendSupport {
    bool reconstruction=false, neural_rendering=false;
    // Exact NR contract must be qualified separately from SR's requirements.
    bool neural_contract_qualified=false;
};
struct Decision { bool ready=false; bool reset=false; std::string reason; };
Decision validate_inputs(const Frame&, Feature);
Decision evaluate(const Frame&, Feature, const BackendSupport&);
class Continuity {
public:
    Decision observe(const Frame&, Feature, const BackendSupport&);
    void invalidate() { valid_=false; }
private:
    bool valid_=false;
    Frame previous_{};
};
const char* route_name(Route);
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\frame_history.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <cstdint>

namespace diagnostics {
enum class ResetReason { none, first_frame, missing_inputs, disabled, discontinuity,
    layout_change, display_reset, adapter_transition, manual };
inline const char* reset_name(ResetReason reason) {
    switch (reason) {
    case ResetReason::none: return "continuous";
    case ResetReason::first_frame: return "first usable frame";
    case ResetReason::missing_inputs: return "missing candidate inputs";
    case ResetReason::disabled: return "capture disabled";
    case ResetReason::discontinuity: return "frame/time discontinuity";
    case ResetReason::layout_change: return "input layout changed";
    case ResetReason::display_reset: return "display resources reset";
    case ResetReason::adapter_transition: return "adapter transition";
    case ResetReason::manual: return "manual reset";
    }
    return "unknown";
}
struct FrameObservation {
    uint64_t frame=0, time_ms=0;
    uint32_t width=0, height=0, color_format=0, depth_format=0, motion_format=0;
    bool enabled=true, usable=false;
};
struct HistoryDecision {
    bool usable=false, reset=false;
    uint64_t generation=0;
    ResetReason reason=ResetReason::first_frame;
};
// Metadata gate only. The caller must establish actual input validity. A backend
// must consume reset before evaluating this frame; this class owns no GPU history.
class FrameHistory {
public:
    void invalidate(ResetReason reason) {
        if (reason!=ResetReason::none) { pending_=reason; continuous_=false; }
    }
    HistoryDecision observe(const FrameObservation& frame) {
        if (!frame.enabled || !frame.usable || !frame.width || !frame.height) {
            if (continuous_ || pending_==ResetReason::first_frame)
                invalidate(frame.enabled ? ResetReason::missing_inputs : ResetReason::disabled);
            return {false,false,generation_,pending_};
        }
        if (continuous_) {
            if (frame.frame<=previous_.frame || frame.frame-previous_.frame!=1 ||
                frame.time_ms<previous_.time_ms || frame.time_ms-previous_.time_ms>250)
                invalidate(ResetReason::discontinuity);
            else if (frame.width!=previous_.width || frame.height!=previous_.height ||
                frame.color_format!=previous_.color_format || frame.depth_format!=previous_.depth_format ||
                frame.motion_format!=previous_.motion_format) invalidate(ResetReason::layout_change);
        }
        const bool reset=!continuous_;
        const auto reason=reset ? pending_ : ResetReason::none;
        if (reset) ++generation_;
        previous_=frame;continuous_=true;pending_=ResetReason::none;
        return {true,reset,generation_,reason};
    }
private:
    FrameObservation previous_{};
    bool continuous_=false;
    uint64_t generation_=0;
    ResetReason pending_=ResetReason::first_frame;
};
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\motion_arbiter.cpp ---

#include "core/motion_arbiter.hpp"

#include <cmath>

namespace na::motion {
namespace {

inline bool finite2(float a, float b) { return std::isfinite(a) && std::isfinite(b); }
inline bool field_ok(const Field2& f, uint32_t w, uint32_t h)
{
    return f.xy && f.width == w && f.height == h && f.stride_floats >= w * 2;
}
inline bool field_ok(const Field1& f, uint32_t w, uint32_t h)
{
    return f.v && f.width == w && f.height == h && f.stride_floats >= w;
}
inline float at1(const Field1& f, uint32_t x, uint32_t y)
{
    return f.v[static_cast<size_t>(y) * f.stride_floats + x];
}
inline const float* at2(const Field2& f, uint32_t x, uint32_t y)
{
    return f.xy + static_cast<size_t>(y) * f.stride_floats + x * 2;
}
// Validity-mask domain (rev2-R2): finite and in (0,1] authorizes; exactly 0
// means "not written"; anything else is malformed and never authorizes.
enum class MaskState { Valid, NotWritten, Malformed };
inline MaskState mask_state(float m)
{
    if (!std::isfinite(m) || m < 0.0f || m > 1.0f) return MaskState::Malformed;
    return m > 0.0f ? MaskState::Valid : MaskState::NotWritten;
}

inline void mul(const float* p, const Mat4& m, float* out)
{
    for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
        out[c] = p[0] * m.m[0 * 4 + c] + p[1] * m.m[1 * 4 + c] + p[2] * m.m[2 * 4 + c] + p[3] * m.m[3 * 4 + c];
}

// Snap/clamp; returns true if a non-zero residual remains. Sets `filtered`
// when either approximation altered the value.
inline bool shape_residual(float& rx, float& ry, const Config& cfg, Stats* s, bool& filtered)
{
    float mag = std::sqrt(rx * rx + ry * ry);
    if (mag < cfg.residual_snap_px) {
        if (mag != 0.0f) filtered = true;
        rx = ry = 0.0f;
        ++s->pixels_residual_snapped;
        return false;
    }
    if (mag > cfg.residual_max_px) {
        float k = cfg.residual_max_px / mag;
        rx *= k;
        ry *= k;
        ++s->pixels_residual_clamped;
        filtered = true;
    }
    return true;
}

} // namespace

bool ValidateConfig(const Config& cfg)
{
    return std::isfinite(cfg.residual_snap_px) && std::isfinite(cfg.residual_max_px) &&
           cfg.residual_snap_px >= 0.0f && cfg.residual_max_px >= cfg.residual_snap_px;
}

bool ReprojectPixel(const Tier2Input& t2, uint32_t width, uint32_t height, uint32_t x, uint32_t y,
                    float depth, float* vx, float* vy, bool* offscreen)
{
    if (!vx || !vy || width == 0 || height == 0 || x >= width || y >= height) return false;
    if (!std::isfinite(depth) || depth < 0.0f || depth > 1.0f || depth == t2.depth_clear) return false;
    if (!finite2(t2.jitter_current_px[0], t2.jitter_current_px[1])) return false;
    // The raster sample at this pixel center was taken under the current
    // frame's jitter: in the UNJITTERED projection it sits at
    // p_raster - jitter_current. Unproject there (rev2-R1); raw depth goes
    // straight through the supplier's actual inverse matrix.
    const float px = static_cast<float>(x) + 0.5f - t2.jitter_current_px[0];
    const float py = static_cast<float>(y) + 0.5f - t2.jitter_current_px[1];
    float ndc[4] = {px / width * 2.0f - 1.0f, 1.0f - py / height * 2.0f, depth, 1.0f};
    float world[4];
    mul(ndc, t2.inv_view_proj_current, world);
    if (!std::isfinite(world[3]) || std::fabs(world[3]) < 1e-20f) return false;
    for (float& c : world) c /= world[3];
    world[3] = 1.0f;
    float clip[4];
    mul(world, t2.view_proj_previous, clip);
    if (!std::isfinite(clip[3]) || clip[3] <= 0.0f) return false; // behind the previous camera
    float nx = clip[0] / clip[3], ny = clip[1] / clip[3];
    if (!finite2(nx, ny)) return false;
    float ppx = (nx + 1.0f) * 0.5f * width, ppy = (1.0f - ny) * 0.5f * height;
    *vx = ppx - px;
    *vy = ppy - py;
    if (offscreen) *offscreen = ppx < 0.0f || ppy < 0.0f || ppx >= width || ppy >= height;
    return finite2(*vx, *vy);
}

bool DeriveResidual(const Field2& raw_flow, const Tier2Input& t2, const float jitter_current_px[2],
                    const float jitter_previous_px[2], float* residual_out, float* valid_out,
                    uint32_t width, uint32_t height)
{
    if (!residual_out || !valid_out || !jitter_current_px || !jitter_previous_px ||
        !field_ok(raw_flow, width, height) || !t2.valid || !field_ok(t2.depth, width, height))
        return false;
    const float jdx = jitter_previous_px[0] - jitter_current_px[0];
    const float jdy = jitter_previous_px[1] - jitter_current_px[1];
    if (!finite2(jdx, jdy)) return false;
    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < height; ++y) {
        for (uint32_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
            const float* f = at2(raw_flow, x, y);
            float cx = 0, cy = 0;
            bool ok = ReprojectPixel(t2, width, height, x, y, at1(t2.depth, x, y), &cx, &cy, nullptr) &&
                      finite2(f[0], f[1]);
            size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(y) * width + x;
            residual_out[i * 2] = ok ? f[0] - cx - jdx : 0.0f;
            residual_out[i * 2 + 1] = ok ? f[1] - cy - jdy : 0.0f;
            valid_out[i] = ok ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool Arbitrate(const Tier1Input& t1, const Tier2Input& t2, const Tier3Input& t3, const Config& cfg,
               Output& out, Stats* stats)
{
    if (!out.xy || !out.provenance || out.width == 0 || out.height == 0 || !ValidateConfig(cfg)) return false;
    const uint32_t w = out.width, h = out.height;
    Stats local{};
    Stats* s = stats ? stats : &local;
    *s = Stats{};

    bool use1 = t1.valid && field_ok(t1.motion, w, h) && (!t1.valid_mask.v || field_ok(t1.valid_mask, w, h));
    bool use2 = t2.valid && field_ok(t2.depth, w, h);
    bool use3 = t3.valid && field_ok(t3.residual, w, h) && (!t3.mask.v || field_ok(t3.mask, w, h)) &&
                (!t3.valid_mask.v || field_ok(t3.valid_mask, w, h));
    const uint64_t all = static_cast<uint64_t>(w) * h;
    if (t1.valid && !use1) s->pixels_rejected += all;
    if (t2.valid && !use2) s->pixels_rejected += all;
    if (t3.valid && !use3) s->pixels_rejected += all;

    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < h; ++y) {
        for (uint32_t x = 0; x < w; ++x) {
            const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(y) * w + x;
            float* o = out.xy + i * 2;
            uint8_t& prov = out.provenance[i];
            o[0] = o[1] = 0.0f;
            prov = NA_MOTION_INVALID;

            if (use1) {
                MaskState ms = t1.valid_mask.v ? mask_state(at1(t1.valid_mask, x, y)) : MaskState::Valid;
                if (ms == MaskState::Malformed) ++s->pixels_rejected;
                if (ms == MaskState::Valid) {
                    const float* e = at2(t1.motion, x, y);
                    if (finite2(e[0], e[1])) {
                        o[0] = e[0];
                        o[1] = e[1];
                        prov = NA_MOTION_ENGINE;
                        ++s->pixels_engine;
                        continue;
                    }
                    ++s->pixels_rejected;
                }
            }

            bool have_cam = false, offscreen = false;
            float cx = 0, cy = 0;
            if (use2) {
                have_cam = ReprojectPixel(t2, w, h, x, y, at1(t2.depth, x, y), &cx, &cy, &offscreen);
                if (!have_cam) ++s->pixels_rejected;
            }
            if (!have_cam) {
                ++s->pixels_invalid;
                continue; // residual is meaningless without its camera vector
            }

            float rx = 0, ry = 0;
            bool have_res = false, filtered = false;
            if (use3) {
                MaskState vs = t3.valid_mask.v ? mask_state(at1(t3.valid_mask, x, y)) : MaskState::Valid;
                if (vs == MaskState::Malformed) ++s->pixels_rejected;
                bool rvalid = vs == MaskState::Valid;
                float m = t3.mask.v ? at1(t3.mask, x, y) : 1.0f;
                if (rvalid && std::isfinite(m) && m >= 0.0f && m <= 1.0f) {
                    if (m > 0.0f) {
                        const float* r = at2(t3.residual, x, y);
                        if (finite2(r[0], r[1])) {
                            rx = r[0] * m;
                            ry = r[1] * m;
                            have_res = shape_residual(rx, ry, cfg, s, filtered);
                        } else {
                            ++s->pixels_rejected;
                        }
                    }
                } else if (rvalid) {
                    ++s->pixels_rejected; // out-of-contract mask rejects the evidence
                }
            }

            o[0] = cx + rx;
            o[1] = cy + ry;
            prov = have_res ? NA_MOTION_CAMERA_RESIDUAL : NA_MOTION_CAMERA;
            if (filtered) prov |= NA_MOTION_F_FILTERED;
            if (offscreen) {
                prov |= NA_MOTION_F_OFFSCREEN;
                ++s->pixels_offscreen;
            }
            ++s->pixels_camera;
            if (have_res) ++s->pixels_residual_added;
        }
    }
    if (s->pixels_engine) s->tier_used = Tier::EngineDxil;
    else if (s->pixels_residual_added) s->tier_used = Tier::ResidualOpticalFlow;
    else if (s->pixels_camera) s->tier_used = Tier::CameraReprojection;
    else s->tier_used = Tier::None;
    return true;
}

} // namespace na::motion


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\motion_arbiter.hpp ---

// MotionArbiter â€” the Cascading Priority Motion Pipeline (proposal M-001, rev2
// after Route B's M-001-REVIEW).
//
// Purpose: produce a DLSS-grade per-pixel motion field for games that never
// generated motion vectors, by arbitrating three sources of decreasing trust:
//
//   Tier 1  Engine ground truth  â€” motion vectors injected via DXIL patching
//                                  of the game's own shaders (exact where valid).
//   Tier 2  Analytical camera    â€” reprojection of the depth buffer through the
//                                  previous and current camera matrices
//                                  (exact for static geometry; nothing for
//                                  moving objects).
//   Tier 3  Residual optical flowâ€” image-space flow AFTER camera motion has
//                                  been removed, masked by depth (so only
//                                  plausible moving-object regions contribute);
//                                  noisy, hence snapped/clamped = ESTIMATED.
//
// Arbitration per pixel:
//   Tier 1 valid at pixel       -> V = V_engine                 provenance ENGINE
//   else Tier 2 valid at pixel  -> V = V_cam [+ residual]       CAMERA / CAMERA_RESIDUAL
//   else                        -> V = 0                         INVALID
// Residual is applied ONLY where a valid camera vector exists at that pixel:
// camera-subtracted flow has no meaning as a total vector, so there is no
// residual-only fallback (review finding 5).
//
// Conventions (binding for every tier and for the output):
//   - Fields are row-major, render-resolution, 2 floats per pixel, in PIXELS.
//   - v = p_previous - p_current (where this pixel's content was one frame
//     ago), +x right, +y down; the value NGX consumes with MV scale (1,1).
//   - Matrices: row-major storage, ROW-VECTOR algebra (p' = p * M), DirectX.
//     Suppliers using column-vector algebra pass the transpose (Route B does).
//   - Matrices are UNJITTERED, but the raster depth samples were taken with
//     the current frame's raster jitter applied. So the sample at raster
//     position p_raster corresponds to unjittered-projection position
//     p_raster - jitter_current; the reprojection unprojects THERE and
//     returns v = p_prev_unjittered - (p_raster - jitter_current). Previous
//     jitter is never added to the vector (it belongs to a previous-sample
//     lookup, not to motion). (review findings 1 / rev2-R1)
//   - Depth is the RAW post-projection z/w in [0,1] and is fed through the
//     supplier's ACTUAL inverse view-projection; `depth_inverted` is metadata
//     (which end is near / which value is "clear"), never a numeric flip.
//     (review finding 2)
//   - Validity is per pixel: a pixel is invalid at a tier when its input is
//     absent, non-finite, out of range, at the clear depth, or reprojects
//     behind the camera. Invalid pixels produce v = 0 and provenance INVALID;
//     consumers must not read a zero as "stationary". (findings 3, 6)
//
// Supplier prerequisites the arbiter does NOT check (documented, enforced by
// the adapter): the two frames form a consecutive pair in one session/epoch,
// no resize/reset between them, all fields share the render subrect origin
// and extent. GPU-port validation tolerance: |Î”v| â‰¤ 1e-3 px per component;
// bit-exactness is NOT claimed for float math across CPU/GPU.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::motion {

enum class Tier : uint32_t { None = 0, EngineDxil = 1, CameraReprojection = 2, ResidualOpticalFlow = 3 };

struct Field2 {          // 2 floats per pixel
    const float* xy = nullptr;
    uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    uint32_t stride_floats = 0; // floats per row (>= width * 2)
};
struct Field1 {          // 1 float per pixel
    const float* v = nullptr;
    uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    uint32_t stride_floats = 0; // floats per row (>= width)
};
struct Mat4 { float m[16]; }; // row-major, row vectors

struct Tier1Input {
    bool valid = false;
    Field2 motion;           // engine motion, arbiter convention
    Field1 valid_mask;       // per-pixel: finite value in (0,1] = written by
                             // the engine; 0 = not written; anything else
                             // (NaN/Inf/<0/>1) = malformed -> INVALID; null = all
};

struct Tier2Input {
    bool valid = false;
    Field1 depth;            // raw z/w in [0,1]
    bool depth_inverted = false;
    float depth_clear = 1.0f;    // value meaning "no geometry" (0 if inverted)
    Mat4 inv_view_proj_current;  // clip -> world, current camera, unjittered
    Mat4 view_proj_previous;     // world -> clip, previous camera, unjittered
    float jitter_current_px[2] = {0, 0}; // raster jitter of the CURRENT frame,
                                          // pixels, same sign the game applied
                                          // to its projection (see header note)
};

struct Tier3Input {
    bool valid = false;
    Field2 residual;         // image flow with camera motion already removed
    Field1 mask;             // in [0,1]: 0 = ignore; null = 1 everywhere
    Field1 valid_mask;       // per-pixel validity, same domain rule as Tier 1's:
                             // finite (0,1] valid, 0 invalid, else malformed
};

struct Config {
    float residual_snap_px = 0.25f;  // |residual| below this -> 0 (approximation!)
    float residual_max_px = 64.0f;   // |residual| above this -> scaled to this
};

// Per-pixel provenance written alongside the vectors.
enum Provenance : uint8_t {
    NA_MOTION_INVALID = 0,          // no valid source; vector is 0, not "still"
    NA_MOTION_ENGINE = 1,           // Tier 1
    NA_MOTION_CAMERA = 2,           // Tier 2 only (static geometry assumption)
    NA_MOTION_CAMERA_RESIDUAL = 3,  // Tier 2 + Tier 3 (estimated)
};
enum ProvenanceFlags : uint8_t {
    NA_MOTION_F_FILTERED = 0x10,    // residual was snapped or clamped
    NA_MOTION_F_OFFSCREEN = 0x20,   // previous position lies outside the frame
};

struct Output {
    float* xy = nullptr;             // caller-owned, width*height*2
    uint8_t* provenance = nullptr;   // caller-owned, width*height (Provenance | flags)
    uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
};

struct Stats {
    Tier tier_used = Tier::None;
    uint64_t pixels_engine = 0;
    uint64_t pixels_camera = 0;
    uint64_t pixels_residual_added = 0;
    uint64_t pixels_residual_snapped = 0;
    uint64_t pixels_residual_clamped = 0;
    uint64_t pixels_offscreen = 0;
    uint64_t pixels_invalid = 0;     // no valid tier at that pixel
    uint64_t pixels_rejected = 0;    // inputs rejected (non-finite/out of contract)
};

// Validates config (finite, non-negative, max >= snap). Arbitrate rejects an
// invalid config.
bool ValidateConfig(const Config& cfg);

// Runs the arbitration for one frame. A tier whose extent mismatches `out`
// is rejected as a whole. Returns false only if `out` or `cfg` is unusable.
bool Arbitrate(const Tier1Input& t1, const Tier2Input& t2, const Tier3Input& t3,
               const Config& cfg, Output& out, Stats* stats);

// Analytical reprojection for one pixel: v = p_prev - p_cur in pixels using
// unjittered pixel centers. Fails (returns false) for depth outside [0,1] or
// at the clear value, non-finite math, or a previous position behind the
// camera (w <= 0). `offscreen` (optional) reports a previous position outside
// the frame; the vector is still returned for such pixels.
bool ReprojectPixel(const Tier2Input& t2, uint32_t width, uint32_t height,
                    uint32_t x, uint32_t y, float depth, float* vx, float* vy,
                    bool* offscreen);

// For suppliers with RAW image flow measured on JITTERED frames:
//   residual = raw_flow - V_cam - (jitter_prev - jitter_cur)
// Writes residual and a per-pixel validity (1 where the camera vector was
// valid, else 0 with residual 0). Returns false on contract violations.
bool DeriveResidual(const Field2& raw_flow, const Tier2Input& t2,
                    const float jitter_current_px[2], const float jitter_previous_px[2],
                    float* residual_out, float* valid_out, uint32_t width, uint32_t height);

} // namespace na::motion


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\motion_arbiter_gpu.cpp ---

#include "core/motion_arbiter_gpu.hpp"

#include <d3dcompiler.h>

#include <cstring>

using Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr;

namespace na::motion::gpu {
namespace {

// The shader mirrors motion_arbiter.cpp. Comments cite the CPU lines.
const char kSource[] = R"(
Texture2D<float2> t_engine   : register(t0);
Texture2D<float>  t_evalid   : register(t1);
Texture2D<float>  t_depth    : register(t2);
Texture2D<float2> t_residual : register(t3);
Texture2D<float>  t_mask     : register(t4);
Texture2D<float>  t_rvalid   : register(t5);
RWTexture2D<float2> u_xy     : register(u0);
RWTexture2D<uint>   u_prov   : register(u1);
RWByteAddressBuffer u_stats  : register(u2);
RWTexture2D<float>  u_valid  : register(u3);   // DeriveResidual: per-pixel validity

cbuffer C : register(b0) {
    row_major float4x4 inv_vp_cur;   // row-major storage (same bytes as the CPU Mat4), row-vector algebra
    row_major float4x4 vp_prev;
    float2 jitter_cur;
    float  depth_clear;
    float  snap_px;
    float  max_px;
    uint   width;
    uint   height;
    uint   flags;          // bit0 tier1, bit1 tier1 mask, bit2 tier2, bit3 tier3, bit4 tier3 mask, bit5 tier3 valid, bit6 stats
    float2 jitter_prev;    // DeriveResidual only
};

#define F_T1      1u
#define F_T1MASK  2u
#define F_T2      4u
#define F_T3      8u
#define F_T3MASK  16u
#define F_T3VALID 32u
#define F_STATS   64u

#define PROV_INVALID 0u
#define PROV_ENGINE 1u
#define PROV_CAMERA 2u
#define PROV_CAMERA_RESIDUAL 3u
#define PROV_F_FILTERED 0x10u
#define PROV_F_OFFSCREEN 0x20u

// Bit test: exponent all ones = Inf or NaN. isnan()/isinf() can be folded away by compilers/drivers.
bool finite1(float a) { return (asuint(a) & 0x7fffffffu) < 0x7f800000u; }
bool finite2(float2 a) { return finite1(a.x) && finite1(a.y); }
void stat(uint slot) { if (flags & F_STATS) { uint old; u_stats.InterlockedAdd(slot * 4, 1u, old); } }

// mask_state: 0 valid, 1 not written, 2 malformed (motion_arbiter.cpp:28)
uint mask_state(float m) {
    if (!finite1(m) || m < 0.0 || m > 1.0) return 2u;
    return m > 0.0 ? 0u : 1u;
}

float4 mulrow(float4 p, float4x4 m) {
    // row-vector product, same summation order as the CPU (p0*m0c + p1*m1c + p2*m2c + p3*m3c)
    return float4(
        p.x * m._11 + p.y * m._21 + p.z * m._31 + p.w * m._41,
        p.x * m._12 + p.y * m._22 + p.z * m._32 + p.w * m._42,
        p.x * m._13 + p.y * m._23 + p.z * m._33 + p.w * m._43,
        p.x * m._14 + p.y * m._24 + p.z * m._34 + p.w * m._44);
}

// ReprojectPixel (motion_arbiter.cpp:69-97)
bool reproject(uint x, uint y, float depth, out float2 v, out bool offscreen) {
    v = 0; offscreen = false;
    if (!finite1(depth) || depth < 0.0 || depth > 1.0 || depth == depth_clear) return false;
    if (!finite2(jitter_cur)) return false;
    float px = (float)x + 0.5 - jitter_cur.x;
    float py = (float)y + 0.5 - jitter_cur.y;
    float4 ndc = float4(px / width * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - py / height * 2.0, depth, 1.0);
    float4 world = mulrow(ndc, inv_vp_cur);
    if (!finite1(world.w) || abs(world.w) < 1e-20) return false;
    world /= world.w;
    world.w = 1.0;
    float4 clip = mulrow(world, vp_prev);
    if (!finite1(clip.w) || clip.w <= 0.0) return false;
    float nx = clip.x / clip.w, ny = clip.y / clip.w;
    if (!finite2(float2(nx, ny))) return false;
    float ppx = (nx + 1.0) * 0.5 * width, ppy = (1.0 - ny) * 0.5 * height;
    v = float2(ppx - px, ppy - py);
    offscreen = ppx < 0.0 || ppy < 0.0 || ppx >= width || ppy >= height;
    return finite2(v);
}

[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void main(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    uint2 p = id.xy;
    float2 o = 0;
    uint prov = PROV_INVALID;

    // ---- Tier 1 (motion_arbiter.cpp:150-164)
    if (flags & F_T1) {
        uint ms = (flags & F_T1MASK) ? mask_state(t_evalid[p]) : 0u;
        if (ms == 2u) stat(7);
        if (ms == 0u) {
            float2 e = t_engine[p];
            if (finite2(e)) {
                u_xy[p] = e;
                u_prov[p] = PROV_ENGINE;
                stat(0);
                return;
            }
            stat(7);
        }
    }

    // ---- Tier 2 (166-175)
    bool have_cam = false, offscreen = false;
    float2 c = 0;
    if (flags & F_T2) {
        have_cam = reproject(p.x, p.y, t_depth[p], c, offscreen);
        if (!have_cam) stat(7);
    }
    if (!have_cam) {
        u_xy[p] = 0;
        u_prov[p] = PROV_INVALID;
        stat(6);
        return;
    }

    // ---- Tier 3 (177-198) with shape_residual (42-59)
    float2 r = 0;
    bool have_res = false, filtered = false;
    if (flags & F_T3) {
        uint vs = (flags & F_T3VALID) ? mask_state(t_rvalid[p]) : 0u;
        if (vs == 2u) stat(7);
        bool rvalid = vs == 0u;
        float m = (flags & F_T3MASK) ? t_mask[p] : 1.0;
        if (rvalid && finite1(m) && m >= 0.0 && m <= 1.0) {
            if (m > 0.0) {
                float2 rr = t_residual[p];
                if (finite2(rr)) {
                    r = rr * m;
                    float mag = sqrt(r.x * r.x + r.y * r.y);
                    if (mag < snap_px) {
                        if (mag != 0.0) filtered = true;
                        r = 0;
                        stat(3);
                        have_res = false;
                    } else {
                        if (mag > max_px) {
                            float k = max_px / mag;
                            r *= k;
                            stat(4);
                            filtered = true;
                        }
                        have_res = true;
                    }
                } else {
                    stat(7);
                }
            }
        } else if (rvalid) {
            stat(7);
        }
    }

    o = c + r;
    prov = have_res ? PROV_CAMERA_RESIDUAL : PROV_CAMERA;
    if (filtered) prov |= PROV_F_FILTERED;
    if (offscreen) { prov |= PROV_F_OFFSCREEN; stat(5); }
    stat(1);
    if (have_res) stat(2);
    u_xy[p] = o;
    u_prov[p] = prov;
}

// DeriveResidual (motion_arbiter.cpp:99-122): residual = raw_flow - V_cam - (jitter_prev - jitter_cur);
// valid = 1 where the camera vector exists, the raw flow is finite and (flag F_T3VALID)
// the supplied raw validity t_rvalid is in the mask domain's valid range; else 0 with residual 0.
// Inputs: t_residual = RAW flow (RG32), t_depth, optional t_rvalid; outputs u_xy = residual, u_valid.
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void derive_residual(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    uint2 p = id.xy;
    float2 jd = jitter_prev - jitter_cur;
    float2 f = t_residual[p];
    float2 c = 0; bool off = false;
    bool rv_ok = (flags & F_T3VALID) ? (mask_state(t_rvalid[p]) == 0u) : true;
    bool ok = rv_ok && finite2(jd) && reproject(p.x, p.y, t_depth[p], c, off) && finite2(f);
    u_xy[p] = ok ? f - c - jd : float2(0, 0);
    u_valid[p] = ok ? 1.0 : 0.0;
}
)";

struct Constants {
    float inv_vp_cur[16];
    float vp_prev[16];
    float jitter[2];
    float depth_clear, snap_px, max_px;
    uint32_t width, height, flags;
    float jitter_prev[2];
};
static_assert(sizeof(Constants) == 42 * 4, "root constant size");

bool create_dummy(ID3D12Device* dev, DXGI_FORMAT fmt, ComPtr<ID3D12Resource>& out)
{
    D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp{};
    hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_DEFAULT;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d{};
    d.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    d.Width = 1;
    d.Height = 1;
    d.DepthOrArraySize = 1;
    d.MipLevels = 1;
    d.Format = fmt;
    d.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    return SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &d,
                                                  D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, nullptr,
                                                  IID_PPV_ARGS(&out)));
}

} // namespace

bool Arbiter::Init(ID3D12Device* device, uint32_t max_frames_in_flight)
{
    if (!device || max_frames_in_flight == 0) return false;
    device_ = device;
    slots_ = max_frames_in_flight;

    D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE ranges[2]{};
    ranges[0].RangeType = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE_TYPE_SRV;
    ranges[0].NumDescriptors = 6;
    ranges[0].BaseShaderRegister = 0;
    ranges[0].OffsetInDescriptorsFromTableStart = 0;
    ranges[1].RangeType = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE_TYPE_UAV;
    ranges[1].NumDescriptors = 4;
    ranges[1].BaseShaderRegister = 0;
    ranges[1].OffsetInDescriptorsFromTableStart = 6;
    D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER params[2]{};
    params[0].ParameterType = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_TABLE;
    params[0].DescriptorTable.NumDescriptorRanges = 2;
    params[0].DescriptorTable.pDescriptorRanges = ranges;
    params[0].ShaderVisibility = D3D12_SHADER_VISIBILITY_ALL;
    params[1].ParameterType = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_32BIT_CONSTANTS;
    params[1].Constants.Num32BitValues = sizeof(Constants) / 4;
    params[1].Constants.ShaderRegister = 0;
    params[1].ShaderVisibility = D3D12_SHADER_VISIBILITY_ALL;
    D3D12_ROOT_SIGNATURE_DESC rs{};
    rs.NumParameters = 2;
    rs.pParameters = params;
    ComPtr<ID3DBlob> sig, err;
    if (FAILED(D3D12SerializeRootSignature(&rs, D3D_ROOT_SIGNATURE_VERSION_1, &sig, &err))) return false;
    if (FAILED(device->CreateRootSignature(0, sig->GetBufferPointer(), sig->GetBufferSize(), IID_PPV_ARGS(&root_))))
        return false;

    ComPtr<ID3DBlob> code;
    // Strict IEEE so NaN/Inf checks behave like the CPU (no fast-math folding).
    UINT cf = D3DCOMPILE_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL3 | D3DCOMPILE_IEEE_STRICTNESS;
    if (FAILED(D3DCompile(kSource, sizeof(kSource) - 1, "motion_arbiter.hlsl", nullptr, nullptr, "main", "cs_5_0", cf, 0,
                          &code, &err)))
        return false;
    D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC pd{};
    pd.pRootSignature = root_.Get();
    pd.CS.pShaderBytecode = code->GetBufferPointer();
    pd.CS.BytecodeLength = code->GetBufferSize();
    if (FAILED(device->CreateComputePipelineState(&pd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&pso_)))) return false;
    ComPtr<ID3DBlob> code2;
    if (FAILED(D3DCompile(kSource, sizeof(kSource) - 1, "motion_arbiter.hlsl", nullptr, nullptr, "derive_residual", "cs_5_0", cf, 0,
                          &code2, &err)))
        return false;
    pd.CS.pShaderBytecode = code2->GetBufferPointer();
    pd.CS.BytecodeLength = code2->GetBufferSize();
    if (FAILED(device->CreateComputePipelineState(&pd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&pso_derive_)))) return false;

    D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_DESC hd{};
    hd.Type = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE_CBV_SRV_UAV;
    hd.NumDescriptors = 10 * slots_;
    hd.Flags = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_FLAG_SHADER_VISIBLE;
    if (FAILED(device->CreateDescriptorHeap(&hd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&heap_)))) return false;
    stride_ = device->GetDescriptorHandleIncrementSize(D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE_CBV_SRV_UAV);

    if (!create_dummy(device, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, dummy_) || !create_dummy(device, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT, dummy2_))
        return false;
    {
        D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp{};
        hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_DEFAULT;
        D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d{};
        d.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
        d.Width = 1; d.Height = 1; d.DepthOrArraySize = 1; d.MipLevels = 1;
        d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT; d.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
        d.Flags = D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_ALLOW_UNORDERED_ACCESS;
        if (FAILED(device->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &d, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS,
                                                   nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&dummy_uav_))))
            return false;
    }
    D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp{};
    hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_DEFAULT;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC bd{};
    bd.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
    bd.Width = 32;
    bd.Height = bd.DepthOrArraySize = bd.MipLevels = 1;
    bd.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    bd.Layout = D3D12_TEXTURE_LAYOUT_ROW_MAJOR;
    bd.Flags = D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_ALLOW_UNORDERED_ACCESS;
    return SUCCEEDED(device->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &bd, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS,
                                                     nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&dummy_stats_)));
}

bool Arbiter::Dispatch(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, const Inputs& in, const Outputs& out, uint32_t frame_slot)
{
    if (!list || !pso_ || !out.xy || !out.provenance || out.width == 0 || out.height == 0 || frame_slot >= slots_)
        return false;
    if (!ValidateConfig(in.config)) return false;
    // Extent checks mirror field_ok(): a tier whose extent mismatches is rejected as a whole.
    auto extent_ok = [&](ID3D12Resource* r) {
        if (!r) return false;
        D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d = r->GetDesc();
        return d.Width == out.width && d.Height == out.height;
    };
    uint32_t flags = 0;
    bool use1 = in.tier1_valid_flag && extent_ok(in.tier1_motion) && (!in.tier1_valid || extent_ok(in.tier1_valid));
    bool use2 = in.tier2_valid_flag && extent_ok(in.depth);
    bool use3 = in.tier3_valid_flag && extent_ok(in.tier3_residual) && (!in.tier3_mask || extent_ok(in.tier3_mask)) &&
                (!in.tier3_valid || extent_ok(in.tier3_valid));
    if (use1) flags |= 1u;
    if (use1 && in.tier1_valid) flags |= 2u;
    if (use2) flags |= 4u;
    if (use3) flags |= 8u;
    if (use3 && in.tier3_mask) flags |= 16u;
    if (use3 && in.tier3_valid) flags |= 32u;
    if (out.stats) flags |= 64u;
    // Whole-tier rejections are counted by the caller-visible stats exactly
    // like the CPU (motion_arbiter.cpp:138-140): add width*height per rejected tier.
    uint32_t whole_rejects = 0;
    if (in.tier1_valid_flag && !use1) ++whole_rejects;
    if (in.tier2_valid_flag && !use2) ++whole_rejects;
    if (in.tier3_valid_flag && !use3) ++whole_rejects;

    uint32_t base = frame_slot * 10;
    auto cpu = [&](uint32_t i) {
        D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE h = heap_->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
        h.ptr += static_cast<SIZE_T>(base + i) * stride_;
        return h;
    };
    auto srv = [&](uint32_t i, ID3D12Resource* r, DXGI_FORMAT fmt, ID3D12Resource* fallback) {
        D3D12_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC d{};
        d.Format = fmt;
        d.ViewDimension = D3D12_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
        d.Shader4ComponentMapping = D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING;
        d.Texture2D.MipLevels = 1;
        device_->CreateShaderResourceView(r ? r : fallback, &d, cpu(i));
    };
    srv(0, use1 ? in.tier1_motion : nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT, dummy2_.Get());
    srv(1, use1 ? in.tier1_valid : nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, dummy_.Get());
    srv(2, use2 ? in.depth : nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, dummy_.Get());
    srv(3, use3 ? in.tier3_residual : nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT, dummy2_.Get());
    srv(4, use3 ? in.tier3_mask : nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, dummy_.Get());
    srv(5, use3 ? in.tier3_valid : nullptr, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, dummy_.Get());
    {
        D3D12_UNORDERED_ACCESS_VIEW_DESC d{};
        d.ViewDimension = D3D12_UAV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
        d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT;
        device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(out.xy, nullptr, &d, cpu(6));
        d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UINT;
        device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(out.provenance, nullptr, &d, cpu(7));
        D3D12_UNORDERED_ACCESS_VIEW_DESC b{};
        b.ViewDimension = D3D12_UAV_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
        b.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_TYPELESS;
        b.Buffer.NumElements = 8;
        b.Buffer.Flags = D3D12_BUFFER_UAV_FLAG_RAW;
        device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(out.stats ? out.stats : dummy_stats_.Get(), nullptr, &b, cpu(8));
        d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT;
        device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(dummy_uav_.Get(), nullptr, &d, cpu(9)); // u3 unused by main
    }

    Constants c{};
    std::memcpy(c.inv_vp_cur, in.inv_view_proj_current.m, sizeof(c.inv_vp_cur));
    std::memcpy(c.vp_prev, in.view_proj_previous.m, sizeof(c.vp_prev));
    c.jitter[0] = in.jitter_current_px[0];
    c.jitter[1] = in.jitter_current_px[1];
    c.depth_clear = in.depth_clear;
    c.snap_px = in.config.residual_snap_px;
    c.max_px = in.config.residual_max_px;
    c.width = out.width;
    c.height = out.height;
    c.flags = flags;

    ID3D12DescriptorHeap* heaps[] = {heap_.Get()};
    list->SetDescriptorHeaps(1, heaps);
    list->SetComputeRootSignature(root_.Get());
    list->SetPipelineState(pso_.Get());
    D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE g = heap_->GetGPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
    g.ptr += static_cast<UINT64>(base) * stride_;
    list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, g);
    list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, sizeof(Constants) / 4, &c, 0);
    list->Dispatch((out.width + 7) / 8, (out.height + 7) / 8, 1);
    // Whole-tier rejections are known on the CPU side (extent checks) and are
    // added by DecodeStats, exactly like motion_arbiter.cpp:138-140.
    whole_rejects_last_ = whole_rejects * static_cast<uint64_t>(out.width) * out.height;
    return true;
}

bool Arbiter::DispatchDeriveResidual(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, ID3D12Resource* raw_flow, ID3D12Resource* depth,
                                     const Inputs& camera, const float jitter_previous_px[2], ID3D12Resource* residual_out,
                                     ID3D12Resource* valid_out, uint32_t width, uint32_t height, uint32_t frame_slot,
                                     ID3D12Resource* raw_valid)
{
    if (!list || !pso_derive_ || !raw_flow || !depth || !residual_out || !valid_out || !jitter_previous_px ||
        width == 0 || height == 0 || frame_slot >= slots_ || !camera.tier2_valid_flag)
        return false;
    auto extent_ok = [&](ID3D12Resource* r) {
        D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d = r->GetDesc();
        return d.Width == width && d.Height == height;
    };
    if (!extent_ok(raw_flow) || !extent_ok(depth) || !extent_ok(residual_out) || !extent_ok(valid_out)) return false;
    if (raw_valid && !extent_ok(raw_valid)) return false;

    uint32_t base = frame_slot * 10;
    auto cpu = [&](uint32_t i) {
        D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE h = heap_->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
        h.ptr += static_cast<SIZE_T>(base + i) * stride_;
        return h;
    };
    auto srv = [&](uint32_t i, ID3D12Resource* r, DXGI_FORMAT fmt) {
        D3D12_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC d{};
        d.Format = fmt;
        d.ViewDimension = D3D12_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
        d.Shader4ComponentMapping = D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING;
        d.Texture2D.MipLevels = 1;
        device_->CreateShaderResourceView(r, &d, cpu(i));
    };
    srv(0, dummy2_.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT);
    srv(1, dummy_.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT);
    srv(2, depth, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT);
    srv(3, raw_flow, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT);
    srv(4, dummy_.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT);
    srv(5, raw_valid ? raw_valid : dummy_.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT);
    D3D12_UNORDERED_ACCESS_VIEW_DESC d{};
    d.ViewDimension = D3D12_UAV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT;
    device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(residual_out, nullptr, &d, cpu(6));
    d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UINT;
    // u1 (provenance) is unused by derive_residual; bind a dummy typed UAV.
    d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT;
    device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(dummy_uav_.Get(), nullptr, &d, cpu(7));
    D3D12_UNORDERED_ACCESS_VIEW_DESC b{};
    b.ViewDimension = D3D12_UAV_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
    b.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_TYPELESS;
    b.Buffer.NumElements = 8;
    b.Buffer.Flags = D3D12_BUFFER_UAV_FLAG_RAW;
    device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(dummy_stats_.Get(), nullptr, &b, cpu(8));
    d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT;
    device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(valid_out, nullptr, &d, cpu(9));

    Constants c{};
    std::memcpy(c.inv_vp_cur, camera.inv_view_proj_current.m, sizeof(c.inv_vp_cur));
    std::memcpy(c.vp_prev, camera.view_proj_previous.m, sizeof(c.vp_prev));
    c.jitter[0] = camera.jitter_current_px[0];
    c.jitter[1] = camera.jitter_current_px[1];
    c.jitter_prev[0] = jitter_previous_px[0];
    c.jitter_prev[1] = jitter_previous_px[1];
    c.depth_clear = camera.depth_clear;
    c.snap_px = 0.f;
    c.max_px = 0.f;
    c.width = width;
    c.height = height;
    c.flags = 4u | (raw_valid ? 32u : 0u); // tier 2 (+ raw validity supplied)
    ID3D12DescriptorHeap* heaps[] = {heap_.Get()};
    list->SetDescriptorHeaps(1, heaps);
    list->SetComputeRootSignature(root_.Get());
    list->SetPipelineState(pso_derive_.Get());
    D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE g = heap_->GetGPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
    g.ptr += static_cast<UINT64>(base) * stride_;
    list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, g);
    list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, sizeof(Constants) / 4, &c, 0);
    list->Dispatch((width + 7) / 8, (height + 7) / 8, 1);
    return true;
}

Stats Arbiter::DecodeStats(const uint32_t counters[8]) const
{
    Stats s{};
    s.pixels_engine = counters[STAT_ENGINE];
    s.pixels_camera = counters[STAT_CAMERA];
    s.pixels_residual_added = counters[STAT_RESIDUAL_ADDED];
    s.pixels_residual_snapped = counters[STAT_RESIDUAL_SNAPPED];
    s.pixels_residual_clamped = counters[STAT_RESIDUAL_CLAMPED];
    s.pixels_offscreen = counters[STAT_OFFSCREEN];
    s.pixels_invalid = counters[STAT_INVALID];
    s.pixels_rejected = counters[STAT_REJECTED] + whole_rejects_last_;
    if (s.pixels_engine) s.tier_used = Tier::EngineDxil;
    else if (s.pixels_residual_added) s.tier_used = Tier::ResidualOpticalFlow;
    else if (s.pixels_camera) s.tier_used = Tier::CameraReprojection;
    else s.tier_used = Tier::None;
    return s;
}

} // namespace na::motion::gpu


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\motion_arbiter_gpu.hpp ---

// MotionArbiter GPU port (M-001, A34). A D3D12 compute pass that runs the
// SAME arbitration as the CPU reference in motion_arbiter.cpp, per pixel,
// with the same conventions, rejection rules, provenance bits and stats.
//
// Contract with the CPU reference (validated by tests/motion_arbiter_gpu_test):
//   |Î”v| â‰¤ 1e-3 px per component, provenance identical, stats identical.
//   Bit-exactness of the float math is NOT claimed (different fma/ordering).
//
// Resource conventions (all render-resolution, extent == output extent):
//   tier1 motion       RG32_FLOAT   SRV   (optional; flag TIER1)
//   tier1 valid mask   R32_FLOAT    SRV   (optional; flag TIER1_MASK)
//   depth              R32_FLOAT    SRV   (optional; flag TIER2) raw z/w
//   tier3 residual     RG32_FLOAT   SRV   (optional; flag TIER3)
//   tier3 mask         R32_FLOAT    SRV   (optional; flag TIER3_MASK)
//   tier3 valid mask   R32_FLOAT    SRV   (optional; flag TIER3_VALID)
//   output xy          RG32_FLOAT   UAV
//   output provenance  R8_UINT      UAV
//   stats              32-byte raw UAV buffer of 8 uint32 counters (optional)
// All SRVs must be in NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, UAVs in UNORDERED_ACCESS,
// when Dispatch records. The caller owns every resource and all barriers
// before/after; Dispatch records only the dispatch (no waits, no readback).
#pragma once

#include <d3d12.h>
#include <wrl/client.h>

#include <cstdint>

#include "core/motion_arbiter.hpp"

namespace na::motion::gpu {

struct Inputs {
    ID3D12Resource* tier1_motion = nullptr;
    ID3D12Resource* tier1_valid = nullptr;
    ID3D12Resource* depth = nullptr;
    ID3D12Resource* tier3_residual = nullptr;
    ID3D12Resource* tier3_mask = nullptr;
    ID3D12Resource* tier3_valid = nullptr;
    // Same meaning as the CPU structs; `valid` flags decide which tiers run.
    bool tier1_valid_flag = false;
    bool tier2_valid_flag = false;
    bool tier3_valid_flag = false;
    float depth_clear = 1.0f;
    Mat4 inv_view_proj_current{};
    Mat4 view_proj_previous{};
    float jitter_current_px[2] = {0, 0};
    Config config{};
};

struct Outputs {
    ID3D12Resource* xy = nullptr;          // RG32_FLOAT, UAV
    ID3D12Resource* provenance = nullptr;  // R8_UINT, UAV
    ID3D12Resource* stats = nullptr;       // optional raw buffer, >= 32 bytes, UAV, zeroed by caller
    uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
};

// Order of the 8 counters in the stats buffer (uint32 each).
enum StatSlot : uint32_t {
    STAT_ENGINE = 0, STAT_CAMERA = 1, STAT_RESIDUAL_ADDED = 2, STAT_RESIDUAL_SNAPPED = 3,
    STAT_RESIDUAL_CLAMPED = 4, STAT_OFFSCREEN = 5, STAT_INVALID = 6, STAT_REJECTED = 7
};

class Arbiter {
public:
    // Compiles the shader (d3dcompiler_47) and creates the pipeline. Returns
    // false on any failure; the object is then unusable.
    bool Init(ID3D12Device* device, uint32_t max_frames_in_flight = 4);
    // Records one dispatch on `list`. `frame_slot` selects a descriptor
    // block so up to max_frames_in_flight dispatches can be in flight.
    // Returns false for a null/invalid input (nothing recorded).
    bool Dispatch(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, const Inputs& in, const Outputs& out, uint32_t frame_slot);
    // DeriveResidual on the GPU (mirrors na::motion::DeriveResidual): for
    // suppliers with RAW image flow measured on jittered frames,
    //   residual = raw_flow - V_cam - (jitter_prev - jitter_cur),
    // valid = 1 where the camera vector exists, the flow is finite AND (when
    // `raw_valid` is given) the supplier's/expansion's validity at the pixel
    // is in the mask domain's valid range (finite (0,1]). This is how
    // expansion validity and supplier validity are folded into ONE tier-3
    // validity: a merely finite raw vector is never trusted on its own.
    // `raw_flow` RG32_FLOAT SRV, `depth` R32_FLOAT SRV, `raw_valid` R32_FLOAT
    // SRV (optional), `residual_out` RG32_FLOAT UAV, `valid_out` R32_FLOAT
    // UAV, all of extent widthÃ—height.
    bool DispatchDeriveResidual(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, ID3D12Resource* raw_flow, ID3D12Resource* depth,
                                const Inputs& camera, const float jitter_previous_px[2], ID3D12Resource* residual_out,
                                ID3D12Resource* valid_out, uint32_t width, uint32_t height, uint32_t frame_slot,
                                ID3D12Resource* raw_valid = nullptr);
    // Converts a read-back stats buffer (from the most recent Dispatch) into
    // the shared Stats struct, adding whole-tier extent rejections that were
    // decided on the CPU side.
    Stats DecodeStats(const uint32_t counters[8]) const;
    ID3D12Device* Device() const { return device_.Get(); }

private:
    uint64_t whole_rejects_last_ = 0;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Device> device_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12RootSignature> root_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12PipelineState> pso_, pso_derive_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12DescriptorHeap> heap_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> dummy_;   // 1x1 R32_FLOAT for absent inputs
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> dummy2_;  // 1x1 RG32_FLOAT
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> dummy_stats_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> dummy_uav_;   // 1x1 R32_FLOAT UAV for unused slots
    UINT stride_ = 0;
    uint32_t slots_ = 0;
};

} // namespace na::motion::gpu


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\temporal_jitter.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace neural_alias {
// Independent sampling math, applied by the verified Alien camera adapter.
// The adapter owns sequence advancement/reset and must select the main scene camera.
struct JitterSample { double x_pixels, y_pixels; };
inline double radical_inverse(uint32_t index, uint32_t base) {
    double result=0, weight=1;
    while(index) { weight/=base; result+=double(index%base)*weight; index/=base; }
    return result;
}
inline JitterSample temporal_jitter(uint32_t phase, uint32_t period) {
    if(period<2 || period>4096) throw std::invalid_argument("jitter period must be 2..4096");
    const uint32_t index=phase%period+1;
    return {radical_inverse(index,2)-0.5,radical_inverse(index,3)-0.5};
}
// Explicit convention: row-major storage, column-vector multiplication, D3D viewport.
// Pixel X points right and pixel Y down. Translate clip X/Y by W so displacement
// is constant after perspective division. Preserve clip Z/W, including reverse-Z.
// Caller must start from an UNJITTERED matrix, not apply this repeatedly to a result.
inline std::array<double,16> jitter_projection(const std::array<double,16>& unjittered,
    JitterSample sample,uint32_t width,uint32_t height) {
    if(!width || !height) throw std::invalid_argument("zero render extent");
    auto result=unjittered;
    const double dx=2*sample.x_pixels/double(width),dy=-2*sample.y_pixels/double(height);
    for(unsigned col=0;col<4;++col) {
        result[col]+=dx*unjittered[12+col];
        result[4+col]+=dy*unjittered[12+col];
    }
    return result;
}
} // namespace neural_alias


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\core\upload_cache.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>
#include <mutex>

namespace dx11_enhancement {
// Copies CPU uploads before Unmap. Never calls DirectX while locked. Resource
// destruction callbacks can run under the driver's lock on another thread, so
// they must never acquire the render-state mutex.
class UploadCache {
    struct Entry {
        std::vector<unsigned char> bytes, valid;
        const unsigned char* mapped=nullptr;
        size_t offset=0, length=0;
    };
    std::unordered_map<uint64_t,Entry> entries_;
    mutable std::recursive_mutex mutex_;
public:
    void create(uint64_t id,size_t size,const void* initial=nullptr) {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        erase(id);
        if(!id || !size || size>65536 || entries_.size()>=2048)return;
        auto& e=entries_[id];e.bytes.resize(size);e.valid.resize(size);
        if(initial)update(id,0,size,initial);
    }
    void erase(uint64_t id){std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);entries_.erase(id);}
    void invalidate(uint64_t id){std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);auto it=entries_.find(id);if(it!=entries_.end()){
        std::fill(it->second.valid.begin(),it->second.valid.end(),uint8_t{0});it->second.mapped=nullptr;
    }}
    bool contains(uint64_t id)const{std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);return entries_.find(id)!=entries_.end();}
    void update(uint64_t id,uint64_t offset,uint64_t size,const void* data) {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        auto it=entries_.find(id);if(it==entries_.end())return;auto& e=it->second;
        if(size==UINT64_MAX && offset<=e.bytes.size())size=e.bytes.size()-offset;
        if(!data || offset>e.bytes.size() || size>e.bytes.size()-offset){invalidate(id);return;}
        std::memcpy(e.bytes.data()+size_t(offset),data,size_t(size));
        std::fill(e.valid.begin()+size_t(offset),e.valid.begin()+size_t(offset+size),uint8_t{1});
    }
    void map(uint64_t id,uint64_t offset,uint64_t size,const void* data,bool discard) {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        auto it=entries_.find(id);if(it==entries_.end())return;auto& e=it->second;
        e.mapped=nullptr;if(discard)invalidate(id);
        if(size==UINT64_MAX && offset<=e.bytes.size())size=e.bytes.size()-offset;
        if(!data || offset>e.bytes.size() || size>e.bytes.size()-offset)return;
        e.mapped=static_cast<const unsigned char*>(data);e.offset=size_t(offset);e.length=size_t(size);
    }
    void unmap(uint64_t id){std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);auto it=entries_.find(id);if(it==entries_.end())return;auto& e=it->second;
        if(e.mapped)update(id,e.offset,e.length,e.mapped);e.mapped=nullptr;
    }
    bool read(uint64_t id,uint64_t offset,void* data,size_t size)const {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        auto it=entries_.find(id);if(it==entries_.end())return false;const auto& e=it->second;
        if(e.mapped || !data || offset>e.bytes.size() || size>e.bytes.size()-offset)return false;
        const auto first=e.valid.begin()+size_t(offset);
        if(std::find(first,first+size,0)!=first+size)return false;
        std::memcpy(data,e.bytes.data()+size_t(offset),size);return true;
    }
};
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\host_observe.cpp ---

// H1 observation host: framing, loss accounting, epochs and status â€” the
// contract frozen as ABI 0.1 (host_observe.h). Producers (D3D12 hooks) attach
// via host_observe_internal.hpp; this file contains no D3D12 code.
//
// Transport (A24, corrected A26 per Route B's A24-TRANSPORT-REVIEW):
//  - Ordinary events: per-producer-thread SPSC byte rings (lock-free).
//  - Snapshot boundary events (EPOCH_BEGIN / INVENTORY_COMPLETE): a
//    serialized CONTROL LANE. Each control entry records every ring's head
//    at emit time; the worker drains rings only up to those marks before
//    delivering the entry. Hence everything published before the boundary
//    (including a helper thread's inventory event) is delivered before it,
//    and nothing published after EPOCH_BEGIN is delivered before it. (R1)
//  - Producer quiescence: emits are counted in/out; stop() waits for the
//    count to reach zero before the final drain, and start() publishes
//    `started` only after all state is reset. (R2)
//  - Status: one spinlock serializes every status writer (validity, drop
//    counters, epoch) around the seqlock, and the eligibility check for
//    validity promotion happens inside the same critical section. (R3)
#include "host_dx12/host_observe.h"

#include "host_dx12/host_observe_internal.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <cstring>
#include <deque>
#include <mutex>
#include <random>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

namespace {

using na::observe_internal::SnapshotFn;

constexpr uint32_t kDefaultBudgetMiB = 32;
constexpr uint32_t kMaxBatchBytes = 64 * 1024;
constexpr uint32_t kRings = 32;               // claimed thread ids per session
constexpr size_t kMinRingBytes = 2 * kMaxBatchBytes; // one max event must fit

struct Ring {
    std::atomic<DWORD> owner{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> head{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> tail{0};
    std::vector<uint8_t> buf;
};

struct ControlEntry {
    std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;   // one event, epoch stamped
    uint64_t heads[kRings];       // ring heads at emit time (drain barrier)
};

struct HostState {
    std::mutex mutex;                 // lifecycle + worker wake
    std::condition_variable cv;
    std::vector<uint8_t> queue;       // worker-private staging
    Ring rings[kRings];
    size_t ring_bytes = 0;
    std::atomic<uint64_t> pending_bytes{0};
    std::mutex control_m;             // snapshot lane (lifecycle threads only)
    std::deque<ControlEntry> control;
    std::atomic<uint32_t> control_count{0};
    std::atomic<bool> started{false};
    std::atomic<bool> stopping{false};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> active_producers{0}; // R2 quiescence counter
    // T2: lifecycle generation. Bumped at every start and stop; a producer
    // records it before admission and bails after counting itself in if it
    // changed, so an admission attempt from an old session can never land in
    // a new one.
    std::atomic<uint64_t> generation{0};
    na_event_sink sink = nullptr;
    void* user = nullptr;
    std::thread worker;
    DWORD worker_tid = 0;

    std::atomic<na_session_id> session{0}; // T3: published under status_lock
    std::atomic<uint64_t> epoch{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> inventory_invalid{1};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> foundational_dropped{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> detail_dropped{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> batches_delivered{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> group_dropped[16];
    std::atomic<uint32_t> pending_loss_mark{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> pending_loss_foundational{0};

    // R3: status writers are serialized by this spinlock; readers use the
    // seqlock. Every mutation of epoch/validity/drop counters goes through
    // status_lock()/status_unlock().
    std::atomic<bool> status_busy{false};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> status_seq{0};
    // T4 (rev2): producers never lock, but a reader must not accept a
    // snapshot while a drop's multi-field update is in flight. Producers
    // count themselves in/out around the update (two wait-free atomics);
    // readers retry while the count is non-zero or the sequence moved.
    std::atomic<uint32_t> drops_in_progress{0};

    std::atomic<uint64_t> pending_valid_epoch{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> pending_valid_drops{0};

    SnapshotFn snapshot;
    std::atomic<bool> force_foundational_overflow{false};
};

HostState g_host;

void status_lock()
{
    while (g_host.status_busy.exchange(true, std::memory_order_acquire)) YieldProcessor();
    g_host.status_seq.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); // odd: writing
}
void status_unlock()
{
    g_host.status_seq.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); // even: stable
    g_host.status_busy.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
}

void append_loss_mark(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint32_t group, uint32_t foundational)
{
    na_ev_loss_mark ev{};
    ev.hdr.size_bytes = sizeof(ev);
    ev.hdr.kind = NA_EV_LOSS_MARK;
    ev.hdr.epoch = g_host.epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    ev.hdr.producer_thread = GetCurrentThreadId();
    ev.hdr.flags = foundational ? NA_EVF_FOUNDATIONAL : 0;
    ev.group = group;
    ev.foundational = foundational;
    const uint8_t* p = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(&ev);
    out.insert(out.end(), p, p + sizeof(ev));
}

void deliver_batch(std::vector<uint8_t>& payload, uint32_t flags, na_epoch batch_epoch)
{
    na_event_batch hdr{};
    hdr.header_bytes = sizeof(na_event_batch);
    hdr.abi_major = NA_OBSERVE_ABI_MAJOR;
    hdr.abi_minor = NA_OBSERVE_ABI_MINOR;
    hdr.session = g_host.session.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    hdr.epoch = batch_epoch;
    hdr.payload_bytes = static_cast<uint32_t>(payload.size());
    uint32_t count = 0;
    for (size_t off = 0; off + sizeof(na_event_header) <= payload.size();) {
        const auto* eh = reinterpret_cast<const na_event_header*>(payload.data() + off);
        off += eh->size_bytes;
        ++count;
    }
    hdr.event_count = count;
    hdr.batch_flags = flags;

    std::vector<uint8_t> batch(sizeof(hdr) + payload.size());
    std::memcpy(batch.data(), &hdr, sizeof(hdr));
    if (!payload.empty()) std::memcpy(batch.data() + sizeof(hdr), payload.data(), payload.size());
    g_host.sink(reinterpret_cast<const na_event_batch*>(batch.data()), g_host.user);
    g_host.batches_delivered.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);

    // H1-R1 + R3: after DELIVERY, promote validity iff this batch carried the
    // pending epoch's INVENTORY_COMPLETE(complete=1); the eligibility check
    // (epoch still current, no foundational loss since queueing) and the
    // store happen atomically under the status lock so loss/resync cannot
    // interleave between check and store.
    uint64_t pend = g_host.pending_valid_epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (pend != 0 && pend == batch_epoch) {
        bool saw_complete = false;
        for (size_t off = 0; off + sizeof(na_event_header) <= payload.size();) {
            const auto* eh = reinterpret_cast<const na_event_header*>(payload.data() + off);
            if (eh->kind == NA_EV_INVENTORY_COMPLETE && eh->size_bytes >= sizeof(na_ev_inventory_complete)) {
                const auto* ic = reinterpret_cast<const na_ev_inventory_complete*>(eh);
                if (ic->epoch == pend && ic->complete == 1) saw_complete = true;
            }
            off += eh->size_bytes;
        }
        if (saw_complete) {
            status_lock();
            uint64_t expect_drops = g_host.pending_valid_drops.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
            if (g_host.pending_valid_epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == pend &&
                g_host.epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == pend &&
                g_host.foundational_dropped.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == expect_drops) {
                g_host.inventory_invalid.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
                g_host.pending_valid_epoch.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
                // T4: drops are lock-free (producer path must not block), so a
                // foundational drop may land between the check and the store.
                // Drops always store invalid=1 AFTER incrementing; re-checking
                // the counter after our store closes every interleaving.
                if (g_host.foundational_dropped.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != expect_drops)
                    g_host.inventory_invalid.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
            }
            status_unlock();
        }
    }
}

// Worker only. Drains each ring up to `limits[i]` (or its current head when
// limits is null) into the private staging buffer.
void drain_rings(const uint64_t* limits)
{
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kRings; ++i) {
        Ring& r = g_host.rings[i];
        uint64_t head = r.head.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (limits && limits[i] < head) head = limits[i];
        uint64_t tail = r.tail.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        if (head <= tail) continue;
        size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(head - tail);
        size_t cap = r.buf.size();
        size_t pos = static_cast<size_t>(tail % cap);
        size_t first = std::min(n, cap - pos);
        g_host.queue.insert(g_host.queue.end(), r.buf.data() + pos, r.buf.data() + pos + first);
        if (first < n) g_host.queue.insert(g_host.queue.end(), r.buf.data(), r.buf.data() + (n - first));
        r.tail.store(head, std::memory_order_release);
        g_host.pending_bytes.fetch_sub(n, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    }
}

// Worker only. Common ordering protocol with emit_control (T1): ring-head
// snapshots â€” both the barrier stored in a control entry and the limit used
// for a "free" drain â€” are taken while holding control_m. So any control
// entry published after the worker's snapshot has barriers >= that snapshot,
// and events its publisher wrote after it lie beyond the snapshot too: a free
// drain can never consume events that belong behind a not-yet-dequeued
// boundary.
void gather()
{
    for (;;) {
        ControlEntry entry;
        uint64_t free_limit[kRings];
        bool have = false;
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_host.control_m);
            if (!g_host.control.empty()) {
                entry = std::move(g_host.control.front());
                g_host.control.pop_front();
                g_host.control_count.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
                have = true;
            } else {
                for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kRings; ++i)
                    free_limit[i] = g_host.rings[i].head.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
            }
        }
        if (!have) {
            drain_rings(free_limit);
            break;
        }
        drain_rings(entry.heads);                       // everything before the boundary
        g_host.queue.insert(g_host.queue.end(), entry.bytes.begin(), entry.bytes.end());
    }
}

void worker_main()
{
    g_host.worker_tid = GetCurrentThreadId();
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(g_host.mutex);
    for (;;) {
        g_host.cv.wait_for(lock, std::chrono::milliseconds(50), [] {
            return g_host.stopping.load(std::memory_order_acquire) ||
                   g_host.pending_bytes.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0 ||
                   g_host.control_count.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0 ||
                   g_host.pending_loss_mark.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0;
        });
        bool final_pass = g_host.stopping.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
