        if (final_pass) {
            // R2: no producer may still be inside emit when we take the final
            // view of the rings. Producers are bounded (a memcpy), so this is
            // short; it is a lifecycle wait, never a hook-path wait.
            while (g_host.active_producers.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0) YieldProcessor();
        }
        gather();
        do {
            std::vector<uint8_t> payload;
            na_epoch batch_epoch = g_host.epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
            size_t take = 0;
            bool first = true;
            while (take < g_host.queue.size()) {
                const auto* eh = reinterpret_cast<const na_event_header*>(g_host.queue.data() + take);
                if (first) {
                    batch_epoch = eh->epoch;
                    first = false;
                } else {
                    if (eh->epoch != batch_epoch) break;
                    if (take + eh->size_bytes > kMaxBatchBytes) break;
                }
                take += eh->size_bytes;
            }
            if (batch_epoch == g_host.epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
                uint32_t marks = g_host.pending_loss_mark.exchange(0, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
                uint32_t fmarks = g_host.pending_loss_foundational.exchange(0, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
                std::vector<uint8_t> mark_bytes;
                for (uint32_t g = 0; g < 16; ++g)
                    if (marks & (1u << g)) append_loss_mark(mark_bytes, g, (fmarks >> g) & 1u);
                payload.insert(payload.end(), mark_bytes.begin(), mark_bytes.end());
            }
            payload.insert(payload.end(), g_host.queue.begin(), g_host.queue.begin() + take);
            g_host.queue.erase(g_host.queue.begin(), g_host.queue.begin() + take);
            if (final_pass) gather();
            bool more = !g_host.queue.empty();
            uint32_t flags = (final_pass && !more) ? NA_BATCHF_FINAL : 0;
            if (!payload.empty() || flags) {
                lock.unlock();
                deliver_batch(payload, flags, batch_epoch);
                lock.lock();
            }
            if (!more) break;
        } while (true);
        if (final_pass) return;
    }
}

Ring* thread_ring()
{
    thread_local Ring* ring = nullptr;
    thread_local uint64_t ring_session = 0;
    uint64_t session = g_host.session.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (ring && ring_session == session) return ring;
    ring = nullptr;
    DWORD tid = GetCurrentThreadId();
    for (Ring& r : g_host.rings) {
        DWORD expect = 0;
        if (r.owner.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == tid ||
            r.owner.compare_exchange_strong(expect, tid, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) {
            ring = &r;
            ring_session = session;
            break;
        }
    }
    return ring;
}

// T4: the overload path must stay nonblocking (it runs inside game hooks).
// No lock: counters are atomics; the seqlock is bumped by +2 (even -> even)
// so a reader that straddled the change retries, and a lifecycle writer's
// odd phase is preserved.
void record_drop(uint32_t group, bool foundational)
{
    g_host.drops_in_progress.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    struct Done { ~Done() { g_host.drops_in_progress.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); } } done;
    if (group < 16) g_host.group_dropped[group].fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_seq_cst);
    if (foundational) {
        g_host.foundational_dropped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_seq_cst);
        g_host.inventory_invalid.store(1, std::memory_order_seq_cst); // after the increment
        g_host.pending_loss_foundational.fetch_or(1u << (group & 15), std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    } else {
        g_host.detail_dropped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_seq_cst);
    }
    g_host.status_seq.fetch_add(2, std::memory_order_seq_cst);
    g_host.pending_loss_mark.fetch_or(1u << (group & 15), std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    g_host.cv.notify_one();
}

// Lock-free producer path. R2/T2: counted in/out and generation-checked so
// stop() can quiesce and a stale admission cannot land in a later session.
bool emit_ring(const void* bytes, uint32_t group)
{
    const auto* hdr = static_cast<const na_event_header*>(bytes);
    bool foundational = (hdr->flags & NA_EVF_FOUNDATIONAL) != 0;
    const uint64_t gen = g_host.generation.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!g_host.started.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return false;
    g_host.active_producers.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    struct Leave { ~Leave() { g_host.active_producers.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); } } leave;
    if (g_host.generation.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != gen ||
        !g_host.started.load(std::memory_order_acquire) ||
        g_host.stopping.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
        return false; // lifecycle moved under us: not admitted
    bool malformed = hdr->size_bytes < sizeof(na_event_header) || (hdr->size_bytes % 8) != 0 ||
                     hdr->size_bytes > kMaxBatchBytes;
    Ring* ring = malformed ? nullptr : thread_ring();
    bool overflow = malformed || !ring;
    if (ring && !overflow) {
        uint64_t head = ring->head.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        uint64_t tail = ring->tail.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (ring->buf.size() - (head - tail) < hdr->size_bytes) overflow = true;
    }
    if (foundational && g_host.force_foundational_overflow.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) overflow = true;
    if (overflow) {
        record_drop(group, foundational);
        return false;
    }
    const size_t size = hdr->size_bytes;
    const size_t cap = ring->buf.size();
    uint64_t head = ring->head.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    na_event_header stamped = *hdr;
    stamped.epoch = g_host.epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    auto put = [&](const uint8_t* src, size_t n, uint64_t at) {
        size_t pos = static_cast<size_t>(at % cap);
        size_t first = std::min(n, cap - pos);
        std::memcpy(ring->buf.data() + pos, src, first);
        if (first < n) std::memcpy(ring->buf.data(), src + first, n - first);
    };
    put(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(&stamped), sizeof(stamped), head);
    put(static_cast<const uint8_t*>(bytes) + sizeof(stamped), size - sizeof(stamped), head + sizeof(stamped));
    g_host.pending_bytes.fetch_add(size, std::memory_order_acq_rel); // before publish: no underflow
    ring->head.store(head + size, std::memory_order_release);
    g_host.cv.notify_one();
    return true;
}

// Snapshot-lane emit (lifecycle threads only): records the drain barrier.
bool emit_control(const void* bytes)
{
    const auto* hdr = static_cast<const na_event_header*>(bytes);
    if (!g_host.started.load(std::memory_order_acquire) || g_host.stopping.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
        return false;
    // The lane is bounded and never overflows in practice, but a lost
    // boundary must remain simulable (H1-R2) and accounted like any
    // foundational loss.
    if (g_host.force_foundational_overflow.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        record_drop(NA_GROUP_SESSION, true);
        return false;
    }
    ControlEntry e;
    e.bytes.assign(static_cast<const uint8_t*>(bytes), static_cast<const uint8_t*>(bytes) + hdr->size_bytes);
    reinterpret_cast<na_event_header*>(e.bytes.data())->epoch = g_host.epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    {
        // T1: barrier snapshot and publication under the same lock the worker
        // uses for its own snapshot (see gather()). Bounded lane: overflow is
        // a foundational loss, never unbounded growth.
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_host.control_m);
        if (g_host.control.size() >= 1024) {
            record_drop(NA_GROUP_SESSION, true);
            return false;
        }
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kRings; ++i) e.heads[i] = g_host.rings[i].head.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        g_host.control.push_back(std::move(e));
        g_host.control_count.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    }
    g_host.cv.notify_one();
    return true;
}

bool run_snapshot()
{
    uint64_t drops_before = g_host.foundational_dropped.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    bool complete = true;

    na_ev_epoch_begin begin{};
    begin.hdr.size_bytes = sizeof(begin);
    begin.hdr.kind = NA_EV_EPOCH_BEGIN;
    begin.hdr.flags = NA_EVF_FOUNDATIONAL;
    begin.hdr.producer_thread = GetCurrentThreadId();
    begin.session = g_host.session.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    begin.epoch = g_host.epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!emit_control(&begin)) complete = false;

    if (g_host.snapshot && !g_host.snapshot()) complete = false;
    uint64_t drops_after = g_host.foundational_dropped.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (drops_after != drops_before) complete = false;

    na_ev_inventory_complete done{};
    done.hdr.size_bytes = sizeof(done);
    done.hdr.kind = NA_EV_INVENTORY_COMPLETE;
    done.hdr.flags = NA_EVF_FOUNDATIONAL;
    done.hdr.producer_thread = GetCurrentThreadId();
    done.epoch = begin.epoch;
    done.complete = complete ? 1u : 0u;

    if (complete) {
        status_lock();
        g_host.pending_valid_drops.store(drops_after, std::memory_order_release);
        g_host.pending_valid_epoch.store(begin.epoch, std::memory_order_release);
        status_unlock();
    }
    if (!emit_control(&done)) complete = false;
    if (!complete) {
        status_lock();
        g_host.pending_valid_epoch.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
        g_host.inventory_invalid.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
        status_unlock();
    }
    g_host.cv.notify_one();
    return complete;
}

} // namespace

namespace na::observe_internal {

bool Emit(const void* event_bytes, uint32_t group) { return emit_ring(event_bytes, group); }
void SetSnapshotProvider(SnapshotFn fn) { g_host.snapshot = std::move(fn); }
void TestForceFoundationalOverflow(bool enable)
{
    g_host.force_foundational_overflow.store(enable, std::memory_order_release);
}

} // namespace na::observe_internal

extern "C" {

int __cdecl na_observe_abi(uint16_t* out_major, uint16_t* out_minor)
{
    if (!out_major || !out_minor) return NA_E_ARG;
    *out_major = NA_OBSERVE_ABI_MAJOR;
    *out_minor = NA_OBSERVE_ABI_MINOR;
    return NA_OK;
}

int __cdecl na_observe_start(const na_observe_config* cfg, na_event_sink sink, void* user)
{
    if (!sink) return NA_E_ARG;
    uint32_t budget_mib = kDefaultBudgetMiB;
    if (cfg) {
        if (cfg->size_bytes < 8 || cfg->size_bytes > 4096) return NA_E_ABI;
        for (uint32_t r : cfg->_reserved)
            if (cfg->size_bytes >= sizeof(na_observe_config) && r != 0) return NA_E_ARG;
        if (cfg->metadata_budget_mib) budget_mib = cfg->metadata_budget_mib;
    }
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_host.mutex);
        if (g_host.started.load()) return NA_E_STATE;
        // R2: a previous session's producers are quiesced by stop(); rings
        // are only reallocated here, after that.
        g_host.sink = sink;
        g_host.user = user;
        size_t budget = static_cast<size_t>(budget_mib) * 1024 * 1024;
        // Budget floor: every ring must hold one maximum event; the effective
        // minimum total is kRings * kMinRingBytes (4 MiB), documented.
        g_host.ring_bytes = std::max(kMinRingBytes, budget / kRings);
        for (Ring& r : g_host.rings) {
            r.owner.store(0);
            r.head.store(0);
            r.tail.store(0);
            r.buf.assign(g_host.ring_bytes, 0);
        }
        g_host.pending_bytes.store(0);
        g_host.queue.clear();
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> cl(g_host.control_m);
            g_host.control.clear();
        }
        g_host.control_count.store(0);
        std::random_device rd;
        status_lock();
        g_host.session.store((static_cast<uint64_t>(rd()) << 32) ^ rd() ^ __rdtsc(),
                             std::memory_order_release); // T3: inside the coherent write
        g_host.epoch.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
        g_host.inventory_invalid.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
        g_host.foundational_dropped.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        g_host.detail_dropped.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        g_host.batches_delivered.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        for (auto& g : g_host.group_dropped) g.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        g_host.pending_valid_epoch.store(0);
        status_unlock();
        g_host.stopping.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
        g_host.worker = std::thread(&worker_main);
        g_host.generation.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); // T2: new lifecycle
        g_host.started.store(true, std::memory_order_release); // last: admission opens
    }
    run_snapshot();
    return NA_OK;
}

int __cdecl na_observe_stop(void)
{
    if (GetCurrentThreadId() == g_host.worker_tid) return NA_E_STATE;
    std::thread joiner;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_host.mutex);
        if (!g_host.started.load()) return NA_E_STATE;
        g_host.stopping.store(true, std::memory_order_release); // closes admission
        g_host.cv.notify_one();
        joiner = std::move(g_host.worker);
    }
    joiner.join(); // worker quiesced producers before its final gather
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_host.mutex);
    g_host.started.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    g_host.generation.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); // T2: closes this lifecycle
    g_host.sink = nullptr;
    g_host.worker_tid = 0;
    return NA_OK;
}

int __cdecl na_observe_query_status(na_observe_status* inout)
{
    if (!inout) return NA_E_ARG;
    uint32_t capacity = inout->size_bytes;
    if (capacity < 16) return NA_E_ARG;
    na_observe_status full{};
    for (;;) {
        uint64_t s1 = g_host.status_seq.load(std::memory_order_seq_cst);
        if ((s1 & 1) || g_host.drops_in_progress.load(std::memory_order_seq_cst) != 0) {
            YieldProcessor();
            continue;
        }
        std::atomic_thread_fence(std::memory_order_seq_cst);
        full.abi_major = NA_OBSERVE_ABI_MAJOR;
        full.abi_minor = NA_OBSERVE_ABI_MINOR;
        full.session = g_host.session.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        full.epoch = g_host.epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        full.inventory_invalid = g_host.inventory_invalid.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        full.foundational_dropped = g_host.foundational_dropped.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        full.detail_dropped = g_host.detail_dropped.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        full.batches_delivered = g_host.batches_delivered.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i)
            full.per_group_dropped[i] = g_host.group_dropped[i].load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        // Closing check ORDER matters: a producer bumps the sequence and THEN
        // clears in_progress. Reading in_progress first means that if we see
        // it clear, the sequence read that follows is ordered after the
        // producer's bump (seq_cst total order) and must observe it. Reading
        // the sequence first could observe a stale value alongside an already
        // cleared counter â€” a torn snapshot (rev2-T4).
        std::atomic_thread_fence(std::memory_order_seq_cst);
        bool quiet = g_host.drops_in_progress.load(std::memory_order_seq_cst) == 0;
        uint64_t s2 = g_host.status_seq.load(std::memory_order_seq_cst);
        if (quiet && s1 == s2) break;
    }
    uint32_t written = capacity < sizeof(full) ? capacity : sizeof(full);
    full.size_bytes = written;
    std::memcpy(inout, &full, written);
    return NA_OK;
}

int __cdecl na_observe_resync(void)
{
    if (GetCurrentThreadId() == g_host.worker_tid) return NA_E_STATE;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_host.mutex);
        if (!g_host.started.load() || g_host.stopping.load()) return NA_E_STATE;
        status_lock();
        g_host.epoch.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
        g_host.inventory_invalid.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
        g_host.pending_valid_epoch.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
        status_unlock();
    }
    run_snapshot();
    return NA_OK;
}

} // extern "C"


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\host_observe.h ---

/*
 * B-002 â€” DX12 host observation ABI. *** FROZEN at ABI 0.1 (2026-09-23) ***
 * Accepted by Route B at rev3 (B-002-REV3-ACK.md, route-b commit 73d2980).
 * Layout/semantics changes now require a MAJOR bump via a new
 * COLLABORATION.md proposal; MINOR additions are append-only per the rules
 * below. Companion prose: OBSERVATION-API.md.
 *
 * Platform/packing contract (R2):
 *  - x64 Windows only, little-endian, MSVC layout, NO bitfields.
 *  - Every struct uses natural alignment; every event's total size is a
 *    multiple of 8 and events are packed back-to-back at 8-byte alignment.
 *  - All functions are __cdecl. All reserved/padding bytes are written as 0
 *    by the host and ignored by consumers.
 *
 * Decode contract (R1/R2): a consumer can decode every H1 event from this
 * header alone. Events advance by na_event_header.size_bytes. An event whose
 * `kind` is unknown is SKIPPED via size_bytes; if an unknown kind carries
 * NA_EVF_FOUNDATIONAL, the consumer must treat its inventory as incomplete
 * (unknown foundational facts exist) rather than silently trusting what it
 * has. A known event whose size_bytes is SMALLER than this header's struct
 * (for the consumer's compiled minor) is malformed: stop decoding the batch
 * and treat it as loss. Counts implying reads past the batch payload are
 * malformed the same way. Fields appended by future MINOR revisions live
 * between the compiled struct end and size_bytes; inline arrays are located
 * ONLY via explicit offset/stride fields, never by struct end.
 */
#ifndef NA_HOST_OBSERVE_H
#define NA_HOST_OBSERVE_H

#include <stdint.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define NA_OBSERVE_ABI_MAJOR 0
#define NA_OBSERVE_ABI_MINOR 1

typedef uint64_t na_object_id;  /* opaque, monotonic, never reused in a session */
typedef uint64_t na_epoch;
typedef uint64_t na_session_id; /* random per na_observe_start (R4): epochs are
                                   only comparable within one session_id */

/* ---- Capability groups (R1): index for status loss counters and the
 * capability report. Values are frozen for MAJOR 0. ---- */
typedef enum na_cap_group {
    NA_GROUP_SESSION          = 0,
    NA_GROUP_RESOURCE         = 1,
    NA_GROUP_DESCRIPTOR       = 2,
    NA_GROUP_LIST             = 3,
    NA_GROUP_QUEUE            = 4,
    NA_GROUP_SWAPCHAIN        = 5,
    NA_GROUP_PIPELINE         = 6,  /* H2 */
    NA_GROUP_PASS             = 7,  /* H2 */
    NA_GROUP_RESOURCE_USE     = 8,  /* H2 */
    NA_GROUP_CPU_CONSTANTS    = 9,  /* H2/H3 */
    NA_GROUP_SNAPSHOT         = 10, /* H3 */
    NA_GROUP_COUNT_V1         = 11  /* remaining slots reserved, zeroed */
} na_cap_group;

typedef enum na_cap_support {
    NA_CAP_UNSUPPORTED = 0,
    NA_CAP_PARTIAL     = 1,
    NA_CAP_SUPPORTED   = 2
} na_cap_support;

/* ---- Event kinds (H1, complete set for ABI 0.1) ---- */
typedef enum na_event_kind {
    NA_EV_CAPABILITY_REPORT       = 1,
    NA_EV_RESOURCE_CREATED        = 2,
    NA_EV_OBJECT_RETIRED          = 3,  /* any tracked object (R1) */
    NA_EV_DESCRIPTOR_WRITE        = 4,
    NA_EV_DESCRIPTOR_COPY         = 5,
    NA_EV_RESOURCE_HEAP_CREATED   = 6,
    NA_EV_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_CREATED = 7,  /* distinct from resource heaps (R1) */
    NA_EV_LIST_CREATED            = 8,
    NA_EV_LIST_RESET              = 9,
    NA_EV_LIST_CLOSE              = 10,
    NA_EV_QUEUE_CREATED           = 11,
    NA_EV_QUEUE_EXECUTE           = 12,
    NA_EV_QUEUE_SIGNAL            = 13,
    NA_EV_QUEUE_WAIT              = 14,
    NA_EV_FENCE_CREATED           = 15,
    NA_EV_SWAPCHAIN_CREATED       = 16,
    NA_EV_SWAPCHAIN_RESIZED       = 17,
    NA_EV_PRESENT                 = 18,
    NA_EV_LOSS_MARK               = 19,
    NA_EV_EPOCH_BEGIN             = 20,
    NA_EV_INVENTORY_COMPLETE      = 21, /* validity boundary (R4) */
    NA_EV_ORDER_AMBIGUITY_RANGE   = 22, /* retroactive revocation (R3) */
    NA_EV_DEVICE_CREATED          = 23
} na_event_kind;

/* ---- Event header flags ---- */
#define NA_EVF_ORDER_AMBIGUOUS   0x1u  /* see R3 rule at na_ev_queue_execute */
#define NA_EVF_METHOD_UNOBSERVED 0x2u  /* produced via partially observed path */
#define NA_EVF_TRUNCATED         0x4u  /* variable payload cut by byte budget */
#define NA_EVF_FOUNDATIONAL      0x8u  /* inventory-bearing; unknown+foundational
                                          => consumer inventory incomplete */
#define NA_EVF_PREEXISTING       0x10u /* object first seen already alive (e.g.
                                          hooked late); creation params carry
                                          NA_UNKNOWN_* markers, never invented */

/* Unknown-value markers (R1): fields the host could not observe. */
#define NA_UNKNOWN_U32 0xFFFFFFFFu
#define NA_UNKNOWN_U64 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFull

typedef struct na_event_header {
    uint32_t size_bytes;      /* total event size incl. header, multiple of 8 */
    uint32_t kind;            /* na_event_kind */
    na_epoch epoch;
    uint64_t thread_seq;      /* per-producer-thread monotonic */
    uint32_t producer_thread; /* OS thread id */
    uint32_t flags;           /* NA_EVF_* */
} na_event_header;

/* ---- Session / capability ---- */
typedef struct na_cap_entry {
    uint32_t group;           /* na_cap_group */
    uint32_t support;         /* na_cap_support */
    uint32_t detail_flags;    /* group-specific, 0 = none; unknown bits ignore */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_cap_entry;
typedef struct na_ev_capability_report {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_session_id session;
    uint32_t host_version_major, host_version_minor;
    uint64_t adapter_luid;
    uint32_t entry_count;
    uint32_t entries_offset;  /* from event start; stride = entry_stride */
    uint32_t entry_stride;    /* >= sizeof(na_cap_entry) */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_ev_capability_report;

typedef struct na_ev_device_created {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id device;
    uint64_t adapter_luid;
    uint32_t hooked_interface_version; /* highest ID3D12Device<N> with hooked
                                          methods; higher versions in use =>
                                          NA_EVF_METHOD_UNOBSERVED events */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_ev_device_created;

/* ---- Object lifecycle ---- */
typedef enum na_object_kind {
    NA_OBJ_DEVICE = 1, NA_OBJ_RESOURCE = 2, NA_OBJ_RESOURCE_HEAP = 3,
    NA_OBJ_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP = 4, NA_OBJ_LIST = 5, NA_OBJ_QUEUE = 6,
    NA_OBJ_FENCE = 7, NA_OBJ_SWAPCHAIN = 8, NA_OBJ_ALLOCATOR = 9
} na_object_kind;

/* Identity retirement (final release sentinel). NOT GPU completion. */
typedef struct na_ev_object_retired {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id object;
    uint32_t object_kind;     /* na_object_kind */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_ev_object_retired;

typedef enum na_creation_kind {
    NA_CREATE_COMMITTED = 1, NA_CREATE_PLACED = 2, NA_CREATE_RESERVED = 3,
    NA_CREATE_OPENED_SHARED = 4, NA_CREATE_SWAPCHAIN_BUFFER = 5,
    NA_CREATE_UNKNOWN = NA_UNKNOWN_U32
} na_creation_kind;

typedef struct na_ev_resource_created {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id resource;
    na_object_id device;
    na_object_id heap;        /* resource heap id; 0 = committed/none */
    uint64_t heap_offset;     /* placed only; else NA_UNKNOWN_U64 */
    uint32_t dimension;       /* D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
    uint32_t format;          /* DXGI_FORMAT or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
    uint64_t width;
    uint32_t height;
    uint16_t depth_or_array;
    uint16_t mip_levels;
    uint32_t sample_count;
    uint32_t creation_kind;   /* na_creation_kind */
    uint64_t resource_flags;  /* D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAGS */
    uint32_t initial_state;   /* D3D12_RESOURCE_STATES or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
    uint32_t heap_type;       /* D3D12_HEAP_TYPE or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
} na_ev_resource_created;

typedef struct na_ev_resource_heap_created {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id heap;
    na_object_id device;
    uint64_t size_in_bytes;
    uint32_t heap_type;       /* D3D12_HEAP_TYPE */
    uint32_t heap_flags;      /* D3D12_HEAP_FLAGS */
} na_ev_resource_heap_created;

typedef struct na_ev_descriptor_heap_created {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id heap;
    na_object_id device;
    uint32_t heap_kind;       /* D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE */
    uint32_t descriptor_count;
    uint32_t shader_visible;  /* 0/1 */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_ev_descriptor_heap_created;

/* Descriptor events are RECORD-TIME CPU-side observations. version increments
 * per (heap, slot) on each observed write/copy-into; use-time shader-visible
 * contents are NOT established by these events (H1 limit). */
typedef enum na_descriptor_kind {
    NA_DESC_SRV = 1, NA_DESC_UAV = 2, NA_DESC_CBV = 3, NA_DESC_RTV = 4,
    NA_DESC_DSV = 5, NA_DESC_SAMPLER = 6, NA_DESC_UNKNOWN = NA_UNKNOWN_U32
} na_descriptor_kind;
typedef struct na_ev_descriptor_write {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id heap;
    uint32_t slot;
    uint32_t descriptor_kind;  /* na_descriptor_kind */
    uint64_t slot_version;     /* post-write version of (heap, slot) */
    na_object_id resource;     /* 0 = null view / not resource-backed */
    uint32_t format;           /* view format or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
    uint32_t first_subresource;/* first mip/array slice or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
    uint32_t subresource_count;/* or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_ev_descriptor_write;
typedef struct na_ev_descriptor_copy {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id src_heap;
    na_object_id dst_heap;
    uint32_t src_slot;
    uint32_t dst_slot;
    uint32_t slot_count;       /* contiguous range copied */
    uint32_t _pad;             /* dst slot versions each increment by 1; dst
                                  contents = src RECORD-TIME contents only if
                                  src versions unchanged since observation,
                                  else unknown */
} na_ev_descriptor_copy;

/* ---- Command lists ---- */
/* Initial recording state: CreateCommandList creates the list OPEN with
 * recording_generation 1; CreateCommandList1 creates it CLOSED with
 * recording_generation 0 (first successful Reset makes generation 1).
 * `initially_open` records which; creation via an unobserved path yields an
 * OBJECT event with NA_EVF_PREEXISTING and generation/openness UNKNOWN â€”
 * never invented. */
typedef struct na_ev_list_created {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id list;
    na_object_id device;
    na_object_id initial_allocator; /* 0 if unobserved/none (CreateCommandList1) */
    uint32_t list_type;        /* D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE */
    uint32_t is_bundle;        /* 0/1 */
    uint32_t initially_open;   /* 0/1, or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_ev_list_created;

#define NA_LISTF_RESET_FAILED 0x1u
#define NA_LISTF_IS_BUNDLE    0x2u
/* recording_generation increments ONLY on successful Reset (hresult >= 0);
 * failed Reset repeats the previous generation with NA_LISTF_RESET_FAILED and
 * does NOT establish a usable recording (R5 principle applied to lists). */
typedef struct na_ev_list_reset_close {
    na_event_header hdr;       /* kind = LIST_RESET or LIST_CLOSE */
    na_object_id list;
    na_object_id allocator;    /* reset only; 0 for close */
    uint64_t recording_generation;
    int32_t  hresult;
    uint32_t list_flags;       /* NA_LISTF_* */
} na_ev_list_reset_close;

/* ---- Queues ---- */
typedef struct na_ev_queue_created {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id queue;
    na_object_id device;
    uint32_t queue_type;       /* D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE */
    uint32_t queue_flags;      /* D3D12_COMMAND_QUEUE_FLAGS */
} na_ev_queue_created;

typedef struct na_list_ref {   /* layout FROZEN for MAJOR 0 (R2); evolution
                                  happens via ref_stride, never in-place */
    na_object_id list;
    uint64_t recording_generation;
} na_list_ref;

/* Ordering rule (R3): queue_ticket is an occurrence identity per queue. An
 * occurrence is AMBIGUOUS if its hook window [enter, exit] overlaps the hook
 * window of ANY other occurrence on the same queue â€” determined at EXIT time,
 * covering the reviewer's A-enter/B-enter/B-exit/A-exit case: overlap marks
 * BOTH. If an affected event was already delivered without the flag (delivery
 * raced finalization), the host MUST later emit NA_EV_ORDER_AMBIGUITY_RANGE
 * covering its ticket; consumers must treat every execute/signal/wait ticket
 * inside any received range as ambiguous regardless of per-event flags.
 * Ticket order therefore proves same-queue call order only for occurrences
 * that are non-overlapping AND outside every ambiguity range. */
typedef struct na_ev_queue_execute {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id queue;
    uint64_t queue_ticket;
    uint32_t list_count;
    uint32_t contains_bundles; /* 1 => derivations carry incomplete-bundles */
    uint32_t refs_offset;      /* from event start (R2) */
    uint32_t ref_stride;       /* >= sizeof(na_list_ref) */
} na_ev_queue_execute;

typedef struct na_ev_order_ambiguity_range {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id queue;
    uint64_t first_ticket;     /* inclusive */
    uint64_t last_ticket;      /* inclusive */
} na_ev_order_ambiguity_range;

/* ---- Fences / sync (R5) ---- */
typedef struct na_ev_fence_created {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id fence;
    na_object_id device;
    uint64_t initial_value;
    uint32_t fence_flags;      /* D3D12_FENCE_FLAGS; SHARED => external
                                  signalers possible, correlation weakens */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_ev_fence_created;

typedef enum na_fence_provenance {
    /* SIGNAL events only: identifies the signal's own occurrence. Its hresult
     * proves successful SUBMISSION of the signal, never completion. */
    NA_FENCE_PROV_OBSERVED_QUEUE_SIGNAL = 1,
    /* Reserved: H1 does not hook CPU ID3D12Fence::Signal; this value is NOT
     * emitted in ABI 0.1 (no invented CPU observations). */
    NA_FENCE_PROV_OBSERVED_CPU_SIGNAL   = 2,
    /* WAIT events: the H1 default and, absent a documented closed-world
     * guarantee, the ONLY wait provenance (see rule below). */
    NA_FENCE_PROV_UNKNOWN               = 3,
    /* Conflicting evidence exists (repeated values, rewind, shared fence,
     * multiple writers): correlation refused, not guessed. */
    NA_FENCE_PROV_AMBIGUOUS             = 4
} na_fence_provenance;

/* H1 WAIT-CORRELATION RULE (B-002-REV2-REVIEW): fence waits are satisfied by
 * a THRESHOLD (completed value >= waited value), and CPU ID3D12Fence::Signal
 * is outside H1 observation coverage â€” an unobserved CPU/external signal, or
 * an initial fence value already at/above the threshold, can satisfy a wait
 * even when exactly one queue signal for that (fence, value) was observed.
 * Therefore H1 wait events carry provenance UNKNOWN (or AMBIGUOUS when
 * conflicting evidence exists) with source_queue == 0 and source_ticket == 0,
 * UNLESS a documented closed-world signal-source guarantee applies (e.g. a
 * host-OWNED fence whose every signaler is the host itself; no game fence
 * qualifies in H1). Consumers must derive NO cross-queue ordering edge from
 * game fence waits in H1. A failed operation (hresult < 0) establishes no
 * dependency of any kind. Ambiguity-range events (NA_EV_ORDER_AMBIGUITY_RANGE)
 * are FOUNDATIONAL: they carry NA_EVF_FOUNDATIONAL, their loss invalidates
 * inventory-derived ordering, and consumers must revalidate retained
 * deductions whenever a range arrives. */
typedef struct na_ev_queue_sync {
    na_event_header hdr;       /* kind = QUEUE_SIGNAL or QUEUE_WAIT */
    na_object_id queue;
    na_object_id fence;
    uint64_t value;
    uint64_t queue_ticket;
    int32_t  hresult;          /* of the Signal/Wait submission itself */
    uint32_t provenance;       /* waits: UNKNOWN/AMBIGUOUS per rule above;
                                  signals: PROV_OBSERVED_QUEUE_SIGNAL (self) */
    na_object_id source_queue; /* 0 in H1 for all game-fence waits (rule
                                  above); nonzero only under a documented
                                  closed-world guarantee */
    uint64_t source_ticket;    /* ditto; else 0 */
} na_ev_queue_sync;

/* ---- Swapchain / present ---- */
typedef struct na_ev_swapchain_created {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id swapchain;
    na_object_id device;
    na_object_id queue;        /* creating queue; 0 if unobserved */
    uint32_t buffer_count;
    uint32_t format;           /* DXGI_FORMAT */
    uint32_t width, height;
    uint32_t color_space;      /* DXGI_COLOR_SPACE_TYPE or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
    uint32_t buffer_generation;/* increments on every successful resize */
} na_ev_swapchain_created;
/* Buffer association: a resource id is tied to (swapchain, buffer index,
 * generation) ONLY when the host observes GetBuffer for it; such resources
 * are emitted as NA_EV_RESOURCE_CREATED with creation_kind
 * NA_CREATE_SWAPCHAIN_BUFFER. Absent that observation the association is
 * UNAVAILABLE â€” never inferred from matching dimensions/format. Later
 * color-space changes are likewise unavailable in H1 unless observed. */
typedef struct na_ev_swapchain_resized {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id swapchain;
    int32_t  hresult;          /* failed resize => old generation persists */
    uint32_t buffer_count;
    uint32_t format;
    uint32_t width, height;
    uint32_t buffer_generation;/* new generation IF hresult >= 0 */
    uint64_t _pad;
} na_ev_swapchain_resized;
/* Present is a correlation boundary, not frame-resource ownership proof. */
typedef struct na_ev_present {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_object_id swapchain;
    uint64_t present_ordinal;  /* per-swapchain attempt counter, incl. failed */
    int32_t  hresult;
    uint32_t sync_interval;
    uint32_t present_flags;    /* DXGI_PRESENT_* */
    uint32_t backbuffer_index; /* at call time, or NA_UNKNOWN_U32 */
} na_ev_present;

/* ---- Loss / epoch (R4) ---- */
typedef struct na_ev_loss_mark {
    na_event_header hdr;       /* stream position marker only; authoritative
                                  counters live in na_observe_status */
    uint32_t group;            /* na_cap_group where loss occurred */
    uint32_t foundational;     /* 0/1 */
} na_ev_loss_mark;

typedef struct na_ev_epoch_begin {
    na_event_header hdr;       /* first event of an epoch */
    na_session_id session;
    na_epoch epoch;
} na_ev_epoch_begin;

/* Epoch validity boundary (R4): an epoch's inventory is usable ONLY after the
 * consumer receives NA_EV_INVENTORY_COMPLETE for it with complete == 1.
 * Between EPOCH_BEGIN and INVENTORY_COMPLETE the host replays a consistent
 * cut of live objects (creation-order snapshot; retirements of snapshot
 * objects are delivered AFTER the snapshot events, never interleaved before
 * their creation event). Foundational loss during replay forces complete == 0:
 * the epoch stays invalid and na_observe_resync() may be called again.
 * inventory_invalid in status clears only when the host has delivered
 * complete == 1 for the current epoch. Prior EXECUTION HISTORY (past
 * submissions, past presents, past descriptor versions) is NOT reconstructed
 * by any resync â€” snapshots restore only current-liveness inventory; history
 * before EPOCH_BEGIN is permanently unknown to the new epoch. */
typedef struct na_ev_inventory_complete {
    na_event_header hdr;
    na_epoch epoch;
    uint32_t complete;         /* 1 = baseline coherent; 0 = failed, retry */
    uint32_t objects_in_snapshot;
} na_ev_inventory_complete;

/* ---- Batch framing (R2) ----
 * A batch is one contiguous host-owned allocation:
 *   [na_event_batch header][payload: packed events]
 * header_bytes  = size of the header region (>= sizeof(na_event_batch) for
 *                 the host's minor; payload starts here).
 * payload_bytes = exact byte length of the packed events after the header.
 * Total readable bytes = header_bytes + payload_bytes. event_count events
 * live in the payload; decoding walks size_bytes until exactly payload_bytes
 * are consumed â€” anything else is malformed (treat as loss, discard batch).
 * Memory is valid only until the sink returns; consumers copy what they keep.
 * Batches arrive serially on one host worker thread. */
#define NA_BATCHF_FINAL 0x1u   /* last batch before na_observe_stop returns */
typedef struct na_event_batch {
    uint32_t header_bytes;
    uint16_t abi_major;        /* consumer must verify == compiled major */
    uint16_t abi_minor;        /* fields appended beyond the consumer's
                                  compiled structs are safely ignorable */
    na_session_id session;
    na_epoch epoch;
    uint32_t payload_bytes;
    uint32_t event_count;
    uint32_t batch_flags;      /* NA_BATCHF_* */
    uint32_t _pad;
} na_event_batch;

typedef void(__cdecl* na_event_sink)(const na_event_batch* batch, void* user);

/* ---- Status (R2/R4) ----
 * Caller sets size_bytes to its buffer capacity before the call; the host
 * fills min(capacity, host struct size) bytes, never more, and rewrites
 * size_bytes to the byte count actually written (old callers keep working;
 * capacity < 16 or capacity == 0 is invalid and the call fails). The returned
 * snapshot is COHERENT: session/epoch/inventory_invalid/counters are captured
 * together (host uses a seqlock internally). Callable from any thread,
 * INCLUDING inside the sink. */
typedef struct na_observe_status {
    uint32_t size_bytes;
    uint16_t abi_major;
    uint16_t abi_minor;
    na_session_id session;
    na_epoch epoch;
    uint32_t inventory_invalid; /* 0/1; clears only per R4 rule above */
    uint32_t _pad;
    uint64_t foundational_dropped;
    uint64_t detail_dropped;
    uint64_t batches_delivered;
    uint64_t per_group_dropped[16]; /* indexed by na_cap_group; >= COUNT_V1
                                       slots are reserved and 0 */
} na_observe_status;

typedef struct na_observe_config {
    uint32_t size_bytes;       /* caller sets to sizeof its compiled struct;
                                  unknown larger sizes rejected by older hosts,
                                  reserved fields must be 0 */
    uint32_t metadata_budget_mib;      /* 0 = default 32 */
    uint32_t detail_event_budget;      /* 0 = default 100000 (H2) */
    uint32_t constant_bytes_budget;    /* 0 = default 4 MiB (H2) */
    uint32_t presentation_boundaries;  /* 0 = default 8 (H2) */
    uint32_t _reserved[8];             /* must be 0 */
} na_observe_config;

/* Error codes (negative), 0 = success. */
#define NA_OK              0
#define NA_E_ABI          -1  /* major mismatch / bad struct sizes */
#define NA_E_STATE        -2  /* wrong lifecycle state / reentrancy */
#define NA_E_ARG          -3
#define NA_E_HOOK         -4  /* hooking backend failure */

/* Lifecycle. start(): registers the sink, generates a fresh random
 * session_id, begins epoch 1 with EPOCH_BEGIN + snapshot + INVENTORY_COMPLETE
 * (possibly complete == 0). A consumer holding records from an earlier start
 * must discard them on seeing a different session_id â€” epochs never resume
 * across sessions (R4). stop(): halts producers, delivers a final batch
 * flagged NA_BATCHF_FINAL, guarantees no sink call after return. stop() and
 * resync() MUST NOT be called from inside the sink (NA_E_STATE): both may
 * wait for the sink to return. query_status IS sink-safe. resync(): bumps
 * epoch and replays snapshot per R4; fails with NA_E_STATE when stopped. */
int  __cdecl na_observe_abi(uint16_t* out_major, uint16_t* out_minor);
int  __cdecl na_observe_start(const na_observe_config* cfg, na_event_sink sink,
                              void* user);
int  __cdecl na_observe_stop(void);
int  __cdecl na_observe_query_status(na_observe_status* inout);
int  __cdecl na_observe_resync(void);

#ifdef __cplusplus
} /* extern "C" */
#endif
#endif /* NA_HOST_OBSERVE_H */


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\host_observe_internal.hpp ---

// Internal producer/testing interface for the H1 observation host.
// Consumers use ONLY host_observe.h; this header is for the host's own
// producers (hooks) and its CPU tests.
//
// Transport (A24): per-producer-thread SPSC byte rings, claimed once per
// thread by CAS, drained by the single worker. Producers never lock, allocate
// or wait. Up to 32 producer threads; further threads drop with loss
// accounting. Semantics (framing, loss, epochs, status) are unchanged from
// the frozen contract and covered by the same tests.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>

#include "host_dx12/host_observe.h"

namespace na::observe_internal {

// Queues one fully-formed event (starting with na_event_header; size_bytes
// set; epoch field is OVERWRITTEN by the host with the current epoch).
// Foundational routing follows NA_EVF_FOUNDATIONAL. `group` selects the loss
// counter on drop. Returns false when the event was dropped (loss recorded).
bool Emit(const void* event_bytes, uint32_t group);

// Snapshot provider used by start()/resync(): must Emit() the inventory
// baseline (foundational events) and return true when the baseline is
// coherent and complete. Absent provider => empty snapshot, complete.
using SnapshotFn = std::function<bool()>;
void SetSnapshotProvider(SnapshotFn fn);

// Test hook: forces the next foundational Emit to be treated as overflow.
void TestForceFoundationalOverflow(bool enable);

} // namespace na::observe_internal


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\settings.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/settings.hpp"

#include <shlobj.h>
#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdio>
#include <cwchar>

// Persistence: games under Program Files run unelevated, so writes next to
// the game DLL fail silently (A17 finding). Defaults are read from the
// deploy-time ini beside the DLL; user changes are saved to and preferred
// from %LOCALAPPDATA%\NeuralAlias\NeuralAliasSL.ini.
namespace na::settings {
namespace {
Settings g_settings;
wchar_t g_ini_defaults[MAX_PATH] = {};
wchar_t g_ini_user[MAX_PATH] = {};
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_last_save{0};

void resolve_user_ini()
{
    if (g_ini_user[0]) return;
    wchar_t* local = nullptr;
    if (SUCCEEDED(SHGetKnownFolderPath(FOLDERID_LocalAppData, 0, nullptr, &local)) && local) {
        wchar_t dir[MAX_PATH];
        swprintf_s(dir, L"%s\\NeuralAlias", local);
        CreateDirectoryW(dir, nullptr);
        swprintf_s(g_ini_user, L"%s\\NeuralAliasSL.ini", dir);
        CoTaskMemFree(local);
    }
}

void load_from(const wchar_t* ini, bool only_if_present)
{
    if (!ini || !ini[0]) return;
    if (only_if_present && GetFileAttributesW(ini) == INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) return;
    g_settings.enabled.store(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"dlss", L"enabled", 1, ini) ? 1u : 0u);
    uint32_t mode = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"dlss", L"mode", 0, ini);
    if (mode >= static_cast<uint32_t>(DlssMode::Count)) mode = 0;
    g_settings.dlss_mode.store(mode);
    int preset = static_cast<int>(
        GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"dlss", L"preset", static_cast<UINT>(-1), ini));
    g_settings.preset.store((preset >= 1 && preset <= 13) ? preset : -1);
    g_settings.ray_reconstruction.store(
        GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"dlss", L"ray_reconstruction", 0, ini) ? 1u : 0u);
    g_settings.text_bold.store(
        GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"overlay", L"text_bold", 0, ini) ? 1u : 0u);
    g_settings.win_x.store(static_cast<int32_t>(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"overlay", L"win_x", 0, ini)));
    g_settings.win_y.store(static_cast<int32_t>(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"overlay", L"win_y", 0, ini)));
    g_settings.win_w.store(static_cast<int32_t>(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"overlay", L"win_w", 0, ini)));
    g_settings.win_h.store(static_cast<int32_t>(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"overlay", L"win_h", 0, ini)));
    auto pct = [&](const wchar_t* key, uint32_t def) {
        int v = static_cast<int>(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"nr", key, def, ini));
        return static_cast<uint32_t>(v < 0 ? 0 : (v > 100 ? 100 : v));
    };
    g_settings.nr_enabled.store(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"nr", L"enabled", 0, ini) ? 1u : 0u);
    g_settings.nr_intensity.store(pct(L"intensity", 100));
    g_settings.nr_structure.store(pct(L"structure", 100));
    g_settings.nr_tone.store(pct(L"tone", 0));
    g_settings.nr_skin.store(pct(L"skin", 100));
    int pw = static_cast<int>(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"nr", L"paper_white", 200, ini));
    g_settings.nr_paper_white.store(static_cast<uint32_t>(pw < 80 ? 80 : (pw > 1000 ? 1000 : pw)));
    uint32_t hm = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"nr", L"hdr_mode", 0, ini);
    g_settings.nr_hdr_mode.store(hm > 2 ? 0 : hm);
    g_settings.motion_enabled.store(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"motion", L"enabled", 0, ini) ? 1u : 0u);
    uint32_t mg = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"motion", L"grid", 4, ini);
    g_settings.motion_grid.store(mg == 1 || mg == 2 || mg == 4 ? mg : 4);
    uint32_t mp = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"motion", L"perf", 5, ini);
    g_settings.motion_perf.store(mp == 5 || mp == 10 || mp == 20 ? mp : 5);
    uint32_t mdz = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"motion", L"downscale", 2, ini);
    g_settings.motion_downscale.store(mdz == 1 || mdz == 2 || mdz == 4 ? mdz : 2);
    int reflex = static_cast<int>(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"latency", L"reflex", 1, ini));
    g_settings.reflex_mode.store(reflex >= 0 && reflex <= 2 ? reflex : 1);
    g_settings.fg_enabled.store(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"fg", L"enabled", 0, ini) ? 1u : 0u);
    g_settings.fg_preset.store(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"fg", L"preset", 1, ini) == 2 ? 2u : 1u);
    g_settings.fg_mode.store(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"fg", L"mode", 1, ini) ? 1u : 0u);
    uint32_t fm = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"fg", L"multiplier", 2, ini);
    g_settings.fg_multiplier.store(fm < 2 ? 2 : (fm > 6 ? 6 : fm));
    uint32_t ft = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"fg", L"target_fps", 0, ini);
    g_settings.fg_target_fps.store(ft > 1000 ? 0 : ft);
    g_settings.fg_frozen_at.store(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"fg", L"frozen_at", 0, ini)); // A69
}
} // namespace

Settings& Get() { return g_settings; }

void SetIniPath(const wchar_t* path)
{
    if (path) wcsncpy_s(g_ini_defaults, path, _TRUNCATE);
}

const wchar_t* UserIniPath()
{
    resolve_user_ini();
    return g_ini_user;
}

void Load()
{
    load_from(g_ini_defaults, false);
    resolve_user_ini();
    load_from(g_ini_user, true); // user choices win when present
}

bool Save()
{
    resolve_user_ini();
    const wchar_t* ini = g_ini_user[0] ? g_ini_user : g_ini_defaults;
    if (!ini[0]) {
        g_last_save.store(2);
        return false;
    }
    wchar_t v[32];
    bool ok = true;
    swprintf_s(v, L"%u", g_settings.enabled.load());
    ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"dlss", L"enabled", v, ini) != 0;
    swprintf_s(v, L"%u", g_settings.dlss_mode.load());
    ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"dlss", L"mode", v, ini) != 0;
    swprintf_s(v, L"%d", g_settings.preset.load());
    ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"dlss", L"preset", v, ini) != 0;
    swprintf_s(v, L"%u", g_settings.ray_reconstruction.load());
    ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"dlss", L"ray_reconstruction", v, ini) != 0;
    swprintf_s(v, L"%u", g_settings.text_bold.load());
    ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"overlay", L"text_bold", v, ini) != 0;
    struct { const wchar_t* key; int32_t value; } win[] = {
        {L"win_x", g_settings.win_x.load()}, {L"win_y", g_settings.win_y.load()},
        {L"win_w", g_settings.win_w.load()}, {L"win_h", g_settings.win_h.load()}};
    for (const auto& kv : win) {
        swprintf_s(v, L"%d", kv.value);
        ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"overlay", kv.key, v, ini) != 0;
    }
    struct { const wchar_t* key; uint32_t value; } nr[] = {
        {L"enabled", g_settings.nr_enabled.load()},   {L"intensity", g_settings.nr_intensity.load()},
        {L"structure", g_settings.nr_structure.load()}, {L"tone", g_settings.nr_tone.load()},
        {L"skin", g_settings.nr_skin.load()},         {L"paper_white", g_settings.nr_paper_white.load()},
        {L"hdr_mode", g_settings.nr_hdr_mode.load()}};
    for (const auto& kv : nr) {
        swprintf_s(v, L"%u", kv.value);
        ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"nr", kv.key, v, ini) != 0;
    }
    struct { const wchar_t* key; uint32_t value; } motion[] = {
        {L"enabled", g_settings.motion_enabled.load()}, {L"grid", g_settings.motion_grid.load()}, {L"perf", g_settings.motion_perf.load()},
        {L"downscale", g_settings.motion_downscale.load()}};
    for (const auto& kv : motion) {
        swprintf_s(v, L"%u", kv.value);
        ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"motion", kv.key, v, ini) != 0;
    }
    swprintf_s(v, L"%d", g_settings.reflex_mode.load());
    ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"latency", L"reflex", v, ini) != 0;
    struct { const wchar_t* key; uint32_t value; } fg[] = {
        {L"enabled", g_settings.fg_enabled.load()}, {L"mode", g_settings.fg_mode.load()}, {L"preset", g_settings.fg_preset.load()},
        {L"multiplier", g_settings.fg_multiplier.load()}, {L"target_fps", g_settings.fg_target_fps.load()},
        {L"frozen_at", g_settings.fg_frozen_at.load()}};
    for (const auto& kv : fg) {
        swprintf_s(v, L"%u", kv.value);
        ok &= WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"fg", kv.key, v, ini) != 0;
    }
    g_last_save.store(ok ? 1u : 2u);
    return ok;
}

uint32_t LastSaveResult() { return g_last_save.load(); }

} // namespace na::settings


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\settings.hpp ---

// Route A runtime settings: the single source the ini, the menu and the NGX
// override layer all agree on. Every field is a std::atomic so cross-thread
// reads are tear-free without locks on the hot path.
//
// Model (A19): the menu mirrors a game's DLSS page. `enabled` is the master
// switch (OFF = the game's own DLSS settings apply, NeuralAlias passes
// everything through). `dlss_mode` selects the render scale NVIDIA defines
// for each quality mode; NeuralAlias answers the engine's optimal-settings
// query with that size. Future features (frame generation, neural
// rendering) add their own fields here and their own menu sections.
//
// A30 (proposal S-001, additive) added a text preference under [overlay];
// A44 replaced it with `text_bold` (0 standard, 1 bold) and added the
// remembered window placement (win_x/y/w/h). A sharpness field is
// deliberately NOT added until a backend exists.
#pragma once

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>

namespace na::settings {

enum class DlssMode : uint32_t {
    DLAA = 0,         // 100% â€” render at output resolution
    Quality = 1,      // 66.7%
    Balanced = 2,     // 58%
    Performance = 3,  // 50%
    UltraPerformance = 4, // 33.3%
    Count = 5
};

// NVIDIA's published per-mode scale factors.
inline float ScaleForMode(DlssMode m)
{
    switch (m) {
    case DlssMode::DLAA: return 1.0f;
    case DlssMode::Quality: return 0.66666667f;
    case DlssMode::Balanced: return 0.58f;
    case DlssMode::Performance: return 0.5f;
    case DlssMode::UltraPerformance: return 0.33333334f;
    default: return 1.0f;
    }
}
inline const char* NameForMode(DlssMode m)
{
    switch (m) {
    case DlssMode::DLAA: return "DLAA";
    case DlssMode::Quality: return "Quality";
    case DlssMode::Balanced: return "Balanced";
    case DlssMode::Performance: return "Performance";
    case DlssMode::UltraPerformance: return "Ultra Performance";
    default: return "?";
    }
}

struct Settings {
    std::atomic<uint32_t> enabled{1};   // master switch (NeuralAlias controls DLSS)
    std::atomic<uint32_t> dlss_mode{0}; // DlssMode
    std::atomic<int32_t> preset{-1};    // -1 = let NVIDIA choose; 1..13 = A..M
    std::atomic<uint32_t> ray_reconstruction{0}; // honored only where the game uses RR
    std::atomic<uint32_t> overlay_open{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> text_bold{0}; // A44: 0 standard weight, 1 bold (replaces text_size)
    // A44: remembered menu window placement in client pixels (0 = default).
    std::atomic<int32_t> win_x{0}, win_y{0}, win_w{0}, win_h{0};
    // Neural Rendering (A30, [nr] section). Off by default; percentages 0..100.
    std::atomic<uint32_t> nr_enabled{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> nr_intensity{100};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> nr_structure{100};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> nr_tone{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> nr_skin{100};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> nr_paper_white{200}; // nits, HDR chains only
    std::atomic<uint32_t> nr_hdr_mode{0};      // 0 auto (swap chain colour space), 1 force HDR10, 2 force SDR
    // A52: live hardware optical flow ([motion] section). Experimental, off by default.
    std::atomic<uint32_t> motion_enabled{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> motion_grid{4};      // 1, 2 or 4 (NVOFA grid)
    std::atomic<uint32_t> motion_perf{5};      // 5 best, 10 medium, 20 fast
    std::atomic<uint32_t> motion_downscale{2}; // A58: engine input = display / downscale (1, 2 or 4); 2 â‰ˆ 4x cheaper
    // A59: Frame Generation ([fg] section). Off by default.
    //  mode: 0 Fixed (always `multiplier`), 1 Dynamic (up to `multiplier`,
    //        chosen every frame to reach `target_fps`, or the display's
    //        refresh rate when target_fps is 0).
    //  multiplier: 2..6 (frames shown per rendered frame).
    std::atomic<int32_t> reflex_mode{1}; // 0 Off, 1 On (default), 2 On + Boost; engine adapter supplies timings
    std::atomic<uint32_t> fg_enabled{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> fg_mode{1};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> fg_preset{1}; // NVIDIA driver-profile request: A=1 (default), B=2; restart after change
    std::atomic<uint32_t> fg_multiplier{2};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> fg_target_fps{0};
    // A69: the freeze watchdog switched Frame Generation off at this time
    // (seconds since 1970, 0 = never). Shown in the menu until FG is re-enabled.
    std::atomic<uint32_t> fg_frozen_at{0};
};

Settings& Get();

// Deploy-time defaults ini (beside the proxy DLL; may be read-only).
void SetIniPath(const wchar_t* path);
// Where user changes are saved: %LOCALAPPDATA%\NeuralAlias\NeuralAliasSL.ini
const wchar_t* UserIniPath();
void Load();  // defaults ini, then user ini overrides when present
// Settings -> user ini (only our keys). Returns false when any write failed;
// the menu then says the choice is active for this session but not saved.
bool Save();
// Result of the most recent Save(): 0 never saved, 1 ok, 2 failed.
uint32_t LastSaveResult();

} // namespace na::settings


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\adapters\tlou1\apply.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/adapters/tlou1/apply.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/hooking/vtable_hook.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/ngx_observe/ngx_observe.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/settings.hpp"
#include <windows.h>
#include <bcrypt.h>
#include <intrin.h>
#include <atomic>
#include <array>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <mutex>

namespace na::tlou1 {
namespace {
constexpr uint32_t kFrame = 0x11c4ecc, kPredicate = 0x1f27be8, kCaller = 0x11ca0ce;
constexpr uint32_t kRebuild = 0x1f29350, kRebuildCaller = 0x11ca0fc;
constexpr size_t kDirty = 0x15a91;
constexpr char kHash[] = "088d37aff3b8e3f65715e7e325547c07e6c4d794966c0573cd0f7af5c6c3503a";
using FrameFn = uintptr_t (*)(void*);
using PredicateFn = bool (*)(void*);
using FreeResourcesFn = bool (__cdecl*)(uint32_t, uint32_t);
FrameFn g_frame = nullptr;
FrameFn g_rebuild = nullptr;
PredicateFn g_predicate = nullptr;
uintptr_t g_caller = 0;
uintptr_t g_rebuild_caller = 0;
FreeResourcesFn g_free_resources = nullptr;
size_t g_dirty = kDirty;
std::mutex g_mutex;
std::once_flag g_init;
ApplyRequest g_request;
bool g_available = false;
bool g_dispatch_active = false; // mutex protected; even cancelled invocations must return
char g_reason[160] = "This game has no automatic picture-mode adapter.";
std::atomic<bool> g_queued{false};
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_frame_calls{0}, g_predicate_calls{0}, g_triggers{0};
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_resource_releases{0}, g_resource_release_failures{0};
uint32_t g_trigger_thread = 0;
struct Invocation { void* context; uint64_t request; bool triggered = false, released = false; };
thread_local Invocation* t_invocation = nullptr;

Choice current_choice() {
    auto& st = na::settings::Get();
    auto opt = na::ngx::GetOptimalStats();
    Choice c{st.dlss_mode.load(), opt.last_user_w, opt.last_user_h, st.preset.load()};
    if (c.mode && c.mode <= 3) {
        float scale = na::settings::ScaleForMode(static_cast<na::settings::DlssMode>(c.mode));
        c.width = (static_cast<uint32_t>(c.width * scale + .5f) + 7u) & ~7u;
        c.height = (static_cast<uint32_t>(c.height * scale + .5f) + 7u) & ~7u;
    }
    return c;
}
void refresh_locked() {
    auto prior = g_request.stage;
    g_request.Tick(GetTickCount64(), na::settings::Get().enabled.load() != 0, current_choice());
    if (g_request.Busy()) {
        auto c = na::ngx::GetSizeConfirmation();
        if (c.id != g_request.id || c.state == 4) g_request.stage = Stage::Failed;
        else g_request.Observe(c.id, c.state);
    }
    if (!g_request.Busy()) g_queued.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    if (prior != g_request.stage) {
        if (g_request.stage == Stage::Confirmed) strcpy_s(g_reason, "Picture mode applied; the game is drawing at the requested resolution.");
        else if (g_request.stage == Stage::Cancelled) {
            strcpy_s(g_reason, "The selection changed. Choose Apply again.");
            if (na::ngx::GetSizeConfirmation().id == g_request.id) na::ngx::CancelSizeConfirmation();
        } else if (g_request.stage == Stage::Failed) strcpy_s(g_reason, "The game did not confirm the requested picture mode. See Details.");
    }
}

bool predicate_detour(void* context) {
    const uintptr_t caller = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(_ReturnAddress());
    // Always let the engine perform its own comparison and side effects first.
    const bool changed = g_predicate(context);
    g_predicate_calls.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    auto* invocation = t_invocation;
    if (!invocation || context != invocation->context || caller != g_caller) return changed;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    refresh_locked();
    if (!g_request.Trigger(invocation->request, true, true)) return changed;
    invocation->triggered = true;
    g_trigger_thread = GetCurrentThreadId();
    g_triggers.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    strcpy_s(g_reason, "The game is rebuilding its picture; checking the following frames.");
    return true; // caller performs its original waits, cleanup and size query
}

uintptr_t rebuild_detour(void* context) {
    const uintptr_t caller = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(_ReturnAddress());
    auto* invocation = t_invocation;
    if (invocation && invocation->triggered && !invocation->released &&
        context == invocation->context && caller == g_rebuild_caller) {
        // The pinned caller has already waited for previous GPU work. TLOU's
        // own cleanup deliberately skips SL when its cached upscaler type is
        // DLSS (2). SL 1.x only notices mode/output-size changes, not our
        // overridden internal size. Release its viewport through the public
        // API here, so its next Evaluate rebuilds all cached DLSS resources.
        // The pinned game's DLSS instance is 0 (its feature-constant calls).
        const bool released = g_free_resources(0, 0);
        invocation->released = true; // exactly one attempt per invocation
        if (released) g_resource_releases.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        else g_resource_release_failures.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        if (released) na::ngx::MarkSizeConfirmationRestored(invocation->request);
        else if (g_request.id == invocation->request) {
            g_request.stage = Stage::Failed;
            na::ngx::CancelSizeConfirmation();
            strcpy_s(g_reason, "DLSS could not release its previous setup. Restart the game before retrying.");
        }
    }
    return g_rebuild(context);
}

uintptr_t frame_detour(void* context) {
    g_frame_calls.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (!context || t_invocation || !g_queued.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return g_frame(context);
    uint64_t id = 0;
    {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex, std::try_to_lock);
        if (!lock.owns_lock()) return g_frame(context);
        refresh_locked();
        if (!g_dispatch_active && g_request.Enter(GetTickCount64(), na::settings::Get().enabled.load() != 0, current_choice())) {
            id = g_request.id;
            g_dispatch_active = true;
            g_queued.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
        }
    }
    if (!id) return g_frame(context);
    // This byte belongs to the engine-owned frame context, never a persistent
    // settings object. Its verified consumers take the normal rebuild path.
    // 11c9f74 recomputes it before calling the predicate. No quality field is
    // edited; the existing optimal-settings override supplies the new size.
    auto* dirty = static_cast<unsigned char*>(context) + g_dirty;
    const unsigned char before = *dirty;
    *dirty = 1;
    Invocation invocation{context, id};
    t_invocation = &invocation;
    const uintptr_t result = g_frame(context);
    t_invocation = nullptr;
    if (!invocation.triggered) {
        // No verified rebuild branch was visited. Do not leave our marker on
        // this invocation's context. Wait for a later eligible frame using the
        // same request and deadline; once triggered, a rebuild is never retried.
        if (*dirty == 1) *dirty = before;
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        refresh_locked();
        g_request.Leave(id);
        if (g_request.stage == Stage::Queued) {
            g_queued.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
            strcpy_s(g_reason, "Waiting for the game's next graphics-settings update.");
        }
    }
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex); g_dispatch_active = false; }
    return result;
}

bool hash_file(const wchar_t* path, char (&hex)[65]) {
    HANDLE file = CreateFileW(path, GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_FLAG_SEQUENTIAL_SCAN, nullptr);
    if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return false;
    BCRYPT_ALG_HANDLE alg = nullptr; BCRYPT_HASH_HANDLE hash = nullptr;
    bool ok = BCryptOpenAlgorithmProvider(&alg, BCRYPT_SHA256_ALGORITHM, nullptr, 0) >= 0;
    if (ok) ok = BCryptCreateHash(alg, &hash, nullptr, 0, nullptr, 0, 0) >= 0;
    std::array<unsigned char, 65536> bytes{};
    DWORD n = 0;
    while (ok) {
        if (!ReadFile(file, bytes.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(bytes.size()), &n, nullptr)) { ok = false; break; }
        if (!n) break;
        ok = BCryptHashData(hash, bytes.data(), n, 0) >= 0;
    }
    std::array<unsigned char, 32> digest{};
    if (ok) ok = BCryptFinishHash(hash, digest.data(), static_cast<ULONG>(digest.size()), 0) >= 0;
    if (hash) BCryptDestroyHash(hash);
    if (alg) BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(alg, 0);
    CloseHandle(file);
    if (ok) for (size_t i = 0; i < digest.size(); ++i) sprintf_s(hex + i * 2, 65 - i * 2, "%02x", digest[i]);
    return ok;
}
bool code_matches(uintptr_t base, uint32_t rva, const unsigned char* bytes, size_t n) {
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mi{};
    auto* p = reinterpret_cast<const void*>(base + rva);
    if (!VirtualQuery(p, &mi, sizeof(mi)) || mi.State != MEM_COMMIT ||
        !(mi.Protect & (PAGE_EXECUTE_READ | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY)) ||
        (mi.Protect & PAGE_GUARD) || reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(mi.AllocationBase) != base) return false;
    if (base + rva + n > reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(mi.BaseAddress) + mi.RegionSize) return false;
    return std::memcmp(p, bytes, n) == 0;
}
bool install(uintptr_t frame, uintptr_t predicate, uintptr_t caller, uintptr_t rebuild, uintptr_t rebuild_caller) {
    g_caller = caller;
    g_rebuild_caller = rebuild_caller;
    if (!na::hooking::BeginInstall()) return false;
    bool ok = na::hooking::PrepareHook(reinterpret_cast<void*>(frame), reinterpret_cast<void*>(&frame_detour), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_frame)) &&
              na::hooking::PrepareHook(reinterpret_cast<void*>(predicate), reinterpret_cast<void*>(&predicate_detour), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_predicate)) &&
              na::hooking::PrepareHook(reinterpret_cast<void*>(rebuild), reinterpret_cast<void*>(&rebuild_detour), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_rebuild));
    if (!ok) { na::hooking::AbandonInstall(); return false; }
    return na::hooking::CommitInstall();
}
} // namespace

void InitializeApply() {
    std::call_once(g_init, [] {
        wchar_t path[32768]{};
        DWORD n = GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, path, 32768);
        if (!n || n == 32768) return;
        const wchar_t* name = wcsrchr(path, L'\\'); name = name ? name + 1 : path;
        if (_wcsicmp(name, L"tlou-i.exe") != 0) return;
        char hash[65]{};
        if (!hash_file(path, hash) || std::strcmp(hash, kHash)) {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
            strcpy_s(g_reason, "Automatic picture-mode Apply is not available for this game version."); return;
        }
        uintptr_t base = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(GetModuleHandleW(nullptr));
        constexpr unsigned char frame[] = {0x48,0x8b,0xc4,0x48,0x89,0x58,0x10,0x48,0x89,0x70,0x18,0x48,0x89,0x78,0x20,0x55,0x41,0x54,0x41,0x55,0x41,0x56,0x41,0x57};
        constexpr unsigned char pred[] = {0x48,0x89,0x5c,0x24,0x08,0x48,0x89,0x6c,0x24,0x10,0x48,0x89,0x74,0x24,0x18,0x57,0x48,0x83,0xec,0x30,0x33,0xdb,0x48,0x8b};
        constexpr unsigned char caller[] = {0xe8,0x1a,0xdb,0xd5,0x00,0x84,0xc0,0x74,0x2a};
        constexpr unsigned char dirty_read[] = {0x44,0x8a,0xfb,0x38,0x99,0x91,0x5a,0x01,0x00,0x74,0x03,0x41,0xb7,0x01};
        constexpr unsigned char rebuild[] = {0x48,0x8b,0xc4,0x48,0x89,0x58,0x08,0x48,0x89,0x68,0x10,0x48,0x89,0x70,0x18,0x48,0x89,0x78,0x20,0x41,0x56,0x48,0x83,0xec};
        constexpr unsigned char rebuild_caller[] = {0xe8,0x54,0xf2,0xd5,0x00};
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        if (!code_matches(base, kFrame, frame, sizeof(frame)) || !code_matches(base, kPredicate, pred, sizeof(pred)) ||
            !code_matches(base, kCaller - 5, caller, sizeof(caller)) || !code_matches(base, 0x11c67f7, dirty_read, sizeof(dirty_read)) ||
            !code_matches(base, kRebuild, rebuild, sizeof(rebuild)) || !code_matches(base, kRebuildCaller - 5, rebuild_caller, sizeof(rebuild_caller))) {
            strcpy_s(g_reason, "Automatic picture-mode Apply found a different engine layout."); return;
        }
        g_available = install(base + kFrame, base + kPredicate, base + kCaller, base + kRebuild, base + kRebuildCaller);
        strcpy_s(g_reason, g_available ? "Automatic picture-mode Apply is ready." : "Automatic picture-mode Apply could not attach.");
    });
}
bool RequestApply(Choice choice) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    refresh_locked();
    if (!g_available || g_dispatch_active || g_request.Busy() || !na::settings::Get().enabled.load() || !Same(choice, current_choice()) ||
        choice.mode > 3 || !choice.width || !choice.height) return false;
    if (!g_free_resources) {
        // The original SL module is already loaded by the game by this point.
        // Never load a library or resolve an export inside a rendering detour.
        HMODULE sl = GetModuleHandleW(L"sl_interposer_real.dll");
        if (sl) g_free_resources = reinterpret_cast<FreeResourcesFn>(reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(sl, "slFreeResources")));
        if (!g_free_resources) {
            strcpy_s(g_reason, "The game's Streamline rebuild API is unavailable.");
            return false;
        }
    }
    uint64_t id = na::ngx::ArmSizeConfirmation(choice.width, choice.height, 30, 2500, 6000, 15000);
    if (!g_request.Arm(id, GetTickCount64(), choice)) return false;
    strcpy_s(g_reason, "Waiting for the game's next graphics-settings update.");
    g_queued.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    return true;
}
ApplyStatus GetApplyStatus() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    refresh_locked();
    ApplyStatus s{};
    s.available = g_available; s.stage = g_request.stage; s.request_id = g_request.id; s.choice = g_request.choice;
    s.frame_calls = g_frame_calls.load(); s.predicate_calls = g_predicate_calls.load(); s.triggers = g_triggers.load();
    s.resource_releases = g_resource_releases.load(); s.resource_release_failures = g_resource_release_failures.load();
    s.trigger_thread = g_trigger_thread; strcpy_s(s.reason, g_reason); return s;
}
#ifdef NA_TLOU_APPLY_TEST
// Linked only into the CPU fixture, never either deployed proxy.
bool InstallApplyForTest(uintptr_t frame, uintptr_t predicate, uintptr_t caller, size_t dirty,
                         uintptr_t rebuild, uintptr_t rebuild_caller, FreeResourcesFn release) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex); g_dirty = dirty; g_free_resources = release;
    g_available = install(frame, predicate, caller, rebuild, rebuild_caller); return g_available;
}
#endif
} // namespace na::tlou1

#pragma once

namespace na::tlou1 {
// Init-thread only, after the shared hooking core is initialized. Exact exe pin;

void InitializeApply();
struct ApplyStatus {
    bool available = false;
    Stage stage = Stage::Idle;
    uint64_t request_id = 0, frame_calls = 0, predicate_calls = 0, triggers = 0;
    uint64_t resource_releases = 0, resource_release_failures = 0;
    uint32_t trigger_thread = 0;
    Choice choice{};

// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\adapters\tlou1\apply_request.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <cstdint>

namespace na::tlou1 {
enum class Stage : uint32_t { Idle, Queued, Entered, Rebuilding, Confirmed, Failed, Cancelled };
struct Choice {
    uint32_t mode = 0, width = 0, height = 0;
    int32_t preset = -1;
};
inline bool Same(const Choice& a, const Choice& b) {
    return a.mode == b.mode && a.width == b.width && a.height == b.height && a.preset == b.preset;
}
// Protected by the adapter mutex. No game pointer survives an invocation.
struct ApplyRequest {
    Stage stage = Stage::Idle;
    uint64_t id = 0, deadline = 0;
    Choice choice{};
    bool Busy() const { return stage == Stage::Queued || stage == Stage::Entered || stage == Stage::Rebuilding; }
    bool Arm(uint64_t request_id, uint64_t now, Choice requested) {
        if (Busy() || !request_id || requested.mode > 3 || !requested.width || !requested.height) return false;
        id = request_id; choice = requested; deadline = now + 15000; stage = Stage::Queued; return true;
    }
    void Tick(uint64_t now, bool enabled, Choice current) {
        if (!Busy()) return;
        if (!enabled || !Same(choice, current)) stage = Stage::Cancelled;
        else if (now >= deadline) stage = Stage::Failed;
    }
    bool Enter(uint64_t now, bool enabled, Choice current) {
        Tick(now, enabled, current);
        if (stage != Stage::Queued) return false;
        stage = Stage::Entered; return true;
    }
    bool Trigger(uint64_t request_id, bool same_context, bool known_caller) {
        if (request_id != id || stage != Stage::Entered || !same_context || !known_caller) return false;
        stage = Stage::Rebuilding; return true;
    }
    void Leave(uint64_t request_id) {
        // Some frame invocations skip the settings branch. No rebuild happened;
        // keep this same request queued without extending its original deadline.
        if (id == request_id && stage == Stage::Entered) stage = Stage::Queued;
    }
    void Observe(uint64_t request_id, uint32_t confirmation_state) {
        if (id != request_id || stage != Stage::Rebuilding) return;
        if (confirmation_state == 3) stage = Stage::Confirmed;
        else if (confirmation_state == 4) stage = Stage::Failed;
    }
};
} // namespace na::tlou1


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\adapters\tlou1\present_batch.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <cstddef>
namespace na::tlou1 {
// Pinned 1.1.5 submit-worker records: stride80, opcode at77, Present=6,
// engine frame at48 (passed to RenderFrameParams lookup immediately before
// DXGI Present). A multi-Present batch is deliberately not attributed.
inline uint64_t PresentBatchFrame(const uint8_t* records, size_t size) {
    if (size%80) return 0;
    uint64_t result=0; unsigned presents=0;
    for(size_t i=0;i<size;i+=80) if(records[i+77]==6) {
        if(++presents!=1) return 0;
        uint64_t frame; std::memcpy(&frame,records+i+48,8);
        if(frame==UINT64_MAX) return 0;
        result=frame+1;
    }
    return result;
}
}



// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\adapters\tlou1\timing.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/adapters/tlou1/timing.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/adapters/tlou1/present_batch.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/latency/nvidia_latency.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/latency/pcl_stats.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/hooking/vtable_hook.hpp"
#include <windows.h>
#include <bcrypt.h>
#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <cstring>
#include <intrin.h>
#include <mutex>

namespace na::tlou1 {
namespace {
using Job = void(*)(void*);
using Call = void(*)();
Job simulation = nullptr, render_job = nullptr;
Call input = nullptr, kick_render = nullptr;
using SubmitBatch = uintptr_t(*)(void*,void*,void*);
SubmitBatch submit_batch=nullptr;
thread_local uint64_t present_id=0;
thread_local bool in_submit_batch=false;
using FrameParams = void*(*)(uint64_t);
FrameParams frame_params=nullptr;
uintptr_t present_frame_return=0;
void* frame_params_detour(uint64_t frame) {
    // Read the exact frame passed by the Present record as it is consumed,
    // not an entry-time scan of the worker's mutable batch.
    if(in_submit_batch && reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(_ReturnAddress())==present_frame_return)
        present_id=frame==UINT64_MAX ? 0 : frame+1;
    return frame_params(frame);
}
uintptr_t submit_batch_detour(void* a,void* batch,void* c) {
    const auto previous=present_id;
    const auto previous_scope=in_submit_batch;
    in_submit_batch=true; present_id=0;
    const auto result=submit_batch(a,batch,c);
    present_id=previous; in_submit_batch=previous_scope;
    return result;
}
thread_local uint64_t render_id = 0;
std::atomic<uint64_t> simulation_id{0};
std::atomic<bool> attached{false};
std::mutex trace_mutex; TimingTrace trace;
// This build's game loop waits for its simulation job before advancing its
// engine frame number. PreProcessUpdate consumes input before rendering starts.
void simulation_detour(void* context) {
    const uint64_t id = context ? *static_cast<const uint64_t*>(context) + 1 : 0;
    simulation_id.store(id, std::memory_order_release);
    na::latency::EngineBegin(id);
    simulation(context);
    simulation_id.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
}
void input_detour() {
    na::latency::EngineMark(simulation_id.load(std::memory_order_acquire), na::latency::Phase::Input);
    input();
}
void kick_detour() {
    const auto id = simulation_id.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    na::latency::EngineMark(id, na::latency::Phase::SimulationEnd);
    kick_render();
}
void render_detour(void* context) {
    const auto previous = render_id;
    render_id = context ? *static_cast<const uint64_t*>(context) + 1 : 0;
    na::latency::EngineMark(render_id, na::latency::Phase::Render);
    render_job(context);
    render_id = previous;
}
bool pinned_executable() {
    wchar_t path[32768]{};
    auto n = GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, path, 32768);
    if (!n || n == 32768) return false;
    auto name = wcsrchr(path, L'\\'); name = name ? name + 1 : path;
    if (_wcsicmp(name, L"tlou-i.exe")) return false;
    HANDLE file = CreateFileW(path, GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_FLAG_SEQUENTIAL_SCAN, nullptr);
    if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return false;
    BCRYPT_ALG_HANDLE alg = nullptr; BCRYPT_HASH_HANDLE hash = nullptr;
    bool ok = BCryptOpenAlgorithmProvider(&alg, BCRYPT_SHA256_ALGORITHM, nullptr, 0) >= 0;
    if (ok) ok = BCryptCreateHash(alg, &hash, nullptr, 0, nullptr, 0, 0) >= 0;
    std::array<unsigned char, 65536> bytes{};
    DWORD read = 0;
    while (ok) {
        if (!ReadFile(file, bytes.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(bytes.size()), &read, nullptr)) { ok = false; break; }
        if (!read) break;
        ok = BCryptHashData(hash, bytes.data(), read, 0) >= 0;
    }
    std::array<unsigned char, 32> digest{};
    if (ok) ok = BCryptFinishHash(hash, digest.data(), 32, 0) >= 0;
    if (hash) BCryptDestroyHash(hash);
    if (alg) BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(alg, 0);
    CloseHandle(file);
    constexpr unsigned char expected[] = {0x08,0x8d,0x37,0xaf,0xf3,0xb8,0xe3,0xf6,0x57,0x15,0xe7,0xe3,0x25,0x54,0x7c,0x07,0xe6,0xc4,0xd7,0x94,0x96,0x6c,0x05,0x73,0xcd,0x0f,0x7a,0xf5,0xc6,0xc3,0x50,0x3a};
    return ok && !std::memcmp(expected, digest.data(), sizeof(expected));
}
}
void InitializeTiming() {
    if (attached.load() || !pinned_executable()) return;
    const auto base = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(GetModuleHandleW(nullptr));
    present_frame_return=base+0x21930cd;
    if (!na::hooking::BeginInstall()) return;
