            publish_pair_locked(r, si, target, true);
            lstrcpynA(v->reason, "thunk pool exhausted for a slot", sizeof(v->reason));
            continue;
        }
        plans[plan_count++] = {si, free_thunk, target};
    }
    if (plan_count) {
        bool ok = na::hooking::BeginInstall();
        for (uint32_t i = 0; ok && i < plan_count; ++i) {
            Slot& s = r.slots[plans[i].slot];
            ok = na::hooking::PrepareHook(plans[i].target, s.thunks[plans[i].thunk], &s.originals[plans[i].thunk]);
            if (!ok) lstrcpynA(v->reason, "prepare failed for a target", sizeof(v->reason));
        }
        if (ok) ok = na::hooking::CommitInstall();
        else na::hooking::AbandonInstall();
        if (ok) {
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < plan_count; ++i) {
                r.slots[plans[i].slot].assigned_target[plans[i].thunk] = plans[i].target;
                publish_pair_locked(r, plans[i].slot, plans[i].target, false);
            }
            hooked += plan_count;
            r.targets_hooked.fetch_add(plan_count, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        } else {
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < plan_count; ++i) {
                Slot& s = r.slots[plans[i].slot];
                if (s.originals[plans[i].thunk]) { s.assigned_target[plans[i].thunk] = plans[i].target; s.dead[plans[i].thunk] = true; }
                publish_pair_locked(r, plans[i].slot, plans[i].target, true);
            }
            missing += plan_count;
            r.installs_failed.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            if (!v->reason[0]) lstrcpynA(v->reason, "install transaction failed", sizeof(v->reason));
        }
    }
    v->slots_hooked = hooked;
    v->slots_missing = missing;
    v->coverage = (missing == 0 && hooked == r.slot_count) ? Coverage::Complete : Coverage::Incomplete;
    return v->coverage;
}

uint32_t drain(Registry& r)
{
    uint32_t drained = 0;
    for (;;) {
        uint32_t rd = r.queue_read.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (rd == r.queue_write.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) break;
        uint32_t i = rd % kQueueCapacity;
        // A claimed slot whose snapshot is not yet written: wait briefly, never for ever.
        int spins = 0;
        while (!r.queue_ready[i].load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
            if (++spins > 1000) { r.queue_drained.fetch_add(drained, std::memory_order_relaxed); return drained; }
            Sleep(0);
        }
        Snapshot snap = r.queue[i];
        r.queue_ready[i].store(0, std::memory_order_release);
        r.queue_read.store(rd + 1, std::memory_order_release);
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(r.mutex);
            r.discovery_started = true;
            ensure_locked(r, snap.targets, snap.count, nullptr);
        }
        ++drained;
    }
    r.queue_drained.fetch_add(drained, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    return drained;
}

DWORD WINAPI DiscoveryThread(LPVOID)
{
    while (!g_stop.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        WaitForSingleObject(g_queue_event, 250);
        DrainQueueNow();
        if (g_idle) g_idle(g_idle_user);
    }
    return 0;
}

struct Frame { const void* receiver; uint32_t slot; };
thread_local Frame t_stack[kScopeDepth];
thread_local uint32_t t_depth = 0;

} // namespace

bool Register(Kind kind, const SlotSpec* specs, uint32_t count)
{
    Registry& r = R(kind);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(r.mutex);
    if (r.discovery_started || !specs || count == 0 || r.slot_count + count > kMaxSlots) return false;
    // Validate the WHOLE input before touching anything (A49 fix 1).
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        const SlotSpec& sp = specs[i];
        if (!sp.thunks || !sp.originals || sp.thunk_count == 0 || sp.thunk_count > kMaxThunksPerSlot) return false;
        for (uint32_t k = 0; k < r.slot_count; ++k) if (r.slots[k].slot == sp.slot) return false;
        for (uint32_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) if (specs[j].slot == sp.slot) return false; // duplicate within the call
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        r.pub_slots[r.slot_count].store(specs[i].slot, std::memory_order_release);
        Slot& s = r.slots[r.slot_count++];
        s.slot = specs[i].slot;
        s.thunk_count = specs[i].thunk_count;
        s.thunks = specs[i].thunks;
        s.originals = specs[i].originals;
        for (uint32_t t = 0; t < kMaxThunksPerSlot; ++t) { s.assigned_target[t] = nullptr; s.dead[t] = false; }
        for (uint32_t t = 0; t < s.thunk_count; ++t) s.originals[t] = nullptr;
    }
    r.pub_slot_count.store(r.slot_count, std::memory_order_release);
    return true;
}

bool Clear()
{
    if (!StopDiscoveryThread(5000)) return false; // never clear under a live worker or an admitted producer
    for (Registry& r : g_reg) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(r.mutex);
        r.slot_count = 0;
        r.table_count = 0;
        r.discovery_started = false;
        r.pub_slot_count.store(0);
        r.pub_count.store(0);
        r.dead_count.store(0);
        r.queue_write.store(0); r.queue_read.store(0);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kQueueCapacity; ++i) r.queue_ready[i].store(0);
        r.targets_hooked.store(0); r.targets_shared.store(0); r.cross_slot.store(0); r.thunks_exhausted.store(0); r.installs_failed.store(0);
        r.queue_pushes.store(0); r.queue_overflow.store(0); r.queue_drained.store(0);
    }
    g_scope_overflow.store(0);
    g_admission_closed.store(false); // reopened only after proven quiescence above
    return true;
}

Coverage EnsureTargets(Kind kind, const void* const* targets, uint32_t count)
{
    Registry& r = R(kind);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(r.mutex);
    r.discovery_started = true;
    return ensure_locked(r, targets, count, nullptr);
}

Coverage EnsureObject(Kind kind, void* object)
{
    if (!object) return Coverage::Unknown;
    Registry& r = R(kind);
    void* snap[kMaxSlots];
    uint32_t n = snapshot_of(r, object, snap);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(r.mutex);
    r.discovery_started = true;
    return ensure_locked(r, snap, n, *static_cast<void**>(object));
}

uint32_t WarmUpCommandLists(ID3D12Device* device, uint32_t type_mask)
{
    if (!device) return 0;
    uint32_t ensured = 0;
    const D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE types[4] = {D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_DIRECT, D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_COMPUTE,
                                              D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_COPY, D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_BUNDLE};
    for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
        if (!(type_mask & (1u << t))) continue;
        ComPtr<ID3D12CommandAllocator> alloc;
        ComPtr<ID3D12GraphicsCommandList> list;
        if (FAILED(device->CreateCommandAllocator(types[t], IID_PPV_ARGS(&alloc)))) continue;
        if (FAILED(device->CreateCommandList(0, types[t], alloc.Get(), nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&list)))) continue;
        EnsureObject(Kind::CommandList, list.Get()); ++ensured;
        list->Close();
        if (SUCCEEDED(list->Reset(alloc.Get(), nullptr))) { EnsureObject(Kind::CommandList, list.Get()); ++ensured; list->Close(); }
        if (SUCCEEDED(list->Reset(alloc.Get(), nullptr))) { EnsureObject(Kind::CommandList, list.Get()); ++ensured; list->Close(); }
    }
    return ensured;
}

Coverage CoverageOf(Kind kind, void* object)
{
    if (!object) return Coverage::Unknown;
    Registry& r = R(kind);
    void* snap[kMaxSlots];
    uint32_t n = snapshot_of(r, object, snap);
    return coverage_of_targets(r, snap, n);
}

bool Active(Kind kind) { return R(kind).pub_slot_count.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0; }

Coverage NoteObject(Kind kind, void* object)
{
    if (!object) return Coverage::Unknown;
    Registry& r = R(kind);
    if (!r.pub_slot_count.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return Coverage::Unknown;
    void* snap[kMaxSlots];
    uint32_t n = snapshot_of(r, object, snap);
    Coverage c = coverage_of_targets(r, snap, n);
    if (c != Coverage::Unknown) return c;
    // A50 (R1/R3): no producer-side duplicate scan (a queue slot can be reused
    // under a scanning producer); duplicates are harmlessly coalesced by the
    // consumer (ensure is idempotent). Producer accounting brackets admission
    // and the write, so Stop/Clear can prove no producer still owns a slot.
    struct ProducerGuard { ProducerGuard() { g_producers_active.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); } ~ProducerGuard() { g_producers_active.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); } } guard;
    if (g_admission_closed.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return Coverage::Unknown;
    if (HANDLE pause = g_pause_producer_for_test.exchange(nullptr, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) WaitForSingleObject(pause, 10000); // test injection only
    for (;;) {
        uint32_t wr = r.queue_write.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        uint32_t rd = r.queue_read.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (wr - rd >= kQueueCapacity) { r.queue_overflow.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed); return Coverage::Unknown; }
        if (r.queue_write.compare_exchange_weak(wr, wr + 1, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) {
            uint32_t k = wr % kQueueCapacity;
            std::memcpy(r.queue[k].targets, snap, n * sizeof(void*));
            r.queue[k].count = n;
            r.queue_ready[k].store(1, std::memory_order_release);
            r.queue_pushes.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            if (g_queue_event) SetEvent(g_queue_event);
            return Coverage::Unknown;
        }
    }
}

uint32_t DrainQueueNow()
{
    uint32_t n = 0;
    for (Registry& r : g_reg) n += drain(r);
    return n;
}

bool StartDiscoveryThread(void (*idle)(void*), void* user)
{
    if (g_thread) return true;
    g_idle = idle;
    g_idle_user = user;
    if (!g_queue_event) g_queue_event = CreateEventW(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, nullptr);
    if (!g_queue_event) return false;
    g_stop.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    g_admission_closed.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    g_thread = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, &DiscoveryThread, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
    return g_thread != nullptr;
}

bool StopDiscoveryThread(uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    // A50 (R3): admission is closed FIRST, whether or not a worker exists;
    // then producers already admitted must finish (bounded wait on this
    // control thread), then the worker must exit. Any timeout returns false
    // and leaves every state in place.
    g_admission_closed.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    ULONGLONG deadline = GetTickCount64() + timeout_ms;
    while (g_producers_active.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        if (GetTickCount64() >= deadline) return false;
        Sleep(1);
    }
    if (!g_thread) return true;
    g_stop.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    SetEvent(g_queue_event);
    ULONGLONG now = GetTickCount64();
    DWORD remaining = now >= deadline ? 0 : static_cast<DWORD>(deadline - now);
    if (WaitForSingleObject(g_thread, remaining) != WAIT_OBJECT_0) return false; // worker still active: keep everything
    CloseHandle(g_thread);
    g_thread = nullptr;
    g_idle = nullptr;
    g_idle_user = nullptr;
    return true;
}

bool ProducersQuiescent() { return g_admission_closed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && g_producers_active.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0; }
void PauseNextProducerForTest(void* event) { g_pause_producer_for_test.store(static_cast<HANDLE>(event), std::memory_order_release); }

bool GetTableInfo(Kind kind, uint32_t index, TableInfo* out)
{
    if (!out) return false;
    Registry& r = R(kind);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(r.mutex);
    if (index >= r.table_count) return false;
    const Table& v = r.tables[index];
    out->table = v.table;
    out->coverage = v.coverage;
    out->slots_hooked = v.slots_hooked;
    out->slots_missing = v.slots_missing;
    lstrcpynA(out->reason, v.reason, sizeof(out->reason));
    lstrcpynA(out->module, v.module, sizeof(out->module));
    return true;
}

Stats GetStats(Kind kind)
{
    Registry& r = R(kind);
    Stats s{};
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(r.mutex);
    s.slots_registered = r.slot_count;
    s.tables_known = r.table_count;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < r.table_count; ++i) {
        if (r.tables[i].coverage == Coverage::Complete) ++s.tables_complete; else ++s.tables_incomplete;
    }
    s.targets_hooked = r.targets_hooked.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.targets_shared = r.targets_shared.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.cross_slot_rejected = r.cross_slot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.thunks_exhausted = r.thunks_exhausted.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.installs_failed = r.installs_failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.queue_pushes = r.queue_pushes.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.queue_overflow = r.queue_overflow.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.queue_drained = r.queue_drained.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.scope_overflow = g_scope_overflow.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.bundles_unsupported = 1;
    return s;
}

CallScope::CallScope(const void* receiver, uint32_t slot)
{
    outermost = true;
    ambiguous = false;
    uint32_t scan = t_depth < kScopeDepth ? t_depth : kScopeDepth;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < scan; ++i)
        if (t_stack[i].receiver == receiver && t_stack[i].slot == slot) { outermost = false; break; }
    if (t_depth < kScopeDepth) {
        t_stack[t_depth] = {receiver, slot};
    } else {
        ambiguous = true; // deeper frames are not recorded; the answer cannot be proven
        g_scope_overflow.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    }
    ++t_depth;
}
CallScope::~CallScope() { if (t_depth) --t_depth; }

} // namespace na::hooking::variants


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\hooking\variant_registry.hpp ---

// A48/A49: implementation-variant registry for COM vtable observation
// (COLLABORATION.md B-005/B-006 Â§3 as amended; corrections per
// A48-REGISTRY-REVIEW).
//
// Problem: a D3D12 command list starts on the runtime's generic vtable and,
// after its first successful Reset, is re-pointed at ANOTHER table: on WARP
// and on NVIDIA hardware that table is allocated per list on the heap, and
// its entries for binding/draw methods live in the user-mode driver.
// Function-level hooks on the first implementation therefore stop seeing
// the list, and a table ADDRESS is not an identity (it is per object and can
// be freed). The concrete functions inside the tables ARE shared, so this
// registry is keyed by (kind, slot, concrete target function).
//
// Model: per KIND of interface (command lists and devices are separate
// registries: their slot numbers would otherwise collide), an observer
// registers, per vtable SLOT, a POOL of thunks (one detour per possible
// implementation variant, each with its own original-pointer storage). The
// first time a target is seen IN THAT SLOT it is assigned the next free
// thunk of the slot and hooked through the shared installation transaction;
// a target shared by several tables in the same slot reuses its thunk. A
// target already hooked for a DIFFERENT slot is not treated as covered
// (signature/observer mismatch): it is reported Incomplete, never Complete.
//
// Coverage of an object = every registered slot of its CURRENT table holds
// a target hooked for that slot. It is CURRENT target coverage only; the
// adapter keeps a recording generation gapped until a fully observed
// Reset-through-Close sequence.
//
// Discovery: (a) warm-up â€” host-owned lists driven through the states that
// swap tables; (b) late â€” a hot path (the host's ExecuteCommandLists
// detour) calls NoteObject on each submitted list: an unknown object's slot
// TARGETS are snapshotted (bounded, immutable copy; the table itself is
// never dereferenced later) into a lock-free queue for the discovery
// thread. Overflow is counted and the object stays Unknown. Producers do
// not scan the queue (a slot can be reused under them); duplicate requests
// are coalesced by the single consumer, whose ensure step is idempotent.
//
// Forwarding: a thunk opens a CallScope(receiver, slot); `outermost` is
// false only when an enclosing scope on the SAME thread has the SAME
// receiver AND slot. Nesting deeper than the bounded stack is reported as
// `ambiguous` (and counted), never read out of bounds.
//
// Bundles are NOT discovered here (they never reach ExecuteCommandLists);
// Stats reports bundle coverage as unsupported.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

struct ID3D12Device;

namespace na::hooking::variants {

constexpr uint32_t kMaxSlots = 32;
constexpr uint32_t kMaxThunksPerSlot = 8;
constexpr uint32_t kMaxTables = 16;      // informational snapshots kept
constexpr uint32_t kQueueCapacity = 16;
constexpr uint32_t kScopeDepth = 32;

enum class Kind : uint32_t { CommandList = 0, Device = 1 };
constexpr uint32_t kKinds = 2;

struct SlotSpec {
    uint32_t slot;          // vtable index (e.g. 30 = ID3D12GraphicsCommandList::SetGraphicsRootSignature)
    uint32_t thunk_count;   // <= kMaxThunksPerSlot
    void* const* thunks;    // thunk_count detour functions with the slot's exact signature
    void** originals;       // thunk_count original-pointer slots, one per thunk (written by the registry)
};

enum class Coverage : uint32_t { Unknown = 0, Complete = 1, Incomplete = 2 };

struct Stats {
    uint32_t slots_registered;
    uint32_t tables_known;        // informational snapshots (capped at kMaxTables)
    uint32_t tables_complete;
    uint32_t tables_incomplete;
    uint32_t targets_hooked;      // distinct (slot, target) pairs hooked
    uint32_t targets_shared;      // slot/target pairs met again in another table (thunk reused)
    uint32_t cross_slot_rejected; // a target seen in a slot other than the one it was hooked for (-> Incomplete)
    uint32_t thunks_exhausted;    // (-> Incomplete)
    uint32_t installs_failed;     // (-> Incomplete)
    uint32_t queue_pushes, queue_overflow, queue_drained;
    uint32_t scope_overflow;      // CallScope nesting beyond kScopeDepth (ambiguous)
    uint32_t bundles_unsupported; // always 1
};

// Registration: once per kind, before any discovery for that kind. The
// whole input is validated first (duplicate slots within the call or
// against earlier registrations are rejected atomically, nothing mutated).
// Thunk and original arrays must outlive the registry.
bool Register(Kind kind, const SlotSpec* specs, uint32_t count);
// Tests: forget everything (hooks are removed by na::hooking::Shutdown).
// A50: refuses (false, nothing touched) unless admission is closed, every
// admitted producer has finished and the worker has exited. A51 contract:
// Clear and re-registration additionally REQUIRE hot-callback quiescence
// enforced by the caller (no thread inside NoteObject/CoverageOf, whose
// target snapshot is read before the producer guard, and no thunk in
// flight); they are test/teardown operations, never performed live.
bool Clear();
// Lifetime assumption (documented for the first Anno use): hooked targets
// are code inside the D3D12 runtime and the user-mode driver, which stay
// loaded for the process lifetime; the registry never unloads or re-hooks.

// Install-thread side (blocking). `object` must be valid for the call; its
// current table is read immediately. Never from a render callback.
Coverage EnsureObject(Kind kind, void* object);
// Same, from an immutable target snapshot (one entry per registered slot,
// in registration order).
Coverage EnsureTargets(Kind kind, const void* const* targets, uint32_t count);

// Warm-up for command lists on the given device: DIRECT/COMPUTE/COPY/BUNDLE
// (mask bits 1/2/4/8) lists driven through create -> Close -> Reset -> Close
// -> Reset with EnsureObject after each state. Returns states ensured.
uint32_t WarmUpCommandLists(ID3D12Device* device, uint32_t type_mask = 1 | 2 | 4 | 8);

// Hot path (any thread, lock-free): coverage of the object's CURRENT table.
// NoteObject additionally snapshots the slot targets of an Unknown object
// into the bounded queue (once per distinct snapshot). Returns Unknown when
// nothing is registered for the kind.
Coverage NoteObject(Kind kind, void* object);
Coverage CoverageOf(Kind kind, void* object);
bool Active(Kind kind);

// Discovery thread: drains the queue (EnsureTargets per snapshot) and calls
// `idle` (may be null) after each drain. StopDiscoveryThread closes admission
// and waits for the worker; it returns false and keeps every state if the
// worker has not exited within the timeout (nothing is cleared then).
bool StartDiscoveryThread(void (*idle)(void*), void* user);
// A50 (R3): closes admission first (worker or not), then waits (bounded, on
// the calling control thread) for every already-admitted producer to finish
// its write, then for the worker to exit. Any timeout returns false and
// keeps every state; call again later.
bool StopDiscoveryThread(uint32_t timeout_ms = 5000);
bool ProducersQuiescent(); // admission closed and no producer between admission and write
uint32_t DrainQueueNow(); // synchronous drain on the calling thread (tests)
// Test injection: the next NoteObject producer, after admission, waits on
// `event` (a Windows event handle) before writing its snapshot.
void PauseNextProducerForTest(void* event);

struct TableInfo {
    void* table;
    Coverage coverage;
    uint32_t slots_hooked, slots_missing;
    char reason[64];
    char module[64];        // module of the first registered slot's target
};
bool GetTableInfo(Kind kind, uint32_t index, TableInfo* out);
Stats GetStats(Kind kind);

// Forwarding scope for thunks (thread-local stack, bounded kScopeDepth).
struct CallScope {
    bool outermost;  // false = an enclosing scope on this thread has the same receiver and slot
    bool ambiguous;  // nesting exceeded the bounded stack: the answer above is not proven
    CallScope(const void* receiver, uint32_t slot);
    ~CallScope();
    CallScope(const CallScope&) = delete;
    CallScope& operator=(const CallScope&) = delete;
};

} // namespace na::hooking::variants


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\hooking\vtable_hook.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/hooking/vtable_hook.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <MinHook.h>

#include <map>
#include <mutex>
#include <vector>

namespace na::hooking {
namespace {

std::mutex g_mutex;
int g_init_refs = 0;

struct HookRecord {
    void* detour = nullptr;
    void* trampoline = nullptr;
    bool enabled = false;
    bool dead = false; // A46: was enabled once, could not be cleanly removed; unpatched, trampoline kept
};
// Keyed by the concrete target function. Guarded by g_mutex.
std::map<void*, HookRecord> g_hooks;

// Transaction state (guarded by g_mutex). A46: one owner thread; every
// original-pointer publication of a pending target is tracked so a
// cancellation can null all of them.
struct Pending {
    void* target;
    std::vector<void**> outs;
};
bool g_in_transaction = false;
DWORD g_transaction_owner = 0;
std::vector<Pending> g_pending;
int g_fail_enable_at_for_test = -1; // A46 test injection: the Nth enable in a commit "fails"
bool g_fail_next_remove_for_test = false;  // A47: the next MinHook removal is treated as failed (not performed)
bool g_fail_next_disable_for_test = false; // A47: the next MinHook disable is treated as failed (not performed)
char g_last_error[160] = "";
uint32_t g_dead_count = 0;

thread_local int t_detour_depth = 0;

void set_error(const char* text)
{
    lstrcpynA(g_last_error, text, sizeof(g_last_error));
}

// A47: MinHook disable/remove with their results honoured (and test
// injection). A failure leaves the record owned and marks it dead: the
// target may still be patched and the trampoline must stay callable.
bool disable_checked(void* target)
{
    if (g_fail_next_disable_for_test) { g_fail_next_disable_for_test = false; return false; }
    return MH_DisableHook(target) == MH_OK;
}
bool remove_checked(void* target)
{
    if (g_fail_next_remove_for_test) { g_fail_next_remove_for_test = false; return false; }
    MH_STATUS st = MH_RemoveHook(target);
    return st == MH_OK || st == MH_ERROR_NOT_CREATED;
}
// Marks a record dead after a failed cleanup: kept, never reused, counted.
void retain_dead_locked(void* target, const char* why)
{
    auto it = g_hooks.find(target);
    if (it == g_hooks.end()) return;
    if (!it->second.dead) ++g_dead_count;
    it->second.dead = true;
    set_error(why);
}

Pending* find_pending(void* target)
{
    for (Pending& p : g_pending)
        if (p.target == target) return &p;
    return nullptr;
}

// Creates the hook and publishes the trampoline. Enables it only when
// `enable` is set; otherwise records it as pending for CommitInstall.
bool hook_target_locked(void* target, void* detour, void** out_original, bool enable)
{
    if (!target || !detour || !out_original) return false;
    auto it = g_hooks.find(target);
    if (it != g_hooks.end()) {
        // Idempotent: same target may be reached through many objects sharing
        // one vtable. A second registration with a different detour is a
        // programming error we refuse rather than silently chain.
        if (it->second.detour != detour || it->second.dead) return false;
        *out_original = it->second.trampoline;
        if (!enable) {
            // A46 (A45 review finding 1): a pending target published to a
            // second variable is tracked too, so Abandon nulls both.
            if (Pending* p = find_pending(target)) p->outs.push_back(out_original);
        }
        return true;
    }
    void* trampoline = nullptr;
    if (MH_CreateHook(target, detour, &trampoline) != MH_OK) {
        set_error("MH_CreateHook failed");
        return false;
    }
    // Publish BEFORE enabling (A45): a caller that reaches the detour the
    // instant the patch lands must find its original pointer already set.
    *out_original = trampoline;
    g_hooks[target] = HookRecord{detour, trampoline, false, false};
    if (enable) {
        if (MH_EnableHook(target) != MH_OK) {
            set_error("MH_EnableHook failed");
            // A47: the failed hook is removed only if MinHook confirms it;
            // otherwise the record is retained dead and the original stays valid.
            if (remove_checked(target)) {
                *out_original = nullptr;
                g_hooks.erase(target);
            } else {
                retain_dead_locked(target, "MH_EnableHook failed and the removal failed too; record retained");
            }
            return false;
        }
        g_hooks[target].enabled = true;
    } else {
        g_pending.push_back({target, {out_original}});
    }
    return true;
}

// Removes never-enabled pending hooks (safe: no thread can be inside them)
// and nulls every published original. A47: a removal MinHook refuses keeps
// its record (dead) and its published originals, and is reported.
bool drop_never_enabled_locked()
{
    bool ok = true;
    for (Pending& p : g_pending) {
        auto it = g_hooks.find(p.target);
        if (it != g_hooks.end() && !it->second.enabled && !it->second.dead) {
            if (remove_checked(p.target)) {
                g_hooks.erase(it);
                for (void** o : p.outs) if (o) *o = nullptr;
            } else {
                retain_dead_locked(p.target, "removal of a never-enabled hook failed; record retained");
                ok = false;
            }
        }
    }
    return ok;
}

} // namespace

bool Initialize()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_init_refs == 0) {
        if (MH_Initialize() != MH_OK) return false;
    }
    ++g_init_refs;
    return true;
}

void Shutdown()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_init_refs == 0) return;
    if (g_in_transaction) {
        // A46: only the transaction owner may tear down; another thread's
        // Shutdown during a transaction is refused (refcount untouched).
        if (g_transaction_owner != GetCurrentThreadId()) {
            set_error("Shutdown refused: another thread owns an open transaction");
            return;
        }
        drop_never_enabled_locked();
        g_pending.clear();
        g_in_transaction = false;
    }
    if (--g_init_refs == 0) {
        // A47: every disable/remove result is honoured. A hook that cannot be
        // cleanly taken out keeps its record (dead) and MinHook stays
        // initialised so its trampoline remains valid; the session is then
        // NOT torn down (refcount held at 1) and LastError says so.
        bool ok = true;
        for (auto it = g_hooks.begin(); it != g_hooks.end();) {
            void* target = it->first;
            bool disabled = !it->second.enabled || disable_checked(target);
            if (disabled) it->second.enabled = false;
            bool removed = disabled && remove_checked(target);
            if (removed) {
                if (it->second.dead && g_dead_count) --g_dead_count; // A48: a removed dead record no longer counts
                it = g_hooks.erase(it);
            } else {
                retain_dead_locked(target, "Shutdown: a hook could not be disabled/removed; record retained, session kept alive");
                ok = false;
                ++it;
            }
        }
        if (!ok) {
            g_init_refs = 1;
            return;
        }
        g_dead_count = 0;
        if (MH_Uninitialize() != MH_OK) {
            set_error("MH_Uninitialize failed");
            g_init_refs = 1;
        }
    }
}

bool HookVTableSlot(void* object, uint32_t slot_index, void* detour,
                    void** out_original)
{
    if (!object) return false;
    void** vtable = *static_cast<void***>(object);
    if (!vtable) return false;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_init_refs == 0 || g_in_transaction) return false;
    return hook_target_locked(vtable[slot_index], detour, out_original, true);
}

bool HookFunction(void* target, void* detour, void** out_original)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_init_refs == 0 || g_in_transaction) return false;
    return hook_target_locked(target, detour, out_original, true);
}

bool BeginInstall()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_init_refs == 0 || g_in_transaction) return false;
    g_in_transaction = true;
    g_transaction_owner = GetCurrentThreadId();
    g_pending.clear();
    g_last_error[0] = 0;
    return true;
}

bool PrepareHook(void* target, void* detour, void** out_original)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_init_refs == 0 || !g_in_transaction || g_transaction_owner != GetCurrentThreadId()) return false;
    return hook_target_locked(target, detour, out_original, false);
}

bool PrepareVTableSlot(void* object, uint32_t slot_index, void* detour, void** out_original)
{
    if (!object) return false;
    void** vtable = *static_cast<void***>(object);
    if (!vtable) return false;
    return PrepareHook(vtable[slot_index], detour, out_original);
}

bool CommitInstall()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (!g_in_transaction || g_transaction_owner != GetCurrentThreadId()) return false;
    // A46 (A45 review finding 2): hooks are enabled one at a time so the
    // exact enabled subset is known. On a failure, hooks that were never
    // enabled are removed and their originals nulled (nothing can be inside
    // them); hooks that WERE enabled are disabled again but their records and
    // trampolines are kept (a detour may still be executing with its
    // original pointer in hand), marked dead and counted; the caller learns
    // the partial outcome through the return value and LastError().
    bool ok = true;
    int index = 0;
    for (Pending& p : g_pending) {
        auto it = g_hooks.find(p.target);
        if (it == g_hooks.end()) { ++index; continue; }
        bool enabled = index != g_fail_enable_at_for_test && MH_EnableHook(p.target) == MH_OK;
        ++index;
        if (enabled) {
            it->second.enabled = true;
            continue;
        }
        ok = false;
        set_error("enable failed during commit; partially installed set rolled back where safe");
        break;
    }
    g_fail_enable_at_for_test = -1;
    if (!ok) {
        for (Pending& p : g_pending) {
            auto it = g_hooks.find(p.target);
            if (it == g_hooks.end()) continue;
            if (it->second.enabled) {
                if (disable_checked(p.target)) {
                    it->second.enabled = false;
                    retain_dead_locked(p.target, "enable failed during commit; partially installed set rolled back where safe");
                } else {
                    retain_dead_locked(p.target, "enable failed during commit AND a rollback disable failed; hook left enabled");
                }
            }
        }
        drop_never_enabled_locked();
    }
    g_pending.clear();
    g_in_transaction = false;
    return ok;
}

void AbandonInstall()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (!g_in_transaction || g_transaction_owner != GetCurrentThreadId()) return;
    drop_never_enabled_locked();
    g_pending.clear();
    g_in_transaction = false;
}

void FailNextCommitEnableForTest(int index)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    g_fail_enable_at_for_test = index;
}
void FailNextRemoveForTest()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    g_fail_next_remove_for_test = true;
}
void FailNextDisableForTest()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    g_fail_next_disable_for_test = true;
}

uint32_t LastError(char* out, uint32_t capacity)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex); // A47: synchronised copy
    uint32_t n = static_cast<uint32_t>(lstrlenA(g_last_error));
    if (out && capacity) lstrcpynA(out, g_last_error, static_cast<int>(capacity));
    return n;
}

uint32_t DeadHookCount()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    return g_dead_count;
}

void* OriginalFor(void* target)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    auto it = g_hooks.find(target);
    return it == g_hooks.end() ? nullptr : it->second.trampoline;
}

int DetourDepth() { return t_detour_depth; }
DetourScope::DetourScope() : depth(t_detour_depth++) {}
DetourScope::~DetourScope() { --t_detour_depth; }

bool Unhook(void* target_function)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_in_transaction) return false; // A46: no mutation of an open transaction's world
    auto it = g_hooks.find(target_function);
    if (it == g_hooks.end() || it->second.dead) return false;
    // A47: results honoured; on failure the record is retained (dead) and
    // the caller's original pointer stays valid.
    if (it->second.enabled && !disable_checked(target_function)) {
        retain_dead_locked(target_function, "Unhook: disable failed; hook left enabled, record retained");
        return false;
    }
    it->second.enabled = false;
    if (!remove_checked(target_function)) {
        retain_dead_locked(target_function, "Unhook: removal failed after disable; record retained");
        return false;
    }
    g_hooks.erase(it);
    return true;
}

uint32_t InstalledCount()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    return static_cast<uint32_t>(g_hooks.size());
}

} // namespace na::hooking


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\hooking\vtable_hook.hpp ---

// Shared hooking core for the DX12 observation host and the SL/NGX
// interception paths (OBSERVATION-API.md). Wraps MinHook with:
//  - one-time library init/deinit with refcounting,
//  - vtable-slot hooking for COM objects: the hook targets the concrete
//    function found in an object's vtable, so every object sharing that
//    vtable (i.e. same implementation) is observed at once,
//  - idempotent per-function registration (hooking the same target twice
//    returns the existing trampoline instead of failing),
//  - ordered removal that restores original bytes before MinHook shutdown,
//  - A45: an installation TRANSACTION (prepare all, publish every original,
//    then enable in one step) so a detour can never run before its original
//    pointer is visible; a per-thread detour depth so an observer can tell an
//    outermost call from a forwarded inner one; OriginalFor() lookup.
//
// Threading (A45 restatement, proposed to Route B in COLLABORATION.md B-005;
// A46 adds the owner/partial-failure rules below):
//  - Patching is safe against concurrent callers: MinHook suspends the other
//    threads while writing and relocates instruction pointers that sit inside
//    the patched bytes. What was NOT safe before A45 was publication: a hook
//    was enabled before its trampoline was written to the caller's variable.
//    Every install path now writes the trampoline first (HookFunction /
//    HookVTableSlot) or before the single enable step (PrepareHook +
//    CommitInstall), and a failed commit leaves the originals null and the
//    targets unpatched.
//  - Detours that read state published by the installer (module records,
//    slot tables) must be able to see that state before enable: prepare the
//    hooks, publish the state, then commit.
//  - Never install or remove under the Windows loader lock, never from inside
//    a detour or a render callback (installation suspends threads and takes
//    the install mutex): queue the work to an install/watch thread.
//  - Removal (Unhook/Shutdown) requires quiescence: no thread may be inside
//    the detour or its trampoline. In a live game hooks therefore persist for
//    the process lifetime; observers stop by flag.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::hooking {

// Begin/end a hooking session. Reference-counted; every Initialize must be
// paired with a Shutdown. Returns false on MinHook failure.
bool Initialize();
void Shutdown();

// Hooks `slot_index` of `object`'s vtable (0-based). On success writes the
// trampoline (original function) to *out_original BEFORE enabling the hook.
// If that concrete function is already hooked, returns the existing
// trampoline and does not double-patch. `object` is a COM interface pointer;
// only its vtable is read, no reference is kept.
bool HookVTableSlot(void* object, uint32_t slot_index, void* detour,
                    void** out_original);

// Hooks a free function (e.g. a resolved export). Same idempotence rule and
// the same publish-before-enable order.
bool HookFunction(void* target, void* detour, void** out_original);

// ---- A45 installation transaction ----
// BeginInstall opens a transaction (one at a time, per process). PrepareHook
// creates the hook DISABLED and writes its trampoline to *out_original at
// once; an already-hooked target returns its existing trampoline and is not
// part of the transaction. CommitInstall enables every prepared hook in one
// MinHook apply; if that fails, every prepared hook is removed and its
// *out_original reset to null, so callers never see a half-installed set.
// AbandonInstall drops the prepared hooks the same way without enabling.
// A46 (per A45-B005-B006-REVIEW): the transaction has ONE owner, the thread
// that called BeginInstall; Prepare/Commit/Abandon from any other thread
// are refused, and Unhook is refused while a transaction is open (Shutdown
// from a non-owner thread is refused too). Every publication of a pending
// target's original (a second PrepareHook of the same target into another
// variable) is tracked, so Abandon nulls all of them. A commit is a BATCH,
// not an all-or-nothing guarantee: hooks are enabled one by one; on a
// failure the never-enabled ones are removed and nulled (nothing can be
// inside them), the already-enabled ones are disabled again but their
// records and trampolines are KEPT (a detour may be mid-flight with its
// original in hand), marked dead and counted in DeadHookCount(); the caller
// gets false and LastError(). Dead targets cannot be re-hooked in this
// session.
bool BeginInstall();
bool PrepareHook(void* target, void* detour, void** out_original);
bool PrepareVTableSlot(void* object, uint32_t slot_index, void* detour, void** out_original);
bool CommitInstall();
void AbandonInstall();
// A47: a synchronised COPY of the most recent failure text (empty when none);
// returns its length. Safe from any thread.
uint32_t LastError(char* out, uint32_t capacity);
uint32_t DeadHookCount();    // hooks retained after a failed enable/disable/removal (dead, never reused)
// A47 cleanup contract: every MinHook disable/remove result is honoured on
// every path (immediate-enable failure, transaction rollback/abandon,
// Unhook, Shutdown). A hook whose cleanup fails keeps its record and
// trampoline (dead, counted), its published originals stay valid, the
// operation returns false/LastError, and Shutdown then keeps the session
// alive (refcount held) instead of uninitialising MinHook under live code.
// Test injection: the `index`-th enable (0-based, in preparation order) of
// the NEXT CommitInstall is treated as failed (-1 clears); the next
// removal / disable is treated as failed and NOT performed.
void FailNextCommitEnableForTest(int index);
void FailNextRemoveForTest();
void FailNextDisableForTest();

// The trampoline for an installed (or prepared) target, or null. Takes the
// install mutex: for setup-time use, not for hot paths (detours keep their
// own original pointers).
void* OriginalFor(void* target);

// Per-thread detour nesting. A detour that wants to distinguish the
// outermost call from a forwarded inner one (a runtime implementation that
// calls a driver implementation we also hook) constructs a DetourScope and
// observes only when `depth == 0`. Forwarding to the original is unaffected.
int DetourDepth();
struct DetourScope {
    int depth; // nesting depth on entry: 0 = outermost
    DetourScope();
    ~DetourScope();
    DetourScope(const DetourScope&) = delete;
    DetourScope& operator=(const DetourScope&) = delete;
};

// Removes one hook by its original target function pointer. Safe to call for
// a target that was never hooked (returns false). Requires quiescence.
bool Unhook(void* target_function);

// Number of currently installed hooks (diagnostics/tests).
uint32_t InstalledCount();

} // namespace na::hooking


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\host_boot\game_adapter.h ---

/* A54: the contract between the shared host bootstrap and a game adapter
 * linked into the same proxy DLL (COLLABORATION.md B-005 Â§2, packaging).
 *
 * The host owns startup: it runs on its own init thread after the loader
 * lock, initialises the hooking core and settings, starts the overlay (which
 * owns Present/ResizeBuffers/ExecuteCommandLists and the discovery thread),
 * then hands over to the adapter in two phases:
 *   na_adapter_prepare()  - BEFORE overlay Start: register menu sections
 *                           (na::menu::Register is not thread-safe and must
 *                           precede Start). Return 0 to opt out.
 *   na_adapter_start()    - AFTER overlay Start on the init thread: register
 *                           variant slots (na::hooking::variants::Register),
 *                           SubscribeDiscovery / SubscribeExecute, warm-up
 *                           when a device is known. Return 0 on failure; the
 *                           host records it and keeps running without the
 *                           adapter.
 *   na_adapter_stop()     - only from test hosts, after the host proved
 *                           quiescence (overlay Stop returned true). Never
 *                           called at process exit (PROCESS_DETACH is a no-op).
 *   na_adapter_name()     - short name for the status line ("Anno 1800").
 * The build links exactly one adapter library (CMake NA_GAME_ADAPTER_LIB) or
 * the host's stub, which reports "no adapter". No frozen ABI is involved:
 * this is a link-time contract inside one binary.
 */
#pragma once

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

int na_adapter_prepare(void);
int na_adapter_start(void);
void na_adapter_stop(void);
const char* na_adapter_name(void);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\host_boot\host_boot.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/adapters/tlou1/apply.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/host_boot/host_boot.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <mutex>

#include "host_dx12/fg/frame_gen.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/hooking/vtable_hook.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/host_boot/game_adapter.h"
#include "host_dx12/ngx_observe/gpu_timing.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/ngx_observe/ngx_observe.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/nr/nr_pass.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/overlay/na_style.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/overlay/overlay.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/overlay/presenter.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/overlay/route_a_panel.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/settings.hpp"

namespace na::host {
namespace {

std::mutex g_mutex;
Status g_status{};
HMODULE g_self = nullptr;
wchar_t g_ini[MAX_PATH] = {};

void set_error(const char* text)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    strncpy_s(g_status.error, text, _TRUNCATE);
}

DWORD WINAPI InitThread(LPVOID)
{
    // Once hooks go in, this module must never unload.
    HMODULE pinned = nullptr;
    GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_PIN | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS,
                       reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(&InitThread), &pinned);
    if (!na::hooking::Initialize()) { set_error("hooking core failed to initialise"); return 0; }
    na::settings::SetIniPath(g_ini);
    na::settings::Load();
    if (GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"overlay", L"enabled", 1, g_ini) == 1) {
        wchar_t wbuf[64] = {};
        GetPrivateProfileStringW(L"overlay", L"scale", L"1.25", wbuf, 64, g_ini);
        na::ui::SetScale(static_cast<float>(_wtof(wbuf)));
        char body[260] = {}, title[260] = {}, ini_a[MAX_PATH];
        WideCharToMultiByte(CP_ACP, 0, g_ini, -1, ini_a, MAX_PATH, nullptr, nullptr);
        GetPrivateProfileStringA("overlay", "font_body", "", body, sizeof(body), ini_a);
        GetPrivateProfileStringA("overlay", "font_title", "", title, sizeof(title), ini_a);
        na::ui::SetFontPaths(body, title);
    }
    // A61: Route A services for a DLSS game WITHOUT Streamline (the game loads
    // nvngx_dlss.dll itself and talks to the driver's NGX core directly). The
    // same steps the sl.interposer proxy takes, in the same order: hook the
    // NGX core before the game reaches it, watch for the core module, wire
    // the runtimes for Neural Rendering and Frame Generation, register the
    // Route A pages, enable the presenter. Off unless [route_a] enabled=1.
    bool route_a = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"route_a", L"enabled", 0, g_ini) == 1;
    bool route_a_started = false;
    if (route_a) {
        na::ngx::PreloadAndHookNgxCore();
        na::tlou1::InitializeApply();
        bool sync_hook = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"observe", L"sync_loader_hook", 0, g_ini) == 1;
        route_a_started = na::ngx::StartModuleWatch(sync_hook);
        na::nr::Runtime rt;
        if (na::ngx::GetRuntimeEntryPoints(&rt.create_feature, &rt.evaluate_feature, &rt.release_feature,
                                           &rt.allocate_parameters, &rt.destroy_parameters, &rt.get_capability_parameters)) {
            na::nr::SetRuntime(rt);
            na::fg::Runtime fr;
            fr.create_feature = rt.create_feature;
            fr.evaluate_feature = rt.evaluate_feature;
            fr.release_feature = rt.release_feature;
            fr.allocate_parameters = rt.allocate_parameters;
            fr.destroy_parameters = rt.destroy_parameters;
            fr.get_capability_parameters = rt.get_capability_parameters;
            na::fg::SetRuntime(fr);
        }
        na::ngx::SetDetailLogging(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"observe", L"detail", 0, g_ini) == 1);
        na::gputime::SetEnabled(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"observe", L"gpu_timing", 1, g_ini) == 1);
        na::overlay::RegisterRouteAMenu();
        na::present::SetEnabled(GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"present", L"proxy", 1, g_ini) == 1);
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
            g_status.route_a_enabled = 1;
            g_status.route_a_started = route_a_started ? 1u : 0u;
            if (!route_a_started) strncpy_s(g_status.error, "NGX module watch failed to start", _TRUNCATE);
        }
    }
    // Adapter phase 1: menu sections before the overlay starts.
    bool adapter_enabled = GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"adapter", L"enabled", 1, g_ini) == 1;
    bool prepared = adapter_enabled && na_adapter_prepare() != 0;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        g_status.adapter_prepared = prepared ? 1u : 0u;
        strncpy_s(g_status.adapter_name, na_adapter_name(), _TRUNCATE);
    }
    char tag[32];
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        strncpy_s(tag, g_status.tag, _TRUNCATE);
    }
    {
        wchar_t status_path[MAX_PATH];
        lstrcpynW(status_path, g_ini, MAX_PATH);
        for (int i = lstrlenW(status_path) - 1; i >= 0; --i)
            if (status_path[i] == L'\\' || status_path[i] == L'/') { status_path[i] = 0; break; }
        lstrcatW(status_path, L"\\NeuralAliasHost-status.log");
        na::overlay::SetStatusLogPath(status_path); // A55
    }
    bool started = na::overlay::Start(tag, nullptr);
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        g_status.host_started = started ? 1u : 0u;
    }
    if (!started) { set_error("overlay/host start failed"); return 0; }
    if (route_a) { // A61: the DLSS call log beside the ini, as the sl proxy writes it
        wchar_t log_path[MAX_PATH], configured[MAX_PATH] = {};
        GetPrivateProfileStringW(L"observe", L"log", L"", configured, MAX_PATH, g_ini);
        if (configured[0]) {
            lstrcpynW(log_path, configured, MAX_PATH);
        } else {
            lstrcpynW(log_path, g_ini, MAX_PATH);
            for (int i = lstrlenW(log_path) - 1; i >= 0; --i)
                if (log_path[i] == L'\\' || log_path[i] == L'/') { log_path[i] = 0; break; }
            lstrcatW(log_path, L"\\NeuralAliasHost-observe.log");
        }
        na::ngx::StartLogger(log_path);
    }
    // Adapter phase 2: subscriptions and registry after the host owns Execute/discovery.
    if (prepared) {
        bool ok = na_adapter_start() != 0;
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        g_status.adapter_started = ok ? 1u : 0u;
        if (!ok) strncpy_s(g_status.error, "adapter start failed; host runs without it", _TRUNCATE);
    }
    return 0;
}

} // namespace

bool StartFromIni(HMODULE self, const wchar_t* ini_name, const char* default_tag)
{
    g_self = self;
    wchar_t dir[MAX_PATH] = {};
    if (!GetModuleFileNameW(self, dir, MAX_PATH)) return false;
    for (int i = lstrlenW(dir) - 1; i >= 0; --i)
        if (dir[i] == L'\\' || dir[i] == L'/') { dir[i] = 0; break; }
    wsprintfW(g_ini, L"%s\\%s", dir, ini_name);
    if (GetFileAttributesW(g_ini) == INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) return false;
    if (GetPrivateProfileIntW(L"host", L"enabled", 0, g_ini) != 1) return false;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        g_status.host_enabled = 1;
        char tag_a[32] = {};
        wchar_t tag_w[32] = {};
        GetPrivateProfileStringW(L"host", L"tag", L"", tag_w, 32, g_ini);
        if (tag_w[0]) WideCharToMultiByte(CP_ACP, 0, tag_w, -1, tag_a, 32, nullptr, nullptr);
        strncpy_s(g_status.tag, tag_a[0] ? tag_a : (default_tag ? default_tag : "HOST"), _TRUNCATE);
    }
    HANDLE t = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, &InitThread, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
    if (!t) return false;
    CloseHandle(t);
    return true;
}

Status GetStatus()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    return g_status;
}

} // namespace na::host


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\host_boot\host_boot.hpp ---

// A54: shared host bootstrap for proxy DLLs that carry a game adapter
// (the DXGI proxy for non-Streamline titles such as Anno 1800; the
// sl.interposer proxy keeps its own Route A-specific init).
//
// Activation is ini-gated exactly like the SL proxy: `<ini_name>` next to
// the DLL with `[host] enabled=1`. Without it the proxy stays a pure
// forwarder with zero hooks. Everything runs on an init thread spawned from
// DllMain (no work under the loader lock): pin the module, hooking core,
// settings, overlay scale/fonts, adapter prepare, overlay Start (which owns
// the swap-chain/queue hooks and the discovery thread), adapter start.
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::host {

// Spawns the init thread when the ini enables the host. Returns true when a
// thread was started (the ini exists and enables the host).
bool StartFromIni(HMODULE self, const wchar_t* ini_name, const char* default_tag);

struct Status {
    uint32_t host_enabled;    // ini said enabled=1
    uint32_t host_started;    // overlay Start succeeded
    uint32_t adapter_prepared;
    uint32_t adapter_started;
    // A61: Route A services in a NON-Streamline DLSS game ([route_a] enabled=1):
    // NGX core hooked before the game reaches it, DLSS/NR/FG runtimes wired,
    // Route A menu pages, presenter (Frame Generation) enabled.
    uint32_t route_a_enabled;
    uint32_t route_a_started; // NGX core hooked and module watch running
    char adapter_name[32];
    char tag[32];
    char error[96];
};
Status GetStatus();

} // namespace na::host


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\latency\engine_frames.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <cstdint>

namespace na::latency {
// IDs come from an engine adapter, never a Present counter. Different frames
// may overlap; every individual frame must traverse the complete phase order.
enum class Phase : uint8_t { Simulation, Input, SimulationEnd, Render, RenderEnd, Present, Presented };
struct EngineFrames {
    struct Frame { uint64_t id = 0; Phase phase = Phase::Simulation; bool valid = false; };
    std::array<Frame, 128> frames{};
    uint64_t newest = 0, complete = 0, rejected = 0;
    bool Accept(uint64_t id, Phase phase) {
        if (!id) { ++rejected; return false; }
        auto& f = frames[id % frames.size()];
        if (phase == Phase::Simulation) {
            if (id <= newest) { ++rejected; return false; }
            newest = id; f = {id, phase, true}; return true;
        }
        if (!f.valid || f.id != id || static_cast<unsigned>(phase) != static_cast<unsigned>(f.phase) + 1) {
            ++rejected;
            if (f.id == id) f.valid = false;
            return false;
        }
        f.phase = phase;
        if (phase == Phase::Presented) ++complete;
        return true;
    }
};
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\latency\native_audit.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <cstdint>

namespace na::latency {
// Observations only: incoming markers do not prove correct engine placement.
// No inferred frame IDs or reconstruction of missing phases.
struct NativeCall {
    uint64_t count = 0, successes = 0, device = 0, frame = 0;
    uint64_t caller = 0, begin_qpc = 0, end_qpc = 0;
    uint32_t thread = 0;
    int32_t result = -1;
};
struct NativeAudit {
    static constexpr uint32_t Sleep = 13, Unknown = 14, Slots = 15;
    std::array<NativeCall, Slots> calls{};
    void Record(uint32_t kind, NativeCall call) {
        auto& slot = calls[kind < Slots ? kind : Unknown];
        call.count = slot.count + 1;
        call.successes = slot.successes + (call.result == 0 ? 1 : 0);
        slot = call;
    }
};
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\latency\nvidia_latency.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/latency/nvidia_latency.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/latency/policy.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/latency/pcl_stats.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/settings.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/hooking/vtable_hook.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/adapters/tlou1/timing.hpp"
#include <wrl/client.h>
#include <dxgi.h>
#include <nvapi.h>
#include <mutex>
#include <atomic>
#include <intrin.h>

namespace na::latency {
namespace {
using Query = void* (__cdecl*)(unsigned);
decltype(&NvAPI_D3D_SetSleepMode) set_mode = nullptr;
decltype(&NvAPI_D3D_GetSleepStatus) get_status = nullptr;
decltype(&NvAPI_D3D_GetLatency) get_latency = nullptr;
decltype(&NvAPI_D3D_Sleep) native_sleep = nullptr;
decltype(&NvAPI_D3D_SetLatencyMarker) native_marker = nullptr;
std::atomic<bool> sleep_observed{false}, marker_observed{false};
std::mutex audit_lock;
NativeAudit audit;
std::atomic<uint64_t> audit_dropped{0};
std::atomic<bool> ready{false};
std::mutex control, stats_lock;
Stats stats;
IUnknown* device_identity = nullptr; // identity only; never dereferenced outside Update/caller
NV_SET_SLEEP_MODE_PARAMS game_params{};
bool have_game_params = false;
int applied = -2;
ULONGLONG last_poll = 0;
uint64_t last_native_frame = 0;
std::atomic<uint64_t> game_calls{0};
std::atomic<bool> external_owner{false};
std::mutex engine_mutex;
Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Device> engine_device;
EngineFrames engine_frames;
uint64_t engine_sleep_count = 0, engine_marker_count = 0, engine_errors = 0;
uint64_t last_sleep_frame = 0;
int32_t engine_result = -1;

NativeCall BeginCall(IUnknown* device, void* caller) {
    LARGE_INTEGER qpc{}; QueryPerformanceCounter(&qpc);
    NativeCall c;
    c.device = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(device);
    c.caller = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(caller);
    c.thread = GetCurrentThreadId(); c.begin_qpc = qpc.QuadPart;
    return c;
}
void EndCall(uint32_t kind, NativeCall c, NvAPI_Status result) {
    LARGE_INTEGER qpc{}; QueryPerformanceCounter(&qpc);
    c.end_qpc = qpc.QuadPart; c.result = result;
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(audit_lock, std::try_to_lock);
    if (lock.owns_lock()) audit.Record(kind, c);
    else audit_dropped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
}
NvAPI_Status __cdecl ObserveSleep(IUnknown* device) {
    external_owner.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    auto c = BeginCall(device, _ReturnAddress());
    const auto result = native_sleep(device);
    EndCall(NativeAudit::Sleep, c, result);
    return result;
}
NvAPI_Status __cdecl ObserveMarker(IUnknown* device, NV_LATENCY_MARKER_PARAMS* p) {
    external_owner.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    auto c = BeginCall(device, _ReturnAddress());
    uint32_t kind = NativeAudit::Unknown;
    if (p && p->version == NV_LATENCY_MARKER_PARAMS_VER) {
        c.frame = p->frameID;
        const auto type = static_cast<uint32_t>(p->markerType);
        if (type < NativeAudit::Sleep) kind = type;
    }
    const auto result = native_marker(device, p); // identical arguments, exactly once
    EndCall(kind, c, result);
    return result;
}

NvAPI_Status __cdecl SetMode(IUnknown* dev, NV_SET_SLEEP_MODE_PARAMS* params) {
    if (!params || params->version != NV_SET_SLEEP_MODE_PARAMS_VER) return set_mode(dev, params);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(control);
    game_calls.fetch_add(1);
    if (device_identity != dev) { device_identity = dev; applied = -2; last_native_frame = 0; }
    game_params = *params; have_game_params = true;
    const int requested = settings::Get().reflex_mode.load();
    auto selected = ModeRequest(*params, requested);
    const auto result = set_mode(dev, &selected);
    applied = result == NVAPI_OK ? requested : -2;
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> out(stats_lock); stats.set_result = result; }
    return result;
}
}
void Initialize() {
    // Never load a game-directory nvapi proxy or alter system-wide profiles.
    HMODULE module = LoadLibraryExW(L"nvapi64.dll", nullptr, LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_SYSTEM32);
    if (!module) return;
    auto query = reinterpret_cast<Query>(reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(module, "nvapi_QueryInterface")));
    if (!query) return;
    auto init = reinterpret_cast<decltype(&NvAPI_Initialize)>(query(0x0150e828));
    if (!init || init() != NVAPI_OK) return;
    auto target = query(0xac1ca9e0);
    get_status = reinterpret_cast<decltype(get_status)>(query(0xaef96ca1));
    get_latency = reinterpret_cast<decltype(get_latency)>(query(0x1a587f9c));
    if (!target || !get_status) return;
    if (!hooking::HookFunction(target, reinterpret_cast<void*>(&SetMode), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&set_mode))) return;
    if (auto sleep_target = query(0x852cd1d2))
        sleep_observed.store(hooking::HookFunction(sleep_target, reinterpret_cast<void*>(&ObserveSleep), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&native_sleep)));
    if (auto marker_target = query(0xd9984c05))
        marker_observed.store(hooking::HookFunction(marker_target, reinterpret_cast<void*>(&ObserveMarker), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&native_marker)));
    // The module and hooking session intentionally live until process exit.
    ready.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
}
void Update(ID3D12Device* device, HWND window) {
    if (!device || !ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return;
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(control, std::try_to_lock);
    if (!lock.owns_lock()) return;
    const auto now = GetTickCount64();
    const int requested = settings::Get().reflex_mode.load();
    if (device_identity != device) {
        device_identity = device; applied = -2; have_game_params = false; last_poll = 0; last_native_frame = 0;
    }
    Stats s;
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> read(stats_lock); s = stats; }
    s.available = 1;
    if (requested != applied) {
        if (requested < 0 && !have_game_params) applied = requested;
        else {
            NV_SET_SLEEP_MODE_PARAMS p = have_game_params ? game_params : NV_SET_SLEEP_MODE_PARAMS{};
            p.version = NV_SET_SLEEP_MODE_PARAMS_VER;
            p = ModeRequest(p, requested);
            s.set_result = set_mode(device, &p);
            // Retry a failure at the next settings change/device, not every frame.
            applied = requested;
        }
        last_poll = 0;
    }
    if (!last_poll || now - last_poll >= 1000) {
        last_poll = now;
        NV_GET_SLEEP_STATUS_PARAMS p{}; p.version = NV_GET_SLEEP_STATUS_PARAMS_VER;
        s.query_result = get_status(device, &p); s.status_valid = s.query_result == NVAPI_OK;
        s.foreground = window && GetForegroundWindow() == window;
        s.low_latency = s.status_valid && p.bLowLatencyMode;
        s.game_sleep = s.status_valid && p.bUseGameSleep;
        s.fullscreen_vrr = s.status_valid && s.foreground && p.bFsVrr;
        s.vsync_override = s.status_valid && p.bCplVsyncOn;
        s.independent_flip = s.status_valid && p.bFullscreenIFlip;
        s.native_samples = 0; s.native_render_ms = 0;
        if (get_latency) {
            NV_LATENCY_RESULT_PARAMS result{}; result.version = NV_LATENCY_RESULT_PARAMS_VER;
            if (get_latency(device, &result) == NVAPI_OK) {
                uint64_t newest = last_native_frame;
                for (const auto& r : result.frameReport) {
                    float ms = 0;
                    if (r.frameID > last_native_frame && NativeDuration(r.simStartTime, r.gpuRenderEndTime, ms)) {
                        ++s.native_samples; s.native_render_ms += ms; newest = (std::max)(newest, r.frameID);
                    }
                }
                last_native_frame = newest;
                if (s.native_samples) s.native_render_ms /= static_cast<float>(s.native_samples);
            }
        }
    }
    s.game_mode_calls = game_calls.load();
    if (tlou1::TimingAttached()) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> engine(engine_mutex);
        if (engine_device.Get() != device) { engine_device = device; engine_frames = {}; last_sleep_frame = 0; }
    }
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> out(stats_lock); stats = s; }
}
Stats GetStats() {
    Stats s;
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(stats_lock); s = stats; }
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(audit_lock); s.audit = audit; }
    s.sleep_observer = sleep_observed.load(); s.marker_observer = marker_observed.load();
    s.audit_dropped = audit_dropped.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.pcl_available = pcl::Available(); s.engine_attached = tlou1::TimingAttached(); s.external_owner = external_owner.load();
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(engine_mutex);
      s.engine_available = engine_device && native_sleep && native_marker && !s.external_owner;
      s.engine_sleep = engine_sleep_count; s.engine_markers = engine_marker_count;
      s.engine_complete = engine_frames.complete; s.engine_rejected = engine_frames.rejected;
      s.engine_errors = engine_errors; s.engine_last_result = engine_result; }
    return s;
}
void EngineBegin(uint64_t frame) {
    if (!frame || !ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire) || external_owner.load() || !native_sleep || !native_marker) return;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Device> device;
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(engine_mutex);
      if (!engine_device || frame <= last_sleep_frame) return;
      last_sleep_frame = frame; device = engine_device; }
    if (!device) return;
    // The driver may wait here. Never hold a lock required by the presentation
    // worker: it must remain able to finish the preceding frame.
    const auto result = native_sleep(device.Get()); // required even with mode Off
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(engine_mutex);
      ++engine_sleep_count; engine_result = result; if (result != NVAPI_OK) ++engine_errors; }
    EngineMark(frame, Phase::Simulation);
}
void EngineMark(uint64_t frame, Phase phase) {
    if (!frame || !ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire) || external_owner.load() || !native_marker) return;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Device> device;
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(engine_mutex);
      if (!engine_device || !engine_frames.Accept(frame, phase)) return;
      device = engine_device; }
    constexpr NV_LATENCY_MARKER_TYPE types[] = {SIMULATION_START, INPUT_SAMPLE, SIMULATION_END, RENDERSUBMIT_START, RENDERSUBMIT_END, PRESENT_START, PRESENT_END};
    NV_LATENCY_MARKER_PARAMS p{}; p.version = NV_LATENCY_MARKER_PARAMS_VER;
    p.frameID = frame; p.markerType = types[static_cast<unsigned>(phase)];
    pcl::Mark(static_cast<uint32_t>(p.markerType), frame);
    if (phase == Phase::Input && pcl::Sample(frame)) {
        NV_LATENCY_MARKER_PARAMS flash = p; flash.markerType = TRIGGER_FLASH;
        native_marker(device.Get(), &flash);
    }
    const auto result = native_marker(device.Get(), &p);
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(engine_mutex);
      ++engine_marker_count; engine_result = result; if (result != NVAPI_OK) ++engine_errors; }
}
void GeneratedPresent(uint64_t frame, bool end) {
    if (!frame || !ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire) || external_owner.load() || !native_marker) return;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Device> device;
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(engine_mutex); device = engine_device; }
    if (!device) return;
    NV_LATENCY_MARKER_PARAMS p{}; p.version = NV_LATENCY_MARKER_PARAMS_VER; p.frameID = frame;
    p.markerType = end ? OUT_OF_BAND_PRESENT_END : OUT_OF_BAND_PRESENT_START;
    pcl::Mark(static_cast<uint32_t>(p.markerType), frame);
    const auto result = native_marker(device.Get(), &p);
    { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(engine_mutex); engine_result = result; if (result != NVAPI_OK) ++engine_errors; }
}
}

namespace na::latency {
struct Stats {
    uint32_t available = 0, status_valid = 0, low_latency = 0;
    uint32_t game_sleep = 0, foreground = 0, fullscreen_vrr = 0, vsync_override = 0, independent_flip = 0;
    int32_t set_result = -1, query_result = -1;
    uint64_t game_mode_calls = 0, native_samples = 0;
    float native_render_ms = 0; // native sim-start to GPU-end, NOT scanout or full FG latency
    uint32_t sleep_observer = 0, marker_observer = 0;
    uint64_t audit_dropped = 0;
    NativeAudit audit;
    uint32_t pcl_available = 0;
    uint32_t engine_attached = 0, engine_available = 0, external_owner = 0;
    uint64_t engine_sleep = 0, engine_markers = 0, engine_complete = 0, engine_rejected = 0, engine_errors = 0;
    int32_t engine_last_result = -1;
};
// Init thread only. Official NVAPI entry points, system driver, process lifetime.
void Initialize();
// Present only binds the device and polls diagnostics. Sleep belongs to the
// hash-pinned engine simulation entry, never to Present or the message loop.
void Update(ID3D12Device* device, HWND window);
void EngineBegin(uint64_t frame);
void EngineMark(uint64_t frame, Phase phase);
void GeneratedPresent(uint64_t frame, bool end);
Stats GetStats();
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\latency\pcl_stats.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/latency/pcl_stats.hpp"
#include <atomic>
#if defined(NA_HAVE_PCL_STATS)
// NVIDIA SDK kept outside source distribution; see dependency pin/document.
#include <pclstats.h>
#pragma warning(push)
#pragma warning(disable: 4100) // unused parameter in NVIDIA's unmodified macro
PCLSTATS_DEFINE();
#pragma warning(pop)
#endif
namespace na::latency::pcl {
namespace {
std::atomic<bool> ready{false}, ping{false}, click{false};
}
void Initialize() {
#if defined(NA_HAVE_PCL_STATS)
    PCLSTATS_INIT(0);
    ready.store(g_PCLStatsQuitEvent && g_PCLStatsPingThread, std::memory_order_release);
#endif
}
bool Available() { return ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }
void Mark(uint32_t marker, uint64_t frame) {
    if (!frame || !Available()) return;
#if defined(NA_HAVE_PCL_STATS)
    // Input-sample marker 6 is deprecated in PCL; NVAPI still accepts it.
    if (marker != 6) PCLSTATS_MARKER(marker, frame);
#else
    (void)marker;
#endif
}
void Message(UINT msg, WPARAM key) {
    if (!Available()) return;
#if defined(NA_HAVE_PCL_STATS)
    if (PCLSTATS_IS_PING_MSG_ID(msg) ||
        (msg == WM_KEYDOWN && g_PCLStatsVirtualKey && key == g_PCLStatsVirtualKey)) ping.store(true);
#else
    (void)key;
#endif
    if (msg == WM_LBUTTONDOWN) click.store(true);
}
bool Sample(uint64_t frame) {
    if (ping.exchange(false)) Mark(8, frame); // next frame consuming this input
    const bool pressed = click.exchange(false);
    if (pressed) Mark(7, frame);
    return pressed;
}
}

// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\latency\policy.hpp ---

#pragma once
#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>

namespace na::latency {
// Explicit user choice overrides only the low-latency/boost bits. Preserve
// the game's limiter and marker configuration. -1 leaves ownership with game.
template<class Params> Params ModeRequest(Params p, int mode) {
    if (mode >= 0) { p.bLowLatencyMode = mode == 1 || mode == 2; p.bLowLatencyBoost = mode == 2; }
    return p;
}
inline bool NativeDuration(uint64_t start, uint64_t end, float& ms) {
    if (!start || end <= start || end - start > 1000000) return false;
    ms = static_cast<float>(end - start) / 1000.f;
    return true;
}
struct Distribution { uint32_t count = 0; float mean = 0, p95 = 0, max = 0; };
struct Samples {
    std::array<float, 256> data{};
    uint32_t next = 0, count = 0;
    void Add(float v) { if (!std::isfinite(v) || v < 0) return; data[next++ % data.size()] = v; if (count < data.size()) ++count; }
    Distribution Read() const {
        Distribution d; d.count = count; if (!count) return d;
        auto sorted = data; std::sort(sorted.begin(), sorted.begin() + count);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) d.mean += sorted[i];
        d.mean /= static_cast<float>(count);
        d.p95 = sorted[(count * 95 + 99) / 100 - 1]; d.max = sorted[count - 1]; return d;
    }
};
}


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\motion\live_flow.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/motion/live_flow.hpp"

#include <d3dcompiler.h>
#include <windows.h>
#include <wrl/client.h>

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <mutex>

#include "host_dx12/color/color_space.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/nvof/flow_expand.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/nvof/nvof_flow.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/settings.hpp"

using Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr;

namespace na::motion {
namespace {

// Back buffer -> BGRA8 display-encoded SDR (what NVOFA expects).
const char kShader[] = R"(
Texture2D<float4> t_in : register(t0);
RWTexture2D<float4> u_out : register(u0);
cbuffer C : register(b0) { float paper_white; uint width; uint height; uint is_pq; };
static const float m1 = 0.1593017578125, m2 = 78.84375, c1 = 0.8359375, c2 = 18.8515625, c3 = 18.6875;
float3 pq_to_nits(float3 e) {
    float3 ep = pow(max(e, 0.0), 1.0 / m2);
    float3 num = max(ep - c1, 0.0), den = c2 - c3 * ep;
    return pow(num / den, 1.0 / m1) * 10000.0;
}
float3 srgb_encode(float3 v) { v = saturate(v); return v <= 0.0031308 ? 12.92 * v : 1.055 * pow(v, 1.0 / 2.4) - 0.055; }
SamplerState s_linear : register(s0);
// width/height = ENGINE input size (display / downscale, A58): the back
// buffer is sampled bilinearly, which is an exact texel read at 1:1.
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void to_bgra8(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    float2 uv = (float2(id.xy) + 0.5) / float2(width, height);
    float4 p = t_in.SampleLevel(s_linear, uv, 0);
    // is_pq is the shared shader mode (A60): 0 SDR (already display-encoded), 1 PQ, 2 scRGB (linear, 1.0 = 80 nits).
    float3 c = is_pq == 1 ? srgb_encode(pq_to_nits(p.rgb) / paper_white)
             : (is_pq == 2 ? srgb_encode(p.rgb * 80.0 / paper_white) : p.rgb);
    u_out[id.xy] = float4(c, 1.0); // UAV format is B8G8R8A8_UNORM: the view swizzles
}
// A57: render-size depth (nearest) and motion (bilinear) -> display size.
// The motion output is in DISPLAY pixels: value * scale (render px) * (display / render).
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void up_depth(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    uint sw, sh; t_in.GetDimensions(sw, sh);
    uint2 src = uint2(min((id.x * sw) / width, sw - 1), min((id.y * sh) / height, sh - 1));
    u_out[id.xy] = t_in[src];
}
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void up_motion(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    uint sw, sh; t_in.GetDimensions(sw, sh);
    float2 uv = (float2(id.xy) + 0.5) / float2(width, height);
    float2 mv = t_in.SampleLevel(s_linear, uv, 0).xy;
    // paper_white carries mv_scale_x, is_pq carries asuint(mv_scale_y) here (constants reuse).
    float2 px = mv * float2(paper_white, asfloat(is_pq)) * float2(width, height) / float2(sw, sh);
    u_out[id.xy] = float4(px, 0.0, 0.0);
}
// A57: endpoint error accumulation (fixed point, 1/256 px) into a 4-uint buffer:
// [0] sum |flow - ref| * 256, [1] count, [2] within 1 px, [3] sum |ref| * 256.
Texture2D<float2> t_flow : register(t1);
Texture2D<float2> t_ref : register(t2);
RWByteAddressBuffer u_acc : register(u1);
groupshared uint g_sum, g_cnt, g_in1, g_mag;
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void compare(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID, uint3 tid : SV_GroupThreadID) {
    if (tid.x == 0 && tid.y == 0) { g_sum = 0; g_cnt = 0; g_in1 = 0; g_mag = 0; }
    GroupMemoryBarrierWithGroupSync();
    // A 40-px border is excluded: content entering the frame has no previous
    // position, and the engine's outer blocks are unreliable there.
    if (id.x < width && id.y < height && id.x >= 40 && id.y >= 40 && id.x + 40 < width && id.y + 40 < height) {
        float2 f = t_flow[id.xy];
        float2 r = t_ref[id.xy];
        bool ok = all(abs(f) < 512.0) && all(abs(r) < 512.0) && !any(isnan(f)) && !any(isnan(r));
        if (ok) {
            float e = length(f - r);
            InterlockedAdd(g_sum, (uint)(e * 256.0));
            InterlockedAdd(g_cnt, 1u);
            if (e <= 1.0) InterlockedAdd(g_in1, 1u);
            InterlockedAdd(g_mag, (uint)(length(r) * 256.0));
        }
    }
    GroupMemoryBarrierWithGroupSync();
    if (tid.x == 0 && tid.y == 0) {
        u_acc.InterlockedAdd(0, g_sum); u_acc.InterlockedAdd(4, g_cnt); u_acc.InterlockedAdd(8, g_in1); u_acc.InterlockedAdd(12, g_mag);
    }
}
)";
struct Constants { float paper_white; uint32_t width, height, is_pq; };

constexpr uint32_t kRing = 4;     // command allocators / fence slots
constexpr uint32_t kInputs = 3;   // BGRA8 inputs: current, previous, one being overwritten
constexpr uint32_t kOutputs = 2;  // published flow/validity pairs (consumer reads one while we write the other)

struct Slot { ComPtr<ID3D12CommandAllocator> alloc; uint64_t fence_value = 0; };

// A53 (session 9: the game froze after 118 frames): the GAME's queue never
// waits on the engine any more. Conversion runs on the game's queue (it
// needs the back buffer) and signals `fence`; the engine waits on that; the
// expansion runs on OUR OWN queue, which waits on the engine fence and
// signals `own_fence` for consumers. An input is reused only when the
// engine that read it has provably finished (CPU poll, else skip). A
// watchdog latches the feature off when the engine has not answered for
// kEngineTimeoutMs, so a stalled engine can only stall our queue, not the game.
constexpr uint32_t kEngineTimeoutMs = 2000;

struct State {
    std::mutex m;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Device> device;
    ComPtr<ID3D12GraphicsCommandList> list;      // conversion (game queue)
