    ComPtr<ID3D12GraphicsCommandList> own_list;  // expansion (own queue)
    ComPtr<ID3D12CommandQueue> own_queue;
    Slot slots[kRing];
    Slot own_slots[kRing];
    uint32_t slot_index = 0;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Fence> fence;      // game-queue conversion work
    uint64_t fence_next = 1;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Fence> own_fence;  // our queue's expansion work (published to consumers)
    uint64_t own_next = 1;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Fence> of_fence;   // signalled by the engine
    uint64_t of_next = 1;
    uint64_t input_of_value[kInputs] = {}; // engine fence value of the last Execute that READ each input
    ULONGLONG of_issued_tick = 0;   // when the outstanding engine job was issued (watchdog)
    ComPtr<ID3D12RootSignature> root;
    ComPtr<ID3D12PipelineState> pso, pso_up_depth, pso_up_motion, pso_compare;
    ComPtr<ID3D12DescriptorHeap> heap;      // per ring slot: 8 descriptors (4 tables of t0,t1,t2 + u0/u1 layout)
    UINT heap_stride = 0;
    // A57 references from the DLSS evaluate (render size), upsampled at Present.
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> depth_copy, motion_copy;   // NPSR at rest
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> depth_up, motion_up;       // R32F / RG32F, display size
    DXGI_FORMAT motion_format = DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN;
    uint32_t ref_w = 0, ref_h = 0;
    float ref_scale_x = 0, ref_scale_y = 0;
    uint32_t have_ref = 0, ref_fresh = 0, ref_has_depth = 0;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> acc_buffer;                // 4 uint per ring slot, UAV
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> acc_readback;
    uint64_t acc_fence_value[kRing] = {};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> ref_available{0};
    std::atomic<float> ref_err{0.f}, ref_within{0.f}, ref_mag{0.f};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> ref_frames{0};
    // Engine.
    na::nvof::Flow flow;
    bool flow_ready = false;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> inputs[kInputs];
    na::nvof::BufferHandle input_handles[kInputs] = {};
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> grid_out;
    na::nvof::BufferHandle grid_handle = nullptr;
    na::nvof::FlowExpand expand;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> flow_out[kOutputs], valid_out[kOutputs];
    uint32_t out_index = 0;
    uint32_t width = 0, height = 0, grid = 4, perf = 5;
    uint32_t downscale = 1, ew = 0, eh = 0; // A58: engine runs at (width/downscale) x (height/downscale)
    uint32_t input_index = 0;    // next input to write
    uint64_t input_frames = 0;   // inputs converted so far
    uint64_t last_of_value = 0;  // engine fence value the last Execute signals
    LARGE_INTEGER of_started{};  // when that Execute was issued
    LARGE_INTEGER qpc_freq{};
    // Depth source.
    ID3D12Resource* depth = nullptr;
    float depth_clear = 1.0f;
    // Published result.
    std::atomic<uint32_t> have_output{0};
    Output latest{};
    // Status.
    std::atomic<uint32_t> active{0}, latched{0}, hdr{0}, available{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> frames{0}, skipped{0};
    std::atomic<float> flow_ms{0.f}, avg_ms{0.f};
    char reason[96] = "Motion analysis is off.";
};
State g;

void set_reason(const char* t) { strncpy_s(g.reason, t, _TRUNCATE); }
void latch(const char* t) { set_reason(t); g.latched.store(1, std::memory_order_release); g.active.store(0, std::memory_order_release); }

D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER transition(ID3D12Resource* r, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATES a, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATES b)
{
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER br{};
    br.Type = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_TYPE_TRANSITION;
    br.Transition.pResource = r;
    br.Transition.Subresource = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_ALL_SUBRESOURCES;
    br.Transition.StateBefore = a;
    br.Transition.StateAfter = b;
    return br;
}
D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER uav(ID3D12Resource* r)
{
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER br{};
    br.Type = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_TYPE_UAV;
    br.UAV.pResource = r;
    return br;
}
bool create_texture(UINT w, UINT h, DXGI_FORMAT fmt, bool uav_flag, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATES state, ComPtr<ID3D12Resource>& out)
{
    D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp{};
    hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_DEFAULT;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d{};
    d.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    d.Width = w; d.Height = h; d.DepthOrArraySize = 1; d.MipLevels = 1; d.Format = fmt; d.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    d.Flags = uav_flag ? D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_ALLOW_UNORDERED_ACCESS : D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_NONE;
    out.Reset();
    return SUCCEEDED(g.device->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &d, state, nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&out)));
}
bool uav_store_supported(DXGI_FORMAT fmt)
{
    D3D12_FEATURE_DATA_FORMAT_SUPPORT fs{};
    fs.Format = fmt;
    if (FAILED(g.device->CheckFeatureSupport(D3D12_FEATURE_FORMAT_SUPPORT, &fs, sizeof(fs)))) return false;
    return (fs.Support2 & D3D12_FORMAT_SUPPORT2_UAV_TYPED_STORE) != 0;
}

bool ensure_device_locked(ID3D12Device* dev)
{
    if (g.device) return true;
    g.device = dev;
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&g.qpc_freq);
    for (Slot& s : g.slots)
        if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommandAllocator(D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_DIRECT, IID_PPV_ARGS(&s.alloc)))) return false;
    for (Slot& s : g.own_slots)
        if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommandAllocator(D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_DIRECT, IID_PPV_ARGS(&s.alloc)))) return false;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommandList(0, D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_DIRECT, g.slots[0].alloc.Get(), nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.list)))) return false;
    g.list->Close();
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommandList(0, D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_DIRECT, g.own_slots[0].alloc.Get(), nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.own_list)))) return false;
    g.own_list->Close();
    D3D12_COMMAND_QUEUE_DESC qd{};
    qd.Type = D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_DIRECT;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommandQueue(&qd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.own_queue)))) return false;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateFence(0, D3D12_FENCE_FLAG_NONE, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.fence)))) return false;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateFence(0, D3D12_FENCE_FLAG_NONE, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.own_fence)))) return false;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateFence(0, D3D12_FENCE_FLAG_NONE, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.of_fence)))) return false;
    // Table layout per dispatch: t0,t1,t2 then u0,u1 (5 descriptors).
    D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE ranges[2]{};
    ranges[0].RangeType = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE_TYPE_SRV; ranges[0].NumDescriptors = 3; ranges[0].BaseShaderRegister = 0;
    ranges[1].RangeType = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE_TYPE_UAV; ranges[1].NumDescriptors = 2; ranges[1].BaseShaderRegister = 0; ranges[1].OffsetInDescriptorsFromTableStart = 3;
    D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER params[2]{};
    params[0].ParameterType = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_TABLE;
    params[0].DescriptorTable.NumDescriptorRanges = 2;
    params[0].DescriptorTable.pDescriptorRanges = ranges;
    params[1].ParameterType = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_32BIT_CONSTANTS;
    params[1].Constants.Num32BitValues = 4;
    D3D12_STATIC_SAMPLER_DESC samp{};
    samp.Filter = D3D12_FILTER_MIN_MAG_MIP_LINEAR;
    samp.AddressU = samp.AddressV = samp.AddressW = D3D12_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_MODE_CLAMP;
    D3D12_ROOT_SIGNATURE_DESC rs{};
    rs.NumParameters = 2; rs.pParameters = params; rs.NumStaticSamplers = 1; rs.pStaticSamplers = &samp;
    ComPtr<ID3DBlob> sig, err;
    if (FAILED(D3D12SerializeRootSignature(&rs, D3D_ROOT_SIGNATURE_VERSION_1, &sig, &err))) return false;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateRootSignature(0, sig->GetBufferPointer(), sig->GetBufferSize(), IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.root)))) return false;
    auto compile = [&](const char* entry, ComPtr<ID3D12PipelineState>& pso) {
        ComPtr<ID3DBlob> code, e2;
        if (FAILED(D3DCompile(kShader, sizeof(kShader) - 1, "live_flow.hlsl", nullptr, nullptr, entry, "cs_5_0", D3DCOMPILE_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL3, 0, &code, &e2))) return false;
        D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC pd{};
        pd.pRootSignature = g.root.Get();
        pd.CS.pShaderBytecode = code->GetBufferPointer();
        pd.CS.BytecodeLength = code->GetBufferSize();
        return SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateComputePipelineState(&pd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&pso)));
    };
    if (!compile("to_bgra8", g.pso) || !compile("up_depth", g.pso_up_depth) || !compile("up_motion", g.pso_up_motion) || !compile("compare", g.pso_compare)) return false;
    D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_DESC hd{};
    hd.Type = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE_CBV_SRV_UAV;
    hd.NumDescriptors = 5 * 4 * kRing; // 4 dispatch tables per ring slot
    hd.Flags = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_FLAG_SHADER_VISIBLE;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateDescriptorHeap(&hd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.heap)))) return false;
    g.heap_stride = dev->GetDescriptorHandleIncrementSize(D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE_CBV_SRV_UAV);
    // Accumulator (UAV) + readback for the ground-truth comparison.
    {
        D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp{}; hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_DEFAULT;
        D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC bd{};
        bd.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_BUFFER; bd.Width = 16 * kRing; bd.Height = bd.DepthOrArraySize = bd.MipLevels = 1;
        bd.SampleDesc.Count = 1; bd.Layout = D3D12_TEXTURE_LAYOUT_ROW_MAJOR; bd.Flags = D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_ALLOW_UNORDERED_ACCESS;
        if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &bd, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.acc_buffer)))) return false;
        D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES rb{}; rb.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_READBACK;
        bd.Flags = D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_NONE;
        if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommittedResource(&rb, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &bd, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.acc_readback)))) return false;
    }
    return g.expand.Init(dev, kRing);
}

// Descriptor table `t` (0..3) of ring slot `slot`: writes SRVs t0..t2 and UAVs u0..u1.
struct TableDesc { ID3D12Resource* srv[3]; DXGI_FORMAT srv_fmt[3]; ID3D12Resource* uav[2]; DXGI_FORMAT uav_fmt[2]; bool uav1_is_buffer; };
D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE write_table(uint32_t slot, uint32_t t, const TableDesc& d)
{
    uint32_t base = (slot * 4 + t) * 5;
    D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE h = g.heap->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
    h.ptr += static_cast<SIZE_T>(base) * g.heap_stride;
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        D3D12_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC sd{};
        sd.Format = d.srv_fmt[i]; sd.ViewDimension = D3D12_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D; sd.Shader4ComponentMapping = D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING; sd.Texture2D.MipLevels = 1;
        if (d.srv[i]) g.device->CreateShaderResourceView(d.srv[i], &sd, h);
        else { sd.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM; g.device->CreateShaderResourceView(nullptr, &sd, h); }
        h.ptr += g.heap_stride;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        D3D12_UNORDERED_ACCESS_VIEW_DESC ud{};
        if (i == 1 && d.uav1_is_buffer && d.uav[1]) {
            ud.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_TYPELESS; ud.ViewDimension = D3D12_UAV_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
            ud.Buffer.NumElements = 4 * kRing; ud.Buffer.Flags = D3D12_BUFFER_UAV_FLAG_RAW;
            g.device->CreateUnorderedAccessView(d.uav[1], nullptr, &ud, h);
        } else {
            ud.Format = d.uav_fmt[i]; ud.ViewDimension = D3D12_UAV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
            if (d.uav[i]) g.device->CreateUnorderedAccessView(d.uav[i], nullptr, &ud, h);
            else { ud.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT; g.device->CreateUnorderedAccessView(nullptr, nullptr, &ud, h); }
        }
        h.ptr += g.heap_stride;
    }
    D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE gh = g.heap->GetGPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
    gh.ptr += static_cast<UINT64>(base) * g.heap_stride;
    return gh;
}

// Engine + textures for the current extent. Registrations use the queue
// fence at its current value as the handshake (nothing pending on them).
bool ensure_engine_locked(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue, uint32_t w, uint32_t h)
{
    if (g.flow_ready && g.width == w && g.height == h) return true;
    if (g.flow_ready) {
        // Extent changed: the engine must be torn down in the documented order.
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kInputs; ++i) if (g.input_handles[i]) { g.flow.Unregister(g.input_handles[i]); g.input_handles[i] = nullptr; }
        if (g.grid_handle) { g.flow.Unregister(g.grid_handle); g.grid_handle = nullptr; }
        for (auto& t : g.inputs) t.Reset();
        g.grid_out.Reset();
        if (!g.flow.Shutdown()) { latch("Motion analysis could not restart after a size change."); return false; }
        g.flow_ready = false;
    }
    na::nvof::Caps caps;
    char err[192];
    if (!na::nvof::QueryCaps(g.device.Get(), &caps, err, sizeof(err)) || !caps.available) {
        g.available.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
        latch("Motion analysis needs NVIDIA hardware optical flow, which this graphics card or driver does not offer.");
        return false;
    }
    g.available.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
    if (!uav_store_supported(DXGI_FORMAT_B8G8R8A8_UNORM)) { latch("Motion analysis cannot write its input format on this graphics card."); return false; }
    g.grid = na::settings::Get().motion_grid.load();
    g.perf = na::settings::Get().motion_perf.load();
    g.downscale = na::settings::Get().motion_downscale.load();
    if (g.downscale != 1 && g.downscale != 2 && g.downscale != 4) g.downscale = 2;
    g.ew = (w + g.downscale - 1) / g.downscale;
    g.eh = (h + g.downscale - 1) / g.downscale;
    if (!g.flow.Init(g.device.Get(), g.ew, g.eh, g.grid, g.perf)) {
        char buf[96];
        snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Motion analysis could not start: %.60s", g.flow.LastError());
        latch(buf);
        return false;
    }
    bool ok = true;
    for (auto& t : g.inputs) ok &= create_texture(g.ew, g.eh, DXGI_FORMAT_B8G8R8A8_UNORM, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COMMON, t);
    ok &= create_texture(g.flow.OutWidth(), g.flow.OutHeight(), DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16_SINT, false, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COMMON, g.grid_out);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kOutputs; ++i) {
        ok &= create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.flow_out[i]);
        ok &= create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.valid_out[i]);
    }
    if (!ok) { latch("Motion analysis could not allocate its buffers."); return false; }
    // Registration handshake on a value the queue has already passed.
    uint64_t hv = g.fence_next++;
    queue->Signal(g.fence.Get(), hv);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kInputs; ++i)
        ok &= g.flow.RegisterInput(g.inputs[i].Get(), g.fence.Get(), hv, hv, &g.input_handles[i]);
    ok &= g.flow.RegisterOutput(g.grid_out.Get(), g.fence.Get(), hv, hv, &g.grid_handle);
    if (!ok) {
        char buf[96];
        snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Motion analysis could not register its buffers: %.50s", g.flow.LastError());
        latch(buf);
        return false;
    }
    g.width = w;
    g.height = h;
    g.input_index = 0;
    g.input_frames = 0;
    for (auto& v : g.input_of_value) v = 0;
    g.last_of_value = 0;
    g.of_issued_tick = 0;
    g.flow_ready = true;
    return true;
}

} // namespace

void SetDepthSource(ID3D12Resource* depth, float depth_clear)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    g.depth = depth;
    g.depth_clear = depth_clear;
}

void OnPresent(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue, ID3D12Resource* backbuffer, DXGI_COLOR_SPACE_TYPE color_space)
{
    if (!queue || !backbuffer) return;
    const na::settings::Settings& st = na::settings::Get();
    if (!st.motion_enabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) { g.active.store(0, std::memory_order_release); return; }
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    if (g.latched.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Device> dev;
    if (FAILED(backbuffer->GetDevice(IID_PPV_ARGS(&dev))) || !ensure_device_locked(dev.Get())) { latch("Motion analysis could not set up on the graphics card."); return; }
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC bb = backbuffer->GetDesc();
    uint32_t w = static_cast<uint32_t>(bb.Width), h = bb.Height;
    na::color::Encoding enc = na::color::Classify(bb.Format, color_space); // A60: PQ, scRGB or SDR
    bool pq = na::color::IsHdr(enc);
    uint32_t shader_mode = na::color::ShaderMode(enc);
    g.hdr.store(pq ? 1u : 0u, std::memory_order_release);
    if (!ensure_engine_locked(queue, w, h)) return;
    Slot& slot = g.slots[g.slot_index];
    Slot& own_slot = g.own_slots[g.slot_index];
    if ((slot.fence_value && g.fence->GetCompletedValue() < slot.fence_value) ||
        (own_slot.fence_value && g.own_fence->GetCompletedValue() < own_slot.fence_value)) {
        g.skipped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return;
    }
    uint64_t of_done = g.of_fence->GetCompletedValue();
    // Watchdog: an engine job outstanding for too long latches the feature off
    // (only OUR queue is waiting on it; the game's queue never does).
    if (g.last_of_value && of_done < g.last_of_value && g.of_issued_tick && GetTickCount64() - g.of_issued_tick > kEngineTimeoutMs) {
        latch("Motion analysis stopped: the optical-flow engine stopped answering.");
        return;
    }
    // Timing of the previous engine run (CPU-observed).
    if (g.last_of_value && of_done >= g.last_of_value && g.of_started.QuadPart) {
        LARGE_INTEGER now; QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
        float ms = 1000.f * static_cast<float>(now.QuadPart - g.of_started.QuadPart) / static_cast<float>(g.qpc_freq.QuadPart);
        g.flow_ms.store(ms);
        float avg = g.avg_ms.load();
        g.avg_ms.store(avg > 0 ? avg * 0.9f + ms * 0.1f : ms);
        g.of_started.QuadPart = 0;
    }
    // The input we are about to overwrite must no longer be read by the engine (CPU check, never a queue wait).
    uint32_t cur = g.input_index;
    if (g.input_of_value[cur] && of_done < g.input_of_value[cur]) { g.skipped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed); return; }

    // Pass 1 (game queue): convert the back buffer into inputs[cur] (BGRA8 SDR),
    // and (A57, DLSS titles) upsample the evaluate-time depth/motion references.
    ID3D12Resource* in_tex = g.inputs[cur].Get();
    slot.alloc->Reset();
    g.list->Reset(slot.alloc.Get(), nullptr);
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b1[2] = {transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PRESENT, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
                                    transition(in_tex, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COMMON, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS)};
    g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, b1);
    ID3D12DescriptorHeap* heaps[] = {g.heap.Get()};
    g.list->SetDescriptorHeaps(1, heaps);
    g.list->SetComputeRootSignature(g.root.Get());
    g.list->SetPipelineState(g.pso.Get());
    g.list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, write_table(g.slot_index, 0, TableDesc{{backbuffer, nullptr, nullptr}, {bb.Format, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM}, {in_tex, nullptr}, {DXGI_FORMAT_B8G8R8A8_UNORM, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT}, false}));
    Constants c{static_cast<float>(st.nr_paper_white.load()), g.ew, g.eh, shader_mode};
    g.list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, 4, &c, 0);
    g.list->Dispatch((g.ew + 7) / 8, (g.eh + 7) / 8, 1);
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b2[3] = {uav(in_tex), transition(in_tex, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COMMON),
                                    transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PRESENT)};
    g.list->ResourceBarrier(3, b2);
    bool use_ref = g.have_ref && g.ref_fresh && g.depth_copy && g.motion_copy;
    g.ref_fresh = 0;
    if (use_ref) {
        bool ok = true;
        if (!g.depth_up || g.depth_up->GetDesc().Width != w || g.depth_up->GetDesc().Height != h) {
            ok &= create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.depth_up);
            ok &= create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.motion_up);
        }
        if (ok) {
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER bu0[2] = {transition(g.depth_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS),
                                             transition(g.motion_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, bu0);
            if (g.ref_has_depth) {
                g.list->SetPipelineState(g.pso_up_depth.Get());
                g.list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, write_table(g.slot_index, 1, TableDesc{{g.depth_copy.Get(), nullptr, nullptr}, {DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM}, {g.depth_up.Get(), nullptr}, {DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT}, false}));
                g.list->Dispatch((w + 7) / 8, (h + 7) / 8, 1);
            }
            g.list->SetPipelineState(g.pso_up_motion.Get());
            g.list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, write_table(g.slot_index, 2, TableDesc{{g.motion_copy.Get(), nullptr, nullptr}, {g.motion_format, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM}, {g.motion_up.Get(), nullptr}, {DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT}, false}));
            Constants cm{g.ref_scale_x, w, h, 0};
            std::memcpy(&cm.is_pq, &g.ref_scale_y, sizeof(float)); // asfloat(is_pq) in the shader
            g.list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, 4, &cm, 0);
            g.list->Dispatch((w + 7) / 8, (h + 7) / 8, 1);
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER bu1[4] = {uav(g.depth_up.Get()), uav(g.motion_up.Get()),
                                             transition(g.depth_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
                                             transition(g.motion_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(4, bu1);
        } else {
            use_ref = false;
        }
    }
    g.list->Close();
    ID3D12CommandList* lists[] = {g.list.Get()};
    queue->ExecuteCommandLists(1, lists);
    uint64_t converted = g.fence_next++;
    queue->Signal(g.fence.Get(), converted);
    ++g.input_frames;
    g.input_index = (cur + 1) % kInputs;

    if (g.input_frames < 2) { // no previous frame yet
        slot.fence_value = converted;
        g.slot_index = (g.slot_index + 1) % kRing;
        g.skipped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        set_reason("Motion analysis is starting.");
        return;
    }
    uint32_t prev = (cur + kInputs - 1) % kInputs;
    // Pass 2: the engine (asynchronous): current -> previous flow, after the conversion completed.
    uint64_t ofv = g.of_next++;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&g.of_started);
    if (!g.flow.Execute(g.input_handles[cur], g.input_handles[prev], g.grid_handle, g.fence.Get(), converted, g.of_fence.Get(), ofv, true)) {
        char buf[96];
        snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Motion analysis stopped: %.60s", g.flow.LastError());
        latch(buf);
        slot.fence_value = converted;
        g.slot_index = (g.slot_index + 1) % kRing;
        return;
    }
    g.last_of_value = ofv;
    g.of_issued_tick = GetTickCount64();
    g.input_of_value[cur] = ofv;
    g.input_of_value[prev] = ofv;
    slot.fence_value = converted;
    // Pass 3 (OUR queue, which waits on the engine; the game's queue never does):
    // expand the grid into the arbiter's raw flow (+ validity).
    g.own_queue->Wait(g.of_fence.Get(), ofv);
    uint32_t oi = g.out_index;
    ID3D12Resource* flow_tex = g.flow_out[oi].Get();
    ID3D12Resource* valid_tex = g.valid_out[oi].Get();
    own_slot.alloc->Reset();
    g.own_list->Reset(own_slot.alloc.Get(), nullptr);
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b3[3] = {transition(g.grid_out.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COMMON, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
                                    transition(flow_tex, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS),
                                    transition(valid_tex, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS)};
    g.own_list->ResourceBarrier(3, b3);
    bool recorded;
    ID3D12Resource* depth_for_expand = g.depth ? g.depth : ((use_ref && g.ref_has_depth) ? g.depth_up.Get() : nullptr);
    g.expand.SetVectorScale(static_cast<float>(g.downscale));   // A58: engine pixels -> display pixels
    uint32_t block = g.grid * g.downscale;                       // block size in display pixels
    if (depth_for_expand) {
        na::nvof::ExpandParams ep;
        ep.depth = depth_for_expand;
        ep.validity_out = valid_tex;
        ep.depth_clear = g.depth ? g.depth_clear : 1.0f; // A13: TLOU non-inverted depth clears to 1
        recorded = g.expand.DispatchDepthAware(g.own_list.Get(), g.grid_out.Get(), block, ep, flow_tex, w, h, g.slot_index);
    } else {
        recorded = g.expand.Dispatch(g.own_list.Get(), g.grid_out.Get(), block, flow_tex, w, h, g.slot_index);
    }
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b4[4] = {uav(flow_tex), transition(g.grid_out.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COMMON),
                                    transition(flow_tex, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
                                    transition(valid_tex, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE)};
    g.own_list->ResourceBarrier(4, b4);
    // A57: compare against the game's motion vectors; accumulate into this slot's 16 bytes, copy to readback.
    if (recorded && use_ref) {
        uint32_t off = g.slot_index * 16;
        // Zero this slot's accumulator (previous readback of it has been consumed: the slot's own fence passed).
        D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER to_copy = transition(g.acc_buffer.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST);
        g.own_list->ResourceBarrier(1, &to_copy);
        // (a fresh zero source: the readback buffer's slot after consumption holds stale data, so use a UAV clear instead)
        D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER back_uav = transition(g.acc_buffer.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS);
        g.own_list->ResourceBarrier(1, &back_uav);
        UINT zeros[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
        D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE tbl = write_table(g.slot_index, 3, TableDesc{{nullptr, flow_tex, g.motion_up.Get()}, {DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT, DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT}, {nullptr, g.acc_buffer.Get()}, {DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_TYPELESS}, true});
        // ClearUnorderedAccessViewUint needs a CPU handle too: recompute it for the u1 descriptor of this table.
        D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE u1_cpu = g.heap->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
        u1_cpu.ptr += static_cast<SIZE_T>((g.slot_index * 4 + 3) * 5 + 4) * g.heap_stride;
        D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE u1_gpu = tbl; u1_gpu.ptr += static_cast<UINT64>(4) * g.heap_stride;
        ID3D12DescriptorHeap* own_heaps[] = {g.heap.Get()};
        g.own_list->SetDescriptorHeaps(1, own_heaps);
        D3D12_RECT rect{static_cast<LONG>(off / 4), 0, static_cast<LONG>(off / 4 + 4), 1};
        g.own_list->ClearUnorderedAccessViewUint(u1_gpu, u1_cpu, g.acc_buffer.Get(), zeros, 1, &rect);
        D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER cleared = uav(g.acc_buffer.Get());
        g.own_list->ResourceBarrier(1, &cleared);
        g.own_list->SetComputeRootSignature(g.root.Get());
        g.own_list->SetPipelineState(g.pso_compare.Get());
        g.own_list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, tbl);
        Constants cc{0.f, w, h, 0};
        g.own_list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, 4, &cc, 0);
        g.own_list->Dispatch((w + 7) / 8, (h + 7) / 8, 1);
        D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b5[2] = {uav(g.acc_buffer.Get()), transition(g.acc_buffer.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE)};
        g.own_list->ResourceBarrier(2, b5);
        g.own_list->CopyBufferRegion(g.acc_readback.Get(), off, g.acc_buffer.Get(), off, 16);
        D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b6 = transition(g.acc_buffer.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS);
        g.own_list->ResourceBarrier(1, &b6);
    }
    g.own_list->Close();
    if (!recorded) { latch("Motion analysis could not expand the flow field."); return; }
    ID3D12CommandList* own_lists[] = {g.own_list.Get()};
    g.own_queue->ExecuteCommandLists(1, own_lists);
    uint64_t done = g.own_next++;
    g.own_queue->Signal(g.own_fence.Get(), done);
    own_slot.fence_value = done;
    g.acc_fence_value[g.slot_index] = use_ref ? done : 0;
    // Read back the comparison of an older slot whose fence has passed (never waits).
    for (uint32_t k = 0; k < kRing; ++k) {
        uint32_t s2 = (g.slot_index + 1 + k) % kRing;
        if (!g.acc_fence_value[s2] || g.own_fence->GetCompletedValue() < g.acc_fence_value[s2]) continue;
        void* m = nullptr;
        D3D12_RANGE rr{static_cast<SIZE_T>(s2) * 16, static_cast<SIZE_T>(s2) * 16 + 16};
        if (SUCCEEDED(g.acc_readback->Map(0, &rr, &m))) {
            uint32_t v[4];
            std::memcpy(v, static_cast<uint8_t*>(m) + s2 * 16, 16);
            D3D12_RANGE none{0, 0};
            g.acc_readback->Unmap(0, &none);
            if (v[1]) {
                g.ref_err.store(static_cast<float>(v[0]) / 256.f / static_cast<float>(v[1]));
                g.ref_within.store(100.f * static_cast<float>(v[2]) / static_cast<float>(v[1]));
                g.ref_mag.store(static_cast<float>(v[3]) / 256.f / static_cast<float>(v[1]));
                g.ref_frames.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
                g.ref_available.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
            }
        }
        g.acc_fence_value[s2] = 0;
    }
    g.slot_index = (g.slot_index + 1) % kRing;
    g.out_index = (oi + 1) % kOutputs;
    g.latest = Output{flow_tex, depth_for_expand ? valid_tex : nullptr, g.own_fence.Get(), done, g.frames.load() + 1, w, h, block};
    g.have_output.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
    g.frames.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g.active.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
    set_reason(pq ? (shader_mode == 2 ? "Motion analysis is active (HDR picture, scRGB)." : "Motion analysis is active (HDR picture).")
                  : "Motion analysis is active.");
}

bool WantsInputs()
{
    return na::settings::Get().motion_enabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != 0 && !g.latched.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
}

void OnEvaluate(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, const EvalRef& in)
{
    if (!list || !in.motion) return; // depth optional: without it the reference is motion-only (plain expansion)
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    if (!WantsInputs()) return;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Device> dev;
    if (FAILED(list->GetDevice(IID_PPV_ARGS(&dev))) || !ensure_device_locked(dev.Get())) return;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC md = in.motion->GetDesc();
    uint32_t w = static_cast<uint32_t>(md.Width), h = md.Height;
    bool with_depth = in.depth != nullptr;
    if (with_depth) {
        D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC dd = in.depth->GetDesc();
        if (static_cast<uint32_t>(dd.Width) != w || dd.Height != h) with_depth = false;
    }
    if (!g.motion_copy || g.ref_w != w || g.ref_h != h || g.motion_format != md.Format) {
        if (!create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, false, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.depth_copy) ||
            !create_texture(w, h, md.Format, false, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.motion_copy)) {
            g.have_ref = 0;
            return;
        }
        g.ref_w = w; g.ref_h = h; g.motion_format = md.Format;
    }
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER pre[4] = {
        transition(in.motion, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE),
        transition(g.motion_copy.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST),
        transition(with_depth ? in.depth : nullptr, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE),
        transition(g.depth_copy.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST)};
    pre[2].Transition.Subresource = 0;
    list->ResourceBarrier(with_depth ? 4 : 2, pre);
    list->CopyResource(g.motion_copy.Get(), in.motion);
    if (with_depth) {
        D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_LOCATION src{}, dst{};
        src.Type = dst.Type = D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_TYPE_SUBRESOURCE_INDEX;
        src.pResource = in.depth; src.SubresourceIndex = 0;
        dst.pResource = g.depth_copy.Get(); dst.SubresourceIndex = 0;
        list->CopyTextureRegion(&dst, 0, 0, 0, &src, nullptr);
    }
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER post[4] = {
        transition(in.motion, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
        transition(g.motion_copy.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
        transition(with_depth ? in.depth : nullptr, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
        transition(g.depth_copy.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE)};
    post[2].Transition.Subresource = 0;
    list->ResourceBarrier(with_depth ? 4 : 2, post);
    g.ref_scale_x = in.mv_scale_x;
    g.ref_scale_y = in.mv_scale_y;
    g.ref_has_depth = with_depth ? 1u : 0u;
    g.have_ref = 1;
    g.ref_fresh = 1;
}

bool Latest(Output* out)
{
    if (!out || !g.have_output.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return false;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    *out = g.latest;
    return true;
}

Stats GetStats()
{
    Stats s{};
    s.enabled = na::settings::Get().motion_enabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.active = g.active.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.latched_off = g.latched.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.hdr = g.hdr.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.available = g.available.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.frames = g.frames.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.skipped = g.skipped.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.flow_ms = g.flow_ms.load();
    s.avg_ms = g.avg_ms.load();
    s.ref_available = g.ref_available.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.ref_mean_err_px = g.ref_err.load();
    s.ref_within_1px_pct = g.ref_within.load();
    s.ref_mean_mag_px = g.ref_mag.load();
    s.ref_frames = g.ref_frames.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(g.m, std::try_to_lock);
    if (lock.owns_lock()) {
        s.width = g.width; s.height = g.height; s.grid = g.grid * g.downscale; s.perf = g.perf; // grid reported in display pixels
        strncpy_s(s.reason, g.reason, _TRUNCATE);
    } else {
        strncpy_s(s.reason, "Updating.", _TRUNCATE);
    }
    return s;
}

void ResetLatch()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    g.latched.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
    set_reason("Motion analysis is off.");
}

void Shutdown()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    auto wait_fence = [](ID3D12Fence* f, uint64_t v) {
        if (f && v && f->GetCompletedValue() < v) {
            HANDLE ev = CreateEventW(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, nullptr);
            f->SetEventOnCompletion(v, ev);
            WaitForSingleObject(ev, 3000);
            CloseHandle(ev);
        }
    };
    wait_fence(g.of_fence.Get(), g.last_of_value);
    wait_fence(g.fence.Get(), g.fence_next - 1);
    wait_fence(g.own_fence.Get(), g.own_next - 1);
    // Teardown rule (A36): unregister and release registered resources, then destroy the engine; fences outlive it.
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kInputs; ++i) if (g.input_handles[i]) { g.flow.Unregister(g.input_handles[i]); g.input_handles[i] = nullptr; }
    if (g.grid_handle) { g.flow.Unregister(g.grid_handle); g.grid_handle = nullptr; }
    for (auto& t : g.inputs) t.Reset();
    g.grid_out.Reset();
    if (g.flow_ready) g.flow.Shutdown();
    g.flow_ready = false;
    for (auto& t : g.flow_out) t.Reset();
    for (auto& t : g.valid_out) t.Reset();
    g.depth_copy.Reset(); g.motion_copy.Reset(); g.depth_up.Reset(); g.motion_up.Reset();
    g.acc_buffer.Reset(); g.acc_readback.Reset();
    for (auto& v : g.acc_fence_value) v = 0;
    g.have_ref = 0; g.ref_fresh = 0; g.ref_w = g.ref_h = 0;
    g.ref_available.store(0); g.ref_frames.store(0); g.ref_err.store(0.f); g.ref_within.store(0.f); g.ref_mag.store(0.f);
    g.have_output.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
    g.latest = Output{};
    g.pso.Reset(); g.pso_up_depth.Reset(); g.pso_up_motion.Reset(); g.pso_compare.Reset();
    g.root.Reset(); g.heap.Reset(); g.list.Reset(); g.own_list.Reset(); g.own_queue.Reset();
    for (Slot& s : g.slots) { s.alloc.Reset(); s.fence_value = 0; }
    for (Slot& s : g.own_slots) { s.alloc.Reset(); s.fence_value = 0; }
    g.fence.Reset(); g.of_fence.Reset(); g.own_fence.Reset();
    g.device.Reset();
    g.width = g.height = 0;
    g.input_frames = 0; g.last_of_value = 0; g.fence_next = 1; g.of_next = 1; g.own_next = 1; g.slot_index = 0; g.out_index = 0;
    g.of_issued_tick = 0;
    for (auto& v : g.input_of_value) v = 0;
    g.active.store(0); g.latched.store(0);
    set_reason("Motion analysis is off.");
}

} // namespace na::motion


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\motion\live_flow.hpp ---

// A52: live hardware optical flow at Present â€” the Tier-3 raw-flow supplier
// running inside the game (M-001 pipeline, "not done: live wiring" in A36).
//
// Every presented frame is converted to the display-encoded BGRA8 image the
// NVIDIA optical-flow engine (NVOFA, driver nvofapi64.dll) expects (PQ HDR10
// back buffers are decoded to SDR at the paper white the NR pass uses; SDR
// back buffers are re-encoded as BGRA8), the engine computes the flow from
// the CURRENT frame to the PREVIOUS one asynchronously, and FlowExpand turns
// the grid result into the arbiter's full-resolution RG32F raw flow
// (pixels, p_previous âˆ’ p_current) plus, when a raw-depth texture is
// supplied, a validity texture. The latest result is published with the
// fence value that completes it; consumers wait on that fence.
//
// Rules kept: no waits on the render thread (a frame whose ring slot is
// still busy is skipped and counted); the engine's teardown rule (A36) is
// honoured on Shutdown; inert unless [motion] enabled=1; a failure latches
// the supplier off with a plain-language reason, like the NR pass.
// Cost: grid 4 / perf 5 measured â‰ˆ 4 ms wall at 3440Ã—1440 (A36); the
// engine work is serialised behind the frame on the direct queue, so this
// is an EXPERIMENTAL, off-by-default feature until measured in a game.
#pragma once

#include <d3d12.h>
#include <dxgi1_4.h>

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::motion {

// Optional raw-depth source (R32_FLOAT, full display resolution, state
// NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE at Present, caller-owned) for depth-aware
// expansion; null = plain bilinear expansion (bleeds at depth edges).
void SetDepthSource(ID3D12Resource* depth_full_res_r32f, float depth_clear);

// A57: inputs seen at the game's DLSS evaluate (DLSS titles only): the
// game's depth and its OWN motion vectors. Copied on the game's list like
// the NR pass does; at Present the depth is upsampled (nearest) for
// depth-aware expansion and the motion vectors (bilinear, scaled to display
// pixels) become the GROUND TRUTH the hardware flow is compared against:
// per-frame mean endpoint error and the share of pixels within 1 px,
// accumulated on the GPU and read back without waiting. This is the live
// accuracy measurement for the universal motion source, recorded in the
// status log; nothing consumes the flow in a DLSS game.
struct EvalRef {
    ID3D12Resource* depth = nullptr;   // NPSR at call time (plane 0 copied)
    ID3D12Resource* motion = nullptr;  // NPSR at call time
    uint32_t render_w = 0, render_h = 0;
    float mv_scale_x = 0, mv_scale_y = 0; // value * scale = render pixels (DLSS convention)
    uint32_t depth_inverted = 0;
};
bool WantsInputs(); // enabled and not latched: the evaluate detour should call OnEvaluate
void OnEvaluate(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, const EvalRef& in);

// At Present, before the original Present (back buffer in PRESENT state).
void OnPresent(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue, ID3D12Resource* backbuffer, DXGI_COLOR_SPACE_TYPE color_space);

struct Output {
    ID3D12Resource* flow_rg32f;     // full resolution, NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE once `fence` >= value
    ID3D12Resource* validity_r32f;  // null without a depth source
    ID3D12Fence* fence;
    uint64_t value;
    uint64_t frame;                 // presents processed so far (0 = none)
    uint32_t width, height, grid;
};
// The most recent completed-or-pending result; false until one exists.
bool Latest(Output* out);

struct Stats {
    uint32_t enabled;       // setting
    uint32_t active;        // produced a result on the last processed frame
    uint32_t latched_off;   // failed; reason set
    uint32_t hdr;           // input decoded from PQ
    uint32_t available;     // NVOFA present on this device (after the first attempt)
    uint64_t frames;        // results published
    uint64_t skipped;       // presents not processed (ring busy / first frame / disabled)
    float flow_ms;          // engine wall time of the last completed flow (CPU-observed, fence to fence)
    float avg_ms;
    uint32_t width, height, grid, perf;
    // A57 ground-truth comparison (DLSS titles): last completed frame.
    uint32_t ref_available;     // 1 when the game's motion vectors are being compared
    float ref_mean_err_px;      // mean endpoint error |flow - game MV| over compared pixels
    float ref_within_1px_pct;   // share of compared pixels within 1 px
    float ref_mean_mag_px;      // mean |game MV| (how much motion there was)
    uint64_t ref_frames;        // comparisons completed
    char reason[96];
};
Stats GetStats();
void ResetLatch();
void Shutdown(); // waits for in-flight work (bounded), honours the engine teardown rule

} // namespace na::motion


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\ngx_observe\gpu_timing.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/ngx_observe/gpu_timing.hpp"

#include <wrl/client.h>

#include <atomic>
#include <cstring>
#include <mutex>

using Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr;

namespace na::gputime {
namespace {

constexpr uint32_t kSlots = 8;     // ring of in-flight measurements
constexpr uint32_t kReadDelay = 3; // presents before a slot is read

struct Slot {
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> readback;   // 16 bytes: begin/end stamps
    uint64_t issued_present = 0;       // present counter when End() ran
    bool armed = false;                // End() recorded, not yet read
};

std::mutex g_m;
ComPtr<ID3D12Device> g_device;
ComPtr<ID3D12QueryHeap> g_heap;
Slot g_slots[kSlots];
uint32_t g_next = 0;         // slot used by the current Begin/End pair
bool g_begun = false;
uint64_t g_present_counter = 0;
std::atomic<bool> g_enabled{true};
std::atomic<bool> g_failed{false};
std::atomic<float> g_last{0.0f}, g_avg{0.0f};
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_samples{0};
uint64_t g_freq = 0;

bool ensure()
{
    if (g_heap) return true;
    if (g_failed.load() || !g_device) return false;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Device> dev = g_device;
    D3D12_QUERY_HEAP_DESC qh{};
    qh.Type = D3D12_QUERY_HEAP_TYPE_TIMESTAMP;
    qh.Count = kSlots * 2;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateQueryHeap(&qh, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g_heap)))) { g_failed.store(true); return false; }
    for (Slot& s : g_slots) {
        D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp{};
        hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_READBACK;
        D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC bd{};
        bd.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
        bd.Width = 16;
        bd.Height = bd.DepthOrArraySize = bd.MipLevels = 1;
        bd.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
        bd.Layout = D3D12_TEXTURE_LAYOUT_ROW_MAJOR;
        if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &bd, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST,
                                                nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&s.readback)))) {
            g_failed.store(true);
            g_heap.Reset();
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

} // namespace

void SetEnabled(bool enabled) { g_enabled.store(enabled); }

void SetDevice(ID3D12Device* device)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_m);
    if (!g_device && device) g_device = device;
}

void Begin(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list)
{
    if (!g_enabled.load() || !list) return;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_m);
    if (!ensure()) return;
    Slot& s = g_slots[g_next];
    if (s.armed) return; // ring full (reader starved): skip this frame
    list->EndQuery(g_heap.Get(), D3D12_QUERY_TYPE_TIMESTAMP, g_next * 2);
    g_begun = true;
}

void End(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list)
{
    if (!g_enabled.load() || !list) return;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_m);
    if (!g_heap || !g_begun) return;
    g_begun = false;
    Slot& s = g_slots[g_next];
    list->EndQuery(g_heap.Get(), D3D12_QUERY_TYPE_TIMESTAMP, g_next * 2 + 1);
    list->ResolveQueryData(g_heap.Get(), D3D12_QUERY_TYPE_TIMESTAMP, g_next * 2, 2, s.readback.Get(), 0);
    s.issued_present = g_present_counter;
    s.armed = true;
    g_next = (g_next + 1) % kSlots;
}

void Poll(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue)
{
    if (!g_enabled.load()) return;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_m);
    ++g_present_counter;
    if (!g_heap) return;
    if (!g_freq && queue) queue->GetTimestampFrequency(&g_freq);
    if (!g_freq) return;
    for (Slot& s : g_slots) {
        if (!s.armed || g_present_counter - s.issued_present < kReadDelay) continue;
        uint64_t stamps[2] = {0, 0};
        void* mapped = nullptr;
        D3D12_RANGE r{0, 16};
        if (SUCCEEDED(s.readback->Map(0, &r, &mapped))) {
            std::memcpy(stamps, mapped, 16);
            D3D12_RANGE none{0, 0};
            s.readback->Unmap(0, &none);
        }
        s.armed = false;
        if (stamps[0] == 0 || stamps[1] < stamps[0]) continue; // not executed / invalid
        float ms = static_cast<float>(static_cast<double>(stamps[1] - stamps[0]) * 1000.0 /
                                      static_cast<double>(g_freq));
        g_last.store(ms);
        float prev = g_avg.load();
        g_avg.store(prev > 0 ? prev * 0.95f + ms * 0.05f : ms);
        g_samples.fetch_add(1);
    }
}

Stats GetStats()
{
    return Stats{g_last.load(), g_avg.load(), g_samples.load(),
                 (g_enabled.load() && !g_failed.load()) ? 1u : 0u};
}

} // namespace na::gputime


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\ngx_observe\gpu_timing.hpp ---

// GPU cost of the DLSS evaluate, measured in-game (A23): timestamp queries
// recorded on the game's own command list immediately around the original
// evaluate call, resolved into a small ring of readback buffers, and read a
// few Presents later (the game executes the list before it presents). No
// fences, no waits: a slot is only read once it is old enough, and a slot
// whose stamps are still zero is skipped. Distinguishes GPU time of the DLSS
// pass from the Present-cadence fps the footer shows.
#pragma once

#include <d3d12.h>

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::gputime {

// The device comes from the overlay host (which owns a real device); until it
// is set, Begin/End are no-ops and the list pointer is never dereferenced
// (the NGX stub tests pass fake list pointers).
void SetDevice(ID3D12Device* device);

// Called around the original evaluate, on the game's list. Safe if the
// device refuses query heaps: the module disables itself and returns.
void Begin(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list);
void End(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list);

// From the Present hook: `queue` provides the timestamp frequency; reads
// completed slots. Cheap; call every frame.
void Poll(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue);

struct Stats {
    float last_ms;      // most recent completed evaluate
    float avg_ms;       // exponential average
    uint64_t samples;   // completed measurements
    uint32_t enabled;   // 0 if the device/driver refused the queries
};
Stats GetStats();
void SetEnabled(bool enabled);

} // namespace na::gputime


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\ngx_observe\ngx_observe.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/ngx_observe/ngx_observe.hpp"

#include "host_dx12/nr/nr_pass.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/motion/live_flow.hpp"

#include "host_dx12/capture/frame_capture.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/fg/frame_gen.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/hooking/vtable_hook.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/ngx_observe/gpu_timing.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/ngx_observe/size_confirm.hpp"
#include "host_dx12/settings.hpp"

#include <d3d12.h>

#include <atomic>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <cwctype>
#include <mutex>
#include <vector>

#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
// Locally pinned NVIDIA SDK headers (never committed; see third_party/README).
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <d3d12.h>
#pragma warning(push, 3)
#include <nvsdk_ngx_defs.h>
#include <nvsdk_ngx_params.h>
#pragma warning(pop)
#endif

namespace na::ngx {
namespace {

// ---- Signatures mirroring the pinned NVIDIA SDK (opaque pointer params). ----
using NgxResult = int32_t;
using PfnInitExt = NgxResult(__cdecl*)(unsigned long long app_id,
                                       const wchar_t* data_path, void* device,
                                       uint32_t sdk_version, const void* params);
using PfnInitProjectId = NgxResult(__cdecl*)(const char* project_id,
                                             uint32_t engine_type,
                                             const char* engine_version,
                                             const wchar_t* data_path,
                                             void* device, uint32_t sdk_version,
                                             const void* params);
using PfnCreateFeature = NgxResult(__cdecl*)(void* cmd_list, uint32_t feature_id,
                                             void* params, void** out_handle);
using PfnEvaluateFeature = NgxResult(__cdecl*)(void* cmd_list, const void* handle,
                                               const void* params, void* callback);
using PfnReleaseFeature = NgxResult(__cdecl*)(void* handle);
using PfnShutdown1 = NgxResult(__cdecl*)(void* device);
using PfnGetCapParams = NgxResult(__cdecl*)(void** out_params);

// A43: one set of ORIGINAL entry points PER HOOKED MODULE. Session 6 root
// cause: a single shared set was overwritten each time another module with
// the same export names was hooked (the driver ships two cores, _nvngx.dll
// and nvngx.dll, and every feature DLL â€” nvngx_dlss.dll, nvngx_dlssnr.dll â€”
// exports the same names). After the Neural Rendering runtime was placed
// beside the game, the game's DLSS CreateFeature was forwarded INTO THE NR
// DLL, which answered 0xBAD00002 for every DLSS creation. Now each module
// gets its own slot and detours, and feature DLLs are never hooked.
struct CoreSlot {
    std::atomic<HMODULE> module{nullptr};
    PfnInitExt init_ext = nullptr;
    PfnInitProjectId init_pid = nullptr;
    PfnCreateFeature create = nullptr;
    PfnEvaluateFeature evaluate = nullptr;
    PfnReleaseFeature release = nullptr;
    PfnShutdown1 shutdown1 = nullptr;
    PfnGetCapParams getcap = nullptr;
    std::atomic<uint32_t> hooked{0};       // exports hooked in this module
    std::atomic<uint32_t> init_succeeded{0}; // the game initialised THIS core
};
constexpr int kMaxSlots = 2; // the driver's two cores; a third core is refused
CoreSlot g_slots[kMaxSlots];

// ---- Counters and record ring (hot path: atomics only). ----
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_inits{0}, g_creates{0}, g_evaluates{0}, g_releases{0},
    g_shutdowns{0}, g_dropped{0};
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_hooked{0};
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_module_attached{0};

constexpr uint32_t kRingSize = 4096; // power of two
CallRecord g_ring[kRingSize];
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_ring_write{0};  // next slot to claim
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_ring_commit{0}; // slots fully written
uint64_t g_ring_read = 0;               // single consumer

void push_record(CallKind kind, int32_t result, uint32_t feature_id,
                 uint64_t app_id, uint64_t handle, uint64_t cmdlist,
                 const EvalParams* params = nullptr)
{
    uint64_t slot = g_ring_write.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    for (;;) {
        if (slot - g_ring_read >= kRingSize) {
            // Drop-newest under overflow; the consumer is a diagnostics
            // thread, so precise g_ring_read staleness only over-drops.
            g_dropped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            return;
        }
        if (g_ring_write.compare_exchange_weak(slot, slot + 1,
                                               std::memory_order_acq_rel))
            break;
    }
    CallRecord& r = g_ring[slot & (kRingSize - 1)];
    LARGE_INTEGER qpc;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&qpc);
    r.qpc_ticks = static_cast<uint64_t>(qpc.QuadPart);
    r.kind = static_cast<uint32_t>(kind);
    r.thread_id = GetCurrentThreadId();
    r.ngx_result = result;
    r.feature_id = feature_id;
    r.app_id = app_id;
    r.handle = handle;
    r.cmdlist = cmdlist;
    if (params)
        r.params = *params;
    else
        std::memset(&r.params, 0, sizeof(r.params));
    // Publish in order: spin until predecessors commit (bounded by producers
    // racing in the same instant; detour work is tiny).
    uint64_t expected = slot;
    while (!g_ring_commit.compare_exchange_weak(expected, slot + 1,
                                                std::memory_order_release))
        expected = slot;
}

// ---- Detours. Each is a thin per-slot template around an Impl that takes
// the slot, so the forwarded ORIGINAL always belongs to the module the game
// actually called (A43). InstallForModule is idempotent per module. ----
// A43: once the game has initialised a core, the live NR pass must create
// its feature through THAT core's entry points (one-time work at Init, not
// per frame; SetRuntime only stores pointers under the pass's mutex).
void on_core_initialised(CoreSlot& s)
{
    HMODULE m = s.module.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!m) return; // cannot happen after A45 (module published before enable); never consume the notification without it
    if (s.init_succeeded.exchange(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) return; // first success only
    na::nr::Runtime rt;
    rt.create_feature = reinterpret_cast<void*>(s.create);
    rt.evaluate_feature = reinterpret_cast<void*>(s.evaluate);
    rt.release_feature = reinterpret_cast<void*>(s.release);
    rt.allocate_parameters = reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(m, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_AllocateParameters"));
    rt.destroy_parameters = reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(m, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_DestroyParameters"));
    rt.get_capability_parameters = reinterpret_cast<void*>(s.getcap);
    na::nr::SetRuntime(rt);
}

NgxResult InitExtImpl(CoreSlot& s, unsigned long long app_id, const wchar_t* data_path, void* device,
                      uint32_t sdk_version, const void* params)
{
    NgxResult r = s.init_ext(app_id, data_path, device, sdk_version, params);
    if (r == 1) on_core_initialised(s);
    g_inits.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    push_record(CallKind::Init, r, 0, app_id, 0, 0);
    return r;
}

NgxResult InitProjectIdImpl(CoreSlot& s, const char* project_id, uint32_t engine_type, const char* engine_version,
                            const wchar_t* data_path, void* device, uint32_t sdk_version, const void* params)
{
    NgxResult r = s.init_pid(project_id, engine_type, engine_version, data_path, device, sdk_version, params);
    if (r == 1) on_core_initialised(s);
    g_inits.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    push_record(CallKind::Init, r, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    return r;
}

std::atomic<bool> g_detail{false};
// A66 (R1): request-scoped size confirmation; the flag keeps the evaluate
// hot path atomics-only while nothing is armed.
std::atomic<bool> g_confirm_active{false};
std::mutex g_confirm_m;
confirm::Monitor g_confirm;
confirm::FeatureFlags g_feature_flags; // A68: creation flags per game handle (guarded by g_confirm_m)
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_rr_seen{0};
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_fg_seen{0};
std::atomic<HMODULE> g_hooked_module{nullptr};
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_sr_creates{0};
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_last_sr_in_w{0}, g_last_sr_in_h{0}, g_last_sr_out_w{0}, g_last_sr_out_h{0};
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_create_failures{0}, g_create_recovered{0};
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_preset_rejected{0}, g_quality_rejected{0};
std::atomic<const void*> g_last_sr_handle{nullptr}; // A46: the feature "Apply now" re-creates
std::mutex g_reapply_mutex;               // rare path only (apply / release)
EvalParams g_last_sr_create{};            // A63: the game's last successful DLSS creation (sizes, quality, flags), guarded by g_reapply_mutex
constexpr uint32_t kFeatureRayReconstruction = 13; // NVSDK_NGX_Feature_RayReconstruction

// A66 (R3): bounded per-creation records. The detour captures only raw
// return addresses, the thread id and the sizes (no module lookups, no
// formatting); GetCreateRecords resolves modules when it is read.
constexpr uint32_t kCreateRecords = 16;
struct RawCreate {
    uint64_t seq, tick, confirm_id;
    uint32_t thread_id, feature_id;
    int32_t result;
    uint32_t in_w, in_h, out_w, out_h;
    uint32_t frame_count;
    void* frames[24];
};
std::mutex g_create_rec_m;
RawCreate g_create_recs[kCreateRecords] = {};
uint64_t g_create_seq = 0; // guarded by g_create_rec_m
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_create_records{0};

// A71: the game's Streamline settings calls (see RecordSlCall), same shape.
struct RawSlCall {
    uint64_t seq, tick;
    uint32_t thread_id, kind, feature;
    uint32_t raw[3];
    uint32_t repeats;
    uint32_t frame_count;
    void* frames[24];
};
constexpr uint32_t kSlCallRecords = 32;
RawSlCall g_sl_recs[kSlCallRecords] = {}; // guarded by g_create_rec_m
uint64_t g_sl_seq = 0;

// Resolves raw return addresses to module + RVA (read time only, never in a
// detour). Frames inside this module are skipped; unknown ones are kept as
// `game = 2` so the chain stays complete.
uint32_t resolve_frames(void* const* raw, uint32_t n, CreateFrame* out, uint32_t cap, HMODULE self, HMODULE exe)
{
    uint32_t count = 0;
    for (uint32_t f = 0; f < n && count < cap; ++f) {
        HMODULE m = nullptr;
        if (!GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                                static_cast<LPCWSTR>(raw[f]), &m) || !m) {
            CreateFrame& cf = out[count++];
            cf = CreateFrame{};
            cf.address = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(raw[f]);
            cf.game = 2;
            continue;
        }
        if (m == self) continue;
        CreateFrame& cf = out[count++];
        cf = CreateFrame{};
        cf.address = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(raw[f]);
        cf.rva = cf.address - reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(m);
        cf.game = m == exe ? 1u : 0u;
        wchar_t path[MAX_PATH] = {};
        GetModuleFileNameW(m, path, MAX_PATH);
        const wchar_t* name = path;
        for (const wchar_t* p = path; *p; ++p) if (*p == L'\\' || *p == L'/') name = p + 1;
        WideCharToMultiByte(CP_ACP, 0, name, -1, cf.module, sizeof(cf.module), nullptr, nullptr);
        cf.module[sizeof(cf.module) - 1] = 0;
    }
    return count;
}

NgxResult CreateFeatureImpl(CoreSlot& s, void* cmd_list, uint32_t feature_id, void* params, void** out_handle)
{
    const PfnCreateFeature g_orig_create = s.create; // this module's original
    void* raw_frames[24] = {};
    USHORT raw_n = 0;
    if (feature_id == 1) raw_n = RtlCaptureStackBackTrace(1, 24, raw_frames, nullptr); // A66/A71: evidence, resolved later

    if (feature_id == kFeatureRayReconstruction) g_rr_seen.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
    if (feature_id == 11) g_fg_seen.store(1, std::memory_order_release); // NVSDK_NGX_Feature_FrameGeneration
    EvalParams before{};
    ReadEvalParams(params, &before); // the game's own quality value, for the fallback
    bool overrode = ApplyCreateOverrides(params); // takeover step: write hints
    EvalParams ep{};
    ReadEvalParams(params, &ep); // read before: capture inputs even on failure
    ep.overridden = overrode ? 1u : 0u;
    NgxResult r = g_orig_create(cmd_list, feature_id, params, out_handle);
    // A42 fallback (session 6 finding: preset L made every DLSS creation fail
    // with 0xBAD00002 and the game ran without DLSS at all). If OUR writes
    // made the creation fail, retry without the preset hints; if that still
    // fails, retry with the game's own quality value. Never leave the game
    // without DLSS because of a hint.
    if (r != 1 && feature_id == 1 && overrode) {
        if (StripPresetHints(params)) {
            g_preset_rejected.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
            na::settings::Get().preset.store(-1, std::memory_order_release); // menu saves this
            r = g_orig_create(cmd_list, feature_id, params, out_handle);
        }
        if (r != 1 && before.perf_quality != 0xFFFFFFFFu && RestoreQualityValue(params, before.perf_quality)) {
            g_quality_rejected.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
            r = g_orig_create(cmd_list, feature_id, params, out_handle);
        }
        if (r == 1) g_create_recovered.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    }
    if (r != 1) g_create_failures.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (r == 1 && feature_id == 1) { // NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success, DLSS super resolution
        // A30: evidence for the menu's "active" state â€” the size the game
        // actually created its DLSS feature with (poison 0xFFFFFFFF = unread).
        g_last_sr_in_w.store(ep.in_width, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        g_last_sr_in_h.store(ep.in_height, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        g_last_sr_out_w.store(ep.out_width, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        g_last_sr_out_h.store(ep.out_height, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        g_sr_creates.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_release);
        if (out_handle) g_last_sr_handle.store(*out_handle, std::memory_order_release); // A46 apply target
        if (ep.valid) { // A63: what a re-creation from scratch needs
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_reapply_mutex);
            g_last_sr_create = ep;
        }
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
            uint64_t hnd = out_handle ? reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(*out_handle) : 0;
            g_feature_flags.Set(hnd, ep.valid ? ep.create_flags : 0xFFFFFFFFu); // A68: flags bound to this feature
            if (g_confirm_active.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) { // A66 (R1): the feature the request waits for
                uint32_t w = ep.valid ? ep.in_width : 0, h = ep.valid ? ep.in_height : 0;
                g_confirm.OnCreate(w, h, hnd, GetTickCount64());
            }
        }
    }
    if (feature_id == 1) { // A66 (R3): bounded record, raw addresses only
        uint64_t confirm_id = 0;
        if (g_confirm_active.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
            confirm_id = g_confirm.snapshot().id;
        }
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_create_rec_m);
        RawCreate& rc = g_create_recs[g_create_seq % kCreateRecords];
        rc = RawCreate{};
        rc.seq = ++g_create_seq;
        rc.tick = GetTickCount64();
        rc.confirm_id = confirm_id;
        rc.thread_id = GetCurrentThreadId();
        rc.feature_id = feature_id;
        rc.result = r;
        rc.in_w = ep.valid ? ep.in_width : 0xFFFFFFFFu;
        rc.in_h = ep.valid ? ep.in_height : 0xFFFFFFFFu;
        rc.out_w = ep.valid ? ep.out_width : 0xFFFFFFFFu;
        rc.out_h = ep.valid ? ep.out_height : 0xFFFFFFFFu;
        rc.frame_count = raw_n;
        for (USHORT i = 0; i < raw_n; ++i) rc.frames[i] = raw_frames[i];
        g_create_records.store(g_create_seq, std::memory_order_release);
    }
    g_creates.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    uint64_t handle =
        (out_handle && *out_handle) ? reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(*out_handle) : 0;
    push_record(CallKind::CreateFeature, r, feature_id, 0, handle,
                reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(cmd_list), &ep);
    return r;
}

// ---- A46 "Apply now": re-create the game's DLSS feature in place ----
// The game only re-creates its DLSS feature when the user changes the DLSS
// mode in its own menu. For settings that do not change the render SIZE
// (preset hints), we can re-create the feature ourselves on the game's own
// command list at the next evaluate, using the same parameter object the
// game evaluates with (Streamline keeps one object per feature with the
// creation keys still set), then map the game's handle to ours: evaluates
// and the eventual release are forwarded to the new feature; the old one is
// released a few evaluates later so in-flight GPU work is never cut off.
// Size changes (DLAA/Quality/...) still need the game to rebuild its render
// targets; that is stated in the menu and cannot be done from here.
constexpr int kMaxHandleMaps = 4;
struct HandleMap {
    std::atomic<const void*> game{nullptr};
    std::atomic<const void*> ours{nullptr};
    std::atomic<void*> params{nullptr}; // A63: our own parameter object when the feature was built from scratch
};
HandleMap g_handle_maps[kMaxHandleMaps];
struct Retired { const void* handle; uint64_t due; };
std::vector<Retired> g_retired;           // guarded by g_reapply_mutex
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_retired_count{0}; // fast check without the mutex
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_reapply_pending{0};
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_reapplies{0}, g_reapply_failures{0};
constexpr uint64_t kRetireAfterEvaluates = 8;

const void* mapped_handle(const void* game)
{
    for (HandleMap& m : g_handle_maps)
        if (m.game.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == game) return m.ours.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    return nullptr;
}

// Writes the current preset choice into the feature's parameter object
// (hints, or Default when Automatic). The quality value and sizes are the
// game's and are left untouched.
bool write_preset_only(void* params)
{
    if (!params) return true; // nothing to write (test stubs pass no parameter object)
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    int32_t preset = na::settings::Get().preset.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (preset >= 1 && preset <= 13) {
        auto* p = static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(params);
        const unsigned int v = static_cast<unsigned int>(preset);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_DLAA, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Quality, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Balanced, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Performance, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_UltraPerformance, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_UltraQuality, v);
        return true;
    }
    return StripPresetHints(params);
#else
    (void)params;
    return false;
#endif
}

// A63: the game's evaluate-time parameter object does not always carry the
// creation keys (session 11: every re-creation answered 0xBAD00005, invalid
// parameter, whatever the preset). Build our own object from the game's last
// successful creation (sizes, quality value, create flags) plus the preset.
// Returns the NGX result; on success *params_out is our object (destroyed
// when the feature is released).
NgxResult create_from_snapshot_locked(CoreSlot& s, void* cmd_list, void** out_handle, void** params_out)
{
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    const EvalParams& c = g_last_sr_create;
    HMODULE m = s.module.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!c.valid || !m || c.in_width == 0xFFFFFFFFu || c.out_width == 0xFFFFFFFFu || c.perf_quality == 0xFFFFFFFFu) return -1;
    using PfnAlloc = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void**);
    using PfnDestroy = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void*);
    auto alloc = reinterpret_cast<PfnAlloc>(GetProcAddress(m, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_AllocateParameters"));
    auto destroy = reinterpret_cast<PfnDestroy>(GetProcAddress(m, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_DestroyParameters"));
    if (!alloc || !destroy) return -1;
    void* raw = nullptr;
    if (alloc(&raw) != 1 || !raw) return -1;
    auto* p = static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(raw);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_CreationNodeMask, 1u);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_VisibilityNodeMask, 1u);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Width, c.in_width);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Height, c.in_height);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_OutWidth, c.out_width);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_OutHeight, c.out_height);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_PerfQualityValue, static_cast<int>(c.perf_quality));
    if (c.create_flags != 0xFFFFFFFFu) p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Feature_Create_Flags, static_cast<int>(c.create_flags));
    write_preset_only(raw);
    NgxResult r = s.create(cmd_list, 1u, raw, out_handle);
    if (r == 1 && out_handle && *out_handle) *params_out = raw;
    else destroy(raw);
    return r;
#else
    (void)s; (void)cmd_list; (void)out_handle; (void)params_out;
    return -1;
#endif
}

NgxResult EvaluateFeatureImpl(CoreSlot& s, void* cmd_list, const void* handle, const void* params, void* callback)
{
    auto* list = static_cast<ID3D12GraphicsCommandList*>(cmd_list);
    const void* game_handle = handle; // A66: identity as the game sees it (creation records use the same)
    // A46: forward to our re-created feature when the game's handle is mapped.
    if (const void* ours = mapped_handle(handle)) handle = ours;
    if (g_reapply_pending.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && handle == g_last_sr_handle.load(std::memory_order_acquire) &&
        g_reapply_pending.exchange(0, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_reapply_mutex);
        void* new_handle = nullptr;
        void* own_params = nullptr;
        bool wrote = write_preset_only(const_cast<void*>(params));
        NgxResult cr = wrote ? s.create(cmd_list, 1u, const_cast<void*>(params), &new_handle) : -1;
        if (cr != 1) cr = create_from_snapshot_locked(s, cmd_list, &new_handle, &own_params); // A63 fallback
        if (cr == 1 && new_handle) {
            // Find the game's key for this handle (the original game handle, or the one already mapped).
            const void* game_key = handle;
            for (HandleMap& m : g_handle_maps)
                if (m.ours.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == handle) { game_key = m.game.load(std::memory_order_acquire); break; }
            HandleMap* slot = nullptr;
            for (HandleMap& m : g_handle_maps)
                if (m.game.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == game_key) { slot = &m; break; }
            if (!slot)
                for (HandleMap& m : g_handle_maps)
                    if (!m.game.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) { slot = &m; break; }
            if (slot) {
                if (void* old = slot->params.exchange(own_params, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) { // previous from-scratch object
                    using PfnDestroy = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void*);
                    if (HMODULE m = s.module.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
                        if (auto destroy = reinterpret_cast<PfnDestroy>(GetProcAddress(m, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_DestroyParameters"))) destroy(old);
                }
                slot->ours.store(new_handle, std::memory_order_release);
                slot->game.store(game_key, std::memory_order_release);
                g_retired.push_back({handle, g_evaluates.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) + kRetireAfterEvaluates});
                g_retired_count.store(static_cast<uint32_t>(g_retired.size()), std::memory_order_release);
                g_last_sr_handle.store(new_handle, std::memory_order_release);
                g_reapplies.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
                handle = new_handle;
            } else {
                s.release(new_handle); // no map slot: keep the old feature
                if (own_params) {
                    using PfnDestroy = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void*);
                    if (HMODULE m = s.module.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
                        if (auto destroy = reinterpret_cast<PfnDestroy>(GetProcAddress(m, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_DestroyParameters"))) destroy(own_params);
                }
                g_reapply_failures.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            }
        } else {
            g_reapply_failures.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        }
        push_record(CallKind::CreateFeature, cr, 1u, 0, reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(new_handle), reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(cmd_list));
    }
    if (g_retired_count.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_reapply_mutex);
        uint64_t now = g_evaluates.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < g_retired.size();) {
            if (g_retired[i].due <= now) {
                s.release(const_cast<void*>(g_retired[i].handle));
                g_retired.erase(g_retired.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(i));
            } else {
                ++i;
            }
        }
        g_retired_count.store(static_cast<uint32_t>(g_retired.size()), std::memory_order_release);
    }
    na::gputime::Begin(list);
    NgxResult r = s.evaluate(cmd_list, handle, params, callback);
    na::gputime::End(list);
    g_evaluates.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    bool detail = g_detail.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    bool capture = na::capture::Pending();
    bool nr = na::nr::WantsInputs();
    bool motion = na::motion::WantsInputs(); // A57: ground-truth comparison for the live flow
    bool fg = na::fg::WantsInputs();         // A59: Frame Generation inputs
    bool watch = g_confirm_active.load(std::memory_order_acquire); // A66: render-size confirmation armed
    if (detail || capture || nr || motion || fg || watch) {
        EvalParams ep{};
        ReadEvalParams(params, &ep);
        if (watch) {
            // A66/A67 (Astra R2/R4): every evaluation reaches the monitor. A
            // failed call or unreadable parameters is an observation of the
            // feature that breaks the streak; it is never skipped.
            confirm::EvalObs obs{};
            obs.handle = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(game_handle);
            obs.ok = ep.valid && r >= 0;
            if (obs.ok) {
                uint32_t rw = ep.subrect_width != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_width : ep.in_width;
                uint32_t rh = ep.subrect_height != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_height : ep.in_height;
                // Structural coherence of the frame's inputs with that size.
                // Jitter and motion scale must be present (not the 1e38f
                // "unread" sentinel), finite (std::isfinite rejects both
                // infinities and NaN), jitter within one pixel, motion scale
                // non-zero; a NEGATIVE finite scale is a legitimate convention
                // and passes. Each resource must cover base + size for its
                // subrect (an unknown base is NOT coherent); motion vectors at
                // the render size when the feature was created with MVLowRes,
                // else at the output size; the output size must be known and
                // non-zero. These are structural checks only: they do not
                // establish motion-vector units, temporal association, depth
                // interpretation or jitter semantics.
                bool coherent = rw != 0xFFFFFFFFu && rh != 0xFFFFFFFFu && rw && rh &&
                                ep.out_width != 0xFFFFFFFFu && ep.out_height != 0xFFFFFFFFu && ep.out_width && ep.out_height &&
                                confirm::ScalarInputsCoherent(ep.jitter_x, ep.jitter_y, ep.mv_scale_x, ep.mv_scale_y);
                if (coherent) {
                    uint32_t flags = ep.create_flags;
                    if (flags == 0xFFFFFFFFu) { // evaluate objects often lack creation keys: use THIS feature's creation flags (A68)
                        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
                        flags = g_feature_flags.Get(reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(game_handle));
                    }
                    bool mv_low_res = flags != 0xFFFFFFFFu && (flags & 0x2u) != 0; // NVSDK_NGX_DLSS_Feature_Flags_MVLowRes
                    uint32_t mv_w = mv_low_res ? rw : ep.out_width, mv_h = mv_low_res ? rh : ep.out_height;
                    auto covers = [](uint64_t res, uint32_t bx, uint32_t by, uint32_t w, uint32_t h) {
                        if (!res) return false; // unknown = unverified
                        D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(res)->GetDesc();
                        return confirm::CoversSubrect(d.Width, d.Height, bx, by, w, h); // A68: 64-bit on both axes
                    };
                    coherent = flags != 0xFFFFFFFFu &&
                               covers(ep.color, ep.color_base_x, ep.color_base_y, rw, rh) &&
                               covers(ep.depth, ep.depth_base_x, ep.depth_base_y, rw, rh) &&
                               covers(ep.motion, ep.mv_base_x, ep.mv_base_y, mv_w, mv_h) &&
                               covers(ep.output, ep.out_base_x, ep.out_base_y, ep.out_width, ep.out_height);
                }
                obs.w = rw;
                obs.h = rh;
                obs.coherent = coherent;
                obs.reset = ep.reset == 1;
            }
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
            g_confirm.OnEvaluate(obs, GetTickCount64());
            if (!g_confirm.Active()) g_confirm_active.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
        }
        if (fg && ep.valid && r >= 0) {
            na::fg::EvalInputs in{};
            in.engine_frame = na::fg::EvaluationFrame();
            in.camera_valid = na::fg::FindCamera(in.engine_frame, na::fg::EvaluationViewport(), in.camera);
            in.depth = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.depth);
            in.motion = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.motion);
            in.render_w = ep.subrect_width != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_width : ep.in_width;
            in.render_h = ep.subrect_height != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_height : ep.in_height;
