            in.out_w = ep.out_width;
            in.out_h = ep.out_height;
            in.jitter_x = ep.jitter_x < 1e30f ? ep.jitter_x : 0.f;
            in.jitter_y = ep.jitter_y < 1e30f ? ep.jitter_y : 0.f;
            in.mv_scale_x = ep.mv_scale_x < 1e30f ? ep.mv_scale_x : 1.f;
            in.mv_scale_y = ep.mv_scale_y < 1e30f ? ep.mv_scale_y : 1.f;
            in.reset = ep.reset == 0xFFFFFFFFu ? 0 : ep.reset;
            // DLSS create flags (nvsdk_ngx_defs.h): 1<<3 depth inverted, 1<<2 motion vectors jittered.
            uint32_t flags = ep.create_flags;
            if (flags == 0xFFFFFFFFu) {
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
                flags = g_feature_flags.Get(reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(game_handle));
            }
            in.mv_low_res = flags != 0xFFFFFFFFu && (flags & 0x2u);
            in.depth_inverted = flags != 0xFFFFFFFFu && (flags & 0x8u) ? 1u : 0u;
            in.mv_jittered = flags != 0xFFFFFFFFu && (flags & 0x4u) ? 1u : 0u;
            in.depth_base_x = ep.depth_base_x; in.depth_base_y = ep.depth_base_y;
            in.mv_base_x = ep.mv_base_x; in.mv_base_y = ep.mv_base_y;
            na::fg::OnEvaluate(list, in);
        }
        if (nr && ep.valid && r >= 0) {
            na::nr::EvalInputs in{};
            in.depth = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.depth);
            in.motion = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.motion);
            in.render_w = ep.subrect_width != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_width : ep.in_width;
            in.render_h = ep.subrect_height != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_height : ep.in_height;
            in.out_w = ep.out_width;
            in.out_h = ep.out_height;
            in.mv_scale_x = ep.mv_scale_x;
            in.mv_scale_y = ep.mv_scale_y;
            in.reset = ep.reset == 0xFFFFFFFFu ? 0 : ep.reset;
            in.depth_inverted = 0; // TLOU I: non-inverted (A13); other titles: read create flags later
            na::nr::OnEvaluate(list, in);
        }
        if (motion && ep.valid && r >= 0) {
            na::motion::EvalRef ref{};
            ref.depth = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.depth);
            ref.motion = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.motion);
            ref.render_w = ep.subrect_width != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_width : ep.in_width;
            ref.render_h = ep.subrect_height != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_height : ep.in_height;
            ref.mv_scale_x = ep.mv_scale_x;
            ref.mv_scale_y = ep.mv_scale_y;
            na::motion::OnEvaluate(list, ref);
        }
        if (detail)
            push_record(CallKind::EvaluateFeature, r, 0, 0,
                        reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(handle),
                        reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(cmd_list), &ep);
        if (capture && ep.valid && r >= 0) {
            na::capture::EvalInputs in{};
            in.color = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.color);
            in.depth = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.depth);
            in.motion = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.motion);
            in.output = reinterpret_cast<ID3D12Resource*>(ep.output);
            in.render_w = ep.subrect_width != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_width : ep.in_width;
            in.render_h = ep.subrect_height != 0xFFFFFFFFu ? ep.subrect_height : ep.in_height;
            in.out_w = ep.out_width;
            in.out_h = ep.out_height;
            in.jitter_x = ep.jitter_x;
            in.jitter_y = ep.jitter_y;
            in.mv_scale_x = ep.mv_scale_x;
            in.mv_scale_y = ep.mv_scale_y;
            in.reset = ep.reset == 0xFFFFFFFFu ? 0 : ep.reset;
            na::capture::OnEvaluate(static_cast<ID3D12GraphicsCommandList*>(cmd_list), in);
        }
    }
    return r;
}

NgxResult ReleaseFeatureImpl(CoreSlot& s, void* handle)
{
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
        g_feature_flags.Erase(reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(handle)); // A68: a released handle value may be reused
    }
    // A46: the game releases ITS handle; if we replaced that feature, release
    // ours instead (the game's original was retired already) and drop the map.
    for (HandleMap& m : g_handle_maps) {
        if (m.game.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == handle) {
            const void* ours = m.ours.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
            m.game.store(nullptr, std::memory_order_release);
            m.ours.store(nullptr, std::memory_order_release);
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_reapply_mutex);
            if (void* own = m.params.exchange(nullptr, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) { // A63: our from-scratch parameter object
                using PfnDestroy = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void*);
                if (HMODULE mod = s.module.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
                    if (auto destroy = reinterpret_cast<PfnDestroy>(GetProcAddress(mod, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_DestroyParameters"))) destroy(own);
            }
            bool still_retiring = false; // the game's own handle may still be in the retire queue: release it now
            for (size_t i = 0; i < g_retired.size();) {
                if (g_retired[i].handle == handle) { still_retiring = true; g_retired.erase(g_retired.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(i)); }
                else ++i;
            }
            g_retired_count.store(static_cast<uint32_t>(g_retired.size()), std::memory_order_release);
            if (still_retiring) s.release(handle);
            handle = const_cast<void*>(ours);
            break;
        }
    }
    NgxResult r = s.release(handle);
    g_releases.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    push_record(CallKind::ReleaseFeature, r, 0, 0,
                reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(handle), 0);
    return r;
}

NgxResult Shutdown1Impl(CoreSlot& s, void* device)
{
    NgxResult r = s.shutdown1(device);
    g_shutdowns.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    push_record(CallKind::Shutdown, r, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    return r;
}

// Per-slot detour entry points (one instantiation per slot index).
template <int S> NgxResult __cdecl DetourInitExt(unsigned long long a, const wchar_t* d, void* dev, uint32_t v, const void* p)
{ return InitExtImpl(g_slots[S], a, d, dev, v, p); }
template <int S> NgxResult __cdecl DetourInitProjectId(const char* id, uint32_t et, const char* ev, const wchar_t* d, void* dev, uint32_t v, const void* p)
{ return InitProjectIdImpl(g_slots[S], id, et, ev, d, dev, v, p); }
template <int S> NgxResult __cdecl DetourCreateFeature(void* cl, uint32_t f, void* p, void** h)
{ return CreateFeatureImpl(g_slots[S], cl, f, p, h); }
template <int S> NgxResult __cdecl DetourEvaluateFeature(void* cl, const void* h, const void* p, void* cb)
{ return EvaluateFeatureImpl(g_slots[S], cl, h, p, cb); }
template <int S> NgxResult __cdecl DetourReleaseFeature(void* h) { return ReleaseFeatureImpl(g_slots[S], h); }
template <int S> NgxResult __cdecl DetourShutdown1(void* dev) { return Shutdown1Impl(g_slots[S], dev); }

struct HookSpec {
    const char* name;
    void* detour;
    void** original;
};

// ---- Module watch via LdrRegisterDllNotification (documented ntdll API).
// The notification callback runs under the loader lock: it only queues and
// signals; a worker thread installs hooks afterwards. ----
typedef struct _NA_UNICODE_STRING { // ntdef UNICODE_STRING layout; not
    USHORT Length;                  // provided under WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
    USHORT MaximumLength;
    PWSTR Buffer;
} NA_UNICODE_STRING;
typedef struct _NA_LDR_DLL_NOTIFICATION_DATA {
    ULONG Flags;
    const NA_UNICODE_STRING* FullDllName;
    const NA_UNICODE_STRING* BaseDllName;
    PVOID DllBase;
    ULONG SizeOfImage;
} NA_LDR_DLL_NOTIFICATION_DATA;
using LdrNotifyFn = VOID(CALLBACK*)(ULONG reason,
                                    const NA_LDR_DLL_NOTIFICATION_DATA* data,
                                    PVOID context);
using LdrRegisterFn = LONG(NTAPI*)(ULONG flags, LdrNotifyFn fn, PVOID context,
                                   PVOID* cookie);
using LdrUnregisterFn = LONG(NTAPI*)(PVOID cookie);
constexpr ULONG kLdrDllLoaded = 1;

std::mutex g_watch_mutex;
std::vector<HMODULE> g_pending;      // guarded by g_watch_mutex
HANDLE g_watch_event = nullptr;
HANDLE g_watch_thread = nullptr;
PVOID g_ldr_cookie = nullptr;
std::atomic<bool> g_watch_stop{false};

VOID CALLBACK LdrNotify(ULONG reason, const NA_LDR_DLL_NOTIFICATION_DATA* data,
                        PVOID)
{
    if (reason != kLdrDllLoaded || !data || !data->BaseDllName ||
        !data->BaseDllName->Buffer)
        return;
    if (!IsNgxCoreModuleName(data->BaseDllName->Buffer,
                             data->BaseDllName->Length / sizeof(wchar_t)))
        return;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_watch_mutex);
        g_pending.push_back(static_cast<HMODULE>(data->DllBase));
    }
    SetEvent(g_watch_event);
}

DWORD WINAPI WatchThread(LPVOID)
{
    while (!g_watch_stop.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        WaitForSingleObject(g_watch_event, 500);
        std::vector<HMODULE> batch;
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_watch_mutex);
            batch.swap(g_pending);
        }
        for (HMODULE m : batch) InstallForModule(m);
    }
    return 0;
}

// ---- Synchronous nvngx attach (closes the Init race): detour
// LoadLibraryExW; AFTER the original returns (loader lock released for this
// load), install hooks in the same call so the module is observed before the
// caller can invoke any of its exports. The async LdrNotification path stays
// as the catch-all for loads that bypass this entry point. ----
using PfnLoadLibraryExW = HMODULE(WINAPI*)(LPCWSTR, HANDLE, DWORD);
PfnLoadLibraryExW g_orig_loadlibexw = nullptr;

HMODULE WINAPI DetourLoadLibraryExW(LPCWSTR name, HANDLE file, DWORD flags)
{
    HMODULE m = g_orig_loadlibexw(name, file, flags);
    if (m && name && !(flags & (LOAD_LIBRARY_AS_DATAFILE |
                                LOAD_LIBRARY_AS_DATAFILE_EXCLUSIVE |
                                LOAD_LIBRARY_AS_IMAGE_RESOURCE))) {
        if (IsNgxCoreModuleName(name, static_cast<size_t>(lstrlenW(name))))
            InstallForModule(m);
    }
    return m;
}

// ---- Logger thread ----
HANDLE g_log_thread = nullptr;
std::atomic<bool> g_log_stop{false};
FILE* g_log_file = nullptr;

DWORD WINAPI LoggerThread(LPVOID)
{
    CallRecord buf[256];
    {
        Stats s = GetStats();
        std::fprintf(g_log_file,
                     "# session start: core_preloaded=%u hooked=%u attached=%u\n",
                     s.core_preloaded, s.hooked_functions, s.module_attached);
        std::fflush(g_log_file);
    }
    while (!g_log_stop.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        Sleep(250);
        uint32_t n = DrainRecords(buf, 256);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            const CallRecord& r = buf[i];
            std::fprintf(g_log_file,
                         "qpc=%llu tid=%u kind=%u result=0x%08X feature=%u "
                         "app=0x%llX handle=0x%llX cmdlist=0x%llX",
                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.qpc_ticks),
                         r.thread_id, r.kind,
                         static_cast<uint32_t>(r.ngx_result), r.feature_id,
                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.app_id),
                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.handle),
                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.cmdlist));
            if (r.params.valid) {
                std::fprintf(
                    g_log_file,
                    " in=%ux%u out=%ux%u jitter=%.6f,%.6f mvscale=%.4f,%.4f "
                    "reset=%u color=0x%llX depth=0x%llX mv=0x%llX "
                    "outres=0x%llX exposure=0x%llX",
                    r.params.in_width, r.params.in_height, r.params.out_width,
                    r.params.out_height, r.params.jitter_x, r.params.jitter_y,
                    r.params.mv_scale_x, r.params.mv_scale_y, r.params.reset,
                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.params.color),
                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.params.depth),
                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.params.motion),
                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.params.output),
                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.params.exposure));
                std::fprintf(g_log_file,
                             " subrect=%ux%u pq=%u cflags=0x%X ovr=%u",
                             r.params.subrect_width, r.params.subrect_height,
                             r.params.perf_quality, r.params.create_flags,
                             r.params.overridden);
            }
            std::fputc('\n', g_log_file);
        }
        if (n) std::fflush(g_log_file);
    }
    return 0;
}

} // namespace

void SetDetailLogging(bool enabled) { g_detail.store(enabled, std::memory_order_release); }
// A66 (R1): request-scoped size confirmation.
uint64_t ArmSizeConfirmation(uint32_t w, uint32_t h, uint32_t need, uint32_t create_timeout_ms, uint32_t confirm_timeout_ms,
                             uint32_t overall_timeout_ms)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
    uint64_t id = g_confirm.Arm(w, h, need, GetTickCount64(), create_timeout_ms, confirm_timeout_ms, overall_timeout_ms);
    g_confirm_active.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    return id;
}
void MarkSizeConfirmationRestored(uint64_t id)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
    if (g_confirm.snapshot().id == id) g_confirm.MarkRestored(GetTickCount64());
    if (!g_confirm.Active()) g_confirm_active.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
}
void CancelSizeConfirmation()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
    g_confirm.Cancel(GetTickCount64());
    g_confirm_active.store(g_confirm.Active(), std::memory_order_release);
}
SizeConfirmation GetSizeConfirmation()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_confirm_m);
    g_confirm.Tick(GetTickCount64()); // deadlines hold even when no evaluate arrives
    if (!g_confirm.Active()) g_confirm_active.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    const confirm::Snapshot& s = g_confirm.snapshot();
    SizeConfirmation o{};
    o.id = s.id; o.state = s.state; o.reason = s.reason;
    o.req_w = s.req_w; o.req_h = s.req_h; o.need = s.need; o.streak = s.streak;
    o.evals = s.evals; o.matching = s.matching; o.mismatched = s.mismatched; o.incoherent = s.incoherent; o.failed = s.failed;
    o.resets = s.resets; o.unknown_handle = s.unknown_handle; o.other_feature = s.other_feature; o.before_restore = s.before_restore;
    o.creations = s.creations;
    o.created_w = s.created_w; o.created_h = s.created_h; o.last_w = s.last_w; o.last_h = s.last_h;
    o.armed_tick = s.armed_tick; o.restored_tick = s.restored_tick; o.created_tick = s.created_tick; o.done_tick = s.done_tick;
    return o;
}

// A66 (R3): resolve modules at read time (log thread), never in the detour.
uint32_t GetCreateRecords(uint64_t after_seq, CreateRecord* out, uint32_t capacity)
{
    RawCreate raw[kCreateRecords];
    uint32_t n = 0;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_create_rec_m);
        uint64_t first = g_create_seq > kCreateRecords ? g_create_seq - kCreateRecords + 1 : 1;
        if (after_seq + 1 > first) first = after_seq + 1;
        for (uint64_t s = first; s <= g_create_seq && n < capacity; ++s) raw[n++] = g_create_recs[(s - 1) % kCreateRecords];
    }
    HMODULE exe = GetModuleHandleW(nullptr);
    HMODULE self = nullptr;
    GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                       reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(&GetCreateRecords), &self);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const RawCreate& rc = raw[i];
        CreateRecord& r = out[i];
        r = CreateRecord{};
        r.seq = rc.seq; r.tick = rc.tick; r.thread_id = rc.thread_id; r.feature_id = rc.feature_id; r.result = rc.result;
        r.in_w = rc.in_w; r.in_h = rc.in_h; r.out_w = rc.out_w; r.out_h = rc.out_h; r.confirm_id = rc.confirm_id;
        r.frame_count = resolve_frames(rc.frames, rc.frame_count, r.frames, 24, self, exe);
    }
    return n;
}

// The constants block is the game's; its size is not ours to know. A guarded
// copy of its first 12 bytes (no C++ objects in this function: SEH rule).
static bool copy_raw3(const void* consts, uint32_t* out)
{
    __try {
        memcpy(out, consts, 12);
        return true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

// A71: called by the proxy's typed Streamline wrappers, on the game's thread,
// with the raw return addresses it captured; nothing is resolved here.
void RecordSlCall(uint32_t kind, uint32_t feature, const void* consts, void* const* frames, uint32_t frame_count)
{
    uint32_t raw[3] = {0xFFFFFFFFu, 0xFFFFFFFFu, 0xFFFFFFFFu};
    if (consts && !copy_raw3(consts, raw)) raw[0] = raw[1] = raw[2] = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_create_rec_m);
    // A73: the game makes this call every frame with the same content (session
    // 13: ~30 identical records per second). A repeat of the previous record
    // (same kind, feature, raw words and first six callers) is folded into it.
    if (g_sl_seq) {
        RawSlCall& last = g_sl_recs[(g_sl_seq - 1) % kSlCallRecords];
        bool same = last.kind == kind && last.feature == feature && last.raw[0] == raw[0] && last.raw[1] == raw[1] && last.raw[2] == raw[2];
        for (uint32_t i = 0; same && i < 6 && i < last.frame_count && i < frame_count; ++i) same = last.frames[i] == frames[i];
        if (same) { ++last.repeats; last.tick = GetTickCount64(); return; }
    }
    RawSlCall& r = g_sl_recs[g_sl_seq % kSlCallRecords];
    r = RawSlCall{};
    r.seq = ++g_sl_seq;
    r.tick = GetTickCount64();
    r.thread_id = GetCurrentThreadId();
    r.kind = kind;
    r.feature = feature;
    r.raw[0] = raw[0]; r.raw[1] = raw[1]; r.raw[2] = raw[2];
    r.frame_count = frame_count > 24 ? 24 : frame_count;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < r.frame_count; ++i) r.frames[i] = frames[i];
}

uint32_t GetSlCallRecords(uint64_t after_seq, SlCallRecord* out, uint32_t capacity)
{
    RawSlCall raw[kSlCallRecords];
    uint32_t n = 0;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_create_rec_m);
        uint64_t first = g_sl_seq > kSlCallRecords ? g_sl_seq - kSlCallRecords + 1 : 1;
        if (after_seq + 1 > first) first = after_seq + 1;
        for (uint64_t s = first; s <= g_sl_seq && n < capacity; ++s) raw[n++] = g_sl_recs[(s - 1) % kSlCallRecords];
    }
    HMODULE exe = GetModuleHandleW(nullptr);
    HMODULE self = nullptr;
    GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                       reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(&GetSlCallRecords), &self);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const RawSlCall& rc = raw[i];
        SlCallRecord& r = out[i];
        r = SlCallRecord{};
        r.seq = rc.seq; r.tick = rc.tick; r.thread_id = rc.thread_id; r.kind = rc.kind; r.feature = rc.feature;
        r.raw[0] = rc.raw[0]; r.raw[1] = rc.raw[1]; r.raw[2] = rc.raw[2];
        r.repeats = rc.repeats;
        r.frame_count = resolve_frames(rc.frames, rc.frame_count, r.frames, 24, self, exe);
    }
    return n;
}

void RequestReapply()
{
    if (g_last_sr_handle.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) g_reapply_pending.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
}

bool ApplyCreateOverrides(void* ngx_parameter)
{
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    if (!ngx_parameter) return false;
    if (!na::settings::Get().enabled.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return false;
    auto* p = static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(ngx_parameter);
    bool wrote = false;
    // A20 perf/correctness: tell DLSS which quality mode WE chose, so the
    // runtime picks the model path matching the render size we imposed
    // (e.g. the DLAA path for native, instead of "Balanced at native").
    auto mode = static_cast<na::settings::DlssMode>(
        na::settings::Get().dlss_mode.load(std::memory_order_acquire));
    int pq = NVSDK_NGX_PerfQuality_Value_DLAA;
    switch (mode) {
    case na::settings::DlssMode::Quality: pq = NVSDK_NGX_PerfQuality_Value_MaxQuality; break;
    case na::settings::DlssMode::Balanced: pq = NVSDK_NGX_PerfQuality_Value_Balanced; break;
    case na::settings::DlssMode::Performance: pq = NVSDK_NGX_PerfQuality_Value_MaxPerf; break;
    case na::settings::DlssMode::UltraPerformance: pq = NVSDK_NGX_PerfQuality_Value_UltraPerformance; break;
    default: break;
    }
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_PerfQualityValue, pq);
    wrote = true;
    int32_t preset = na::settings::Get().preset.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (preset >= 1 && preset <= 13) {
        const unsigned int v = static_cast<unsigned int>(preset);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_DLAA, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Quality, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Balanced, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Performance, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_UltraPerformance, v);
        p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_UltraQuality, v);
    }
    return wrote;
#else
    (void)ngx_parameter;
    return false;
#endif
}

bool StripPresetHints(void* ngx_parameter)
{
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    if (!ngx_parameter) return false;
    auto* p = static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(ngx_parameter);
    const unsigned int def = NVSDK_NGX_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Default;
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_DLAA, def);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Quality, def);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Balanced, def);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_Performance, def);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_UltraPerformance, def);
    p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Hint_Render_Preset_UltraQuality, def);
    return true;
#else
    (void)ngx_parameter;
    return false;
#endif
}

bool RestoreQualityValue(void* ngx_parameter, uint32_t perf_quality)
{
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    if (!ngx_parameter) return false;
    static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(ngx_parameter)->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_PerfQualityValue, static_cast<int>(perf_quality));
    return true;
#else
    (void)ngx_parameter; (void)perf_quality;
    return false;
#endif
}

// ---- Optimal-settings takeover ----
namespace {
using PfnOptimalCallback = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void* params);
std::atomic<void*> g_orig_optimal{nullptr};
std::atomic<uint64_t> g_opt_calls{0};
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_opt_user_w{0}, g_opt_user_h{0}, g_opt_out_w{0},
    g_opt_out_h{0}, g_opt_overridden{0};

inline uint32_t align8(uint32_t v) { return (v + 7u) & ~7u; }
} // namespace

void SetOriginalOptimalCallbackForTest(void* original_callback)
{
    g_orig_optimal.store(original_callback, std::memory_order_release);
}

int32_t OptimalSettingsCallback(void* ngx_parameter)
{
    int32_t r = 1; // NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success when no original (tests)
    if (void* orig = g_orig_optimal.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
        r = reinterpret_cast<PfnOptimalCallback>(orig)(ngx_parameter);
    g_opt_calls.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    if (!ngx_parameter || r < 0) return r;
    auto* p = static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(ngx_parameter);
    unsigned int user_w = 0, user_h = 0, out_w = 0, out_h = 0;
    p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Width, &user_w);
    p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Height, &user_h);
    p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_OutWidth, &out_w);
    p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_OutHeight, &out_h);
    g_opt_user_w.store(user_w); g_opt_user_h.store(user_h);

    bool enabled = na::settings::Get().enabled.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0;
    auto mode = static_cast<na::settings::DlssMode>(
        na::settings::Get().dlss_mode.load(std::memory_order_acquire));
    bool overridden = false;
    if (enabled && user_w && user_h) {
        unsigned int tw = user_w, th = user_h;
        if (mode != na::settings::DlssMode::DLAA) {
            float ratio = na::settings::ScaleForMode(mode);
            tw = align8(static_cast<unsigned int>(user_w * ratio + 0.5f));
            th = align8(static_cast<unsigned int>(user_h * ratio + 0.5f));
            if (tw > user_w) tw = user_w;
            if (th > user_h) th = user_h;
        }
        if (tw != out_w || th != out_h) {
            p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_OutWidth, tw);
            p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_OutHeight, th);
            p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Get_Dynamic_Max_Render_Width, tw);
            p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Get_Dynamic_Max_Render_Height, th);
            p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Get_Dynamic_Min_Render_Width, tw);
            p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Get_Dynamic_Min_Render_Height, th);
            out_w = tw; out_h = th;
            overridden = true;
        }
    }
    g_opt_out_w.store(out_w); g_opt_out_h.store(out_h);
    g_opt_overridden.store(overridden ? 1u : 0u);
#else
    (void)ngx_parameter;
#endif
    return r;
}

OptimalStats GetOptimalStats()
{
    OptimalStats s{};
    s.calls = g_opt_calls.load();
    s.last_user_w = g_opt_user_w.load(); s.last_user_h = g_opt_user_h.load();
    s.last_out_w = g_opt_out_w.load(); s.last_out_h = g_opt_out_h.load();
    s.last_overridden = g_opt_overridden.load();
    return s;
}

namespace {
// After the runtime hands out capability parameters, swap the optimal-
// settings callback pointer inside them for ours (idempotent: never
// re-capture our own pointer as the "original").
int32_t GetCapabilityParametersImpl(CoreSlot& s, void** out_params)
{
    int32_t r = s.getcap(out_params);
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    if (r >= 0 && out_params && *out_params) {
        auto* p = static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(*out_params);
        void* cb = nullptr;
        if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSSOptimalSettingsCallback, &cb) ==
                NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success && cb &&
            cb != reinterpret_cast<void*>(&OptimalSettingsCallback)) {
            g_orig_optimal.store(cb, std::memory_order_release);
            p->Set(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSSOptimalSettingsCallback,
                   reinterpret_cast<void*>(&OptimalSettingsCallback));
        }
    }
#endif
    return r;
}
template <int S> int32_t __cdecl DetourGetCapabilityParameters(void** out) { return GetCapabilityParametersImpl(g_slots[S], out); }

// Base file name of a module name or full path.
const wchar_t* base_name(const wchar_t* name, size_t len, size_t* out_len)
{
    size_t start = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i)
        if (name[i] == L'\\' || name[i] == L'/') start = i + 1;
    *out_len = len - start;
    return name + start;
}
bool equals_nocase(const wchar_t* a, size_t len, const wchar_t* lit)
{
    size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(lstrlenW(lit));
    if (n != len) return false;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)
        if (towlower(a[i]) != towlower(lit[i])) return false;
    return true;
}
} // namespace

bool IsNgxCoreModuleName(const wchar_t* name, size_t len)
{
    if (!name) return false;
    size_t blen = 0;
    const wchar_t* b = base_name(name, len, &blen);
    // The driver's cores, plus the test stub. Feature DLLs (nvngx_dlss.dll,
    // nvngx_dlssnr.dll, nvngx_dlssg.dll, ...) export the same names but are
    // NOT entry points the game calls; hooking them mis-routes calls (A43).
    return equals_nocase(b, blen, L"_nvngx.dll") || equals_nocase(b, blen, L"nvngx.dll") ||
           equals_nocase(b, blen, L"nvngx_stub.dll");
}

bool ReadEvalParams(const void* ngx_parameter, EvalParams* out)
{
    if (!out) return false;
    std::memset(out, 0, sizeof(*out));
    out->reset = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    out->in_width = out->in_height = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    out->out_width = out->out_height = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    out->jitter_x = out->jitter_y = 1e38f;
    out->mv_scale_x = out->mv_scale_y = 1e38f;
    out->subrect_width = out->subrect_height = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    out->perf_quality = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    out->create_flags = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    out->color_base_x = out->color_base_y = out->depth_base_x = out->depth_base_y = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    out->mv_base_x = out->mv_base_y = out->out_base_x = out->out_base_y = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    if (!ngx_parameter) return false;
    auto* p = static_cast<const NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(ngx_parameter);
    unsigned int u = 0;
    float f = 0;
    void* v = nullptr;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Width, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->in_width = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Height, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->in_height = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_OutWidth, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->out_width = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_OutHeight, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->out_height = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Reset, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->reset = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Jitter_Offset_X, &f) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->jitter_x = f;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Jitter_Offset_Y, &f) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->jitter_y = f;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_MV_Scale_X, &f) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->mv_scale_x = f;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_MV_Scale_Y, &f) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->mv_scale_y = f;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Color, &v) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->color = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(v);
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Depth, &v) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->depth = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(v);
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_MotionVectors, &v) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->motion = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(v);
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_Output, &v) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->output = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(v);
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_ExposureTexture, &v) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->exposure = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(v);
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Render_Subrect_Dimensions_Width, &u) ==
        NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->subrect_width = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Render_Subrect_Dimensions_Height, &u) ==
        NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->subrect_height = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_PerfQualityValue, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->perf_quality = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Feature_Create_Flags, &u) ==
        NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success)
        out->create_flags = u;
    // A67: subrect origins (coverage is base + size, not size alone).
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Input_Color_Subrect_Base_X, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success) out->color_base_x = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Input_Color_Subrect_Base_Y, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success) out->color_base_y = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Input_Depth_Subrect_Base_X, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success) out->depth_base_x = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Input_Depth_Subrect_Base_Y, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success) out->depth_base_y = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Input_MV_SubrectBase_X, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success) out->mv_base_x = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Input_MV_SubrectBase_Y, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success) out->mv_base_y = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Output_Subrect_Base_X, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success) out->out_base_x = u;
    if (p->Get(NVSDK_NGX_Parameter_DLSS_Output_Subrect_Base_Y, &u) == NVSDK_NGX_Result_Success) out->out_base_y = u;
    out->valid = 1;
    return true;
#else
    (void)ngx_parameter;
    return false;
#endif
}

namespace {
std::mutex g_install_mutex;

template <int S> uint32_t install_slot(HMODULE ngx_module)
{
    CoreSlot& slot = g_slots[S];
    const HookSpec specs[] = {
        {"NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_Init_Ext", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&DetourInitExt<S>), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&slot.init_ext)},
        {"NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_Init_ProjectID", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&DetourInitProjectId<S>), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&slot.init_pid)},
        {"NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_CreateFeature", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&DetourCreateFeature<S>), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&slot.create)},
        {"NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_EvaluateFeature", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&DetourEvaluateFeature<S>), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&slot.evaluate)},
        {"NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_ReleaseFeature", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&DetourReleaseFeature<S>), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&slot.release)},
        {"NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_Shutdown1", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&DetourShutdown1<S>), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&slot.shutdown1)},
        {"NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_GetCapabilityParameters", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&DetourGetCapabilityParameters<S>), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&slot.getcap)},
    };
    // A45 installation transaction (answers A43-NGX-ACK finding 2): every
    // original is prepared and published, then the slot's module record is
    // published, and only then are the detours enabled together. A detour
    // therefore never runs against a half-filled slot, and a failed commit
    // leaves nothing patched.
    if (!na::hooking::BeginInstall()) return 0;
    uint32_t hooked = 0;
    for (const HookSpec& s : specs) {
        void* target = reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(ngx_module, s.name));
        if (!target) continue;
        if (na::hooking::PrepareHook(target, s.detour, s.original)) ++hooked;
    }
    if (!hooked) {
        na::hooking::AbandonInstall();
        return 0;
    }
    slot.hooked.store(hooked, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    slot.module.store(ngx_module, std::memory_order_release); // published before enable
    if (!na::hooking::CommitInstall()) {
        slot.module.store(nullptr, std::memory_order_release);
        slot.hooked.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return 0;
    }
    return hooked;
}
} // namespace

uint32_t InstallForModule(HMODULE ngx_module)
{
    if (!ngx_module) return 0;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_install_mutex);
    // A43: only NGX CORE modules are hooked, verified by the module's own file
    // name (the notification/known-name paths pre-filter, this is the guard).
    wchar_t path[MAX_PATH] = {};
    DWORD n = GetModuleFileNameW(ngx_module, path, MAX_PATH);
    if (!n || !IsNgxCoreModuleName(path, n)) return 0;
    // Idempotent per module: a module already in a slot is not re-counted.
    int free_slot = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < kMaxSlots; ++i) {
        HMODULE m = g_slots[i].module.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (m == ngx_module) return g_slots[i].hooked.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        if (!m && free_slot < 0) free_slot = i;
    }
    if (free_slot < 0) return 0; // both driver cores already hooked; refuse a third
    uint32_t hooked = free_slot == 0 ? install_slot<0>(ngx_module) : install_slot<1>(ngx_module);
    if (hooked) { // install_slot published the slot's module/count before enabling (A45)
        g_hooked.fetch_add(hooked, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        g_hooked_module.store(ngx_module, std::memory_order_release);
        g_module_attached.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
    }
    return hooked;
}

bool GetRuntimeEntryPoints(void** create, void** evaluate, void** release, void** allocate_params,
                           void** destroy_params, void** get_capability_params)
{
    // A43: the core the game INITIALISED is the one whose entry points the NR
    // pass must use (a feature created on the other core would have no
    // initialised state). Fall back to the first hooked slot before any Init.
    CoreSlot* s = nullptr;
    for (int i = 0; i < kMaxSlots; ++i)
        if (g_slots[i].module.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && g_slots[i].init_succeeded.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) { s = &g_slots[i]; break; }
    if (!s)
        for (int i = 0; i < kMaxSlots; ++i)
            if (g_slots[i].module.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) { s = &g_slots[i]; break; }
    if (!s || !s->create || !s->evaluate || !s->release) return false;
    HMODULE m = s->module.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (create) *create = reinterpret_cast<void*>(s->create);
    if (evaluate) *evaluate = reinterpret_cast<void*>(s->evaluate);
    if (release) *release = reinterpret_cast<void*>(s->release);
    if (allocate_params) *allocate_params = reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(m, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_AllocateParameters"));
    if (destroy_params) *destroy_params = reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(m, "NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_DestroyParameters"));
    if (get_capability_params) *get_capability_params = reinterpret_cast<void*>(s->getcap);
    return true;
}

namespace {
std::atomic<uint32_t> g_core_preloaded{0};
std::mutex g_preload_mutex;
}

bool PreloadAndHookNgxCore()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_preload_mutex);
    if (g_core_preloaded.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return true;
    wchar_t dir[MAX_PATH] = {};
    DWORD size = sizeof(dir);
    HKEY key = nullptr;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
                      L"SOFTWARE\\NVIDIA Corporation\\Global\\NGXCore", 0,
                      KEY_READ | KEY_WOW64_64KEY, &key) != ERROR_SUCCESS)
        return false;
    LONG rc = RegQueryValueExW(key, L"FullPath", nullptr, nullptr,
                               reinterpret_cast<LPBYTE>(dir), &size);
    RegCloseKey(key);
    if (rc != ERROR_SUCCESS || !dir[0]) return false;
    wchar_t path[MAX_PATH];
    wsprintfW(path, L"%s\\_nvngx.dll", dir);
    HMODULE core = LoadLibraryW(path); // reference intentionally kept
    if (!core) return false;
    uint32_t hooked = InstallForModule(core);
    if (hooked) g_core_preloaded.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
    return hooked != 0;
}

Stats GetStats()
{
    Stats s{};
    s.core_preloaded = g_core_preloaded.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.rr_seen = g_rr_seen.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.fg_seen = g_fg_seen.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.inits = g_inits.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.creates = g_creates.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.evaluates = g_evaluates.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.releases = g_releases.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.shutdowns = g_shutdowns.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.records_dropped = g_dropped.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.hooked_functions = g_hooked.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.module_attached = g_module_attached.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.sr_creates = g_sr_creates.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.create_failures = g_create_failures.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.create_recovered = g_create_recovered.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.preset_rejected = g_preset_rejected.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.quality_rejected = g_quality_rejected.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.reapplies = g_reapplies.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.reapply_failures = g_reapply_failures.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.reapply_pending = g_reapply_pending.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.create_records = g_create_records.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.last_sr_in_w = g_last_sr_in_w.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.last_sr_in_h = g_last_sr_in_h.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.last_sr_out_w = g_last_sr_out_w.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.last_sr_out_h = g_last_sr_out_h.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    return s;
}

uint32_t DrainRecords(CallRecord* out, uint32_t capacity)
{
    uint64_t committed = g_ring_commit.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    uint32_t count = 0;
    while (g_ring_read < committed && count < capacity) {
        out[count++] = g_ring[g_ring_read & (kRingSize - 1)];
        ++g_ring_read;
    }
    return count;
}

bool StartModuleWatch(bool sync_loader_hook)
{
    if (g_watch_thread) return true;
    HMODULE ntdll = GetModuleHandleW(L"ntdll.dll");
    if (!ntdll) return false;
    auto reg = reinterpret_cast<LdrRegisterFn>(reinterpret_cast<void*>(
        GetProcAddress(ntdll, "LdrRegisterDllNotification")));
    if (!reg) return false;
    g_watch_event = CreateEventW(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, nullptr);
    if (!g_watch_event) return false;
    g_watch_stop.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    if (reg(0, &LdrNotify, nullptr, &g_ldr_cookie) != 0) {
        CloseHandle(g_watch_event);
        g_watch_event = nullptr;
        return false;
    }
    // Catch modules loaded before the watch started.
    for (const wchar_t* known : {L"_nvngx.dll", L"nvngx.dll", L"nvngx_stub.dll"}) { // cores only (A43)
        if (HMODULE m = GetModuleHandleW(known)) {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_watch_mutex);
            g_pending.push_back(m);
        }
    }
    SetEvent(g_watch_event);
    g_watch_thread = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, &WatchThread, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
    if (!g_watch_thread) {
        StopModuleWatch();
        return false;
    }
    // Synchronous fast path (Init race): hook LoadLibraryExW so nvngx modules
    // are hooked before the loader call returns to the game. OPT-IN ONLY â€”
    // correlated with early-startup crashes in TLOU (WORKLOG A12). Failure is
    // not fatal; the async notification path still covers attachment.
    if (sync_loader_hook) {
        // Prefer kernelbase's function body: kernel32's export forwards there
        // and LoadLibraryW funnels into the same body.
        HMODULE loader_mod = GetModuleHandleW(L"kernelbase.dll");
        if (!loader_mod) loader_mod = GetModuleHandleW(L"kernel32.dll");
        if (loader_mod) {
            void* target = reinterpret_cast<void*>(
                GetProcAddress(loader_mod, "LoadLibraryExW"));
            if (target)
                na::hooking::HookFunction(
                    target, reinterpret_cast<void*>(&DetourLoadLibraryExW),
                    reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_orig_loadlibexw));
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void StopModuleWatch()
{
    if (g_ldr_cookie) {
        HMODULE ntdll = GetModuleHandleW(L"ntdll.dll");
        if (ntdll) {
            auto unreg = reinterpret_cast<LdrUnregisterFn>(
                reinterpret_cast<void*>(
                    GetProcAddress(ntdll, "LdrUnregisterDllNotification")));
            if (unreg) unreg(g_ldr_cookie);
        }
        g_ldr_cookie = nullptr;
    }
    if (g_watch_thread) {
        g_watch_stop.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
        SetEvent(g_watch_event);
        WaitForSingleObject(g_watch_thread, 5000);
        CloseHandle(g_watch_thread);
        g_watch_thread = nullptr;
    }
    if (g_watch_event) {
        CloseHandle(g_watch_event);
        g_watch_event = nullptr;
    }
}

bool StartLogger(const wchar_t* log_path)
{
    if (g_log_thread || !log_path) return false;
    if (_wfopen_s(&g_log_file, log_path, L"ab") != 0 || !g_log_file)
        return false;
    g_log_stop.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    g_log_thread = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, &LoggerThread, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
    if (!g_log_thread) {
        std::fclose(g_log_file);
        g_log_file = nullptr;
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

void StopLogger()
{
    if (!g_log_thread) return;
    g_log_stop.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    WaitForSingleObject(g_log_thread, 5000);
    CloseHandle(g_log_thread);
    g_log_thread = nullptr;
    if (g_log_file) {
        std::fclose(g_log_file);
        g_log_file = nullptr;
    }
}

} // namespace na::ngx


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\ngx_observe\ngx_observe.hpp ---

// NGX-layer observation for Route A (OBSERVATION-API.md).
//
// Hooks the NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_* exports of the NGX CORE modules (nvngx.dll /
// _nvngx.dll; never feature DLLs, A43) to observe the DLSS calls a Streamline title
// actually makes: init identity, feature creation, per-frame evaluation
// cadence and result codes. First increment records call metadata only â€”
// no NVSDK_NGX_Parameter contents are interpreted (the Parameter object is a
// C++ interface whose layout we only trust from the locally pinned NVIDIA
// SDK, used in a local-only build config later).
//
// Function typedefs below mirror the pinned SDK's signatures
// (nvsdk_ngx.h, lines noted in WORKLOG-ROUTE-A A7). Pointer-typed parameters
// are opaque here on purpose.
//
// Hot-path rules: detours touch atomics and a preallocated ring only, then
// forward. No allocation, I/O, locks or GPU work in a detour.
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::ngx {

enum class CallKind : uint32_t {
    Init = 1,          // any NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_Init* flavor
    CreateFeature = 2,
    EvaluateFeature = 3,
    ReleaseFeature = 4,
    Shutdown = 5,
};

// DLSS parameter snapshot read from NVSDK_NGX_Parameter at Create/Evaluate.
// Populated only when the module is built against the locally pinned NVIDIA
// SDK (NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK); otherwise `valid` stays 0. Values the runtime did
// not provide keep their poison defaults â€” never invented.
struct EvalParams {
    uint32_t valid;         // 1 = fields below were read from the params object
    uint32_t reset;         // history reset flag; 0xFFFFFFFF unknown
    uint32_t in_width, in_height;    // render size; 0xFFFFFFFF unknown
    uint32_t out_width, out_height;  // output size; 0xFFFFFFFF unknown
    float jitter_x, jitter_y;        // NaN-free poison: 1e38f unknown
    float mv_scale_x, mv_scale_y;
    uint64_t color, depth, motion, output, exposure; // opaque resource ptrs
    // A13 disambiguation: the authoritative render size per evaluate.
    uint32_t subrect_width, subrect_height; // DLSS.Render.Subrect.Dimensions.*
    uint32_t perf_quality;   // PerfQualityValue at create; 0xFFFFFFFF unknown
    uint32_t create_flags;   // DLSS.Feature.Create.Flags; 0xFFFFFFFF unknown
    uint32_t overridden;     // 1 = host overrides were applied to this call
    // A67 (Astra R4): subrect origins per input; 0xFFFFFFFF unknown. Coverage
    // of a resource is base + size, not size alone.
    uint32_t color_base_x, color_base_y, depth_base_x, depth_base_y;
    uint32_t mv_base_x, mv_base_y, out_base_x, out_base_y;
};

// Takeover writes, all driven by na::settings (ini + overlay):
//  - ApplyCreateOverrides: render-preset hints written INTO the game's
//    parameter object at CreateFeature, before forwarding.
//  - Optimal-settings interception: after NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_GetCapabilityParameters
//    returns, the DLSSOptimalSettingsCallback pointer inside the capability
//    parameters is replaced with ours, which calls the original and then, per
//    settings.resolution_mode, rewrites OutWidth/OutHeight and the dynamic
//    min/max so the ENGINE renders at native (DLAA) or a chosen ratio.
bool ApplyCreateOverrides(void* ngx_parameter);
// The replacement callback itself (exposed for tests): `original` is the
// runtime's callback (may be null in tests), `ngx_parameter` the params.
int32_t OptimalSettingsCallback(void* ngx_parameter);
// Test hook: installs `original_callback` as the forwarded callback.
void SetOriginalOptimalCallbackForTest(void* original_callback);

struct OptimalStats {
    uint64_t calls;             // times our callback ran
    uint32_t last_user_w, last_user_h;   // what the game asked about
    uint32_t last_out_w, last_out_h;     // what we answered
    uint32_t last_overridden;   // 1 if we rewrote the answer
};
OptimalStats GetOptimalStats();

struct CallRecord {
    uint64_t qpc_ticks;     // QueryPerformanceCounter at detour entry
    uint32_t kind;          // CallKind
    uint32_t thread_id;
    int32_t  ngx_result;    // NVSDK_NGX_Result returned by the runtime
    uint32_t feature_id;    // CreateFeature only; else 0
    uint64_t app_id;        // Init only; else 0
    uint64_t handle;        // feature handle value (opaque identity); 0 if n/a
    uint64_t cmdlist;       // opaque ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* value; 0 if n/a
    EvalParams params;      // Create/Evaluate only; zeroed otherwise
};

// Reads the DLSS-relevant keys from an NVSDK_NGX_Parameter object. Returns
// false (out untouched apart from valid=0) when built without the SDK or on
// null input. Exposed for tests.
bool ReadEvalParams(const void* ngx_parameter, EvalParams* out);

struct Stats {
    uint64_t inits;
    uint64_t creates;
    uint64_t evaluates;
    uint64_t releases;
    uint64_t shutdowns;
    uint64_t records_dropped;   // ring overflow (drop-newest)
    uint32_t hooked_functions;  // how many exports were found and hooked
    uint32_t module_attached;   // 0/1
    uint32_t core_preloaded;    // 0/1 PreloadAndHookNgxCore succeeded
    uint32_t rr_seen;           // 0/1 the game created a Ray Reconstruction feature
    uint32_t fg_seen;           // 0/1 the game created a Frame Generation feature (id 11)
    // A30 menu evidence: successful DLSS super-resolution feature creations
    // and the sizes the LAST one was created with (0xFFFFFFFF = not read).
    uint64_t sr_creates;
    uint32_t last_sr_in_w, last_sr_in_h, last_sr_out_w, last_sr_out_h;
    // A42: creation failures and the fallback that keeps DLSS running.
    uint64_t create_failures;   // creations the runtime refused (after fallback)
    uint64_t create_recovered;  // creations rescued by dropping our hints
    uint32_t preset_rejected;   // 0/1 the runtime refused our preset hint; preset reset to Automatic
    uint32_t quality_rejected;  // 0/1 the runtime refused our quality value; game's value restored
    // A46 "Apply now": in-place re-creations of the game's DLSS feature.
    uint64_t reapplies;         // successful re-creations
    uint64_t reapply_failures;  // re-creations the runtime refused (old feature kept)
    uint32_t reapply_pending;   // 0/1 a request waits for the next evaluate
    // A66: game-driven DLSS creations recorded (CreateRecord ring), total.
    uint64_t create_records;
};

// A66/A67 (Astra's A65/A66 reviews): request-scoped render-size confirmation.
// Arm pins the requested size BEFORE the window is touched, so a creation the
// game performs from inside the resize is observed; MarkSizeConfirmationRestored
// sets the boundary after which evaluations count. The evaluate detour then
// counts CONSECUTIVE successful evaluations of the eligible feature (known
// non-zero handle created since arming), each at that size with coherent
// inputs (structural checks only: colour/depth/motion/output resources cover
// base + size for their subrect, motion at render or output size per the
// creation's MVLowRes flag, known non-zero output size, finite sub-pixel
// jitter, finite non-zero motion scale; anything unknown counts as NOT
// coherent). Failed or unreadable evaluations of that feature and evaluations
// with no handle break the streak. Deadlines: `create_timeout_ms` from the
// restoration for the creation, `confirm_timeout_ms` from the creation for
// the streak, `overall_timeout_ms` from arming for everything; enforced
// before every event and on every GetSizeConfirmation() read, so no menu
// page needs to stay open. One request runs; a new one supersedes. Returns
// the request id.
uint64_t ArmSizeConfirmation(uint32_t w, uint32_t h, uint32_t need, uint32_t create_timeout_ms, uint32_t confirm_timeout_ms,
                             uint32_t overall_timeout_ms);
// The window is back; evaluations from now on count (no-op for another id).
void MarkSizeConfirmationRestored(uint64_t id);
// Withdraws the running request (e.g. the user edited the mode meanwhile).
void CancelSizeConfirmation();
struct SizeConfirmation {
    uint64_t id;
    uint32_t state, reason;        // confirm::State / confirm::Reason (size_confirm.hpp)
    uint32_t req_w, req_h, need, streak;
    uint64_t evals, matching, mismatched, incoherent, failed, resets, unknown_handle, other_feature, before_restore, creations;
    uint32_t created_w, created_h, last_w, last_h;
    uint64_t armed_tick, restored_tick, created_tick, done_tick;
};
SizeConfirmation GetSizeConfirmation();

// A66 (R3): one bounded record per game-driven DLSS creation seen by the
// detour (the host's own re-creations bypass it): sequence, time, thread,
// feature, result, sizes, the size-confirmation request active at the time
// (0 = none: a baseline or game-menu creation), and the raw return addresses
// captured IN the detour. Modules are resolved when the record is READ (log
// thread), never in the detour. `game` marks frames inside the process's
// main executable; the rest are labelled by module and kept, so the chain is
// complete, not just its game part.
struct CreateFrame {
    uint64_t address;    // raw return address
    uint64_t rva;        // offset from the containing module's base (0 if unresolved)
    uint32_t game;       // 1 = main executable, 0 = other module, 2 = unresolved
    char module[40];     // file name of the containing module ("" if unresolved)
};
struct CreateRecord {
    uint64_t seq;                 // 1-based, increasing
    uint64_t tick;                // GetTickCount64 at the creation
    uint32_t thread_id;
    uint32_t feature_id;
    int32_t result;               // NVSDK_NGX_Result returned (1 = success)
    uint32_t in_w, in_h, out_w, out_h; // requested by the game's parameters (0xFFFFFFFF = unread)
    uint64_t confirm_id;          // ArmSizeConfirmation id active at the time (0 = none)
    uint32_t frame_count;
    CreateFrame frames[24];       // A71: 24 raw return addresses (was 10)
};
// Copies records with seq > `after_seq`, oldest first, up to `capacity`;
// returns the count. Keeps the last 16.
uint32_t GetCreateRecords(uint64_t after_seq, CreateRecord* out, uint32_t capacity);

// A71 (Astra A70 response: "follow the job producer path"): the game's calls
// at the Streamline boundary that carry its DLSS settings â€” the proxy's typed
// wrappers for slSetFeatureConstants (kind 1) and slGetFeatureSettings
// (kind 2) record them here. These run on the thread that applies the
// game's video settings (synchronously, in the game's own code), so their
// caller chains name the settings-apply path that the asynchronous creation
// job cannot show. The first three 32-bit words of the constants block are
// kept RAW and labelled raw: the pinned SL 1.1.1 header's layout for the
// game's SL 1.4.7 is unverified, so nothing is interpreted from them.
struct SlCallRecord {
    uint64_t seq, tick;
    uint32_t thread_id, kind, feature;
    uint32_t raw[3];              // first three dwords of the constants block (0xFFFFFFFF = none)
    uint32_t repeats;             // A73: identical calls (kind, feature, raw, first six callers) folded into this record
    uint32_t frame_count;
    CreateFrame frames[24];
};
void RecordSlCall(uint32_t kind, uint32_t feature, const void* consts, void* const* frames, uint32_t frame_count);
uint32_t GetSlCallRecords(uint64_t after_seq, SlCallRecord* out, uint32_t capacity);

// A46: ask the evaluate detour to re-create the game's current DLSS feature
// with the current preset choice at the next evaluate (same sizes, same
// quality value; the game keeps using its handle, forwarded to ours). No-op
// until the game has created a DLSS feature.
void RequestReapply();

// A42 fallback helpers (exposed for tests): clear all six preset hint keys
// to Default; write back a quality value.
bool StripPresetHints(void* ngx_parameter);
bool RestoreQualityValue(void* ngx_parameter, uint32_t perf_quality);

// Per-evaluate parameter reads + log records are diagnostics; off by default
// in play sessions to keep the hot path minimal (counters always run).
void SetDetailLogging(bool enabled);

// Deterministic attach (A17): loads the driver's NGX core (_nvngx.dll from
// HKLM\SOFTWARE\NVIDIA Corporation\Global\NGXCore\FullPath) BEFORE Streamline
// does and hooks it immediately, so Init/GetCapabilityParameters are
// observed from the very first call. The module reference is kept for the
// process lifetime. Idempotent; returns true when the core is hooked. Must
// not be called under the loader lock.
bool PreloadAndHookNgxCore();

// A30: the ORIGINAL runtime entry points (bypassing our detours) plus the
// parameter allocators, for the live Neural Rendering pass. Returns false
// until a module has been hooked.
bool GetRuntimeEntryPoints(void** create, void** evaluate, void** release, void** allocate_params,
                           void** destroy_params, void** get_capability_params);

// Resolves and hooks whichever observed exports `ngx_module` provides.
// Requires na::hooking::Initialize() to have succeeded. Returns the number
// of functions hooked (0 = nothing matched, or the module is not an NGX
// core). Must NOT be called under the Windows loader lock. Idempotent per
// module. A43: each hooked module keeps its OWN original entry points (the
// driver's two cores may both be hooked); feature DLLs such as
// nvngx_dlss.dll / nvngx_dlssnr.dll export the same names and are refused â€”
// hooking them forwarded the game's DLSS calls into the wrong DLL (session 6).
uint32_t InstallForModule(HMODULE ngx_module);

// True for the NGX core module names (_nvngx.dll, nvngx.dll) and the test
// stub (nvngx_stub.dll); `name` may be a bare name or a full path. False for
// every feature DLL. Exposed for tests.
bool IsNgxCoreModuleName(const wchar_t* name, size_t len);

// Snapshot of counters (atomic reads; internally consistent enough for
// diagnostics, not a coherent seqlock snapshot).
Stats GetStats();

// Drains up to `capacity` records into `out`, oldest first; returns count.
// Single-consumer; intended for the logger thread or tests.
uint32_t DrainRecords(CallRecord* out, uint32_t capacity);

// Starts a watcher that installs hooks automatically when an NGX core module
// (IsNgxCoreModuleName; never a feature DLL, A43) is loaded. Uses
// LdrRegisterDllNotification; the notification only queues the module and
// signals a worker thread, which installs OUTSIDE the loader lock. Also
// scans modules already loaded at call time. Returns false on failure.
// `sync_loader_hook` additionally patches kernelbase LoadLibraryExW so nvngx
// modules are hooked synchronously inside the load call (closes the Init
// race). DEFAULT OFF: correlated with early-startup crashes in TLOU
// (windows.storage/ucrtbase faults, WORKLOG A12); re-enable only for a
// dedicated diagnosis session.
bool StartModuleWatch(bool sync_loader_hook = false);
void StopModuleWatch();   // unregisters, joins the worker; hooks stay until
                          // na::hooking teardown

// Starts/stops a background thread that appends drained records as text
// lines to `log_path` (UTF-16 path, UTF-8 content) every ~250 ms. Never
// called from detours. One logger at a time.
bool StartLogger(const wchar_t* log_path);
void StopLogger();

} // namespace na::ngx


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\ngx_observe\size_confirm.hpp ---

// A66/A67 (Astra's A65 and A66 reviews): request-scoped render-size confirmation.
//
// One "Apply changes" request pins the requested render size immutably at
// admission, BEFORE the window is touched, so a DLSS creation the game
// performs from inside the resize itself (or on a worker before the window is
// back) is observed by the request. The caller marks the restoration
// boundary; only evaluations after it count. The monitor then requires
// `need` CONSECUTIVE successful evaluations of the eligible feature (the
// feature the game created since the request was armed, identified by a
// known non-zero handle), each at exactly the requested size with coherent
// inputs. Any evaluation at another size, with inputs that do not fit, with
// an unknown handle, or that failed, resets the streak. A replacing creation
// restarts the streak but never the request's own deadlines. Deadlines are
// enforced before every event, including creations, and a finished request
// is never revived.
//
// Pure state machine (no D3D, no time source of its own): callers feed
// events plus a millisecond tick. Exercised by tests/size_confirm_test.cpp.
#pragma once

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>

namespace na::ngx::confirm {

// A66 (Astra's A65 review, R2): scalar input checks for one evaluation.
// `missing` is the "never provided" sentinel ReadEvalParams leaves in place
// (1e38f). Jitter must be present, finite and within one pixel; the motion
// scale must be present, finite and non-zero on both axes. std::isfinite
// rejects +inf, -inf and NaN alike. A NEGATIVE finite scale is a legitimate
// coordinate convention and passes.
inline bool ScalarInputsCoherent(float jitter_x, float jitter_y, float mv_scale_x, float mv_scale_y, float missing = 1e38f)
{
    auto present_finite = [missing](float v) { return v != missing && std::isfinite(v); };
    return present_finite(jitter_x) && present_finite(jitter_y) &&
           std::fabs(jitter_x) <= 1.0f && std::fabs(jitter_y) <= 1.0f &&
           present_finite(mv_scale_x) && present_finite(mv_scale_y) &&
           mv_scale_x != 0.f && mv_scale_y != 0.f;
}

// A68 (Astra's A67 review, 2): subrect coverage in 64-bit on both axes, so a
// malformed base near 2^32 cannot wrap into a passing value. Unknown base
// (0xFFFFFFFF) or a missing resource is NOT coverage.
inline bool CoversSubrect(uint64_t res_w, uint64_t res_h, uint32_t bx, uint32_t by, uint32_t w, uint32_t h)
{
    if (bx == 0xFFFFFFFFu || by == 0xFFFFFFFFu || res_w == 0 || res_h == 0 || w == 0 || h == 0) return false;
    return res_w >= static_cast<uint64_t>(bx) + w && res_h >= static_cast<uint64_t>(by) + h;
}

// A68 (Astra's A67 review, 1): creation flags bound to the feature they were
// created with, keyed by the handle the game uses, so a feature created
// between two evaluations cannot change the MVLowRes reading of another.
// Bounded (8 live features); a re-created handle value overwrites its entry.
class FeatureFlags {
public:
    static constexpr uint32_t kUnknown = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    void Set(uint64_t handle, uint32_t flags)
    {
        if (!handle) return;
        for (Entry& e : e_) if (e.handle == handle) { e.flags = flags; return; }
        for (Entry& e : e_) if (e.handle == 0) { e = {handle, flags}; return; }
        e_[next_++ % kEntries] = {handle, flags}; // full: overwrite round-robin
    }
    uint32_t Get(uint64_t handle) const
    {
        if (!handle) return kUnknown;
        for (const Entry& e : e_) if (e.handle == handle) return e.flags;
        return kUnknown;
    }
    void Erase(uint64_t handle)
    {
        for (Entry& e : e_) if (e.handle == handle) e = {};
    }

private:
    static constexpr uint32_t kEntries = 8;
    struct Entry { uint64_t handle = 0; uint32_t flags = kUnknown; };
    Entry e_[kEntries]{};
    uint32_t next_ = 0;
};

enum class State : uint32_t {
    None = 0,
    Armed = 1,      // request admitted; creations are observed; the window is not back yet
    Counting = 2,   // window restored; evaluations of the eligible feature are counted
    Confirmed = 3,
    Failed = 4,
};
enum class Reason : uint32_t {
    None = 0,
    NoCreation = 1,        // no DLSS feature was created for this request in time
    DrawnAtOtherSize = 2,  // evaluations came, but at a size other than the requested one
    Incoherent = 3,        // evaluations at the requested size had inputs that did not fit / could not be verified
    TooFewEvaluations = 4, // the streak never reached `need` before the deadline
    Superseded = 5,        // a newer request replaced this one
    Cancelled = 6,         // the caller withdrew it (settings edited, nudge failed)
    UnknownFeature = 7,    // the creation(s) carried no usable handle, so nothing could be attributed
    NoRestoration = 8,     // the window was never reported restored within the request's lifetime
};

struct Snapshot {
    uint64_t id;                 // request generation (0 = never armed)
    uint32_t state, reason;      // State / Reason
    uint32_t req_w, req_h;       // immutable requested render size
    uint32_t need, streak;       // consecutive matching coherent evaluations required / current
    uint64_t evals;              // evaluations of the eligible feature seen while counting (incl. failed)
    uint64_t matching;           // ... successful, at the requested size, with coherent inputs
    uint64_t mismatched;         // ... at another size
    uint64_t incoherent;         // ... at the requested size with inputs that did not fit or could not be verified
    uint64_t failed;             // ... whose evaluate call failed or could not be read
    uint64_t resets;             // ... with the history-reset flag raised
    uint64_t unknown_handle;     // evaluations with no handle identity (break the streak, never match)
    uint64_t other_feature;      // evaluations of a different known feature (ignored for the streak)
    uint64_t before_restore;     // evaluations seen before the restoration boundary (not counted)
    uint64_t creations;          // DLSS features created since the request was armed
    uint32_t created_w, created_h; // size of the latest creation (0 = none yet)
    uint32_t last_w, last_h;     // size of the last counted evaluation (0 = none yet)
    uint64_t armed_tick, restored_tick, created_tick, done_tick;
};

struct EvalObs {
    uint32_t w, h;    // the evaluation's render size (subrect if present)
    bool coherent;    // inputs fit that size (caller's structural checks all verified)
    bool reset;       // history-reset flag raised
    bool ok;          // the evaluate call succeeded and its parameters were readable
    uint64_t handle;  // the feature handle the game evaluated (0 = unknown)
};

class Monitor {
public:
    // Arms a new request; anything running is superseded. `create_timeout_ms`
    // runs from the restoration boundary (a creation seen before it counts);
    // `confirm_timeout_ms` runs from the eligible creation; `overall_timeout_ms`
    // runs from arming and bounds everything, replacements included.
    uint64_t Arm(uint32_t w, uint32_t h, uint32_t need, uint64_t now, uint64_t create_timeout_ms, uint64_t confirm_timeout_ms,
                 uint64_t overall_timeout_ms)
    {
        if (Active()) finish(State::Failed, Reason::Superseded, now);
        Snapshot n{};
        n.id = ++last_id_;
        n.state = static_cast<uint32_t>(State::Armed);
        n.req_w = w;
        n.req_h = h;
        n.need = need ? need : 1;
        n.armed_tick = now;
        s_ = n;
        handle_ = 0;
        eligible_ = false;
        create_timeout_ = create_timeout_ms;
        confirm_timeout_ = confirm_timeout_ms;
        overall_deadline_ = now + overall_timeout_ms;
        return n.id;
    }
    // Withdraws the running request (no effect on a finished one).
    void Cancel(uint64_t now)
    {
        if (Active()) finish(State::Failed, Reason::Cancelled, now);
    }
    // The window is back at its size; evaluations from now on count.
    void MarkRestored(uint64_t now)
    {
        Tick(now);
        if (state() != State::Armed) return;
        s_.restored_tick = now;
        s_.state = static_cast<uint32_t>(State::Counting);
        s_.streak = 0;
    }
    // A successful DLSS super-resolution creation by the game. `handle` 0 =
    // unknown identity: recorded, but nothing can be attributed to it.
    void OnCreate(uint32_t w, uint32_t h, uint64_t handle, uint64_t now)
    {
        Tick(now);
        if (!Active()) return;
        ++s_.creations;
        s_.created_w = w;
        s_.created_h = h;
        s_.created_tick = now;
        handle_ = handle;
        eligible_ = handle != 0;
        s_.streak = 0; // a replaced feature starts its own streak; deadlines are unchanged
    }
    // One DLSS evaluation, successful or not.
    void OnEvaluate(const EvalObs& e, uint64_t now)
    {
        Tick(now);
        if (!Active()) return;
        if (state() == State::Armed) {
            ++s_.before_restore;
            return;
        }
        if (e.handle == 0) { // unknown identity: never a match, and it breaks the streak
            ++s_.unknown_handle;
            s_.streak = 0;
            return;
        }
        if (!eligible_ || e.handle != handle_) {
            ++s_.other_feature;
            return;
        }
        ++s_.evals;
        if (!e.ok) {
            ++s_.failed;
            s_.streak = 0;
            return;
        }
        s_.last_w = e.w;
        s_.last_h = e.h;
        if (e.reset) ++s_.resets;
        if (e.w != s_.req_w || e.h != s_.req_h) {
            ++s_.mismatched;
            s_.streak = 0;
            return;
        }
        if (!e.coherent) {
            ++s_.incoherent;
            s_.streak = 0;
            return;
        }
        ++s_.matching;
        if (++s_.streak >= s_.need) finish(State::Confirmed, Reason::None, now);
    }
    // Deadlines; safe to call from any poller. Runs before every event.
    void Tick(uint64_t now)
    {
        if (!Active()) return;
        if (now >= overall_deadline_) {
            finish(State::Failed, state() == State::Armed ? Reason::NoRestoration : failure_reason(), now);
            return;
        }
        if (state() != State::Counting) return;
        if (!eligible_) {
            // No usable creation yet (or an unattributable one): creation deadline from restoration.
            if (now - s_.restored_tick >= create_timeout_)
                finish(State::Failed, s_.creations ? Reason::UnknownFeature : Reason::NoCreation, now);
            return;
        }
        uint64_t since = s_.created_tick > s_.restored_tick ? s_.created_tick : s_.restored_tick;
        if (now - since >= confirm_timeout_) finish(State::Failed, failure_reason(), now);
    }
    bool Active() const { return state() == State::Armed || state() == State::Counting; }
    State state() const { return static_cast<State>(s_.state); }
    const Snapshot& snapshot() const { return s_; }

private:
    Reason failure_reason() const
    {
        if (!eligible_) return s_.creations ? Reason::UnknownFeature : Reason::NoCreation;
        if (s_.matching == 0 && s_.mismatched > 0) return Reason::DrawnAtOtherSize;
        if (s_.incoherent > 0 && s_.incoherent >= s_.mismatched) return Reason::Incoherent;
        if (s_.mismatched > 0) return Reason::DrawnAtOtherSize;
        return Reason::TooFewEvaluations;
    }
    void finish(State st, Reason r, uint64_t now)
    {
        s_.state = static_cast<uint32_t>(st);
        s_.reason = static_cast<uint32_t>(r);
        s_.done_tick = now;
    }
    Snapshot s_{};
    uint64_t last_id_ = 0;
    uint64_t handle_ = 0;
    bool eligible_ = false;
    uint64_t create_timeout_ = 0, confirm_timeout_ = 0, overall_deadline_ = 0;
};

} // namespace na::ngx::confirm

using Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr;

namespace na::nr {
namespace {

// ---- Compute shaders (compiled once at first use with d3dcompiler_47) ----
// hdr_to_sdr: HDR back buffer (mode 1 = PQ / HDR10, mode 2 = scRGB linear
// fp16, 1.0 = 80 nits) -> sRGB-encoded SDR working image for NR.
// sdr_to_hdr: applies NR's change as a ratio on the ORIGINAL HDR light
// values, so highlights above paper white are preserved, then re-encodes in
// the back buffer's own encoding (A60: any HDR format, not only HDR10).
const char kShaderSource[] = R"(
Texture2D<float4> t_a : register(t0);
Texture2D<float4> t_b : register(t1);
Texture2D<float4> t_c : register(t2);
RWTexture2D<float4> u_out : register(u0);
cbuffer C : register(b0) { float paper_white; uint width; uint height; uint mode; };

static const float m1 = 0.1593017578125, m2 = 78.84375, c1 = 0.8359375, c2 = 18.8515625, c3 = 18.6875;
float3 pq_to_nits(float3 e) {
    float3 ep = pow(saturate(e), 1.0 / m2);
    float3 num = max(ep - c1, 0.0), den = c2 - c3 * ep;
    return pow(num / den, 1.0 / m1) * 10000.0;
}
float3 nits_to_pq(float3 n) {
    float3 y = pow(saturate(n / 10000.0), m1);
    return pow((c1 + c2 * y) / (1.0 + c3 * y), m2);
}
// mode 2 (scRGB): linear, 1.0 = 80 nits (sRGB reference white), may exceed 1 or be negative.
float3 hdr_to_nits(float3 v) { return mode == 2 ? v * 80.0 : pq_to_nits(v); }
float3 nits_to_hdr(float3 n) { return mode == 2 ? n / 80.0 : nits_to_pq(n); }
float3 srgb_encode(float3 v) {
    v = saturate(v);
    return v <= 0.0031308 ? 12.92 * v : 1.055 * pow(v, 1.0 / 2.4) - 0.055;
}
float3 srgb_decode(float3 s) {
    s = saturate(s);
    return s <= 0.04045 ? s / 12.92 : pow((s + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
}
// Phase-4 scaled modes: bring render-size depth/motion to display size.
// Depth and motion choose the same nearest source pixel, preserving their
// surface correspondence. MVecScale carries the pixel-unit conversion.
SamplerState s_linear : register(s0);
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void upsample_depth(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    uint sw, sh; t_a.GetDimensions(sw, sh);
    uint2 src = uint2(min((id.x * sw) / width, sw - 1), min((id.y * sh) / height, sh - 1));
    u_out[id.xy] = t_a[src];
}
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void upsample_motion(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    uint sw, sh; t_a.GetDimensions(sw, sh);
    uint2 src = uint2(min((id.x * sw) / width, sw - 1), min((id.y * sh) / height, sh - 1));
    u_out[id.xy] = t_a[src];
}
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void hdr_to_sdr(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    float3 n = hdr_to_nits(t_a[id.xy].rgb) / paper_white;
    u_out[id.xy] = float4(srgb_encode(n), 1.0);
}
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void sdr_to_hdr(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= width || id.y >= height) return;
    float4 orig = t_a[id.xy];                            // original HDR pixel
    float3 nr = srgb_decode(t_b[id.xy].rgb);             // NR result, linear 0..1
    float3 sdr = srgb_decode(t_c[id.xy].rgb);            // NR input, linear 0..1
    // `precise`: the fast reciprocal would make x/x a hair under 1, and an
    // fp16 store truncates, so an unchanged pixel would lose a step per frame.
    precise float3 ratio = clamp((nr + 0.002) / (sdr + 0.002), 0.25, 4.0);
    ratio = all(nr == sdr) ? 1.0 : ratio;
    // scRGB is linear already: scale the stored value directly (a ratio of 1
    // reproduces the input bit for bit; a nits round trip would not).
    float3 out_rgb = mode == 2 ? orig.rgb * ratio : nits_to_pq(pq_to_nits(orig.rgb) * ratio);
    u_out[id.xy] = float4(out_rgb, orig.a);
}
)";

struct Constants { float paper_white; uint32_t width, height, mode; };

constexpr uint32_t kRing = 8; // A63: was 4; a game running 3+ frames ahead made the pass skip alternate frames (flicker)

using PfnCreate = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void*, uint32_t, void*, void**);
using PfnEvaluate = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void*, const void*, const void*, void*);
using PfnRelease = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void*);
using PfnAllocParams = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void**);
using PfnDestroyParams = int32_t(__cdecl*)(void*);

struct Frame {
    ComPtr<ID3D12CommandAllocator> alloc;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> depth, motion; // retain a submitted extent until its fence passes
    UINT64 fence_value = 0;
};

struct PendingInputs {
    ID3D12CommandList* list = nullptr; // identity only; never dereferenced/retained
    EvalInputs in{};
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> depth, motion;
};

struct State {
    std::mutex m;
    Runtime rt;
    std::atomic<uint32_t> has_rt{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> wants_inputs{0};
    // Device objects (game device).
    ComPtr<ID3D12Device> device;
    ComPtr<ID3D12GraphicsCommandList> list;
    Frame frames[kRing];
    uint32_t frame_index = 0;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Fence> fence;
    UINT64 fence_next = 1;
    bool signal_failed = false, reset_pending = true;
    uint64_t last_evaluate_tick = 0, setting_signature = 0;
    ComPtr<ID3D12RootSignature> root;
    ComPtr<ID3D12PipelineState> pso_hdr_to_sdr, pso_sdr_to_hdr, pso_up_depth, pso_up_motion;
    ComPtr<ID3D12DescriptorHeap> heap; // shader-visible, 4 tables x 4 slots x ring
    UINT heap_stride = 0;
    ComPtr<ID3D12QueryHeap> queries;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> query_readback;
    // NA-owned textures.
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> depth_copy, motion_copy;   // NPSR at rest (render size)
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> recording_depth, recording_motion;
    PendingInputs pending[16]; // bounded command-list recordings, no per-frame heap growth
    std::atomic<uint32_t> pending_count{0};
    ComPtr<ID3D12CommandQueue> input_queue;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> depth_up, motion_up;       // UAV at rest (display size, scaled modes only)
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> color_in;                  // NPSR at rest
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> nr_out;                    // UAV at rest
    ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> hdr_out;                   // UAV at rest (HDR only; back-buffer format)
    uint32_t hdr_mode_used = 0;                       // A60: shader mode of the current resources (1 PQ, 2 scRGB)
    DXGI_FORMAT motion_format = DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN;
    uint32_t in_w = 0, in_h = 0;         // depth/motion extent
    EvalInputs last_in{};
    uint32_t have_inputs = 0;
    uint32_t inputs_fresh = 0;          // a DLSS input copy was SUBMITTED since the last Present
    // Feature.
    void* params = nullptr;
    void* feature = nullptr;
    uint32_t feat_w = 0, feat_h = 0, feat_hdr = 0;
    DXGI_FORMAT feat_format = DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN;
    uint32_t feature_pending = 0;       // created this frame; evaluate from the next
    UINT64 feature_fence = 0;
    // Status.
    std::atomic<uint32_t> active{0}, latched{0}, hdr{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> evaluates{0}, skipped{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> inputs_seen{0}, input_overwrites{0}, missing_inputs{0}, busy_slots{0};
    std::atomic<float> last_ms{0.f}, avg_ms{0.f};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> samples{0};
    char reason[96] = "Neural Rendering is off.";
    uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
};
State g;
thread_local bool internal_submit = false;

void submit_locked(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue, UINT n, ID3D12CommandList* const* lists)
{
    // Our list can re-enter the host's Execute hook while g.m is held.
    const bool previous = internal_submit;
