    internal_submit = true;
    queue->ExecuteCommandLists(n, lists);
    internal_submit = previous;
}

void set_reason(const char* text)
{
    strncpy_s(g.reason, text, _TRUNCATE);
}
void latch(const char* text)
{
    set_reason(text);
    g.latched.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
    g.active.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
}

D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER transition(ID3D12Resource* r, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATES a, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATES b)
{
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER br{};
    br.Type = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_TYPE_TRANSITION;
    br.Transition.pResource = r;
    br.Transition.Subresource = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_ALL_SUBRESOURCES;
    br.Transition.StateBefore = a;
    br.Transition.StateAfter = b;
    return br;
}
D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER uav_barrier(ID3D12Resource* r)
{
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER br{};
    br.Type = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_TYPE_UAV;
    br.UAV.pResource = r;
    return br;
}

bool create_texture(UINT w, UINT h, DXGI_FORMAT fmt, bool uav, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATES state, ComPtr<ID3D12Resource>& out)
{
    D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp{};
    hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_DEFAULT;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d{};
    d.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    d.Width = w;
    d.Height = h;
    d.DepthOrArraySize = 1;
    d.MipLevels = 1;
    d.Format = fmt;
    d.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    d.Flags = uav ? D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_ALLOW_UNORDERED_ACCESS : D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_NONE;
    out.Reset();
    return SUCCEEDED(g.device->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &d, state, nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&out)));
}

bool uav_store_supported(DXGI_FORMAT fmt)
{
    D3D12_FEATURE_DATA_FORMAT_SUPPORT fs{};
    fs.Format = fmt;
    if (FAILED(g.device->CheckFeatureSupport(D3D12_FEATURE_FORMAT_SUPPORT, &fs, sizeof(fs)))) return false;
    return (fs.Support2 & D3D12_FORMAT_SUPPORT2_UAV_TYPED_STORE) != 0;
}

bool compile(const char* entry, ComPtr<ID3D12PipelineState>& pso)
{
    ComPtr<ID3DBlob> code, err;
    if (FAILED(D3DCompile(kShaderSource, sizeof(kShaderSource) - 1, "nr_pass.hlsl", nullptr, nullptr, entry, "cs_5_0",
                          D3DCOMPILE_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL3, 0, &code, &err)))
        return false;
    D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC pd{};
    pd.pRootSignature = g.root.Get();
    pd.CS.pShaderBytecode = code->GetBufferPointer();
    pd.CS.BytecodeLength = code->GetBufferSize();
    return SUCCEEDED(g.device->CreateComputePipelineState(&pd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&pso)));
}

bool ensure_device_locked(ID3D12Device* dev)
{
    if (g.device) return true;
    g.device = dev;
    for (Frame& f : g.frames)
        if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommandAllocator(D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_DIRECT, IID_PPV_ARGS(&f.alloc)))) return false;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateCommandList(0, D3D12_COMMAND_LIST_TYPE_DIRECT, g.frames[0].alloc.Get(), nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.list))))
        return false;
    g.list->Close();
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateFence(0, D3D12_FENCE_FLAG_NONE, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.fence)))) return false;

    // Root signature: table(t0..t2, u0) + 4 root constants.
    D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE ranges[2]{};
    ranges[0].RangeType = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE_TYPE_SRV;
    ranges[0].NumDescriptors = 3;
    ranges[0].BaseShaderRegister = 0;
    ranges[0].OffsetInDescriptorsFromTableStart = 0;
    ranges[1].RangeType = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE_TYPE_UAV;
    ranges[1].NumDescriptors = 1;
    ranges[1].BaseShaderRegister = 0;
    ranges[1].OffsetInDescriptorsFromTableStart = 3;
    D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER params[2]{};
    params[0].ParameterType = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_TABLE;
    params[0].DescriptorTable.NumDescriptorRanges = 2;
    params[0].DescriptorTable.pDescriptorRanges = ranges;
    params[0].ShaderVisibility = D3D12_SHADER_VISIBILITY_ALL;
    params[1].ParameterType = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_32BIT_CONSTANTS;
    params[1].Constants.Num32BitValues = 4;
    params[1].Constants.ShaderRegister = 0;
    params[1].ShaderVisibility = D3D12_SHADER_VISIBILITY_ALL;
    D3D12_STATIC_SAMPLER_DESC samp{};
    samp.Filter = D3D12_FILTER_MIN_MAG_MIP_LINEAR;
    samp.AddressU = samp.AddressV = samp.AddressW = D3D12_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_MODE_CLAMP;
    samp.ShaderRegister = 0;
    samp.ShaderVisibility = D3D12_SHADER_VISIBILITY_ALL;
    D3D12_ROOT_SIGNATURE_DESC rs{};
    rs.NumParameters = 2;
    rs.pParameters = params;
    rs.NumStaticSamplers = 1;
    rs.pStaticSamplers = &samp;
    ComPtr<ID3DBlob> sig, err;
    if (FAILED(D3D12SerializeRootSignature(&rs, D3D_ROOT_SIGNATURE_VERSION_1, &sig, &err))) return false;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateRootSignature(0, sig->GetBufferPointer(), sig->GetBufferSize(), IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.root)))) return false;
    if (!compile("hdr_to_sdr", g.pso_hdr_to_sdr) || !compile("sdr_to_hdr", g.pso_sdr_to_hdr) ||
        !compile("upsample_depth", g.pso_up_depth) || !compile("upsample_motion", g.pso_up_motion))
        return false;

    D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_DESC hd{};
    hd.Type = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE_CBV_SRV_UAV;
    hd.NumDescriptors = 16 * kRing;
    hd.Flags = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_FLAG_SHADER_VISIBLE;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateDescriptorHeap(&hd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.heap)))) return false;
    g.heap_stride = dev->GetDescriptorHandleIncrementSize(D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE_CBV_SRV_UAV);

    D3D12_QUERY_HEAP_DESC qh{};
    qh.Type = D3D12_QUERY_HEAP_TYPE_TIMESTAMP;
    qh.Count = 2 * kRing;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateQueryHeap(&qh, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.queries)))) return false;
    D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES rb{};
    rb.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_READBACK;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC bd{};
    bd.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
    bd.Width = 16 * kRing;
    bd.Height = bd.DepthOrArraySize = bd.MipLevels = 1;
    bd.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    bd.Layout = D3D12_TEXTURE_LAYOUT_ROW_MAJOR;
    return SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateCommittedResource(&rb, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &bd, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST,
                                                  nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&g.query_readback)));
}

void write_srv(uint32_t slot, ID3D12Resource* r, DXGI_FORMAT fmt)
{
    D3D12_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC d{};
    d.Format = fmt;
    d.ViewDimension = D3D12_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    d.Shader4ComponentMapping = D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING;
    d.Texture2D.MipLevels = 1;
    D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE h = g.heap->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
    h.ptr += static_cast<SIZE_T>(slot) * g.heap_stride;
    g.device->CreateShaderResourceView(r, &d, h);
}
void write_uav(uint32_t slot, ID3D12Resource* r, DXGI_FORMAT fmt)
{
    D3D12_UNORDERED_ACCESS_VIEW_DESC d{};
    d.Format = fmt;
    d.ViewDimension = D3D12_UAV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE h = g.heap->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
    h.ptr += static_cast<SIZE_T>(slot) * g.heap_stride;
    g.device->CreateUnorderedAccessView(r, nullptr, &d, h);
}
D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE table_gpu(uint32_t slot)
{
    D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE h = g.heap->GetGPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
    h.ptr += static_cast<UINT64>(slot) * g.heap_stride;
    return h;
}

void release_feature_locked()
{
    if (g.feature && g.rt.release_feature) reinterpret_cast<PfnRelease>(g.rt.release_feature)(g.feature);
    g.feature = nullptr;
    g.feature_pending = 0;
}

#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
NVSDK_NGX_Parameter* P() { return static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(g.params); }
#endif

// Creates (or re-creates) the NR feature for the current extent/format on
// the open list. Returns false and latches on failure.
bool ensure_feature_locked(uint32_t w, uint32_t h, bool hdr, DXGI_FORMAT bb_format)
{
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    if (g.feature && g.feat_w == w && g.feat_h == h && g.feat_hdr == (hdr ? 1u : 0u) && g.feat_format == bb_format)
        return true;
    release_feature_locked();
    if (!g.params) {
        void* p = nullptr;
        int32_t r = reinterpret_cast<PfnAllocParams>(g.rt.allocate_parameters)(&p);
        if (!NVSDK_NGX_SUCCEED(static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Result>(r)) || !p) {
            latch("Neural Rendering could not allocate its settings object.");
            return false;
        }
        g.params = p;
    }
    // Capability check once per size: DLSSNR.Available on the capability params.
    if (g.rt.get_capability_parameters) {
        void* cap = nullptr;
        int32_t r = reinterpret_cast<PfnAllocParams>(g.rt.get_capability_parameters)(&cap);
        int avail = 0;
        if (NVSDK_NGX_SUCCEED(static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Result>(r)) && cap)
            static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Parameter*>(cap)->Get("DLSSNR.Available", &avail);
        if (!avail) {
            latch("Neural Rendering runtime not found beside the game (nvngx_dlssnr.dll).");
            return false;
        }
    }
    const na::settings::Settings& st = na::settings::Get();
    NVSDK_NGX_Parameter* p = P();
    p->Set("CreationNodeMask", 1u);
    p->Set("VisibilityNodeMask", 1u);
    p->Set("Width", w);
    p->Set("Height", h);
    p->Set("OutWidth", w);
    p->Set("OutHeight", h);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Enabled", 1u);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Width", w);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Height", h);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Hint.Render.Preset", 0u);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Style", 0u);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Intensity", st.nr_intensity.load() / 100.f);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.LocalStructureStrength", st.nr_structure.load() / 100.f);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.LocalToneStrength", st.nr_tone.load() / 100.f);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.SkinStructureStrength", st.nr_skin.load() / 100.f);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.UseAutoMask", 1u);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.UICorrection", 0u);
    void* handle = nullptr;
    int32_t r = reinterpret_cast<PfnCreate>(g.rt.create_feature)(g.list.Get(), 18u, g.params, &handle);
    if (!NVSDK_NGX_SUCCEED(static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Result>(r)) || !handle) {
        char buf[96];
        snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Neural Rendering could not start (NVIDIA code 0x%08X).", static_cast<unsigned>(r));
        latch(buf);
        return false;
    }
    g.feature = handle;
    g.feat_w = w;
    g.feat_h = h;
    g.feat_hdr = hdr ? 1u : 0u;
    g.feat_format = bb_format;
    g.feature_pending = 1;
    g.reset_pending = true;
    return true;
#else
    (void)w; (void)h; (void)hdr; (void)bb_format;
    latch("Neural Rendering needs a build with the NVIDIA SDK.");
    return false;
#endif
}

bool evaluate_locked(bool hdr)
{
#if defined(NA_HAVE_NGX_SDK)
    const na::settings::Settings& st = na::settings::Get();
    NVSDK_NGX_Parameter* p = P();
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Intensity", st.nr_intensity.load() / 100.f);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.LocalStructureStrength", st.nr_structure.load() / 100.f);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.LocalToneStrength", st.nr_tone.load() / 100.f);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.SkinStructureStrength", st.nr_skin.load() / 100.f);
    bool scaled = g.in_w != g.width || g.in_h != g.height;
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Color", g.color_in.Get());
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Depth", scaled ? g.depth_up.Get() : g.depth_copy.Get());
    p->Set("DLSSNR.MVec", scaled ? g.motion_up.Get() : g.motion_copy.Get());
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Output", g.nr_out.Get());
    p->Set("DLSSNR.Reset", g.reset_pending || g.last_in.reset ? 1u : 0u);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.DepthInverted", g.last_in.depth_inverted);
    // Motion values are in normalised units; the scale carries pixels. When
    // upsampled to display size the scale grows by the same ratio.
    float sx = scaled ? static_cast<float>(g.width) / static_cast<float>(g.in_w) : 1.f;
    float sy = scaled ? static_cast<float>(g.height) / static_cast<float>(g.in_h) : 1.f;
    p->Set("DLSSNR.MVecScaleX", g.last_in.mv_scale_x * sx);
    p->Set("DLSSNR.MVecScaleY", g.last_in.mv_scale_y * sy);
    for (const char* k : {"Color", "Depth", "MVec", "Output"}) {
        char key[64];
        snprintf(key, sizeof(key), "DLSSNR.%sSubrectBaseX", k); p->Set(key, 0u);
        snprintf(key, sizeof(key), "DLSSNR.%sSubrectBaseY", k); p->Set(key, 0u);
        snprintf(key, sizeof(key), "DLSSNR.%sSubrectWidth", k); p->Set(key, g.width);
        snprintf(key, sizeof(key), "DLSSNR.%sSubrectHeight", k); p->Set(key, g.height);
    }
    (void)hdr;
    int32_t r = reinterpret_cast<PfnEvaluate>(g.rt.evaluate_feature)(g.list.Get(), g.feature, g.params, nullptr);
    if (!NVSDK_NGX_SUCCEED(static_cast<NVSDK_NGX_Result>(r))) {
        char buf[96];
        snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Neural Rendering stopped (NVIDIA code 0x%08X).", static_cast<unsigned>(r));
        latch(buf);
        return false;
    }
    g.reset_pending = false;
    g.last_evaluate_tick = GetTickCount64();
    g.evaluates.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    return true;
#else
    (void)hdr;
    return false;
#endif
}

void read_timings_locked(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue)
{
    // Read the slot from kRing-1 frames ago if its fence has passed.
    uint32_t old = (g.frame_index + 1) % kRing;
    Frame& f = g.frames[old];
    if (!f.fence_value || g.fence->GetCompletedValue() < f.fence_value) return;
    UINT64 freq = 0;
    if (FAILED(queue->GetTimestampFrequency(&freq)) || !freq) return;
    void* m = nullptr;
    D3D12_RANGE rr{static_cast<SIZE_T>(old) * 16, static_cast<SIZE_T>(old) * 16 + 16};
    if (FAILED(g.query_readback->Map(0, &rr, &m))) return;
    UINT64 st[2];
    std::memcpy(st, static_cast<uint8_t*>(m) + old * 16, 16);
    D3D12_RANGE none{0, 0};
    g.query_readback->Unmap(0, &none);
    if (st[1] > st[0] && st[0]) {
        float ms = 1000.f * static_cast<float>(st[1] - st[0]) / static_cast<float>(freq);
        g.last_ms.store(ms);
        float avg = g.avg_ms.load();
        g.avg_ms.store(avg > 0 ? avg * 0.9f + ms * 0.1f : ms);
        g.samples.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    }
    f.fence_value = 0; // consumed
}

} // namespace

void SetRuntime(const Runtime& rt)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    g.rt = rt;
    bool ok = rt.create_feature && rt.evaluate_feature && rt.release_feature && rt.allocate_parameters;
    g.has_rt.store(ok ? 1u : 0u, std::memory_order_release);
}
bool HasRuntime() { return g.has_rt.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0; }

bool WantsInputs()
{
    return HasRuntime() && na::settings::Get().nr_enabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != 0 &&
           !g.latched.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
}

void OnEvaluate(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, const EvalInputs& in)
{
    if (!list || !in.depth || !in.motion) return;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    if (!WantsInputs()) return;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Device> dev;
    if (FAILED(list->GetDevice(IID_PPV_ARGS(&dev)))) return;
    if (!ensure_device_locked(dev.Get())) {
        latch("Neural Rendering could not set up on the graphics card.");
        return;
    }
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC dd = in.depth->GetDesc(), md = in.motion->GetDesc();
    uint32_t w = static_cast<uint32_t>(dd.Width), h = dd.Height;
    if (static_cast<uint32_t>(md.Width) != w || md.Height != h) {
        g.skipped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return;
    }
    PendingInputs* pending = nullptr;
    for (auto& p : g.pending) if (p.list == list) { pending = &p; break; }
    if (!pending) for (auto& p : g.pending) if (!p.list) { pending = &p; break; }
    if (!pending) {
        latch("Neural Rendering stopped: too many unsubmitted input lists.");
        return;
    }
    if (!g.recording_depth || !g.recording_motion || g.recording_depth->GetDesc().Width != w ||
        g.recording_depth->GetDesc().Height != h || g.recording_motion->GetDesc().Format != md.Format) {
        // Depth: the game's depth plane copied into a plain float texture.
        if (!create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, false, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.recording_depth) ||
            !create_texture(w, h, md.Format, false, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.recording_motion)) {
            latch("Neural Rendering could not allocate its motion buffers.");
            return;
        }
    }
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER pre[4] = {
        transition(in.depth, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE),
        transition(in.motion, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE),
        transition(g.recording_depth.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST),
        transition(g.recording_motion.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST)};
    pre[0].Transition.Subresource = 0; // depth plane only
    list->ResourceBarrier(4, pre);
    D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_LOCATION src{}, dst{};
    src.Type = dst.Type = D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_TYPE_SUBRESOURCE_INDEX;
    src.pResource = in.depth;
    src.SubresourceIndex = 0;
    dst.pResource = g.recording_depth.Get();
    dst.SubresourceIndex = 0;
    list->CopyTextureRegion(&dst, 0, 0, 0, &src, nullptr);
    list->CopyResource(g.recording_motion.Get(), in.motion);
    D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER post[4] = {
        transition(in.depth, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
        transition(in.motion, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
        transition(g.recording_depth.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
        transition(g.recording_motion.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE)};
    post[0].Transition.Subresource = 0;
    list->ResourceBarrier(4, post);
    if (!pending->list) g.pending_count.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_release);
    pending->list = list;
    pending->in = in;
    pending->in.depth = pending->in.motion = nullptr; // no borrowed resource pointers outlive the call
    pending->depth = g.recording_depth;
    pending->motion = g.recording_motion;
    g.inputs_seen.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

void Execute(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue, UINT count, ID3D12CommandList* const* lists, ExecuteFn forward)
{
    if (internal_submit || !g.pending_count.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        forward(queue, count, lists);
        return;
    }
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    bool found = false;
    for (UINT i = 0; i < count; ++i)
        for (const auto& p : g.pending) if (p.list && p.list == lists[i]) found = true;
    if (!found) {
        lock.unlock();
        forward(queue, count, lists);
        return;
    }
    // Hold the short CPU lock through the actual forward/publication, so a
    // concurrent Present cannot consume a receipt before its copies are queued.
    forward(queue, count, lists);
    for (UINT i = 0; i < count; ++i) for (auto& p : g.pending) {
        if (!p.list || p.list != lists[i]) continue;
        if (g.input_queue && g.input_queue.Get() != queue) {
            latch("Neural Rendering stopped: input queues changed.");
        } else {
            g.input_queue = queue;
            g.last_in = p.in;
            g.depth_copy = p.depth;
            g.motion_copy = p.motion;
            g.in_w = static_cast<uint32_t>(p.depth->GetDesc().Width);
            g.in_h = p.depth->GetDesc().Height;
            g.motion_format = p.motion->GetDesc().Format;
            g.have_inputs = 1;
            if (g.inputs_fresh) g.input_overwrites.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            g.inputs_fresh = 1;
        }
        p = {};
        g.pending_count.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_release);
    }
}

void OnPresent(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue, ID3D12Resource* backbuffer, DXGI_COLOR_SPACE_TYPE color_space)
{
    if (!queue || !backbuffer) return;
    const na::settings::Settings& st = na::settings::Get();
    if (!st.nr_enabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
        g.active.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(g.m, std::try_to_lock);
        if (!lock.owns_lock()) return;
        g.inputs_fresh = 0; g.reset_pending = true;
        // Free the expensive feature/work images after their OWN GPU fence.
        // Keep the small input-copy set: recorded game lists can still refer
        // to it. No wait, working-set trim or process-wide cache purge.
        if (!g.signal_failed && g.feature && g.fence && g.fence->GetCompletedValue() != UINT64_MAX &&
            g.fence->GetCompletedValue() >= g.fence_next - 1) {
            release_feature_locked();
            if (g.params && g.rt.destroy_parameters)
                reinterpret_cast<PfnDestroyParams>(g.rt.destroy_parameters)(g.params);
            g.params = nullptr;
            g.color_in.Reset(); g.nr_out.Reset(); g.hdr_out.Reset();
            g.depth_up.Reset(); g.motion_up.Reset();
            for (auto& frame : g.frames) { frame.depth.Reset(); frame.motion.Reset(); }
            set_reason("Neural Rendering is off; processing memory released.");
        }
        return;
    }
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    if (!HasRuntime()) {
        set_reason("Neural Rendering is waiting for the DLSS connection.");
        return;
    }
    if (g.latched.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return;
    if (g.input_queue && g.input_queue.Get() != queue) {
        latch("Neural Rendering stopped: picture and inputs use different queues.");
        return;
    }
    if (!g.have_inputs) {
        set_reason("Waiting for the game's first DLSS frame.");
        g.skipped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return;
    }
    // A44 (session 7: the pause menu was being processed on stale depth and
    // motion): only a frame the game rendered through DLSS is processed.
    // Menu-only frames, loading screens and videos pass untouched.
    if (!g.inputs_fresh) {
        g.reset_pending = true;
        g.missing_inputs.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        set_reason("Neural Rendering is paused: the game is not drawing through DLSS right now (menus, videos).");
        g.active.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
        g.skipped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return;
    }
    g.inputs_fresh = 0;
    const uint64_t signature = st.nr_intensity.load() | (uint64_t(st.nr_structure.load()) << 8) |
        (uint64_t(st.nr_tone.load()) << 16) | (uint64_t(st.nr_skin.load()) << 24) | (uint64_t(st.nr_paper_white.load()) << 32);
    if (signature != g.setting_signature || GetTickCount64() - g.last_evaluate_tick > 250) g.reset_pending = true;
    g.setting_signature = signature;
    ComPtr<ID3D12Device> dev;
    if (FAILED(backbuffer->GetDevice(IID_PPV_ARGS(&dev))) || !ensure_device_locked(dev.Get())) {
        latch("Neural Rendering could not set up on the graphics card.");
        return;
    }
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC bb = backbuffer->GetDesc();
    uint32_t w = static_cast<uint32_t>(bb.Width), h = bb.Height;
    if (w < g.in_w || h < g.in_h) {
        g.reset_pending = true;
        set_reason("Neural Rendering is waiting: the drawing size is larger than the screen.");
        g.active.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
        g.skipped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return;
    }
    bool scaled = w != g.in_w || h != g.in_h; // Quality/Balanced/Performance: upsample depth/MV
    // A60: the chain's encoding decides (PQ / scRGB / SDR); the user can force
    // "HDR picture" (then the format picks PQ or scRGB) or "Standard picture".
    uint32_t hdr_mode = st.nr_hdr_mode.load();
    na::color::Encoding enc = na::color::Classify(bb.Format, color_space);
    if (hdr_mode == 2) enc = na::color::Encoding::SDR;
    else if (hdr_mode == 1 && enc == na::color::Encoding::SDR)
        enc = bb.Format == DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT ? na::color::Encoding::scRGB : na::color::Encoding::HDR10;
    if (enc == na::color::Encoding::HDR10 && bb.Format != DXGI_FORMAT_R10G10B10A2_UNORM) enc = na::color::Encoding::SDR;
    if (enc == na::color::Encoding::scRGB && bb.Format != DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT) enc = na::color::Encoding::SDR;
    bool hdr = na::color::IsHdr(enc);
    uint32_t shader_mode = na::color::ShaderMode(enc);
    // Frame slot: never wait; skip when the allocator from kRing frames ago is busy.
    Frame& f = g.frames[g.frame_index];
    const auto completed = g.fence->GetCompletedValue();
    if (completed == UINT64_MAX || g.signal_failed) { latch("Neural Rendering stopped: GPU completion is unavailable."); return; }
    if (f.fence_value && completed < f.fence_value) {
        g.reset_pending = true;
        g.busy_slots.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        set_reason("Neural Rendering skipped: its command buffer is still in use.");
        g.skipped.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return;
    }
    read_timings_locked(queue);
    // All eight in-flight frames can reference the same work textures and
    // feature. Replacing them requires the LAST submission, not just this slot.
    const bool work_changes = !g.color_in || g.width != w || g.height != h || g.hdr.load() != (hdr ? 1u : 0u) ||
        g.hdr_mode_used != shader_mode || g.feat_format != bb.Format;
    const bool scaled_changes = scaled && (!g.depth_up || g.depth_up->GetDesc().Width != w || g.depth_up->GetDesc().Height != h ||
        !g.motion_up || g.motion_up->GetDesc().Format != g.motion_format);
    if ((work_changes || scaled_changes) && completed < g.fence_next - 1) {
        g.reset_pending = true; g.active.store(0); g.skipped.fetch_add(1);
        set_reason("Neural Rendering is waiting for its previous picture size to finish.");
        return; // nonblocking: retry a later frame, keep every old resource alive
    }
    // Resources for this extent/mode.
    if (!g.color_in || g.width != w || g.height != h || g.hdr.load() != (hdr ? 1u : 0u) || g.hdr_mode_used != shader_mode ||
        g.feat_format != bb.Format) {
        bool ok = true;
        if (hdr) {
            ok &= uav_store_supported(DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT) && uav_store_supported(bb.Format);
            ok &= create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.color_in);
            ok &= create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, g.nr_out);
            ok &= create_texture(w, h, bb.Format, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, g.hdr_out); // re-encoded in the chain's format
        } else {
            ok &= uav_store_supported(bb.Format);
            ok &= create_texture(w, h, bb.Format, false, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, g.color_in);
            ok &= create_texture(w, h, bb.Format, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, g.nr_out);
            g.hdr_out.Reset();
        }
        if (!ok) {
            latch("Neural Rendering does not support this picture format on this graphics card.");
            return;
        }
        g.width = w;
        g.height = h;
        g.hdr.store(hdr ? 1u : 0u, std::memory_order_release);
        g.hdr_mode_used = shader_mode;
    }
    if (scaled && (!g.depth_up || g.depth_up->GetDesc().Width != w || g.depth_up->GetDesc().Height != h ||
                   !g.motion_up || g.motion_up->GetDesc().Format != g.motion_format)) {
        if (!uav_store_supported(DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT) || !uav_store_supported(g.motion_format) ||
            !create_texture(w, h, DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, g.depth_up) ||
            !create_texture(w, h, g.motion_format, true, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, g.motion_up)) {
            latch("Neural Rendering could not prepare the scaled-mode buffers on this graphics card.");
            return;
        }
    }

    f.depth = g.depth_copy;
    f.motion = g.motion_copy;
    if (FAILED(f.alloc->Reset()) || FAILED(g.list->Reset(f.alloc.Get(), nullptr))) {
        latch("Neural Rendering could not prepare its command buffer.");
        return;
    }
    if (!ensure_feature_locked(w, h, hdr, bb.Format)) {
        g.list->Close();
        return;
    }
    bool run = g.feature_pending == 0; // the create recorded this frame executes first
    uint32_t qbase = g.frame_index * 2;
    g.list->EndQuery(g.queries.Get(), D3D12_QUERY_TYPE_TIMESTAMP, qbase);
    if (run) {
        ID3D12DescriptorHeap* heaps[] = {g.heap.Get()};
        uint32_t slot = g.frame_index * 16;
        Constants c{static_cast<float>(st.nr_paper_white.load()), w, h, shader_mode};
        if (scaled) {
            // Phase 4: render-size depth/MV -> display size (nearest / bilinear).
            write_srv(slot + 8, g.depth_copy.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT);
            write_srv(slot + 9, g.depth_copy.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT);
            write_srv(slot + 10, g.depth_copy.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT);
            write_uav(slot + 11, g.depth_up.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT);
            write_srv(slot + 12, g.motion_copy.Get(), g.motion_format);
            write_srv(slot + 13, g.motion_copy.Get(), g.motion_format);
            write_srv(slot + 14, g.motion_copy.Get(), g.motion_format);
            write_uav(slot + 15, g.motion_up.Get(), g.motion_format);
            g.list->SetDescriptorHeaps(1, heaps);
            g.list->SetComputeRootSignature(g.root.Get());
            g.list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, 4, &c, 0);
            g.list->SetPipelineState(g.pso_up_depth.Get());
            g.list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, table_gpu(slot + 8));
            g.list->Dispatch((w + 7) / 8, (h + 7) / 8, 1);
            g.list->SetPipelineState(g.pso_up_motion.Get());
            g.list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, table_gpu(slot + 12));
            g.list->Dispatch((w + 7) / 8, (h + 7) / 8, 1);
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER bu[4] = {
                uav_barrier(g.depth_up.Get()), uav_barrier(g.motion_up.Get()),
                transition(g.depth_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
                transition(g.motion_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(4, bu);
        }
        if (hdr) {
            // Pass 1: PQ back buffer -> SDR working image.
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b1[2] = {
                transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PRESENT, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
                transition(g.color_in.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, b1);
            write_srv(slot + 0, backbuffer, bb.Format);
            write_srv(slot + 1, backbuffer, bb.Format);
            write_srv(slot + 2, backbuffer, bb.Format);
            write_uav(slot + 3, g.color_in.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT);
            g.list->SetDescriptorHeaps(1, heaps);
            g.list->SetComputeRootSignature(g.root.Get());
            g.list->SetPipelineState(g.pso_hdr_to_sdr.Get());
            g.list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, table_gpu(slot));
            g.list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, 4, &c, 0);
            g.list->Dispatch((w + 7) / 8, (h + 7) / 8, 1);
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b2[2] = {
                uav_barrier(g.color_in.Get()),
                transition(g.color_in.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, b2);
        } else {
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b1[2] = {
                transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PRESENT, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE),
                transition(g.color_in.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, b1);
            g.list->CopyResource(g.color_in.Get(), backbuffer);
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b2[2] = {
                transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE),
                transition(g.color_in.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, b2);
        }
        // NR evaluate: Color=color_in (NPSR), Depth/MVec (NPSR), Output=nr_out (UAV).
        if (!evaluate_locked(hdr)) {
            // Leave the back buffer presentable and give up this frame.
            g.reset_pending = true;
            if (scaled) {
                D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER restore[2] = {
                    transition(g.depth_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS),
                    transition(g.motion_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS)};
                g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, restore);
            }
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER back = transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PRESENT);
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(1, &back);
            g.list->Close();
            ID3D12CommandList* lists[] = {g.list.Get()};
            submit_locked(queue, 1, lists);
            f.fence_value = g.fence_next++;
            if (FAILED(queue->Signal(g.fence.Get(), f.fence_value))) {
                g.signal_failed = true; latch("Neural Rendering stopped: GPU completion signal failed.");
            }
            g.frame_index = (g.frame_index + 1) % kRing;
            return;
        }
        D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER nr_done = uav_barrier(g.nr_out.Get());
        g.list->ResourceBarrier(1, &nr_done);
        if (scaled) {
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER back_up[2] = {
                transition(g.depth_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS),
                transition(g.motion_up.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, back_up);
        }
        if (hdr) {
            // Pass 2: ratio on the original HDR -> PQ, then copy into the back buffer.
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b3[1] = {
                transition(g.nr_out.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(1, b3);
            write_srv(slot + 4, backbuffer, bb.Format);
            write_srv(slot + 5, g.nr_out.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT);
            write_srv(slot + 6, g.color_in.Get(), DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT);
            write_uav(slot + 7, g.hdr_out.Get(), bb.Format);
            g.list->SetDescriptorHeaps(1, heaps); // NGX changed state
            g.list->SetComputeRootSignature(g.root.Get());
            g.list->SetPipelineState(g.pso_sdr_to_hdr.Get());
            g.list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, table_gpu(slot + 4));
            g.list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, 4, &c, 0);
            g.list->Dispatch((w + 7) / 8, (h + 7) / 8, 1);
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b4[3] = {
                uav_barrier(g.hdr_out.Get()),
                transition(g.hdr_out.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE),
                transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(3, b4);
            g.list->CopyResource(backbuffer, g.hdr_out.Get());
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b5[3] = {
                transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PRESENT),
                transition(g.hdr_out.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS),
                transition(g.nr_out.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(3, b5);
        } else {
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b3[2] = {
                transition(g.nr_out.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE),
                transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, b3);
            g.list->CopyResource(backbuffer, g.nr_out.Get());
            D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b4[2] = {
                transition(backbuffer, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PRESENT),
                transition(g.nr_out.Get(), D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS)};
            g.list->ResourceBarrier(2, b4);
        }
    }
    g.list->EndQuery(g.queries.Get(), D3D12_QUERY_TYPE_TIMESTAMP, qbase + 1);
    g.list->ResolveQueryData(g.queries.Get(), D3D12_QUERY_TYPE_TIMESTAMP, qbase, 2, g.query_readback.Get(),
                             static_cast<UINT64>(g.frame_index) * 16);
    if (FAILED(g.list->Close())) {
        latch("Neural Rendering could not finish its command buffer.");
        return;
    }
    ID3D12CommandList* lists[] = {g.list.Get()};
    submit_locked(queue, 1, lists);
    f.fence_value = g.fence_next++;
    if (FAILED(queue->Signal(g.fence.Get(), f.fence_value))) {
        g.signal_failed = true; latch("Neural Rendering stopped: GPU completion signal failed."); return;
    }
    g.frame_index = (g.frame_index + 1) % kRing;
    if (g.feature_pending) {
        g.feature_pending = 0; // executes before the next frame's evaluate on the same queue
        set_reason("Neural Rendering is starting.");
    } else {
        g.active.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
        char why[96];
        snprintf(why, sizeof(why), "Neural Rendering is active%s%s%s.", hdr ? " (" : "", hdr ? (shader_mode == 2 ? "HDR picture, scRGB" : "HDR picture") : "",
                 scaled ? (hdr ? ", scaled mode)" : " (scaled mode)") : (hdr ? ")" : ""));
        set_reason(why);
    }
}

Stats GetStats()
{
    Stats s{};
    s.runtime_present = g.has_rt.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.active = g.active.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.latched_off = g.latched.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.hdr = g.hdr.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    s.evaluates = g.evaluates.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.skipped = g.skipped.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.inputs_seen = g.inputs_seen.load();
    s.input_overwrites = g.input_overwrites.load();
    s.missing_inputs = g.missing_inputs.load();
    s.busy_slots = g.busy_slots.load();
    s.last_ms = g.last_ms.load();
    s.avg_ms = g.avg_ms.load();
    s.gpu_samples = g.samples.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(g.m, std::try_to_lock);
    if (lock.owns_lock()) {
        s.gpu_submitted = g.fence_next - 1;
        s.gpu_completed = g.fence ? g.fence->GetCompletedValue() : 0;
        s.width = g.width;
        s.height = g.height;
        strncpy_s(s.reason, g.reason, _TRUNCATE);
    } else {
        strncpy_s(s.reason, "Updating.", _TRUNCATE);
    }
    return s;
}

void ResetLatch()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    if (g.signal_failed) { set_reason("Restart the game: Neural Rendering lost its GPU completion signal."); return; }
    g.reset_pending = true;
    g.latched.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
    set_reason("Neural Rendering is off.");
}

void Shutdown()
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g.m);
    if (g.signal_failed) { latch("Neural Rendering shutdown retained resources after a failed GPU signal."); return; }
    if (g.fence && g.fence_next > 1 && g.fence->GetCompletedValue() < g.fence_next - 1) {
        HANDLE ev = CreateEventW(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, nullptr);
        if (ev) {
            if (SUCCEEDED(g.fence->SetEventOnCompletion(g.fence_next - 1, ev))) WaitForSingleObject(ev, 2000);
            CloseHandle(ev);
        }
        if (g.fence->GetCompletedValue() < g.fence_next - 1) {
            latch("Neural Rendering shutdown is waiting for GPU completion; resources retained."); return;
        }
    }
    release_feature_locked();
    if (g.params && g.rt.destroy_parameters) reinterpret_cast<PfnDestroyParams>(g.rt.destroy_parameters)(g.params);
    g.params = nullptr;
    g.depth_copy.Reset();
    g.motion_copy.Reset();
    g.recording_depth.Reset();
    g.recording_motion.Reset();
    for (auto& p : g.pending) p = {};
    g.pending_count.store(0);
    g.input_queue.Reset();
    g.depth_up.Reset();
    g.motion_up.Reset();
    g.color_in.Reset();
    g.nr_out.Reset();
    g.hdr_out.Reset();
    g.hdr_mode_used = 0;
    g.pso_hdr_to_sdr.Reset();
    g.pso_sdr_to_hdr.Reset();
    g.pso_up_depth.Reset();
    g.pso_up_motion.Reset();
    g.root.Reset();
    g.heap.Reset();
    g.queries.Reset();
    g.query_readback.Reset();
    g.list.Reset();
    for (Frame& f : g.frames) { f.alloc.Reset(); f.depth.Reset(); f.motion.Reset(); f.fence_value = 0; }
    g.fence.Reset();
    g.device.Reset();
    g.have_inputs = 0;
    g.inputs_fresh = 0;
    g.in_w = g.in_h = g.width = g.height = 0;
    g.active.store(0);
    g.latched.store(0);
    g.frame_index = 0;
    g.fence_next = 1; g.reset_pending = true; g.last_evaluate_tick = 0; g.setting_signature = 0;
    set_reason("Neural Rendering is off.");
}

} // namespace na::nr


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\nr\nr_pass.hpp ---

// Live Neural Rendering pass (phase 3 of docs/NR-INSERTION-DESIGN.md, A30).
//
// Runs NVIDIA's neural renderer (NGX feature 18, "DLSSNR" community
// contract) on the finished frame at Present, on the game's direct queue,
// using the game's own NGX instance (entry points handed in by the proxy).
// Depth and motion vectors are copied into NeuralAlias-owned textures at the
// DLSS evaluate (the game's buffers rotate), published at queue submission.
// Scaled modes upsample depth/motion to the display extent before evaluation.
//
// Colour handling (docs/NR-TLOU-I-REPLAY.md): NR is proven on display-encoded
// SDR. An HDR10 (PQ, RGB10A2) chain is decoded by a compute pass
// (PQ -> nits / paper white -> sRGB-encoded RGBA16F) before NR and
// re-encoded after. SDR chains are processed in their own format.
//
// Safety: any NGX or D3D failure latches the pass OFF with a reason; the
// frame is then presented untouched. Never waits on the GPU in hooks. All
// settings come from na::settings (menu + ini); nothing runs unless
// `nr_enabled` is set and the runtime entry points are present.
#pragma once

#include <d3d12.h>
#include <dxgi1_4.h>

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::nr {

// NGX runtime entry points (the ORIGINAL functions of the loaded NGX core,
// not our detours), supplied by the proxy after the core is hooked.
struct Runtime {
    void* create_feature = nullptr;      // NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_CreateFeature
    void* evaluate_feature = nullptr;    // NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_EvaluateFeature
    void* release_feature = nullptr;     // NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_ReleaseFeature
    void* allocate_parameters = nullptr; // NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_AllocateParameters
    void* destroy_parameters = nullptr;  // NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_DestroyParameters
    void* get_capability_parameters = nullptr; // NVSDK_NGX_D3D12_GetCapabilityParameters
};
void SetRuntime(const Runtime& rt);
bool HasRuntime();

// Inputs seen at the DLSS evaluate (same meaning as capture::EvalInputs).
struct EvalInputs {
    ID3D12Resource* depth = nullptr;   // NPSR at call time
    ID3D12Resource* motion = nullptr;  // NPSR at call time
    uint32_t render_w = 0, render_h = 0, out_w = 0, out_h = 0;
    float mv_scale_x = 0, mv_scale_y = 0;
    uint32_t reset = 0;
    uint32_t depth_inverted = 0;
};

// True when the evaluate detour should read parameters and call OnEvaluate.
bool WantsInputs();
// Inside the DLSS evaluate detour, after the original evaluate, on the
// game's open list: copies depth + motion into our textures.
void OnEvaluate(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, const EvalInputs& in);

// Wrap the actual queue forward. Recording a future list is NOT a ready frame.
// Publish its inputs only after submission, serialized with OnPresent. No GPU
// wait is added. The host calls this for every ExecuteCommandLists; test hosts
// must do likewise. This does not consume the adapter's Execute subscription.
using ExecuteFn = void (STDMETHODCALLTYPE*)(ID3D12CommandQueue*, UINT, ID3D12CommandList* const*);
void Execute(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue, UINT count, ID3D12CommandList* const* lists, ExecuteFn forward);

// At Present, before the original Present, with the back buffer in PRESENT
// state. Runs the pass on `queue` if enabled and healthy; otherwise returns
// immediately. `color_space` is the swap chain's last SetColorSpace1 value
// (DXGI_COLOR_SPACE_CUSTOM when never set).
void OnPresent(ID3D12CommandQueue* queue, ID3D12Resource* backbuffer, DXGI_COLOR_SPACE_TYPE color_space);

struct Stats {
    uint32_t runtime_present;   // entry points supplied
    uint32_t active;            // feature created and passes running
    uint32_t latched_off;       // a failure turned the pass off (reason below)
    uint32_t hdr;               // 1 = HDR10 transform path in use
    uint32_t width, height;     // pass extent
    uint64_t evaluates;         // NR evaluates submitted
    uint64_t skipped;           // frames skipped (no inputs / extent mismatch)
    uint64_t inputs_seen, input_overwrites, missing_inputs, busy_slots;
    uint64_t gpu_submitted, gpu_completed;
    float last_ms, avg_ms;      // GPU time of the whole pass (transform + NR)
    uint32_t gpu_samples;
    char reason[96];            // plain-language reason when not active
};
Stats GetStats();
// Clears a latch so the pass may try again (e.g. after the user re-enables).
void ResetLatch();
void Shutdown(); // releases the feature and all resources (game exit / tests)

} // namespace na::nr


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\nvof\flow_expand.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/nvof/flow_expand.hpp"

#include <d3dcompiler.h>

using Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr;

namespace na::nvof {
namespace {
const char kSource[] = R"(
Texture2D<int2>  t_grid  : register(t0);
Texture2D<float> t_depth : register(t1);
RWTexture2D<float2> u_out   : register(u0);
RWTexture2D<float>  u_valid : register(u1);
cbuffer C : register(b0) { uint grid; uint out_w; uint out_h; uint pad; float depth_clear; float depth_tol; float consistency_px; float vector_scale; };

float2 cell(int2 c, int2 dim) {
    c = clamp(c, int2(0, 0), dim - 1);
    // S10.5 -> engine pixels (NVOFA-specific), then x vector_scale when the
    // engine ran on a downscaled image (A58): output pixels = engine px * scale.
    return float2(t_grid[c]) / 32.0 * vector_scale;
}
// Raw depth at a block centre (clamped to the frame).
float cell_depth(int2 c, int2 dim) {
    c = clamp(c, int2(0, 0), dim - 1);
    int2 p = int2(c.x * grid + grid / 2, c.y * grid + grid / 2);
    p = min(p, int2(out_w - 1, out_h - 1));
    return t_depth[p];
}
// A39-DEPTH-EXPAND-REVIEW R1: the same finite / [0,1] / not-clear rule for
// the destination depth AND every neighbouring block-centre depth.
bool depth_ok(float d) {
    return (asuint(d) & 0x7fffffffu) < 0x7f800000u && d != depth_clear && d >= 0.0 && d <= 1.0;
}
void neighbours(uint2 id, int2 dim, out int2 c0, out float2 f) {
    float2 g = (float2(id) + 0.5) / float(grid) - 0.5;
    c0 = int2(floor(g));
    f = g - float2(c0);
}

[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void expand_plain(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= out_w || id.y >= out_h) return;
    uint gw, gh; t_grid.GetDimensions(gw, gh);
    int2 dim = int2(gw, gh), c0; float2 f;
    neighbours(id.xy, dim, c0, f);
    float2 v00 = cell(c0, dim), v10 = cell(c0 + int2(1, 0), dim);
    float2 v01 = cell(c0 + int2(0, 1), dim), v11 = cell(c0 + int2(1, 1), dim);
    u_out[id.xy] = lerp(lerp(v00, v10, f.x), lerp(v01, v11, f.x), f.y);
}

[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void expand_depth(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    if (id.x >= out_w || id.y >= out_h) return;
    uint gw, gh; t_grid.GetDimensions(gw, gh);
    int2 dim = int2(gw, gh), c0; float2 f;
    neighbours(id.xy, dim, c0, f);
    float d = t_depth[id.xy];
    bool d_ok = depth_ok(d);
    int2 cs[4] = { c0, c0 + int2(1, 0), c0 + int2(0, 1), c0 + int2(1, 1) };
    float bw[4] = { (1 - f.x) * (1 - f.y), f.x * (1 - f.y), (1 - f.x) * f.y, f.x * f.y };
    float2 acc = 0; float wsum = 0;
    float2 vmin = 1e9, vmax = -1e9;
    float tol = depth_tol * (abs(d) + 0.01);
    [unroll] for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        float cd = cell_depth(cs[i], dim);
        float2 v = cell(cs[i], dim);
        bool same = d_ok && depth_ok(cd) && abs(cd - d) <= tol;
        float w = same ? bw[i] : 0.0;
        acc += v * w; wsum += w;
        if (same) { vmin = min(vmin, v); vmax = max(vmax, v); }
    }
    if (wsum > 0.0) {
        float2 spread = vmax - vmin;
        bool consistent = max(spread.x, spread.y) <= consistency_px;
        u_out[id.xy] = acc / wsum;
        u_valid[id.xy] = consistent ? 1.0 : 0.0;
    } else {
        // No block shares this pixel's surface (or invalid depth): not usable.
        u_out[id.xy] = 0;
        u_valid[id.xy] = 0.0;
    }
}
)";
struct Constants { uint32_t grid, out_w, out_h, pad; float depth_clear, depth_tol, consistency_px, vector_scale; };

bool compile(ID3D12Device* dev, ID3D12RootSignature* root, const char* entry, ComPtr<ID3D12PipelineState>& pso)
{
    ComPtr<ID3DBlob> code, err;
    if (FAILED(D3DCompile(kSource, sizeof(kSource) - 1, "flow_expand.hlsl", nullptr, nullptr, entry, "cs_5_0",
                          D3DCOMPILE_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL3 | D3DCOMPILE_IEEE_STRICTNESS, 0, &code, &err)))
        return false;
    D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC pd{};
    pd.pRootSignature = root;
    pd.CS.pShaderBytecode = code->GetBufferPointer();
    pd.CS.BytecodeLength = code->GetBufferSize();
    return SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateComputePipelineState(&pd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&pso)));
}
} // namespace

bool FlowExpand::Init(ID3D12Device* device, uint32_t max_frames_in_flight)
{
    if (!device || !max_frames_in_flight) return false;
    device_ = device;
    slots_ = max_frames_in_flight;
    D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE ranges[2]{};
    ranges[0].RangeType = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE_TYPE_SRV;
    ranges[0].NumDescriptors = 2;
    ranges[1].RangeType = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE_TYPE_UAV;
    ranges[1].NumDescriptors = 2;
    ranges[1].OffsetInDescriptorsFromTableStart = 2;
    D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER params[2]{};
    params[0].ParameterType = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_TABLE;
    params[0].DescriptorTable.NumDescriptorRanges = 2;
    params[0].DescriptorTable.pDescriptorRanges = ranges;
    params[1].ParameterType = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_32BIT_CONSTANTS;
    params[1].Constants.Num32BitValues = sizeof(Constants) / 4;
    D3D12_ROOT_SIGNATURE_DESC rs{};
    rs.NumParameters = 2;
    rs.pParameters = params;
    ComPtr<ID3DBlob> sig, err;
    if (FAILED(D3D12SerializeRootSignature(&rs, D3D_ROOT_SIGNATURE_VERSION_1, &sig, &err))) return false;
    if (FAILED(device->CreateRootSignature(0, sig->GetBufferPointer(), sig->GetBufferSize(), IID_PPV_ARGS(&root_)))) return false;
    if (!compile(device, root_.Get(), "expand_plain", pso_plain_) || !compile(device, root_.Get(), "expand_depth", pso_depth_)) return false;
    D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_DESC hd{};
    hd.Type = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE_CBV_SRV_UAV;
    hd.NumDescriptors = 4 * slots_;
    hd.Flags = D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_FLAG_SHADER_VISIBLE;
    if (FAILED(device->CreateDescriptorHeap(&hd, IID_PPV_ARGS(&heap_)))) return false;
    stride_ = device->GetDescriptorHandleIncrementSize(D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_HEAP_TYPE_CBV_SRV_UAV);
    // 1x1 dummies for unused slots.
    D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp{};
    hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_DEFAULT;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d{};
    d.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    d.Width = 1; d.Height = 1; d.DepthOrArraySize = 1; d.MipLevels = 1; d.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT; d.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    if (FAILED(device->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &d, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&dummy_srv_))))
        return false;
    d.Flags = D3D12_RESOURCE_FLAG_ALLOW_UNORDERED_ACCESS;
    return SUCCEEDED(device->CreateCommittedResource(&hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &d, D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_UNORDERED_ACCESS, nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(&dummy_uav_)));
}

bool FlowExpand::Dispatch(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, ID3D12Resource* grid_tex, uint32_t grid, ID3D12Resource* out,
                          uint32_t out_w, uint32_t out_h, uint32_t frame_slot)
{
    return dispatch(list, grid_tex, grid, nullptr, out, out_w, out_h, frame_slot);
}

bool FlowExpand::DispatchDepthAware(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, ID3D12Resource* grid_tex, uint32_t grid,
                                    const ExpandParams& params, ID3D12Resource* out, uint32_t out_w, uint32_t out_h,
                                    uint32_t frame_slot)
{
    if (!params.depth) return false;
    // A39-DEPTH-EXPAND-REVIEW R2: reject bad numeric configuration before recording.
    auto finite = [](float v) { return v == v && v - v == 0.0f; };
    if (!finite(params.depth_tolerance) || params.depth_tolerance < 0.0f) return false;
    if (!finite(params.consistency_px) || params.consistency_px < 0.0f) return false;
    if (!finite(params.depth_clear) || params.depth_clear < 0.0f || params.depth_clear > 1.0f) return false;
    return dispatch(list, grid_tex, grid, &params, out, out_w, out_h, frame_slot);
}

bool FlowExpand::dispatch(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, ID3D12Resource* grid_tex, uint32_t grid, const ExpandParams* p,
                          ID3D12Resource* out, uint32_t out_w, uint32_t out_h, uint32_t frame_slot)
{
    if (!list || !pso_plain_ || !grid_tex || !out || !grid || !out_w || !out_h || frame_slot >= slots_) return false;
    if (p) {
        D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC dd = p->depth->GetDesc();
        if (dd.Width != out_w || dd.Height != out_h) return false;
        if (p->validity_out) {
            D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC vd = p->validity_out->GetDesc();
            if (vd.Width != out_w || vd.Height != out_h) return false;
        }
    }
    uint32_t base = frame_slot * 4;
    auto cpu = [&](uint32_t i) {
        D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE h = heap_->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
        h.ptr += static_cast<SIZE_T>(base + i) * stride_;
        return h;
    };
    D3D12_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC sd{};
    sd.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16_SINT;
    sd.ViewDimension = D3D12_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    sd.Shader4ComponentMapping = D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING;
    sd.Texture2D.MipLevels = 1;
    device_->CreateShaderResourceView(grid_tex, &sd, cpu(0));
    sd.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT;
    device_->CreateShaderResourceView(p ? p->depth : dummy_srv_.Get(), &sd, cpu(1));
    D3D12_UNORDERED_ACCESS_VIEW_DESC ud{};
    ud.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32_FLOAT;
    ud.ViewDimension = D3D12_UAV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(out, nullptr, &ud, cpu(2));
    ud.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT;
    device_->CreateUnorderedAccessView(p && p->validity_out ? p->validity_out : dummy_uav_.Get(), nullptr, &ud, cpu(3));
    Constants c{grid, out_w, out_h, 0, p ? p->depth_clear : 1.0f, p ? p->depth_tolerance : 0.0f, p ? p->consistency_px : 0.0f, vector_scale_};
    ID3D12DescriptorHeap* heaps[] = {heap_.Get()};
    list->SetDescriptorHeaps(1, heaps);
    list->SetComputeRootSignature(root_.Get());
    list->SetPipelineState(p ? pso_depth_.Get() : pso_plain_.Get());
    D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE g = heap_->GetGPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart();
    g.ptr += static_cast<UINT64>(base) * stride_;
    list->SetComputeRootDescriptorTable(0, g);
    list->SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(1, sizeof(Constants) / 4, &c, 0);
    list->Dispatch((out_w + 7) / 8, (out_h + 7) / 8, 1);
    return true;
}

} // namespace na::nvof


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\nvof\flow_expand.hpp ---

// Expands the NVOFA output grid (R16G16_SINT, S10.5 fixed point, one vector
// per gridÃ—grid block; NVOFA-specific decode Ã·32) into the MotionArbiter's
// raw-flow texture (RG32_FLOAT, full resolution, pixels, p_previous âˆ’
// p_current).
//
// Two modes:
//  - Plain: bilinear between block centres (no depth). Bleeds motion across
//    depth edges (A37-MOTION-CHAIN-ACK); use only when no depth exists.
//  - Depth-aware (recommended): the four neighbouring block vectors are
//    weighted by bilinear weight Ã— depth similarity between THIS pixel's raw
//    depth and the raw depth at each block centre, so a background pixel next
//    to a moving foreground takes the background block's vector. A validity
//    texture (arbiter mask domain: 1 valid, 0 not usable) is written: 0 when
//    no neighbour block matches the pixel's depth, or when the depth-matching
//    neighbours disagree by more than `consistency_px` (flow inconsistency at
//    disocclusions), or when the pixel's depth is the clear value. Every
//    neighbouring block-centre depth is validated with the same
//    finite / [0,1] / not-clear rule before it can contribute
//    (A39-DEPTH-EXPAND-REVIEW R1).
// Parameters are validated before recording (R2): tolerance and
// consistency finite and >= 0, clear depth finite in [0,1]; otherwise
// DispatchDepthAware returns false and records nothing.
// CONSUMER CONTRACT: the validity output must be consumed. A pixel can hold a
// finite vector with validity 0; feed the validity as `raw_valid` to
// Arbiter::DispatchDeriveResidual so it is folded into the derived validity,
// and combine any supplier validity the same way. "Finite" never means
// "trusted".
// Dispatch records only; caller owns resources and barriers:
//   grid and depth in NON_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE, outputs in UNORDERED_ACCESS.
#pragma once

#include <d3d12.h>
#include <wrl/client.h>

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::nvof {

struct ExpandParams {
    ID3D12Resource* depth = nullptr;      // R32_FLOAT raw z/w, full resolution (optional)
    ID3D12Resource* validity_out = nullptr; // R32_FLOAT, full resolution (optional; requires depth)
    float depth_clear = 1.0f;
    float depth_tolerance = 0.02f;        // |Î” raw depth| accepted as "same surface" (relative to |depth|+0.01)
    float consistency_px = 2.0f;          // max spread among depth-matching neighbours before validity = 0
};

class FlowExpand {
public:
    bool Init(ID3D12Device* device, uint32_t max_frames_in_flight = 4);
    // A58: when the engine ran on an image downscaled by `s` (block size in
    // output pixels = grid * s), decoded vectors are multiplied by `s`.
    // Rejects non-finite or non-positive values (keeps the previous scale).
    bool SetVectorScale(float s)
    {
        if (!(s == s) || s <= 0.0f || s - s != 0.0f) return false;
        vector_scale_ = s;
        return true;
    }
    // Plain bilinear expansion.
    bool Dispatch(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, ID3D12Resource* grid_rg16_sint, uint32_t grid,
                  ID3D12Resource* out_rg32f, uint32_t out_w, uint32_t out_h, uint32_t frame_slot);
    // Depth-aware expansion with validity.
    bool DispatchDepthAware(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, ID3D12Resource* grid_rg16_sint, uint32_t grid,
                            const ExpandParams& params, ID3D12Resource* out_rg32f, uint32_t out_w, uint32_t out_h,
                            uint32_t frame_slot);

private:
    bool dispatch(ID3D12GraphicsCommandList* list, ID3D12Resource* grid_tex, uint32_t grid, const ExpandParams* params,
                  ID3D12Resource* out, uint32_t out_w, uint32_t out_h, uint32_t frame_slot);
    float vector_scale_ = 1.0f;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Device> device_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12RootSignature> root_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12PipelineState> pso_plain_, pso_depth_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12DescriptorHeap> heap_;
    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D12Resource> dummy_srv_, dummy_uav_;
    UINT stride_ = 0;
    uint32_t slots_ = 0;
};

} // namespace na::nvof


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\nvof\nvof_flow.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/nvof/nvof_flow.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

#pragma warning(push, 3)
#include <nvOpticalFlowCommon.h>
#include <nvOpticalFlowD3D12.h>
#pragma warning(pop)

namespace na::nvof {
namespace {

using PfnCreateInstance = NV_OF_STATUS(NVOFAPI*)(uint32_t, NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST*);

const char* status_name(NV_OF_STATUS s)
{
    switch (s) {
    case NV_OF_SUCCESS: return "success";
    case NV_OF_ERR_OF_NOT_AVAILABLE: return "optical flow hardware not available";
    case NV_OF_ERR_UNSUPPORTED_DEVICE: return "unsupported device";
    case NV_OF_ERR_DEVICE_DOES_NOT_EXIST: return "device does not exist";
    case NV_OF_ERR_INVALID_PTR: return "invalid pointer";
    case NV_OF_ERR_INVALID_PARAM: return "invalid parameter";
    case NV_OF_ERR_INVALID_CALL: return "invalid call order";
    case NV_OF_ERR_INVALID_VERSION: return "invalid API version";
    case NV_OF_ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY: return "out of memory";
    case NV_OF_ERR_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialised";
    default: return "error";
    }
}

bool load_api(void** module, NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST** api, char* error, size_t cap)
{
    HMODULE m = LoadLibraryW(L"nvofapi64.dll");
    if (!m) {
        snprintf(error, cap, "nvofapi64.dll not found (NVIDIA driver required)");
        return false;
    }
    auto create = reinterpret_cast<PfnCreateInstance>(GetProcAddress(m, "NvOFAPICreateInstanceD3D12"));
    if (!create) {
        snprintf(error, cap, "NvOFAPICreateInstanceD3D12 missing from nvofapi64.dll");
        return false; // module stays loaded (never unloaded on any path)
    }
    auto* list = new NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST{};
    NV_OF_STATUS s = create(NV_OF_API_VERSION, list);
    if (s != NV_OF_SUCCESS) {
        snprintf(error, cap, "NvOFAPICreateInstanceD3D12 (API %u.%u): %s", NV_OF_API_MAJOR_VERSION, NV_OF_API_MINOR_VERSION, status_name(s));
        delete list;
        return false; // module stays loaded
    }
    *module = m;
    *api = list;
    return true;
}

} // namespace

bool QueryCaps(ID3D12Device* device, Caps* out, char* error, size_t error_cap)
{
    if (!device || !out) return false;
    *out = Caps{};
    void* module = nullptr;
    NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST* api = nullptr;
    char err[192] = {};
    if (!load_api(&module, &api, err, sizeof(err))) {
        if (error) strncpy_s(error, error_cap, err, _TRUNCATE);
        return false;
    }
    NvOFHandle h = nullptr;
    NV_OF_STATUS s = api->nvCreateOpticalFlowD3D12(device, &h);
    if (s != NV_OF_SUCCESS || !h) {
        if (error) snprintf(error, error_cap, "nvCreateOpticalFlowD3D12: %s", status_name(s));
        delete api;
        return false; // module stays loaded
    }
    uint32_t n = 0;
    if (api->nvOFGetCaps(h, NV_OF_CAPS_SUPPORTED_OUTPUT_GRID_SIZES, nullptr, &n) == NV_OF_SUCCESS && n > 0 && n <= 8) {
        api->nvOFGetCaps(h, NV_OF_CAPS_SUPPORTED_OUTPUT_GRID_SIZES, out->grid_sizes, &n);
        out->grid_count = n;
    }
    uint32_t one = 1;
    api->nvOFGetCaps(h, NV_OF_CAPS_WIDTH_MIN, &out->min_w, &one);
    one = 1; api->nvOFGetCaps(h, NV_OF_CAPS_HEIGHT_MIN, &out->min_h, &one);
    one = 1; api->nvOFGetCaps(h, NV_OF_CAPS_WIDTH_MAX, &out->max_w, &one);
    one = 1; api->nvOFGetCaps(h, NV_OF_CAPS_HEIGHT_MAX, &out->max_h, &one);
    out->available = true;
    api->nvOFDestroy(h);
    delete api;
    (void)module; // nvofapi64.dll stays loaded for the process lifetime (unloading it
                  // while D3D12 objects are alive crashed at exit; same policy as the NGX core)
    return true;
}

Flow::~Flow() { Shutdown(); }

void Flow::set_error(const char* what, int status)
{
    snprintf(error_, sizeof(error_), "%s: %s", what, status_name(static_cast<NV_OF_STATUS>(status)));
}

bool Flow::Init(ID3D12Device* device, uint32_t width, uint32_t height, uint32_t grid, uint32_t perf_level)
{
    if (registered_) {
        snprintf(error_, sizeof(error_), "Init refused: %u registrations outstanding", registered_);
        return false;
    }
    Shutdown();
    if (!device || width == 0 || height == 0 || (grid != 1 && grid != 2 && grid != 4)) {
        snprintf(error_, sizeof(error_), "invalid Init arguments");
        return false;
    }
    NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST* api = nullptr;
    if (!load_api(&module_, &api, error_, sizeof(error_))) return false;
    api_ = api;
    NvOFHandle h = nullptr;
    NV_OF_STATUS s = api->nvCreateOpticalFlowD3D12(device, &h);
    if (s != NV_OF_SUCCESS || !h) {
        set_error("nvCreateOpticalFlowD3D12", s);
        return false;
    }
    handle_ = h;
    width_ = width;
    height_ = height;
    grid_ = grid;
    NV_OF_INIT_PARAMS ip{};
    ip.width = width;
    ip.height = height;
    ip.outGridSize = static_cast<NV_OF_OUTPUT_VECTOR_GRID_SIZE>(grid);
    ip.hintGridSize = NV_OF_HINT_VECTOR_GRID_SIZE_UNDEFINED;
    ip.mode = NV_OF_MODE_OPTICALFLOW;
    ip.perfLevel = static_cast<NV_OF_PERF_LEVEL>(perf_level);
    ip.enableExternalHints = NV_OF_FALSE;
    ip.enableOutputCost = NV_OF_FALSE;
    ip.disparityRange = NV_OF_STEREO_DISPARITY_RANGE_UNDEFINED;
    ip.enableRoi = NV_OF_FALSE;
    ip.predDirection = NV_OF_PRED_DIRECTION_FORWARD;
    ip.enableGlobalFlow = NV_OF_FALSE;
    ip.inputBufferFormat = NV_OF_BUFFER_FORMAT_ABGR8;
    s = api->nvOFInit(h, &ip);
    if (s != NV_OF_SUCCESS) {
        set_error("nvOFInit", s);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool Flow::RegisterInput(ID3D12Resource* bgra8, ID3D12Fence* fence, uint64_t wait_value, uint64_t signal_value, BufferHandle* out)
{
    if (!handle_ || !bgra8 || !fence || !out) return false;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d = bgra8->GetDesc();
    if (d.Format != DXGI_FORMAT_B8G8R8A8_UNORM || d.Width < width_ || d.Height < height_) {
        snprintf(error_, sizeof(error_), "input must be B8G8R8A8_UNORM of at least %ux%u", width_, height_);
        return false;
    }
    auto* api = static_cast<NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST*>(api_);
    NV_OF_REGISTER_RESOURCE_PARAMS_D3D12 rp{};
    rp.resource = bgra8;
    rp.inputFencePoint.fence = fence;
    rp.inputFencePoint.value = wait_value;
    NvOFGPUBufferHandle hb = nullptr;
    rp.hOFGpuBuffer = &hb;
    rp.outputFencePoint.fence = fence;
    rp.outputFencePoint.value = signal_value;
    NV_OF_STATUS s = api->nvOFRegisterResourceD3D12(static_cast<NvOFHandle>(handle_), &rp);
    if (s != NV_OF_SUCCESS) {
        set_error("nvOFRegisterResourceD3D12 (input)", s);
        return false;
    }
    *out = hb;
    ++registered_;
    return true;
}

bool Flow::RegisterOutput(ID3D12Resource* rg16_sint, ID3D12Fence* fence, uint64_t wait_value, uint64_t signal_value, BufferHandle* out)
{
    if (!handle_ || !rg16_sint || !fence || !out) return false;
    D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC d = rg16_sint->GetDesc();
    if (d.Format != DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16_SINT || d.Width < OutWidth() || d.Height < OutHeight()) {
        snprintf(error_, sizeof(error_), "output must be R16G16_SINT of at least %ux%u", OutWidth(), OutHeight());
        return false;
    }
    auto* api = static_cast<NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST*>(api_);
    NV_OF_REGISTER_RESOURCE_PARAMS_D3D12 rp{};
    rp.resource = rg16_sint;
    rp.inputFencePoint.fence = fence;
    rp.inputFencePoint.value = wait_value;
    NvOFGPUBufferHandle hb = nullptr;
    rp.hOFGpuBuffer = &hb;
    rp.outputFencePoint.fence = fence;
    rp.outputFencePoint.value = signal_value;
    NV_OF_STATUS s = api->nvOFRegisterResourceD3D12(static_cast<NvOFHandle>(handle_), &rp);
    if (s != NV_OF_SUCCESS) {
        set_error("nvOFRegisterResourceD3D12 (output)", s);
        return false;
    }
    *out = hb;
    ++registered_;
    return true;
}

bool Flow::Unregister(BufferHandle h)
{
    if (!api_ || !h) return false;
    NV_OF_UNREGISTER_RESOURCE_PARAMS_D3D12 up{};
    up.hOFGpuBuffer = static_cast<NvOFGPUBufferHandle>(h);
    NV_OF_STATUS s = static_cast<NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST*>(api_)->nvOFUnregisterResourceD3D12(&up);
    if (s != NV_OF_SUCCESS) {
        set_error("nvOFUnregisterResourceD3D12", s);
        return false;
    }
    if (registered_) --registered_;
    return true;
}

bool Flow::Execute(BufferHandle input_current, BufferHandle reference_previous, BufferHandle output, ID3D12Fence* wait_fence,
                   uint64_t wait_value, ID3D12Fence* signal_fence, uint64_t signal_value, bool disable_temporal_hints)
{
    if (!handle_ || !input_current || !reference_previous || !output || !wait_fence || !signal_fence) return false;
    auto* api = static_cast<NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST*>(api_);
    NV_OF_FENCE_POINT in_fp{wait_fence, wait_value};
    NV_OF_FENCE_POINT out_fp{signal_fence, signal_value};
    NV_OF_EXECUTE_INPUT_PARAMS_D3D12 ip{};
    ip.inputFrame = static_cast<NvOFGPUBufferHandle>(input_current);
    ip.referenceFrame = static_cast<NvOFGPUBufferHandle>(reference_previous);
    ip.externalHints = nullptr;
    ip.disableTemporalHints = disable_temporal_hints ? NV_OF_TRUE : NV_OF_FALSE;
    ip.hPrivData = nullptr;
    ip.numRois = 0;
    ip.roiData = nullptr;
    ip.numFencePoints = 1;
    ip.fencePoint = &in_fp;
    NV_OF_EXECUTE_OUTPUT_PARAMS_D3D12 op{};
    op.outputBuffer = static_cast<NvOFGPUBufferHandle>(output);
    op.outputCostBuffer = nullptr;
    op.hPrivData = nullptr;
    op.fencePoint = &out_fp;
    NV_OF_STATUS s = api->nvOFExecuteD3D12(static_cast<NvOFHandle>(handle_), &ip, &op);
    if (s != NV_OF_SUCCESS) {
        char last[160] = {};
        uint32_t n = sizeof(last) - 1;
        api->nvOFGetLastError(static_cast<NvOFHandle>(handle_), last, &n);
        snprintf(error_, sizeof(error_), "nvOFExecuteD3D12: %s%s%s", status_name(s), last[0] ? " - " : "", last);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool Flow::Shutdown()
{
    if (registered_) {
        snprintf(error_, sizeof(error_), "Shutdown refused: %u registrations outstanding", registered_);
        return false;
    }
    if (api_ && handle_) static_cast<NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST*>(api_)->nvOFDestroy(static_cast<NvOFHandle>(handle_));
    handle_ = nullptr;
    delete static_cast<NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST*>(api_);
    api_ = nullptr;
    module_ = nullptr; // never unloaded (see QueryCaps)
    return true;
}

} // namespace na::nvof


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\nvof\nvof_flow.hpp ---

// NVIDIA hardware optical flow (NVOFA) through the driver's nvofapi64.dll â€”
// the Route A Tier-3 raw-flow supplier for the MotionArbiter (M-001).
//
// Thin C++ wrapper over the Optical Flow SDK 5.0 D3D12 interface
// (third_party/nvidia-optical-flow-sdk, MIT-licensed headers; the runtime is
// the driver's). No SDK helper classes are used. Everything is asynchronous:
// Execute() waits on a fence the caller signals when the inputs are ready and
// signals another fence when the flow is written; nothing here blocks.
//
// Conventions: with input = CURRENT frame and reference = PREVIOUS frame the
// forward flow at a pixel is p_previous - p_current in pixels (S10.5 fixed
// point in the output), which is exactly the arbiter's v convention.
// Input frames: B8G8R8A8_UNORM textures (state COMMON), display-encoded SDR.
// Output: R16G16_SINT texture of (width/grid) x (height/grid), state COMMON.
//
// TEARDOWN RULE (found empirically with driver 32.0.16.1692, A36): every
// ID3D12Resource that was registered with the engine must be Unregistered
// AND have its last reference released BEFORE Shutdown()/destruction of the
// Flow object, and nvofapi64.dll is never unloaded. Releasing a registered
// resource after nvOFDestroy crashes at that release (the engine attaches
// per-resource state that Destroy frees). Fences handed to Execute must
// also outlive the Flow object. nvofapi64.dll is never unloaded, on any
// path, once LoadLibrary succeeded. Shutdown() refuses while registrations
// are outstanding; the class is non-copyable and non-movable.
// Output dimensions are ceil(width/grid) x ceil(height/grid).
#pragma once

#include <d3d12.h>
#include <wrl/client.h>

#include <cstdint>

namespace na::nvof {

struct Caps {
    bool available = false;
    uint32_t grid_sizes[8] = {};
    uint32_t grid_count = 0;
    uint32_t min_w = 0, min_h = 0, max_w = 0, max_h = 0;
};
// Loads nvofapi64.dll and asks the driver; false when NVOFA is not present.
bool QueryCaps(ID3D12Device* device, Caps* out, char* error, size_t error_cap);

using BufferHandle = void*;

class Flow {
public:
    Flow() = default;
    ~Flow();
    // Owns raw engine/API pointers: never copied or moved (A36-NVOF-ACK).
    Flow(const Flow&) = delete;
    Flow& operator=(const Flow&) = delete;
    Flow(Flow&&) = delete;
    Flow& operator=(Flow&&) = delete;
    // perf_level: 5 slow/best, 10 medium, 20 fast. grid: 1, 2 or 4 (must be
    // in Caps::grid_sizes). Returns false with LastError() filled on failure.
    // Refuses to re-initialise while registrations are outstanding.
    bool Init(ID3D12Device* device, uint32_t width, uint32_t height, uint32_t grid, uint32_t perf_level);
    // Registers caller-owned resources. `fence` is used for the registration
    // handshake (the API waits for `wait_value` and signals `signal_value`).
    bool RegisterInput(ID3D12Resource* bgra8, ID3D12Fence* fence, uint64_t wait_value, uint64_t signal_value, BufferHandle* out);
    bool RegisterOutput(ID3D12Resource* rg16_sint, ID3D12Fence* fence, uint64_t wait_value, uint64_t signal_value, BufferHandle* out);
    // Returns false (LastError() filled) when the driver refuses; the
    // registration then still counts as outstanding.
    bool Unregister(BufferHandle h);
    // Registrations not yet unregistered. Must be 0 before Shutdown().
    uint32_t OutstandingRegistrations() const { return registered_; }
    // Asynchronous flow computation input -> reference. The engine waits until
    // `wait_fence` >= wait_value and sets `signal_fence` = signal_value when done.
    bool Execute(BufferHandle input_current, BufferHandle reference_previous, BufferHandle output,
                 ID3D12Fence* wait_fence, uint64_t wait_value, ID3D12Fence* signal_fence, uint64_t signal_value,
                 bool disable_temporal_hints);
    // Destroys the engine. Returns false and does NOTHING while registrations
    // are outstanding (destroying then would break the teardown rule). The
    // destructor calls Shutdown() and, if refused, leaks the engine rather
    // than crash; callers verify with OutstandingRegistrations() first.
    bool Shutdown();
    const char* LastError() const { return error_; }
    uint32_t Grid() const { return grid_; }
    uint32_t OutWidth() const { return (width_ + grid_ - 1) / grid_; }
    uint32_t OutHeight() const { return (height_ + grid_ - 1) / grid_; }
    // S10.5 -> pixels.
    static float Decode(int16_t v) { return static_cast<float>(v) / 32.0f; }

private:
    void set_error(const char* what, int status);
    void* api_ = nullptr;      // NV_OF_D3D12_API_FUNCTION_LIST*
    void* handle_ = nullptr;   // NvOFHandle
    void* module_ = nullptr;   // HMODULE
    uint32_t width_ = 0, height_ = 0, grid_ = 1;
    uint32_t registered_ = 0;
    char error_[192] = {};
};

} // namespace na::nvof


// --- FILE: C:\Users\cortf\Documents\Codex\2026-09-21\we-re-starting-a-reusable-dx11\.worktrees\route-a\outputs\NeuralAliasAbundance\src\host_dx12\overlay\freeze_watchdog.cpp ---

#include "host_dx12/overlay/freeze_watchdog.hpp"

#include <windows.h>
#include <dbghelp.h>
#include <tlhelp32.h>

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

namespace na::freeze {

namespace {

// x64 unwinding with the kernel's own tables: no dbghelp, no symbol
// handler, no loader lock. Safe to run while the target thread is suspended.
uint32_t walk_context(CONTEXT& ctx, uint64_t* frames, uint32_t capacity)
{
    uint32_t n = 0;
    while (n < capacity && ctx.Rip) {
        frames[n++] = ctx.Rip;
        DWORD64 base = 0;
        RUNTIME_FUNCTION* rf = RtlLookupFunctionEntry(ctx.Rip, &base, nullptr);
        if (!rf) { // leaf function: the return address is at RSP
            if (!ctx.Rsp) break;
            ctx.Rip = *reinterpret_cast<DWORD64*>(ctx.Rsp);
            ctx.Rsp += 8;
            continue;
        }
        void* handler_data = nullptr;
        DWORD64 establisher = 0;
        RtlVirtualUnwind(UNW_FLAG_NHANDLER, base, ctx.Rip, rf, &ctx, &handler_data, &establisher, nullptr);
    }
    return n;
}

} // namespace

uint32_t CaptureAllThreads(Capture* out)
{
    if (!out) return 0;
    std::memset(out, 0, sizeof(*out));
    DWORD pid = GetCurrentProcessId(), self = GetCurrentThreadId();
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return 0;
    THREADENTRY32 te{};
    te.dwSize = sizeof(te);
    if (Thread32First(snap, &te)) {
        do {
            if (te.th32OwnerProcessID != pid || te.th32ThreadID == self) continue;
            ++out->threads_total;
            if (out->thread_count >= kMaxThreads) continue;
            ThreadStack& t = out->threads[out->thread_count];
            t.thread_id = te.th32ThreadID;
            HANDLE h = OpenThread(THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME | THREAD_GET_CONTEXT | THREAD_QUERY_INFORMATION, FALSE, te.th32ThreadID);
            if (!h) { ++out->thread_count; continue; }
            if (SuspendThread(h) != static_cast<DWORD>(-1)) {
                t.suspended = 1;
                CONTEXT ctx{};
                ctx.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_CONTROL | CONTEXT_INTEGER;
                if (GetThreadContext(h, &ctx)) {
                    __try {
                        t.frame_count = walk_context(ctx, t.frames, kMaxFrames);
                    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
                        // a torn stack: keep what was read
                    }
                }
                ResumeThread(h);
            }
            CloseHandle(h);
            ++out->thread_count;
        } while (Thread32Next(snap, &te));
    }
    CloseHandle(snap);
    return out->thread_count;
}

uint32_t FormatStack(const ThreadStack& t, char* out, uint32_t capacity)
{
    if (!out || capacity < 32) return 0;
